- **Sātyaki serves at the Rājasūya** — The Vṛṣṇi chief Sātyaki attends Yudhiṣṭhira's imperial consecration at Indraprastha and holds the royal umbrella in the coronation rites _(actors: Sātyaki, Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/satyaki.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva)
- **Sātyaki survives the Sauptika night** — Away from the camp with the Pāṇḍavas and Kṛṣṇa during Aśvatthāman's night-massacre, Sātyaki stands among the seven survivors of the Pāṇḍava side _(actors: Sātyaki, the Pāṇḍavas, Kṛṣṇa, Aśvatthāman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/satyaki.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sauptika Parva)
- **Sātyaki's Day-14 drive through Droṇa's array** — Sent by Yudhiṣṭhira on Arjuna's trail, Sātyaki circumvents Droṇa, defeats Kṛtavarma and Duryodhana, and kills Jalasandha and Sudarśana, one of only three cars to cross the Kaurava host _(actors: Sātyaki, Yudhiṣṭhira, Droṇa, Kṛtavarma, Jalasandha, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/satyaki.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva)
- **Satyavatī bears Vyāsa, fulfilling the Pitṛs' promise** — By the sage Parāśara the reborn river-maiden bears the island-born Kṛṣṇa Dvaipāyana Vyāsa, divider of the Veda — the redemption clause of her sentence _(actors: Satyavatī, Parāśara, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/acchoda-satyavati-rebirth.md) — (Matsya Purāṇa, 14; Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~57)
- **Satyavatī weds Śāntanu and bears Citrāṅgada and Vicitravīrya** — The final terms of the decree play out as the Pitṛ-daughter becomes Kuru queen and mother of the doomed heirs whose deaths force the niyoga _(actors: Satyavatī, Śāntanu, Citrāṅgada, Vicitravīrya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/acchoda-satyavati-rebirth.md) — (Matsya Purāṇa, 14; Harivaṃśa, Pitṛ-kalpa)
- **Śiva and Pārvatī feed the cursing sage** — disguised as householders, Pārvatī (Annapūrṇā/Viśālākṣī) and Śiva feast Vyāsa and his disciples lavishly, then expose the hypocrisy of a dharma-teacher who curses over food _(actors: Śiva, Pārvatī/Annapūrṇā, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa-banished-from-kashi.md) — (Skanda Purāṇa, Kāśī-khaṇḍa, Uttarārdha 96)
- **Śiva and Umā grant Kṛṣṇa sixteen boons** — After months of the vow Śiva appears with Umā and grants eight boons each, including the promised son _(actors: Śiva, Umā, Kṛṣṇa, Upamanyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/upamanyu.md) — (Mahābhārata, Anuśāsana Parva XV)
- **Slaying of Dhenuka and Pralamba** — the boy Balarāma kills the ass-demon of Tālavana and fist-smashes the disguised cowherd-demon Pralamba _(actors: Balarāma, Dhenuka, Pralamba)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/balarama.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa, 10.15, 10.18)
- **Slaying of Vajranābha** — revived from a club-blow by the blast of Kṛṣṇa's Pāñcajanya, Pradyumna beheads Vajranābha with Kṛṣṇa's discus; Gada slays Sunābha and Nikumbha flees to Ṣaṭpura _(actors: Pradyumna, Vajranābha, Kṛṣṇa, Gada, Sāmba)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pradyumna-prabhavati-vajranabha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva 98, Dutt)
- **Somaka refuses heaven without his priest** — dying, Somaka learns his chaplain burns in hell for Jantu's killing and demands to share or wholly assume the punishment, overriding Yama's law that each soul reaps its own deed alone _(actors: Somaka, the purohita, Yama/Dharma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jantu-somaka.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva CXXVIII)
- **Somaka's hundred wives and single son** — the Pāñcāla king Somaka, long childless despite a hundred queens of equal rank, at last begets in old age one frail son, Jantu, on whom every mother's whole hope hangs _(actors: Somaka, his hundred queens, Jantu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jantu-somaka.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva CXXVII)
- **Sudakṣiṇa's abhicāra against Dwārakā** — the Kāśī prince Sudakṣiṇa wins a conditional boon from Śiva at Avimukta and raises the Dakṣiṇāgni as a blazing kṛtyā fire-demon that marches on Dwārakā _(actors: Sudakṣiṇa, Śiva, the kṛtyā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/paundraka-the-false-vasudeva.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.66.27–34)
- **Sudarśana freed by Kṛṣṇa's touch** — Kṛṣṇa touches the python with his foot, and it sheds its reptile body to rise as the radiant vidyādhara Sudarśana, cursed into a snake by the Āṅgirasa sages for mocking them in pride _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Sudarśana, the Āṅgirasa sages)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudarshana-shankhachuda-vyoma.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.34.7–17)
- **Śuka critiques prāyaścitta as incomplete** — asked by Parīkṣit how to escape the narakas, Śukadeva gives the orthodox answer of penance, then judges it partial — like an elephant that bathes and again throws dust — for it removes the act but not the sinful propensity _(actors: Śukadeva, Parīkṣit)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/papa-punya-and-prayaschitta.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 6.1.9–18)
- **Śuka sent to Janaka's court** — Vyāsa sends his born-perfect son Śuka to king Janaka as final examiner of his liberation-knowledge; Janaka confirms renunciation is knowledge, not costume _(actors: Śuka, Janaka, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/videha-mithila-line-janaka.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śānti Parva ~325–326)
- **Śuka's flight — bodiless liberation** — Śuka rises by yoga through the parting peaks of Himavat and merges into all creation; the grieving Vyāsa calls "O Śuka!" and the whole world echoes back in his son's voice, Śiva consoling him with a shadow-Śuka _(actors: Śuka, Vyāsa, Nārada, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shuka-shukadeva.md) — (Mahābhārata XII, Mokṣadharma, close of the Śuka-carita)
- **Sulabhā tests Janaka's liberation** — The mendicant yoginī Sulabhā enters king Janaka Dharmadhvaja's mind by yoga-power; his gendered rebuke proves he still identifies with body and office, and she teaches that the ātman is not gendered _(actors: Sulabhā, Janaka Dharmadhvaja)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/videha-mithila-line-janaka.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śānti Parva 308)
- **Sūrya gives the Syamantaka to Satrājit** — The sun-god rewards his votary Satrājit with the radiant Syamantaka jewel that yields gold daily and wards off calamity _(actors: Sūrya, Satrājit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13)
- **Sudama and Krishna at Sandipani's gurukula** — The poor brahmin Sudama (Kuchela) and Krishna study together under guru Sandipani at Avanti, forming a boyhood friendship _(actors: Sudama/Kuchela, Krishna, Balarama, guru Sandipani)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.80.6; cf. 10.45)
- **Sudāmā's poverty and the wife's urging** — The poor brāhmaṇa Sudāmā, boyhood schoolmate of Kṛṣṇa, is urged by his starving wife to seek aid from his now-royal friend at Dvārakā _(actors: Sudāmā (Kucela), Sudāmā's wife, Kṛṣṇa (referenced))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.80.6–11)
- **Sweetness of the Braj *līlā*** — The hymn's subject: the flute-playing cowherd Kṛṣṇa of Vṛndāvana — his lips, smile, gait, *veṇu*, the Yamunā, gopīs and cows — each contemplated as *madhuram* _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, gopīs, cows of Braj)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/madhurashtakam.md) — (Madhurāṣṭakam, verses 1–8)
- **The ant-bite that shamed a kingdom** — an ant stings Jantu on the hip and all hundred queens spring up wailing at once, an uproar that convinces Somaka one son among a hundred women is a curse, not a blessing _(actors: Somaka, the hundred queens, Jantu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jantu-somaka.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva CXXVII)
- **The aṣṭamī–caturdaśī concession** — Pārvatī intercedes and wins Vyāsa leave to enter Kāśī only on the eighth and fourteenth lunar days, after which he settles on the east Gaṅgā bank south-east of Lolārka _(actors: Pārvatī, Śiva, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa-banished-from-kashi.md) — (Skanda Purāṇa, Kāśī-khaṇḍa, Uttarārdha 96)
- **The battle of Ṣaṭpura (Six-Cities)** — Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna, and Pradyumna storm the dānava stronghold; Nikumbha's māyā multiplies him into thousands, fells Pradyumna, and bears Arjuna skyward _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna, Pradyumna, Nikumbha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nikumbha-bhanumati-shatpura.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva ch. 91, Dutt)
- **The Bhāṇḍīravana wedding** — Kṛṣṇa weds Rādhā in the Bhāṇḍīra forest with Brahmā himself officiating, making the pair an eternally wedded divine couple _(actors: Rādhā, Kṛṣṇa, Brahmā)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/radha.md) — (Brahma-vaivarta Purāṇa, Kṛṣṇa-janma-khaṇḍa; Garga Saṃhitā) [late text, disputed by Gauḍīya parakīyā doctrine]
- **The Brahma-hrada vision of Vaikuṇṭha** — reading their hearts, Krishna dips the cowherds in the Brahma-hrada of the Yamunā and lifts them up to behold the effulgent abode beyond material darkness, where he is praised by the personified Vedas _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Nanda, cowherds, the Śrutis)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nanda-varuna-vaikuntha-darshana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.28.13–17)
- **The Brāhmaṇa's araṇi stolen by a deer** — As the exile nears its end a deer bounds off with a Brāhmaṇa's fire-churning sticks, robbing him of his agni-hotra and setting the Yakṣa-praśna in motion _(actors: a Brāhmaṇa, the deer)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/yaksha-prashna.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 311)
- **The brāhmaṇas repent their ritual pride** — shamed by their wives' example, the sacrificers curse their own birth, learning and ritual expertise as worthless without devotion, yet dare not approach Krishna for fear of Kaṃsa _(actors: the brāhmaṇa sacrificers, Kṛṣṇa, Kaṃsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/yajna-patni-mokshana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.23.40–52)
- **The Brahmaśiras aimed at Uttarā's womb** — Unable to recall the Brahmaśiras he launched on a blade of grass against Arjuna's counter-weapon, Aśvatthāman redirects it into the wombs of the Pāṇḍava women to end the line _(actors: Aśvatthāman, Arjuna, Vyāsa, Nārada, Uttarā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ashvatthama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sauptika Parva 13-16 (Aiṣīka))
- **The court-parables fail and the embassy collapses** — despite Paraśurāma's tale, Kaṇva's Mātali story, and Nārada's Gālava story, Duryodhana refuses to yield and the war the tales were told to avert follows _(actors: Duryodhana, Paraśurāma, Kaṇva, Nārada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/dambhodbhava-nara-narayana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva, Bhagavad-yāna)
- **The crest-jewel to Balarāma** — Kṛṣṇa overtakes the fleeing Yakṣa, strikes off his head with a fist-blow, and gives the shining crest-jewel from his head to his elder brother Balarāma _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, Śaṅkhacūḍa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudarshana-shankhachuda-vyoma.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.34.25–32)
- **The crocodile-tīrthas of the southern sea (epic original)** — in the Mahābhārata's Ādi Parva Arjuna frees the apsarās Vargā, Saurabheyī, Samīcī, Budbudā and Latā at five southern-ocean tīrthas — the tale the Purāṇa relocates to the Gulf of Khambhāt _(actors: Arjuna, five apsarās)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mahisagara-sangama-kumarika-kshetra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~208–210)
- **The curse of Kindama** — Pāṇḍu shoots a mating pair of deer who are the sage Kindama and his wife; the dying sage curses him to die the moment he approaches a wife in desire _(actors: Pāṇḍu, Kindama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/pandu.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **The descent to Sutala and Bali's worship** — Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma pass by yoga-māyā into the netherworld Sutala, where Bali Mahārāja worships them as the Soul of the universe and yields the six infants held in his keeping _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, Bali)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/devaki-six-sons-shadgarbha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.85.34–50)
- **The dice-game assents** — Dhṛtarāṣṭra overrides Vidura to authorize Śakuni's rigged game, grants Draupadī's boons after evil omens, then permits the second exile-stake throw _(actors: Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Duryodhana, Śakuni, Vidura, Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dhritarashtra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva)
- **The digging of Rādhā-kuṇḍa and Śyāma-kuṇḍa** — Kṛṣṇa digs a pond in atonement for slaying the bull-demon Ariṣṭāsura and Rādhā with her sakhīs digs its twin, creating the holiest Gauḍīya tīrtha near Govardhana _(actors: Rādhā, Kṛṣṇa, Ariṣṭāsura)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/radha.md) — (Garga Saṃhitā; Gauḍīya tradition) [late text]
- **The divine-father decision** — Kuntī reveals Durvāsā's invocation-mantra and at Pāṇḍu's direction bears Yudhiṣṭhira, Bhīma and Arjuna of Dharma, Vāyu and Indra, then lends one use to Mādrī for the Aśvin twins _(actors: Pāṇḍu, Kuntī, Mādrī, Dharma, Vāyu, Indra, Aśvins)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/pandu.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **The Durvāsā-mantra resolution** — unable to father sons himself, Pāṇḍu is freed of the deadlock when Kuntī discloses the deva-invoking mantra, leading to the divine births of Yudhiṣṭhira, Bhīma and Arjuna _(actors: Kuntī, Pāṇḍu, Dharma, Vāyu, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyushitashva-bhadra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva; Ganguli §§CXXII–CXXIII)
- **The elephant-lie breaks Droṇa** — Bhīma slays an elephant named Aśvatthāman and Yudhiṣṭhira's half-truth convinces Droṇa his son is dead, leading to his beheading in yoga by Dhṛṣṭadyumna _(actors: Aśvatthāman, Droṇa, Bhīma, Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa, Dhṛṣṭadyumna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ashvatthama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva ~190-193)
- **The embassy of the false Vāsudeva** — Pauṇḍraka of Karūṣa, decked in counterfeit conch, discus, Śrīvatsa and Garuḍa banner, sends an envoy to Dwārakā demanding Kṛṣṇa renounce the name and emblems of Vāsudeva; Ugrasena's assembly laughs and Kṛṣṇa promises to 'release the emblem' upon him _(actors: Pauṇḍraka, Kṛṣṇa, Ugrasena)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/paundraka-the-false-vasudeva.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.66)
- **The final parting of Vraja and Dvārakā** — after the sacrifice Nanda stays some months, then returns with the Vraja folk to Vṛndāvana while the Vṛṣṇis go back to Dvārakā, never to meet again _(actors: Nanda, Yaśodā, Kṛṣṇa, the gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kurukshetra-reunion-gopis.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.84.58–71)
- **The flowers fall in Rukmiṇī's yard** — planted at Satyabhāmā's door, the Pārijāta lets its blossoms drift into Rukmiṇī's courtyard, a sign that quiet devotion outdraws proud possession _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Satyabhāmā, Rukmiṇī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parijata-harana.md) — (popular Vaiṣṇava retelling; cf. Bhāgavata X.59) [folk]
- **The forest picnic after Aghasura** — Krishna, Balarama and the cowherd boys take their lunch into the forest the morning after Aghasura's death; the calves stray and Krishna goes to fetch them _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, cowherd boys)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/brahma-vimohana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.13.8-15)
- **The gem surrendered and Kṛṣṇa's 3,000-year curse** — Aśvatthāman's forehead-gem is cut away leaving an unhealing wound, and Kṛṣṇa curses him to wander deathless for 3,000 years, diseased and shunned, while vowing the struck embryo shall live as Parīkṣit _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Aśvatthāman, Draupadī, Vyāsa, Parīkṣit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ashvatthama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sauptika Parva 16)
- **The gopīs' Kātyāyanī month-vrata** — through Hemanta's first month (Mārgaśīrṣa) the maidens of Vraja bathe at dawn in the Yamunā and worship Kātyāyanī to win Kṛṣṇa _(actors: the gopīs, Kātyāyanī, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/masa-mahatmyas-kartika-magha-vaishakha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.22)
- **The haṃsa embassy to Vajrapura** — Indra's celestial swans enter the sealed city; the she-swan Śucimukhī kindles Prabhāvatī's love for the unseen Pradyumna and baits Vajranābha with tales of a wonder-working actor _(actors: Śucimukhī, Prabhāvatī, Indra, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pradyumna-prabhavati-vajranabha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva 93, Dutt)
- **The hundred sons and the golden-marked eldest** — every queen conceives and bears a son, and Jantu returns as the firstborn to his own mother Kausalyā, known by the golden mark on his left side foretold by the priest _(actors: Jantu reborn, Kausalyā, the hundred queens)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jantu-somaka.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva CXXVIII)
- **The impostor's liberation by meditation** — because Pauṇḍraka had constantly meditated on the Lord's form by wearing His emblems, his bonds are cut and he attains absorption in Kṛṣṇa, glossed by commentators as sārūpya/sāyujya mukti _(actors: Pauṇḍraka, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/paundraka-the-false-vasudeva.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.66.24)
- **The interrupted Indra-yajña and Kṛṣṇa's argument** — Kṛṣṇa questions Nanda's annual Indra-sacrifice and argues for worshipping Govardhana, the cows and brāhmaṇas instead, on karma/svadharma grounds _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Nanda, the cowherd elders of Vraja)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/govardhana-dharana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.24; Harivaṃśa (Viṣṇu Parva); Viṣṇu Purāṇa Book 5)
- **The iron statue of Bhima** — Intending to crush Bhima in revenge for Duryodhana, the blind Dhritarashtra is given an iron effigy by Krishna; he crushes it to powder and weeps with remorse before learning of the substitution _(actors: Dhritarashtra, Bhima, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/11-stri-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Stri Parva 11-12)
- **The iron-Bhīma embrace** — Kṛṣṇa substitutes Duryodhana's practice statue for Bhīma, and the grieving king crushes it to pieces before accepting the Pāṇḍavas _(actors: Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Kṛṣṇa, Bhīma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dhritarashtra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Strī Parva ~11–12)
- **The Janaka householder-test** — sent on foot to Mithilā to settle whether the āśramas are mandatory, Śuka stays equanimous through neglect at the gates and through offered luxuries, and Janaka declares him already free by knowledge _(actors: Śuka, Vyāsa, Janaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shuka-shukadeva.md) — (Mahābhārata XII, Mokṣadharma; Yoga Vāsiṣṭha, Vairāgya Prakaraṇa variant)
- **The Kurukṣetra eclipse pilgrimage** — a great solar eclipse draws the Vṛṣṇis from Dvārakā and the Vraja folk from Vṛndāvana to Samanta-pañcaka to bathe at the sacred field _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, Nanda, Yaśodā, the gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kurukshetra-reunion-gopis.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.82)
- **The māyā-beheading of 'Vasudeva'** — in the sea-battle Śālva's illusion shows Kṛṣṇa his father seized and beheaded; Kṛṣṇa grieves a moment, then pierces the deceit _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Śālva, phantom Vasudeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shalva-saubha-vadha.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva CE 3.22–23)
- **The milk of liberation** — restored to Dvārakā, the six infants sip Devakī's breast-milk — remnant of Kṛṣṇa's own touch — regain their divine forms, and ascend liberated to the abode of the gods, closing the infanticide arc _(actors: Devakī, the six Ṣaḍgarbha, Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, Vasudeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/devaki-six-sons-shadgarbha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.85.52–56)
- **The Mudal Āḻvārs at Tirukkōvilūr** — three lotus-born saints crowd a narrow storm-porch (one lies, two sit, three stand) and feel Viṣṇu jostle in as an unseen fourth, sparking the three Tiruvandhādhis _(actors: Poigai, Bhūtam, Pey Āḻvār, Viṣṇu-Trivikrama)_ → [details](../07-acharyas-and-sampradayas/alvar-hagiographies-guruparampara.md) — (Guruparampara Prabhāvam 6000-paḍi; Divya Sūri Caritam)
- **The musala-curse at Piṇḍāraka** — At the Saurāṣṭra coastal tīrtha of Piṇḍāraka near Dvārakā, the sages Viśvāmitra, Kaṇva and Nārada, mocked by Yādava youths who disguised Sāmba as a pregnant woman, curse that Sāmba will bear an iron pestle (musala) that will annihilate the Vṛṣṇi–Andhaka race — the trigger of the Yādava self-slaughter and Kṛṣṇa's departure at the Dvāpara–Kali hinge _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Kaṇva, Nārada, Sāmba, the Yādava (Vṛṣṇi–Andhaka) youths)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mahabharata-tirtha-yatra-fords.md) — (Mahābhārata Mausala Parva 1; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.37)
- **The Nārāyaṇāstra unleashed and neutralised** — After Droṇa's death Aśvatthāman releases the once-only Nārāyaṇāstra, which Kṛṣṇa defeats by ordering the whole army to lay down arms and offer no resistance _(actors: Aśvatthāman, Kṛṣṇa, Bhīma, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ashvatthama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva ~199)
- **The pīṣṭodaka humiliation** — The boy Aśvatthāman drinks rice-flour water believing it milk, and the shame drives Droṇa to seek patronage at the Kuru court _(actors: Aśvatthāman, Droṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ashvatthama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **The priest prescribes the sacrifice of Jantu** — Somaka's ṛtvij reveals a rite by which, if Jantu is offered in the fire and the queens inhale the smoke of his burning fat, each will bear a son and Jantu be reborn eldest with a golden mark _(actors: Somaka, the purohita, Jantu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jantu-somaka.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva CXXVII)
- **The python at Ambikāvana** — on the Vraja pilgrimage to worship Śiva by the Sarasvatī, a hungry python seizes the sleeping Nanda and begins to swallow him, unmoved by the cowherds' firebrands _(actors: Nanda, the cowherds, the ajagara python)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudarshana-shankhachuda-vyoma.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.34.1–6)
- **The Raivataka festival and abduction of Subhadrā** — On the Raivataka (Girnar) festival slopes beside Dvārakā, Arjuna abducts Kṛṣṇa's sister Subhadrā on Kṛṣṇa's counsel _(actors: Arjuna, Subhadrā, Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, Vṛṣṇi-Andhakas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/girnar-raivataka-datta-tirtha.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~218-223; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10)
- **The ritualists refuse the hungry boys** — engrossed in ritual scruple and contemptuous of a request made in a cowherd's name, the sacrificing brāhmaṇas give no food and no answer, and the Bhāgavata condemns their devotionless learning _(actors: the brāhmaṇa sacrificers, cowherd boys)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/yajna-patni-mokshana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.23.4–12)
- **The sages' tīrtha discourse at Kurukṣetra** — before Vyāsa, Nārada and a great company of ṛṣis, Kṛṣṇa teaches that the living sādhu, not clay or water, is the true place of pilgrimage _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Vyāsa, Nārada, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kurukshetra-reunion-gopis.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.84.11–13)
- **The salt-tribute embassy** — the brothers send Janārdana to demand tribute and salt from Kṛṣṇa for Brahmadatta's Rājasūya; Kṛṣṇa's laughing refusal in the Sudharmā hall makes war inevitable _(actors: Janārdana, Kṛṣṇa, Haṃsa, Ḍimbhaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hamsa-dimbhaka.md) — (Harivaṃśa vulgate, Bhaviṣya Parva 115)
- **The secret breaks; night battle in Vajrapura** — the wonder-born sons of the hidden marriages are spotted, Vajranābha orders their seizure, and Prabhāvatī herself arms Pradyumna for the night-long fight _(actors: Vajranābha, Pradyumna, Prabhāvatī, Gada, Sāmba)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pradyumna-prabhavati-vajranabha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva 97, Dutt)
- **The shared term of hell, then heaven together** — Yama grants Somaka the measured term of Naraka needed to exhaust the priest's demerit, after which king and priest ascend to the higher worlds together _(actors: Somaka, the purohita, Yama/Dharma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jantu-somaka.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva CXXVIII)
- **The Sixteen Kings and the revival** — Nārada consoles the grieving Sṛñjaya with the deaths of sixteen supreme emperors of old, then, exceeding his own lesson, restores the slain Suvarṇaṣṭhīvin to a full span of life _(actors: Nārada, Sṛñjaya, Suvarṇaṣṭhīvin)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/suvarnashthivin-srinjaya-narada-parvata.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śānti Parva ch. ~31; Ganguli XXXI)
- **The Śruti-gīta (Veda-stuti)** — Answering how the Vedas can indicate the nirguṇa Brahman, Śuka relays Nārāyaṇa Ṛṣi's account of the personified śrutis hymning the Lord at the dawn of creation _(actors: Śuka, Parīkṣit, Nārāyaṇa Ṛṣi, Nārada, the personified Vedas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.87)
- **The Sudarśana burns Kāśī** — Kṛṣṇa dispatches the Sudarśana cakra from his dice-game; the repelled kṛtyā rebounds to consume Sudakṣiṇa and his priests, and the pursuing discus incinerates the whole city of Vārāṇasī before returning to Kṛṣṇa's side _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Sudarśana cakra, Sudakṣiṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/paundraka-the-false-vasudeva.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.66.38–43; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.34)
- **The twins in disguise at Virāṭa's court** — For the incognito thirteenth year Nakula serves as Granthika the horse-keeper and Sahadeva as Tantipāla the superintendent of cattle _(actors: Nakula, Sahadeva, Virāṭa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/nakula-sahadeva.md) — (Mahabharata, Virata Parva)
- **The twins' vows after the dice-fraud** — Amid the dice-hall vows Sahadeva swears to slay Śakuni, architect of the crooked game, and Nakula vows to slay the partisan sons of Dhṛtarāṣṭra _(actors: Sahadeva, Nakula, Śakuni, Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/nakula-sahadeva.md) — (Mahabharata, Sabha Parva)
- **The Vasus Steal Nandini and Are Cursed** — The eight Vasus steal the wish-cow Nandini from Vasishtha, who curses them to be born as mortals, with the chief offender Dyaus to live a full long human life _(actors: The Vasus, Vasishtha, Dyaus)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhishma.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~99)
- **The Vidura-nīti night** — the sleepless king summons Vidura for an all-night discourse on dharma and statecraft, praises it, and follows none of it _(actors: Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dhritarashtra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva ~33–40)
- **The Vrishni widows enter the fire** — After the clan's destruction, Vasudeva's four wives Devakī, Rohiṇī, Bhadrā and Madirā mount his pyre, and several of Krishna's and Balarāma's wives follow at the great cremations while Satyabhāmā retires to the forest _(actors: Devakī, Rohiṇī, Bhadrā, Madirā, Rukmiṇī, Jāmbavatī, Arjuna)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/sati-jauhar-and-the-immolation-rites.md) — (Mahābhārata, Mausala Parva 6–7)
- **The Vṛṣṇis and Andhakas annihilate one another at Prabhāsa** — Doubly cursed by Gāndhārī and the sages of Piṇḍāraka, the drunken clans seize the iron-turning erakā reeds and slaughter each other, Sātyaki beheading Kṛtavarmā over the Sauptika massacre _(actors: Sātyaki, Kṛtavarmā, Sāmba, Kṛṣṇa, the Yādavas)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/yadava-vamsha.md) — (Mahābhārata Mausala Parva 1-4; Bhāgavata XI.30; Viṣṇu Purāṇa V.37)
- **The Vyadha-gita: the righteous butcher teaches Kaushika** — A proud brahmana is humbled by a housewife and a righteous butcher who teaches that liberation lies in honestly doing one's appointed work _(actors: Kaushika, Dharma-vyadha, Markandeya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/lesser-gitas-catalog.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva ~3.197-206)
- **The Vyāsa–Śuka anupraśna** — Śuka questions his father on the highest good and Vyāsa teaches the path to Brahman, the Śānti Parva's central renunciation-cycle _(actors: Śuka, Vyāsa, Bhīṣma as narrator)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shuka-shukadeva.md) — (Mahābhārata XII.224–250 [Ganguli])
- **The war-council at Upaplavya** — With the exile fulfilled, the allied kings assemble; Krishna declares the Pandavas want their rightful kingdom and that an envoy should be sent, while Balarama counsels moderation and Satyaki force _(actors: Krishna, Pandavas, Drupada, Balarama, Satyaki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva, Sainyodyoga)
- **The wife delivered in meditation** — one wife, held back in the house by her husband, absorbs herself wholly in Krishna, sheds her body of the three guṇas and attains him directly without ever seeing him _(actors: the restrained brāhmaṇa wife, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/yajna-patni-mokshana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.23.35)
- **The wives defy their menfolk to carry the feast** — hearing Krishna's name, the brāhmaṇa wives seize the four kinds of food and rush to him like rivers to the sea, disobeying husbands, fathers, brothers and kin who forbid them _(actors: yajña-patnīs, their husbands and kinsmen)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/yajna-patni-mokshana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.23.18–21)
- **The Yādava migration to Dvārakā** — Under Jarāsandha's seventeen sieges and Kālayavana's advance, Kṛṣṇa evacuates the Vṛṣṇi-Andhaka-Bhoja federation from Mathurā to the new sea-fort of Dvārakā in Saurāṣṭra _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Jarāsandha, Kālayavana, the Yādavas)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/yadava-vamsha.md) — (Bhāgavata X.50-52; Viṣṇu Purāṇa V.22-23; Harivaṃśa)
- **The Yādava sea-excursion to Piṇḍāraka** — Kṛṣṇa leads the clan from Dvārakā on a pleasure voyage of sweetened seas, water-sports, and the celestial Chālikya song near Raivataka _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, Revatī, the Yādavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nikumbha-bhanumati-shatpura.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva chs. 89–90, Dutt)
- **The Yādavas infiltrate Vajrapura as actors** — Pradyumna, Sāmba and Gada enter the demon city with Bhadra's troupe and stage a Rāmāyaṇa play and the Rambhābhisāra — among the earliest theatre-within-story scenes in Sanskrit _(actors: Pradyumna, Sāmba, Gada, Bhadra, Vajranābha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pradyumna-prabhavati-vajranabha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva 94, Dutt)
- **Three handfuls of sand become the Tridevī** — each day Vyāsa moulds three fistfuls of Godāvarī sand that take form as Sarasvatī, Lakṣmī and Mahākālī _(actors: Vyāsa, Sarasvatī, Lakṣmī, Mahākālī)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/basara-gnana-saraswati-vyasa.md) — (Basara sthala-tradition; claimed Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa)
- **Ulūpī abducts Arjuna into the Gaṅgā** — During Arjuna's twelve-year tīrtha-exile, the Nāga princess Ulūpī draws the bathing Arjuna down beneath the Gaṅgā into her father Kauravya's serpent-palace _(actors: Ulūpī, Arjuna, Kauravya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ulupi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~206 (vulgate 213–219))
- **Umpire of the mace-duel** — returning from pilgrimage, Balarāma referees the Bhīma–Duryodhana duel of his two pupils and raises his plough in fury at the illegal thigh-blow before Kṛṣṇa restrains him _(actors: Balarāma, Bhīma, Duryodhana, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/balarama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śalya Parva, Gadā-yuddha)
- **Upamanyu initiates Kṛṣṇa into the Pāśupata vow** — The sage gives Kṛṣṇa dīkṣā into the pāśupata-vrata with ash and mantra and directs his austerity _(actors: Upamanyu, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/upamanyu.md) — (Śiva Purāṇa, Umā-saṃhitā 1–4; Mahābhārata, Anuśāsana Parva)
- **Upamanyu teaches Kṛṣṇa Śiva-tattva** — The Kṛṣṇa–Upamanyu dialogues expound Pāśupata doctrine and Śiva's greatness, framing the Umā- and Vāyavīya-saṃhitās _(actors: Upamanyu, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/upamanyu.md) — (Śiva Purāṇa, Vāyavīya-saṃhitā pūrva 34–35, uttara 1–4)
- **Upanayana of Krishna and Balarama** — After Kamsa's death the brothers receive the sacred thread from the priest Garga, entering brahmacarya _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Garga (Gargacharya))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sandipani-pancajana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.45)
- **Uprooting of the Pārijāta** — Kṛṣṇa uproots the celestial coral-tree from Indra's Nandana grove over the protest of Śacī, to plant it in Satyabhāmā's Dwārakā courtyard _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Satyabhāmā, Śacī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parijata-harana.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva 69; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.30)
- **Urvaśī's curse converted to asset** — her curse of eunuch-hood on Arjuna is capped by Indra at one chosen year, becoming the Bṛhannaḍā disguise at Virāṭa _(actors: Urvaśī, Arjuna, Indra)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/tapas-boons-and-curses.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana & Virāṭa Parvas)
- **Uttaṅka's quest for the celestial earrings** — The disciple Uttaṅka journeys to fetch a queen's earrings as guru-fee, swallowing dung that is secretly amṛta and surviving a curse-duel _(actors: Uttaṅka, Veda, King Pauṣya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva, Paushya sub-parva)
- **Uttanka's guru-fee demand for the celestial earrings** — After completing his studies Uttanka is set the impossible gurudakshina of fetching the queen's celestial earrings within a fixed time _(actors: Uttanka, Veda, Veda's wife)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva Paushya sub-parva)
- **Vajra, the last Yādava, crowned** — after the Yādavas destroy themselves at Prabhāsa and Kṛṣṇa departs, Arjuna installs Kṛṣṇa's great-grandson Vajra (Pradyumna → Aniruddha → Vajra) as king, the sole heir of the ruined clan _(actors: Vajra, Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna, Aniruddha)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/vishnudharmottara-purana.md) — (Viṣṇudharmottara Purāṇa, frame)
- **Vajranābha's boon and the sealed city Vajrapura** — the dānava wins from Brahmā an impregnable city that not even the wind may enter unbidden, then claims Indra's three worlds _(actors: Vajranābha, Brahmā, Indra, Kaśyapa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pradyumna-prabhavati-vajranabha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva 92, Dutt)
- **Varuṇa worships the cowherd boy** — the lokapāla of the waters, overwhelmed with joy, worships Krishna as the Supreme Person, apologizes for his ignorant servant, and returns Nanda _(actors: Varuṇa, Kṛṣṇa, Nanda)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nanda-varuna-vaikuntha-darshana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.28.5–10)
- **Vasiṣṭha foretells Kṛṣṇa's teaching of Arjuna** — Within the Tretā-frame discourse Vasiṣṭha prophesies that in Dvāpara Viṣṇu will descend as Kṛṣṇa and give Arjuna the non-dual battlefield teaching, in verses echoing the Bhagavad-Gītā [late text] _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Rāma, Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/yoga-vasishtha-tales-catalog.md) — (Yoga-Vāsiṣṭha VI.1, Nirvāṇa Pūrvārdha)
- **Vasu's tapas and Indra's intervention** — The devout Cedi king Vasu Uparicara performs severe tapas; Indra, fearing rivalry, lures him back to kingship with gifts _(actors: Vasu Uparicara, Indra, the gods)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/uparichara-vasu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva (Sambhava ~63))
- **Śvetaki's twelve-year sacrifice and Agni's indigestion** — King Śvetaki's unbroken sacrifice gorges Agni on ghee until the fire-god grows sick and dyspeptic _(actors: Śvetaki, Agni)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/khandava-vana-and-the-burning.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi-parva 214-215)
- **Vasudeva and Devakī inherit the icon; Kṛṣṇa's four-armed birth-vision** — the image passes to the Yadu couple, and the newborn Kṛṣṇa reveals the four-armed form the mūrti depicts _(actors: Vasudeva, Devakī, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/guruvayur-krishna-kshetra.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.3)
- **Vasudeva's great sacrifice** — confessing bondage despite fathering the Lord, Vasudeva asks the sages the means of release and is led by Nārada through a mahā-yajña on the eclipse-field _(actors: Vasudeva, Nārada, the ṛṣis)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kurukshetra-reunion-gopis.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.84.24–57)
- **Veṇu-gītā (Song of the Flute)** — The gopīs rapturously describe Kṛṣṇa's flute-playing as the autumn season opens, the lyric overture that kindles desire for the rāsa _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, the gopīs of Vṛndāvana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rasa-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.21)
- **Vidura summons Sanatsujata to teach Dhritarashtra** — On the war's eve the sleepless Dhritarashtra asks for higher wisdom; Vidura, pleading his shudra birth, meditates upon and summons the deathless sage Sanatsujata to teach in his stead _(actors: Dhritarashtra, Vidura, Sanatsujata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sanatsujatiya.md) — (Mahabharata, Udyoga Parva 5.41)
- **Vishvamitra's penance broken by Menaka** — Fearing Vishvamitra's severe tapas, Indra sends the apsara Menaka, who with Vayu and Kamadeva breaks the sage's penance, and he unites with her _(actors: Vishvamitra, Indra, Menaka, Vayu, Kamadeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shakuntala-bharata.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva, Sambhava)
- **Viṣṇu concedes Śiva's supremacy** — Viṣṇu appears secretly, tells the humbled Vyāsa that Śiva is supreme, restores his speech, and Vyāsa sings the Vyāsāṣṭaka and installs the Vyāseśvara liṅga _(actors: Vyāsa, Viṣṇu, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa-banished-from-kashi.md) — (Skanda Purāṇa, Kāśī-khaṇḍa, Uttarārdha 95)
- **Viṣṇu dispatches Yoganidrā/Yogamāyā** — Viṣṇu sends his sleep-power Yogamāyā to transfer Balarāma to Rohiṇī and be born to Yaśodā as the substitute for the infant Kṛṣṇa _(actors: Viṣṇu, Yoganidrā/Yogamāyā, Devakī, Balarāma, Rohiṇī, Yaśodā)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/vindhyavasini.md) — (Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.1; Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.2–3)
- **Viśvakarmā fashions the bāla-Kṛṣṇa icon for Rukmiṇī** — The Udupi churning-child Kṛṣṇa mūrti is made by the cosmic craftsman Viśvakarmā and worshipped by Rukmiṇī at Dvārakā in Kṛṣṇa's own lifetime _(actors: Viśvakarmā, Rukmiṇī, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/udupi-krishna-matha.md) — (Mādhva hagiographic tradition)
- **Vraja meets Dvārakā** — Vasudeva and Nanda embrace, Devakī and Rohiṇī weep with Yaśodā, and Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma bow to their foster-parents in the one reunion of his two worlds _(actors: Vasudeva, Nanda, Yaśodā, Devakī, Rohiṇī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kurukshetra-reunion-gopis.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.82)
- **Vṛkāsura wins the incinerate-by-touch boon from Śiva** — After a flesh-offering penance at Kedāra, the asura Vṛka extracts a death-by-touch boon and turns it on Śiva, who flees to Vaikuṇṭha _(actors: Vṛkāsura, Śiva, Nārada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhasmasura-and-vrikasura.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.88)
- **Vāraṇāvata escape into the forest** — The Pāṇḍavas and Kuntī flee the burning lac-house and halt exhausted in the rākṣasa-haunted forest where Bhīma keeps watch _(actors: Bhīma, Kuntī, Yudhiṣṭhira and the Pāṇḍava brothers)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/hidimba-hidimbi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva, Hiḍimba-vadha Parva (~154))
- **Vyāsa banished from Kāśī as a Rudra-piśāca** — declared unworthy to dwell in the city, Vyāsa is expelled from the kṣetra by the householder-Śiva _(actors: Śiva, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa-banished-from-kashi.md) — (Skanda Purāṇa, Kāśī-khaṇḍa, Uttarārdha 96)
- **Vyasa Divides the Veda into Four** — Krishna-Dvaipayana Veda-Vyasa, the 28th and current Vyasa, splits the single Veda into Rig, Yajur, Sama and Atharva and teaches them to four pupils, also authoring the Mahabharata _(actors: Krishna-Dvaipayana Vyasa; Paila; Vaishampayana; Jaimini; Sumantu)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/README.md) — (Vishnu Purana 3.3-3.4; Bhagavata Purana 1.4; Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Vyāsa and Kṛṣṇa prescribe the Aśvamedha to console Yudhiṣṭhira** — Yudhiṣṭhira sinks back into grief over the slain kinsmen; Vyāsa and Kṛṣṇa console him and prescribe the horse-sacrifice as expiation and a seal of universal sovereignty _(actors: Vyāsa, Kṛṣṇa, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.1-3)
- **Vyāsa institutes the Nirjalā (Bhīmasenī) Ekādaśī** — unable to fast twice a month, Bhīma is prescribed a single absolute waterless fast whose merit equals all 24 Ekādaśīs of the year _(actors: Bhīma, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ekadashi-mahatmyas-catalog.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Uttara-khaṇḍa 39–63) [late text]
- **Vyāsa narrates the Devī Bhāgavata to Janamejaya** — Vyāsa instructs King Janamejaya in the Goddess's Bhāgavata at the Naimiṣa assembly so the listener may attain mukti through Goddess-devotion _(actors: Vyāsa, Janamejaya)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/devi-bhagavata-upapurana.md) — (Devī Bhāgavata, frame)
- **Vyāsa's arm paralysed at Jñānavāpī** — proclaiming Viṣṇu's supremacy in Śiva's own city, Vyāsa raises his hand to swear the point and Nandin freezes it "playfully" _(actors: Vyāsa, Nandin/Śailādi, Viśveśvara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa-banished-from-kashi.md) — (Skanda Purāṇa, Kāśī-khaṇḍa, Uttarārdha 95)
- **Vyāsa's curse on Kāśī rebounds** — enraged, Vyāsa curses the city to lose learning, wealth, and liberation for three generations, but the eternal Avimukta kṣetra cannot be bound and the curse recoils on him _(actors: Vyāsa, the citizens of Kāśī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa-banished-from-kashi.md) — (Skanda Purāṇa, Kāśī-khaṇḍa, Uttarārdha 96)
- **Vyāsa's southward sojourn after Kurukṣetra** — grieved by the war's carnage, the Veda-arranger wanders down the Deccan seeking peace and settles to do tapas on the Godāvarī at the future Basara _(actors: Vyāsa, Viśvāmitra, disciples)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/basara-gnana-saraswati-vyasa.md) — (Basara sthala-tradition; Mahābhārata, Strī/Śānti-parvas)
- **Vyāsa's tīrthas and cave on the Godāvarī** — the sage's penance seats the aṣṭa-tīrtha bathing-fords and the Vyāsa-guṇḍam cave-shrine around Basara _(actors: Vyāsa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/basara-gnana-saraswati-vyasa.md) — (Basara sthala-tradition)
- **Vyāsapurī → Vāsara → Basara** — the town takes its name from Vyāsa's stay, worn down to Vāsara and softened to Basara under Marāṭhī v→b speech _(actors: Vyāsa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/basara-gnana-saraswati-vyasa.md) — (Basara sthala-tradition)
- **Vyoma steals the sheep-boys** — in the boys' game of thieves and shepherds, Vyoma the son of the asura Maya, disguised as a cowherd, carries off most of the boys playing sheep and seals them in a mountain cave _(actors: Vyomāsura, Maya his father, the cowherd boys)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudarshana-shankhachuda-vyoma.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.37.28–31)
- **Vyomāsura crushed at the cave** — Kṛṣṇa detects the magician-thief, seizes him as a lion seizes a jackal, crushes him despite his mountain-sized form, and breaks open the cave to free the trapped boys _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Vyomāsura, the cowherd boys)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudarshana-shankhachuda-vyoma.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.37.32–33)
- **Womb-transfer of Saṅkarṣaṇa** — Yogamāyā draws Devakī's seventh embryo to Rohiṇī's womb in Gokula, and Balarāma is born as Rauhiṇeya _(actors: Balarāma, Devakī, Rohiṇī, Yogamāyā, Kaṃsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/balarama.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa, 10.2)
- **Yadavas destroy themselves at Prabhasa** — Cursed by the brahmanas and maddened by drink, the Yadavas slaughter one another at Prabhasa as Krishna's avatara nears its end _(actors: Yadavas, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uddhava-gita.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11.1; Mahabharata Mausala Parva)
- **Yoga-māyā transfers the six to Devakī's womb** — Viṣṇu's deluding potency carries the six cursed souls into Devakī's womb to be born one by one in Mathurā's prison _(actors: Yoga-māyā, Devakī, Vasudeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/devaki-six-sons-shadgarbha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.85.49–50; cf. 10.2)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira approaches the dying Bhīṣma** — After Kurukṣetra, the grief-struck new king approaches Bhīṣma on his bed of arrows for instruction on dharma and liberation _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Bhīṣma, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/vishnu-sahasranama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Anuśāsana Parva XIII.135)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira asks Bhīṣma for the thousand names of Śiva** — Having heard the Viṣṇu Sahasranāma, Yudhiṣṭhira requests the thousand names of Mahādeva/Śiva _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/shiva-sahasranama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Anuśāsana Parva XIII)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira chooses Nakula at the Yakṣa's lake** — Offered one dead brother back after answering the Yakṣa's questions, Yudhiṣṭhira revives Nakula so that Mādrī's line, like Kuntī's, keeps a living son, and Dharma restores all four _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Nakula, Sahadeva, the Yakṣa/Dharma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/nakula-sahadeva.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva, Araneya)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira resolves to renounce the kingdom** — Sickened by the slaughter of kin and elders, Yudhiṣṭhira declares the kingdom worthless and resolves to abandon it for the forest as an ascetic _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/12-shanti-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 12 (Shanti Parva))
- **Abhimanyu Learns the Cakravyuha in the Womb** — While in Subhadra's womb Abhimanyu overhears Arjuna narrate how to penetrate the cakravyuha but not how to exit it, as Subhadra falls asleep mid-telling _(actors: Abhimanyu, Arjuna, Subhadra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/abhimanyu.md) — (Mahabharata tradition)
- **Śakaṭāsura the cart-demon** — A demon enters a heavy handcart to crush the babe, who overturns and smashes it with a kick of his tiny foot _(actors: Krishna (infant), Śakaṭāsura, Kaṃsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-balya-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.7; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Akrūra's vision in the Yamunā** — Bathing en route, Akrūra sees Krishna and Balarāma within the river as Viṣṇu on Śeṣa, confirming their divinity _(actors: Akrūra, Krishna, Balarāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.39)
- **Śambara abducts the infant and casts him into the sea** — The asura Śambara, fated to die by Pradyumna, steals the ten-day-old child and flings him into the ocean, where a fish swallows him _(actors: Śambara, infant Pradyumna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/pradyumna.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.55; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Ambā released to wed Śālva** — Ambā confesses she had chosen Śālva; Bhīṣma honorably releases her to her beloved while her sisters wed Vicitravīrya _(actors: Ambā, Bhīṣma, Śālva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/amba-shikhandi.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva ~173)
- **Arjuna and Duryodhana seek Krishna's aid** — Both go to Dvaraka; Krishna offers either himself unarmed or his Narayani army; Arjuna chooses Krishna (who becomes his charioteer and vows to bear no weapon), Duryodhana takes the army _(actors: Arjuna, Duryodhana, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva, Sainyodyoga)
- **Arjuna and the Eye of the Bird** — In Drona's archery test only Arjuna sees nothing but the wooden bird's eye, and Drona declares his one-pointedness the mark of a master _(actors: Arjuna, Drona)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~123)
- **Arjuna Defeats Chitraratha (Aṅgāraparṇa)** — The gandharva-king Chitraratha challenges the Pāṇḍavas at the Gaṅgā; Arjuna burns his chariot with the Āgneyāstra and, sparing him, exchanges weapons and receives the Cākṣuṣī-mantra _(actors: Arjuna, Chitraratha/Aṅgāraparṇa, Kumbhīnasī)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/gandharvas-and-kinnaras.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva, Caitrarathaparva 169-172)
- **Aśvamedha horse reaches Manipura** — Yudhishthira's sacrificial horse, guarded by Arjuna, enters Manipura, where King Babhruvahana greets his father with homage rather than battle _(actors: Babhruvāhana, Arjuna, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/babhruvahana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~71–79)
- **Bhīṣma defers and Kṛṣṇa recites the Śiva Sahasranāma** — The dying Bhīṣma declines to expound Śiva's names himself and names Kṛṣṇa as the fit reciter, who then recites them _(actors: Bhīṣma, Kṛṣṇa, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/shiva-sahasranama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Anuśāsana Parva XIII)
- **Bhīṣma resumes teaching the still-grieving Yudhiṣṭhira** — Despite all of the Śānti Parva, Yudhiṣṭhira remains crushed by guilt, so Bhīṣma resumes his deathbed teaching on dāna-dharma, fate and effort _(actors: Bhīṣma, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/13-anushasana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 13.1)
- **Birth and abandonment of Shakuntala** — A daughter is born to Menaka on the Malini river and abandoned; sheltered by shakunta birds, she is found and adopted by sage Kanva, who names her Shakuntala _(actors: Menaka, Vishvamitra, Shakuntala, Kanva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shakuntala-bharata.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva, Sambhava)
- **Birth of Vidura by niyoga** — When Ambikā sends her handmaid in her place, Vyāsa begets upon the gracious maid the supremely wise Vidura, born of a Śūdra-status mother and barred from the throne — the incarnation of Dharma fulfilling Maṇḍavya's curse _(actors: Vyāsa, the maidservant, Vidura, Satyavatī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vidura.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Boyhood warnings against Duryodhana's plots** — As the one righteous Kaurava insider, Yuyutsu (in retelling tradition) alerts the Pandavas/Vidura to Duryodhana's schemes against Bhima, including the poisoning at Pramanakoti _(actors: Yuyutsu, Duryodhana, Bhima, the Pandavas, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yuyutsu.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva [folk])
- **Brahma asks Krishna to return to his abode** — Brahma with the gods comes to Dvaraka and asks Krishna to return home; Krishna consents and turns to settle his devotees _(actors: Brahma, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uddhava-gita.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11.6)
- **Brahma's curiosity and test** — Astonished by the slaying of Aghasura, Brahma resolves to test Krishna's divinity _(actors: Brahma, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/brahma-vimohana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.13.15)
- **Brahmā prescribes the burning of Khāṇḍava** — Brahmā tells Agni he must consume the Khāṇḍava forest to cure his digestion, but only with Nara-Nārāyaṇa's aid _(actors: Brahmā, Agni)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/khandava-vana-and-the-burning.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi-parva 215-216)
- **Charvaka the rakshasa reviles Yudhishthira** — A rakshasa disguised as a brahmin named Charvaka denounces Yudhishthira after the Kurukshetra war and is destroyed by the assembled brahmins _(actors: Charvaka (rakshasa), Yudhishthira, the brahmins)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/nastika-darshanas-and-the-astika-nastika-frame.md) — (Mahabharata, Shanti Parva 38-39)
- **Conception of Droṇa from Bharadvāja and Ghṛtācī** — At the Gaṅgā the sage Bharadvāja sees Ghṛtācī; a breeze disrobes her, his seed escapes, and he catches it in a wooden trough (droṇa) from which the ayonija boy Droṇa develops, making Ghṛtācī ancestress of the weapons-master line _(actors: Bharadvāja, Ghṛtācī, Droṇa)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/apsaras.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~130)
- **Consecration of Citrāṅgada** — On Śāntanu's death, Bhīṣma installs the young Citrāṅgada as king of Hastināpura while acting as protecting elder _(actors: Citrāṅgada, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vichitravirya-and-chitrangada.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~101)
- **Disqualification by skin-affliction and forest renunciation** — A skin-disease (kuṣṭha/skin-blemish) bars Devāpi from royal consecration; he withdraws to the forest as an ascetic, clearing the throne for Śāntanu _(actors: Devāpi, brāhmaṇas/elders, the people)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/devapi.md) — (Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.20; Bhāgavata Purāṇa IX.22; Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Division of the Veda** — Vyasa splits the one Veda into Rig, Yajur, Sama, Atharva and teaches them to four disciples _(actors: Vyasa, Paila, Vaishampayana, Jaimini, Sumantu)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Vishnu Purana 3.4; Bhagavata Purana 1.4, 12.6)
- **Division of the Veda into four** — Vyasa splits the primordial Veda into Rig, Yajur, Sama, and Atharva for the declining age and teaches them to his four disciples Paila, Vaishampayana, Jaimini, and Sumantu _(actors: Vyasa, Paila, Vaishampayana, Jaimini, Sumantu)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Vishnu Purana 3.4; Bhagavata Purana 1.4, 12.6)
- **Draupadi Born of Fire** — The Panchala princess Draupadi is born from the sacrificial fire and becomes common wife of the five Pandavas _(actors: Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/README.md) — (Mahabharata, Sabha/Vana Parvas)
- **Drupada humiliates the impoverished Droṇa** — Now a proud king, Drupada repudiates the boyhood promise and insults Droṇa, declaring friendship exists only between equals _(actors: Drupada, Droṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drupada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~129)
- **Duhshasana drags Draupadi by the hair** — After Draupadi is lost at dice, Duhshasana seizes her by the hair and hauls her, menstruating and in one garment, into the men's assembly _(actors: Duḥśāsana, Draupadī, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dushasana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva 60)
- **Durvāsa grants Kuntī the deity-invocation mantra** — After Kuntī serves the volatile sage Durvāsa flawlessly, he grants her an ātharvaṇa mantra to summon any deity to beget a child upon her _(actors: Durvāsa, Kuntī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Durvāsā arrives to test Mudgala** — The irascible sage Durvāsā comes feigning madness, naked and reviling, deliberately to break Mudgala's reputed equanimity and hospitality _(actors: Durvāsā, Mudgala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Mārkaṇḍeya-samāsya (Mudgala-Upākhyāna))
- **Durvāsā tests Mudgala by devouring his food** — Durvāsā arrives repeatedly in a mad/naked guise, consumes Mudgala's entire gleaned store each fortnight, mocks him, and leaves the family starving _(actors: Durvāsā, Mudgala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva 246)
- **Duryodhana's hospitality trap and the boon** — Duryodhana serves Śalya's marching army lavishly in disguise; Śalya, thinking the host was Yudhiṣṭhira, grants a boon and is bound to fight for the Kauravas _(actors: Śalya, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shalya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva)
- **Ekalavya self-trains before a clay Droṇa** — Undeterred, Ekalavya fashions an earthen image of Droṇa as his guru and practises archery before it with total devotion until he attains the highest skill, entirely self-taught _(actors: Ekalavya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ekalavya.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Enrollment at Sandipani's gurukula** — Krishna and Balarama choose the sage Sandipani of Avanti (Ujjayini) as their teacher and serve him as model pupils _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Sandipani)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sandipani-pancajana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.45; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **Four Pāṇḍavas fall dead at the lake** — Chasing the deer and parched, each brother in turn ignores the guardian voice's warning, drinks from the lake, and dies, until four lie dead _(actors: Nakula, Sahadeva, Arjuna, Bhīma, the Yakṣa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/yaksha-prashna.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 311-312)
- **Gaṇeśa breaks his tusk as Mahābhārata scribe** — Serving as Vyāsa's scribe for the Mahābhārata, Gaṇeśa snaps off his own tusk to keep writing when his pen breaks, becoming Ekadanta _(actors: Gaṇeśa, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganesha-and-kartikeya.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva (frame narrative))
- **Gajānana's teaching of the yogas** — Gajānana instructs Vareṇya in karma-, jñāna-, and bhakti-yoga across the discourse, declaring himself the imperishable self and Para-brahman, with ~90% of verses adapted from the Bhagavad Gītā _(actors: Gajānana (Gaṇeśa), King Vareṇya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ganesha-gita.md) — (Gaṇeśa Purāṇa, Krīḍā-khaṇḍa chs. 138–148)
- **Gandhari's glance scorches Yudhishthira** — As Yudhishthira approaches, Gandhari's wrath escapes through her blindfold and scorches the king's nails; Vyasa and Krishna restrain her and she relents before her grandsons' killer _(actors: Gandhari, Yudhishthira, Vyasa, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/11-stri-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Stri Parva)
- **Hiding the weapons in the shami tree** — Before entering the city the Pandavas wrap their celestial weapons in a corpse-shroud and bind them high in a shami tree, claiming it is a relative's body so none will touch it _(actors: Pandavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/04-virata-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Virata Parva 5)
- **Hiḍimba sends Hiḍimbī to fetch prey** — The man-eating rākṣasa Hiḍimba smells the sleeping humans and dispatches his sister Hiḍimbī to kill and bring them as food _(actors: Hiḍimba, Hiḍimbī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/hidimba-hidimbi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva (~154))
- **Import of the eighteen Maga families from Śākadvīpa** — Unable to find a local brāhmaṇa to tend the Sun-image, Sāmba uses Garuḍa to bring eighteen Maga priest families from Śākadvīpa, founding the Bhojaka Sun-temple priesthood _(actors: Sāmba, Garuḍa, Maga/Bhojaka brāhmaṇas)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/bhavishya-purana.md) — (Bhaviṣya Purāṇa (Brahma Parva))
- **Indra's appeal to Kṛṣṇa** — Indra, robbed of his mother Aditi's earrings, comes to Dwārakā and begs Kṛṣṇa to destroy Naraka _(actors: Indra, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/narakasura-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.59; Harivaṃśa)
- **Infant Bhima Shatters a Rock** — As an infant Bhima falls from Kunti's lap onto a rock and smashes it to powder while remaining unhurt, the first omen of his strength _(actors: Bhima, Kunti)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhima.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~123)
- **Kṛṣṇa counsels abduction** — Kṛṣṇa advises Arjuna to seize Subhadrā by rākṣasa-form capture, pre-empting Balarāma's plan to wed her to Duryodhana _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna, Balarāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subhadra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva (Subhadrā-haraṇa Parva))
- **Kṛṣṇa drives the cows into the forest** — Kṛṣṇa, with Balarāma and the cowherd boys, leads the cattle into the flowering autumn woods of Vṛndāvana _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, gopas, cows)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/venu-gita.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.21.1–4)
- **Kalayavana marches on Mathura** — The invincible Yavana king Kalayavana, a vengeance-son born of the sage Gargya, advances on Mathura with a vast mleccha host, posing a threat Krishna cannot meet by force _(actors: Kalayavana, Gargya, Yavanas, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/muchukunda-and-kalayavana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.50-51; Vishnu Purana V.23; Harivamsha)
- **Kaṃsa imprisons Devakī and slays her children** — On the prophecy that Devakī's eighth child will kill him, Kaṃsa imprisons Devakī and Vasudeva and slays their first children _(actors: Kaṃsa, Devakī, Vasudeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/harivamsha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva)
- **Kṛṣṇa's request for the gem refused** — Kṛṣṇa asks Satrājit to give the jewel to King Ugrasena for the common good; the greedy Satrājit refuses, sowing suspicion _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Satrājit, Ugrasena)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13)
- **Kṛṣṇa Subdues Kāliya (Kāliya-mardana)** — The poison-spewing nāga Kāliya infests a Yamunā pool until the boy Kṛṣṇa leaps into his coils and dances upon his hoods, subduing but sparing him and banishing him back to Ramaṇaka Dvīpa _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Kāliya)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/nagas-and-serpents.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.16)
- **Kauśika burns the crane with his gaze** — The proud ascetic Kauśika, soiled by a bird while reciting, burns it to ashes with an angry glance and is elated, the flaw the parable exists to cure _(actors: Kauśika, the crane)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyadha-gita.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva ~198)
- **Ākāśavāṇī Prophesies Kaṃsa's Death** — At the wedding of Devakī to Vasudeva a heavenly voice warns Kaṃsa that her eighth child will kill him, triggering the whole birth-cycle of Kṛṣṇa _(actors: Kaṃsa, Devakī, Vasudeva)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.1)
- **Kirmīra-vadha** — Bhīma slays the cannibal rākṣasa Kirmīra (kin of Baka and Hiḍimba) bare-handed at the threshold of Kāmyaka forest _(actors: Bhīma, Kirmīra)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Kirmīra-vadha Parva ~11)
- **Kunti summons Surya and conceives Karna** — Testing the mantra out of curiosity, Kunti invokes Surya, who begets a son in her and restores her virginity afterward _(actors: Kunti, Surya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva ~305-306)
- **Maṇipura succession pact** — By marriage-pact the son stays in Maṇipura as its king and never joins the Pāṇḍava household _(actors: Citravāhana, Arjuna, Babhruvāhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/babhruvahana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~207; Āśvamedhika Parva ~79)
- **Maitreya's curse on Duryodhana** — The mocked sage Maitreya curses that Bhima will smash Duryodhana's thigh in war, sealing the Sabha-Parva oath _(actors: Maitreya, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 10-11)
- **Marriage to Vicitravīrya** — Ambikā and Ambālikā are wed to Vicitravīrya at Hastināpura after the eldest sister Ambā is released _(actors: Ambikā, Ambālikā, Vicitravīrya, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ambika-ambalika.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~102)
- **Nala and Damayantī Fall in Love by Reputation** — Each hearing the other endlessly praised, Nala and Damayantī fall in love before ever meeting _(actors: Nala, Damayantī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 50)
- **Nala and Damayantī fall in love via the golden swan** — Nala and Damayantī fall in love by reputation; a golden haṃsa carries Nala's praise to Vidarbha _(actors: Nala, Damayantī, the haṃsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 50–52)
- **Narada describes the celestial sabhas** — Narada describes the assembly-halls of Indra, Yama, Varuna, Kubera, and Brahma, noting Hariscandra alone among humans sits with the gods for completing the Rajasuya, planting Yudhishthira's ambition _(actors: Narada, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva 5-12)
- **Niyoga births of Dhritarashtra, Pandu, Vidura** — Vyasa fathers Dhritarashtra (blind), Pandu (pale), and Vidura (Dharma incarnate) on the widowed Kashi princesses and a maidservant _(actors: Vyasa, Ambika, Ambalika, Satyavati)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Nārada and Dhaumya lay out the four-quarter pilgrimage plan** — Nārada and the Pāṇḍava priest Dhaumya describe the east-south-west-north circuit of fords as a route-plan for the exiled Yudhiṣṭhira _(actors: Nārada, Dhaumya, Yudhiṣṭhira, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mahabharata-tirtha-yatra-fords.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 86–88)
- **Parāśara, the boons, and the birth of Vyāsa** — The sage Parāśara unites with her under a mist, restores her virginity and turns her fish-smell to a yojana-fragrance (Yojanagandhā); on a Yamunā island she bears Kṛṣṇa Dvaipāyana Vyāsa, who promises to come whenever she remembers him _(actors: Parāśara, Satyavatī, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/satyavati.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~57, ~63; Devī Bhāgavata Purāṇa)
- **Piṅgalā's sudden vairāgya (turn to detachment)** — Crushed by disappointment, Piṅgalā grows disgusted with desire itself and perceives that the Self/Lord within is the true neglected beloved _(actors: Piṅgalā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pingala-gita.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śānti Parva, Mokṣa-dharma ~XII.168)
- **Prophecy of Shishupala's slayer** — An aerial voice foretells that the child's surplus eye and arms will vanish when his future slayer takes him on his lap _(actors: Shishupala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shishupala.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha-parva 40)
- **Pursuit, capture, and sparing of Jayadratha** — The Pāṇḍavas overtake and capture Jayadratha; Yudhiṣṭhira spares his life for Duḥśalā's sake _(actors: Bhīma, Arjuna, Yudhiṣṭhira, Draupadī, Jayadratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jayadratha.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva 268–271)
- **Renaming of the Bhārata clan as Kuru** — After Kuru, the dynasty formerly called Bhārata becomes the Kuru clan, naming both Kauravas and Pāṇḍavas _(actors: Kuru, the Pauravā/Bhārata dynasty)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/kuru-the-eponym.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.19)
- **Rescue and naming by Shantanu** — King Shantanu finds the abandoned twins, is moved to pity (kripa), rears them and names them Kripa and Kripi _(actors: Shantanu, Kripa, Kripi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kripa.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva)
- **Retirement to the Himālaya** — Pāṇḍu renounces the throne and withdraws to the Himālaya as an ascetic, accompanied by both queens Kuntī and Mādrī _(actors: Pāṇḍu, Kuntī, Mādrī, Dhṛtarāṣṭra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/madri.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Reunion of Arjuna and Irāvān in Indraloka** — Father and son meet in Indra's heaven; Irāvān pledges to fight for the Pāṇḍavas and rides to Kurukṣetra _(actors: Arjuna, Irāvān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/aravan-iravan.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Rukmiṇī's secret love-letter via the Brāhmaṇa messenger** — Rukmiṇī sends a trusted Brāhmaṇa to Dwārakā with a secret letter declaring her love and begging Kṛṣṇa to abduct her before the wedding, dictating the temple-procession plan _(actors: Rukmiṇī, the Brāhmaṇa envoy, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rukmini-harana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.52.37-43)
- **Sahadeva slays Shakuni** — Sahadeva confronts Shakuni, the dice-master architect of the war, and severs his head, fulfilling his dice-hall vow; Shakuni's son Uluka is also slain _(actors: Sahadeva, Shakuni, Uluka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/09-shalya-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Shalya Parva (Shalya-vadha))
- **Sanatsujata delivers the teaching that heedlessness is death** — Sanatsujata expounds that death is pramada (heedlessness) not extinction, that the deathless state is vigilance, and that the Vedas alone do not save without realization of the Self _(actors: Sanatsujata, Dhritarashtra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sanatsujatiya.md) — (Mahabharata, Udyoga Parva 5.42-46)
- **Sarayū named as a tīrtha in the Mahābhārata pilgrim-lists** — The Sarayū and its Gopratāra ford are listed in the Tīrtha-yātrā catalogue recited to Yudhiṣṭhira as sin-destroying, heaven-conferring fords _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Pulastya/Dhaumya/Nārada (reciter))_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sarayu-ayodhya-river.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Tīrtha-yātrā Parva)
- **Satyavatī's virginity and fragrance restored** — Parāśara restores Satyavatī's virginity and changes her fish-odor to musk, enabling her later marriage to Śantanu and the Kuru line _(actors: Parāśara, Satyavatī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Saṃvaraṇa beholds Tapatī on the mountain** — King Saṃvaraṇa, hunting, sees Sūrya's daughter Tapatī on a mountain; they fall in mutual love but she vanishes _(actors: Saṃvaraṇa, Tapatī, Sūrya (Vivasvān))_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/samvarana-tapati.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~171 (Tāpatya upākhyāna))
- **Shalya undermines Karna with the crow-and-swan parable** — Forced to drive Karna's chariot, Shalya repeatedly praises Arjuna and belittles Karna, mocking him with the fable of the arrogant crow who challenged a swan, fulfilling his secret promise to demoralize him _(actors: Shalya, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/08-karna-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva 27-43)
- **Shibi offers his own flesh on the scale** — To resolve the dove's claim to sanctuary against the hawk's claim to food, Shibi cuts flesh equal to the dove's weight onto a balance and finally offers his whole body _(actors: Shibi, Indra, Agni)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shibi.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva)
- **Slaughter of the first six children and transfer of Balarama** — Kamsa kills Devaki's first six infants; the seventh, Balarama, is mystically transferred by Yogamaya to Rohini's womb in Gokula _(actors: Kamsa, Devaki, Balarama, Rohini)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.2)
- **Slaying of Ariṣṭāsura the bull-demon** — Krishna seizes the rampaging bull-demon by the horns, hurls him back, and wrings his neck until he vomits blood and dies _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Ariṣṭāsura, the gopas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/keshi-arishta-keshava.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.36; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5; Harivaṃśa)
- **Sulabhā Debates Janaka from Within** — Testing Janaka's claim of liberation, the yoginī Sulabhā enters his mind by yoga-bala and argues the self is ungendered and unembodied, exposing his lingering identification _(actors: Sulabhā, Janaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mokshadharma-dialogues.md) — (Mahabharata Shanti Parva 308)
- **Takṣaka steals the earrings; Uttaṅka burns Nāgaloka** — Takṣaka snatches the earrings into the serpent-world; Uttaṅka digs after them, sees the cosmic loom-vision, and floods Nāgaloka with fire from Agni's horse until the nāgas return the jewels _(actors: Uttaṅka, Takṣaka, Indra, Agni)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva, Paushya sub-parva)
- **The boon of the crystal vimāna, Vaijayantī garland, and bamboo staff** — Indra grants Vasu a crystal aerial car (whence 'Uparicara'), an unfading Vaijayantī garland, and a protective bamboo staff _(actors: Indra, Vasu Uparicara)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/uparichara-vasu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva (~63))
- **The brahmacarya debate and one-night marriage** — Ulūpī woos Arjuna, who objects on his celibacy vow; she argues the vow binds him only regarding Draupadī and that refusing a desiring woman breaches dharma, and they wed for one night _(actors: Ulūpī, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ulupi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~206)
- **The brothers and Draupadī argue against renunciation** — Arjuna, Bhīma, Nakula, Sahadeva, Draupadī, and Devasthāna refute Yudhiṣṭhira's wish to renounce, defending kṣatriya duty, wealth, and righteous rule over barren asceticism _(actors: Arjuna, Bhīma, Nakula, Sahadeva, Draupadī, Devasthāna, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/12-shanti-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 12.8-22)
- **The double cattle-raid and Virata's capture** — Susharma of Trigarta strikes from the south and captures Virata while the Kauravas raid from the north; Bhima routs Trigarta and frees the king _(actors: Virata, Susharma, Bhima, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/virata-and-his-children.md) — (Mahabharata, Virata Parva 30-33)
- **The first Annakūṭa and the śailo 'smi self-manifestation** — The Vrajavāsīs redirect the offerings to Govardhana with parikramā and a mountain of food; Kṛṣṇa manifests a colossal form on the summit and eats it, declaring 'I am the hill.' _(actors: Kṛṣṇa (as Giri-rāja), the cowherds of Vraja)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/govardhana-dharana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.24)
- **The Five Re-Emergences and Founding of the Pancha Kedar** — The bull-form Shiva's body-parts surface at five Garhwal sites where the Pandavas raise the Pancha Kedar temples (hump-Kedarnath, arms-Tungnath, face-Rudranath, navel-Madhyamaheshwar, hair-Kalpeshwar), freeing them of sin _(actors: Pandavas, Shiva)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/pancha-kedar-pancha-badri.md) — (Skanda Purana (Kedara-khanda); Mahabharata)
- **The Gandhāra grievance (variant backstory)** — In late/folk tradition the Kurus starve the imprisoned Subala family, and Śakuni survives to avenge them with dice made from his father's bones; absent from the critical text _(actors: Śakuni, Subala, the hundred Saubala brothers, Bhīṣma, the Kurus)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shakuni.md) — (Regional/televised retellings [folk] [late text]; not in BORI Critical Edition)
- **The gift of flattened rice (pṛthuka/poha)** — Having nothing, the wife begs four fistfuls of flattened rice from neighbours and ties them in a tattered cloth as Sudāmā's gift for Kṛṣṇa _(actors: Sudāmā's wife, Sudāmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.80.13)
- **The Gita teaches nishkama karma and the three margas** — In the Bhagavad Gita the teaching of desireless action and of karma-, jnana- and bhakti-yoga as complementary paths to liberation is delivered _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/purusharthas-and-karma-samsara-moksha.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 2.47; karma/jnana/bhakti chapters)
- **The gold expedition to recover King Marutta's hoard** — To fund the impoverished kingdom's sacrifice, a party journeys near Mount Meru/Himavat to recover King Marutta's ancient buried gold, worshipping Śiva and the guardian Yakṣas _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira's party, Yakṣas, Kinnaras)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.3-5, 63-65)
- **The infant swap at Gokula** — Vasudeva carries Kṛṣṇa to Gokula and exchanges him for Yaśodā's newborn daughter, the goddess, returning her to Kaṃsa's prison _(actors: Vasudeva, Kṛṣṇa, Yaśodā, the infant goddess)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/vindhyavasini.md) — (Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.3; Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.3)
- **The Kama-gita: Desire sings of its indestructibility** — Desire personified sings that he cannot be slain by force and is transcended only by knowledge, an inset in Krishna's instruction _(actors: Kama, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/lesser-gitas-catalog.md) — (Mahabharata Ashvamedhika Parva ~14.13)
- **The matrilineal heir-pact and marriage** — King Citravāhana gives his only daughter Citrāṅgadā (a putrikā) to Arjuna on the condition that her son remain in Maṇipura as Citravāhana's heir; Arjuna accepts and weds her _(actors: Citravāhana, Citrāṅgadā, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/chitrangada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~207)
- **The owl-omen inspires the night raid** — Sleepless under a banyan, Ashvatthaman watches an owl kill sleeping crows one by one, which gives him the plan to massacre the sleeping enemy camp _(actors: Ashvatthaman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/10-sauptika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sauptika Parva 1)
- **The poisoned pool** — Kāliya's venom boils a reach of the Yamunā, killing birds, cattle, and people who touch the water _(actors: Kāliya, river creatures and cowherds)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kaliya-mardana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.16; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **The sages curse Samba's iron club** — At Pindaraka, idle Yadava princes dress Krishna's son Samba as a pregnant woman to mock visiting rishis (Vishvamitra, Kanva, Narada and others), who curse that Samba will deliver an iron club that destroys the whole Vrishni-Andhaka race _(actors: Samba, Vishvamitra, Kanva, Narada, Durvasa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/16-mausala-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 16.1; Vishnu Purana 5.37)
- **The Sañjaya-yāna peace-embassy to the Pāṇḍavas** — Dhṛtarāṣṭra sends Sañjaya as envoy to the Pāṇḍava camp at Upaplavya with a hollow peace-offer; Yudhiṣṭhira and Kṛṣṇa see through it, and Sañjaya reports back faithfully, rebuking the king _(actors: Sañjaya, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sanjaya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva (~22–32))
- **The Samshaptaka oath and Arjuna drawn off** — The Trigarta king Susharman and his brothers swear a death-pact to kill Arjuna or die, challenging him to a separate sector to pull him from Yudhishthira _(actors: Susharman, Arjuna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva 17-18 (Samshaptaka-vadha))
- **The six questions** — Yudhiṣṭhira asks the one supreme deity, the highest goal, whom to worship for the auspicious, and the greatest/easiest dharma _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/vishnu-sahasranama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Anuśāsana Parva XIII.135)
- **The summons of the flute** — On the autumn full-moon night Kṛṣṇa's flute draws the gopīs from their homes, some leaving their bodies through love-meditation _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, the gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rasa-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.29)
- **The Syamantaka conspiracy against Satrājit** — A spurned suitor of Satyabhāmā, Kṛtavarma joins Akrūra in inciting Śatadhanvā to murder the sleeping Satrājit and seize the Syamantaka jewel _(actors: Kṛtavarma, Akrūra, Śatadhanvā, Satrājit, Satyabhāmā, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kritavarma.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.57; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13)
- **The three infallible arrows** — Through tapas Barbarīka earns three infallible arrows (Tīn Bāṇ) from Śiva and/or the Goddess that mark and annihilate any target, making him unconquerable _(actors: Barbarīka, Śiva, the Goddess (Kāmākhyā / Nava-Durgās))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/barbarika-khatu-shyam.md) — (Folk Mahābhārata; Khāṭū Śyām sthala-tradition)
- **The Vasus' bargain with Gaṅgā** — The eight Vasus, cursed by Vasiṣṭha for stealing his cow, ask Gaṅgā to be their mortal mother and drown them to free them _(actors: Eight Vasus, Vasiṣṭha, Gaṅgā, Dyaus)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~96–97)
- **The wife's urging and the gift of flattened rice** — Sudama's starving wife begs a few fistfuls of prithuka (flattened rice) from neighbours and ties them in a cloth as his only gift for Krishna _(actors: Sudama, his wife)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.80.9-13)
- **Twelve-year tapas to Sūrya at Candrabhāgā** — Counselled that only the Sun can cure disease, Sāmba performs severe penance to Sūrya at Mitravana on the Candrabhāgā _(actors: Sāmba, Sūrya)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/konark-sun-temple-arka-kshetra.md) — (Sāmba Purāṇa)
- **Twin-birth of Draupadī** — Draupadī (Kṛṣṇā) rises unasked from the same altar immediately after Dhṛṣṭadyumna, his fire-born twin _(actors: Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Draupadī, Yāja)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dhrishtadyumna.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Uddhava consoles Nanda and Yaśodā** — Reaching Vraja at sunset, Uddhava hears Nanda and Yaśodā pour out their parental grief and reassures them that Kṛṣṇa as the Supreme Self is never truly absent _(actors: Uddhava, Nanda, Yaśodā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhramara-gita-uddhava-sandesha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.46.12–50)
- **Uṣā's boon-dream** — Granted a boon by Gaurī/Pārvatī, Uṣā dreams of and makes love to an unknown youth, then wakes longing for the lover she cannot name _(actors: Uṣā, Pārvatī (Gaurī))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/banasura-usha-aniruddha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.62)
- **Uttanka eats the bull's dung (Indra's amrita)** — On the road a giant on a bull bids Uttanka eat dung and urine, which are secretly amrita given by Indra on Airavata, making him proof against death _(actors: Uttanka, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahabharata Paushya Parva)
- **Varuṇa gives Arjuna the Gāṇḍīva at Khāṇḍava** — At the burning of the Khāṇḍava forest Varuṇa bestows on Arjuna the great bow Gāṇḍīva, two inexhaustible quivers, and a divine chariot _(actors: Varuṇa, Arjuna, Agni)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/varuna.md) — (Mahābhārata)
