- **Vatsāsura the calf-demon slain** — A demon disguised as a stray calf is seized by the hind legs, whirled, and dashed against a wood-apple tree by Krishna _(actors: Krishna, Vatsāsura, cowherd boys)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vraja-boyhood-demons.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.11; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Śveta condemned to devour his own body** — After death Śveta is punished by being made to eat his own flesh repeatedly, because his gifts fed only himself and never another _(actors: King Śveta (in the afterworld))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/dana-upakhyanas-shveta-and-the-mongoose.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva)
- **Vikarṇa's protest in the dice-hall** — Alone among the hundred Kauravas, Vikarṇa rises and argues that Draupadī was never lawfully staked, since Yudhiṣṭhira had already lost himself before wagering her _(actors: Vikarṇa, Draupadī, Yudhiṣṭhira, Bhīṣma, Droṇa, the Kuru assembly)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vikarna.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva ~61)
- **Vishnu deceives Vrinda and the origin of Tulsi** — To break the invincible asura Jalandhara, Vishnu deceives his chaste wife Vrinda in his guise; she curses Vishnu and self-immolates, reborn as the tulsi plant (origin of Tulsi Vivaha) _(actors: Vrinda, Jalandhara, Vishnu, Shiva)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/padma-purana.md) — (Padma Purana)
- **Vishvakarman Builds Dvaraka in a Single Day** — Krishna asks the ocean-god Samudra for land and Vishvakarman raises the golden sea-fortress city of Dvaraka in a single day on reclaimed sea-land _(actors: Krishna, Samudra, Vishvakarman)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dwarka-bet-dwarka-saurashtra.md) — (Harivamsha 2.58; Bhagavata 10.50.50)
- **Viṣṇu tricks the asura into destroying himself** — Viṣṇu (as a brāhmaṇa boy, or as Mohinī in the Bhasmāsura folk version) dupes the demon into laying his own hand on his head, killing himself _(actors: Viṣṇu, Vṛkāsura/Bhasmāsura, Mohinī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhasmasura-and-vrikasura.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.88; regional tradition)
- **Viśvāvasu fathers Pramadvarā by Menakā** — The gandharva Viśvāvasu fathers Pramadvarā by the apsarā Menakā; the abandoned child is reared by the sage Sthūlakeśa and betrothed to the Bhārgava Ruru, setting up the half-lifespan revival and ahiṃsā parable _(actors: Viśvāvasu; Menakā; Pramadvarā; Sthūlakeśa; Ruru)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/gandharvas-and-kinnaras.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva, Pauloma Parva)
- **Vyāsa's boon of a hundred sons** — The pleased sage Vyāsa grants Gāndhārī the boon of a hundred sons equal to her husband _(actors: Gāndhārī, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/gandhari.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira's Rājasūya and the killing of Jarāsandha** — To claim sovereignty Yudhiṣṭhira performs the Rājasūya yajña; on Kṛṣṇa's counsel the obstacle-king Jarāsandha is killed by Bhīma before the rite _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa, Bhīma, Jarāsandha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yudhishthira.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva)
- **Yudhishthira honours the elders before battle** — Yudhishthira lays down his weapons and walks unarmed across to touch the feet of Bhishma, Drona, Kripa and Shalya, seeking their leave to fight; each blesses him with victory _(actors: Yudhishthira, Bhishma, Drona, Kripa, Shalya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva 43)
- **Yudhishthira's tour of Naraka** — In the Svargarohana Parva, Yudhishthira is given a brief tour of the hells before the final reckoning _(actors: Yudhishthira, Yama)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/narakas-and-afterlife.md) — (Mahabharata, Svargarohana Parva)
- **Abduction of Subhadrā** — Arjuna carries Subhadrā off in his chariot as she returns from worship at Raivataka, scattering the guards _(actors: Arjuna, Subhadrā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subhadra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva (Subhadrā-haraṇa Parva))
- **Abhimanyu Weds Uttara** — Abhimanyu marries Uttara, daughter of King Virata, sealing the Matsya-Panchala-Yadava alliance for the coming war _(actors: Abhimanyu, Uttara, Virata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/abhimanyu.md) — (Mahabharata, Virata Parva)
- **Arjuna Captures Drupada** — As Drona's guru-fee Arjuna defeats and binds King Drupada of Panchala, sowing the enmity that produces Draupadi and Dhrishtadyumna _(actors: Arjuna, Drupada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~137-138)
- **Arjuna rebukes Babhruvahana** — Bound by kshatriya code, Arjuna reproaches his son as unwarriorlike for offering submission and demands combat _(actors: Arjuna, Babhruvāhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/babhruvahana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~79–80)
- **Arjuna's despondency between the armies** — Arjuna has Krishna drive between the armies; seeing his kin arrayed to kill one another he is overcome with grief, his limbs failing and Gandiva slipping from his hand _(actors: Arjuna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva 25 / Gita 1)
- **Arjuna's tapas and battle with Śiva-as-Kirāta on Indrakīla** — During the Pāṇḍavas' forest exile Arjuna performs severe tapas on Mount Indrakīla, fights Śiva disguised as a Kirāta hunter over a slain boar, and on recognising the god is granted the Pāśupatāstra — the episode dramatised in Bhāravi's Kirātārjunīya _(actors: Arjuna, Śiva (as Kirāta))_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/meru-kailash-and-cosmic-mountains.md) — (Mahābhārata (Vana-parva, Kairāta-parva); Bhāravi, Kirātārjunīya)
- **Ashvatthaman slays the Pandya king** — On the 16th day Ashvatthaman, bent on vengeance, slays the mighty Pandya king Malayadhvaja in a long archery duel, cutting off his head and arms _(actors: Ashvatthaman, Malayadhvaja)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/08-karna-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva 16-22)
- **Ashvatthaman worships Shiva at the camp gate** — Blocked by a divine three-eyed guardian, Ashvatthaman offers his own body as oblation to Shiva, who grants him a celestial sword and enters his body so Rudra's destructive energy works through him _(actors: Ashvatthaman, Shiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/10-sauptika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sauptika Parva 7)
- **Attempted disrobing of Draupadi (vastraharana)** — Duhshasana tries to strip Draupadi in open court, but endless garments appear until he collapses exhausted, unable to bare her _(actors: Duḥśāsana, Draupadī, Karṇa, Kṛṣṇa (variant))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dushasana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva 61)
- **Avatara of Gajanana** — Ganesha descends in Dvapara Yuga as the mouse-mounted Gajanana, born to Shiva and Parvati, slaying the demon of greed/ego (Sindura) _(actors: Ganesha (Gajanana), Shiva, Parvati, Sindura)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/ganesha-and-mudgala-upapuranas.md) — (Ganesha Purana (Krida-khanda))
- **Bakāsura the crane-demon torn at the beak** — A giant crane swallows Krishna, is forced to disgorge him by burning heat, then is split apart by his beak _(actors: Krishna, Bakāsura, cowherd boys)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vraja-boyhood-demons.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.11; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Balarama marries Revati and shrinks her with his plough** — On Brahma's counsel Revati weds Balarama, who taps her with his plough-tip to reduce her to the stature of his own era _(actors: Balarama, Revati)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-05-raivata.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 9.3; Vishnu Purana 4.1)
- **Bhima Poisoned and Given the Naga-nectar** — Duryodhana feeds Bhima poison and casts him into the Ganga, but the Naga king Vasuki gives him eight jars of nectar conferring immense strength _(actors: Bhima, Duryodhana, Vasuki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhima.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~128)
- **Bhima slays Kirmira** — In the Kamyaka woods Bhima kills the man-eating rakshasa Kirmira bare-handed _(actors: Bhima, Kirmira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 11)
- **Bhishma's Terrible Vow and the Boon of Icchha-mrityu** — Devavrata renounces the throne and takes the dreadful vow of lifelong celibacy so Satyavati's son may rule, earning the name Bhishma and the boon of death-at-will _(actors: Devavrata, Shantanu, Satyavati, Dasharaja)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhishma.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~100)
- **Bhīṣma names Viṣṇu/Vāsudeva as supreme refuge** — Bhīṣma answers that Viṣṇu/Vāsudeva is the supreme deity and that praising Him by the thousand names is the highest and easiest dharma _(actors: Bhīṣma, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/vishnu-sahasranama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Anuśāsana Parva XIII.135)
- **Birth of Babhruvāhana and Arjuna's departure** — After three years at Maṇipura, Citrāṅgadā bears Babhruvāhana, who is left in Maṇipura as crown-prince per the pact, and Arjuna resumes his pilgrimage alone _(actors: Citrāṅgadā, Arjuna, Babhruvāhana, Citravāhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/chitrangada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~207–208)
- **Birth of Karna in armor** — Karna is born already wearing a golden kavacha and kundala fused to his skin, dipped in amrita, making him invulnerable while he keeps them _(actors: Karna, Surya, Kunti)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva ~308; Adi Parva)
- **Birth of Karna to Kunti and Surya** — Using Durvasa's mantra Kunti summons Surya and bears Karna armored with kavacha-kundala, then sets the infant adrift on the river _(actors: Kunti, Surya, Karna, Adhiratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Adi/Vana Parva)
- **Birth of Krishna** — Krishna is born at midnight on Janmashtami as the dark-hued eighth child, displaying his four-armed Vishnu form before assuming infant shape _(actors: Krishna, Devaki, Vasudeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.3)
- **Birth of Krishna and the substituted goddess** — Balarāma's embryo is moved to Rohiṇī and Krishna is born and carried across the Yamunā to Nanda and Yaśodā, swapped for the infant goddess Ekānaṃśā/Nidrā who escapes Kaṃsa's hand and prophesies his doom _(actors: Krishna, Vasudeva, Nanda, Yaśodā, Ekānaṃśā, Kaṃsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/harivamsha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva)
- **Brahma abducts and hides the calves and boys** — Brahma secretly carries off first the calves and then the cowherd boys, hiding both in a mountain cave in yogic sleep _(actors: Brahma, cowherd boys, calves)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/brahma-vimohana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.13.16-18)
- **Capture by Arjuna and loss of half Pāñcāla** — As Droṇa's teaching-fee, Arjuna defeats and binds Drupada; Droṇa seizes the northern half of Pāñcāla _(actors: Drupada, Arjuna, Droṇa, the Kauravas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drupada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~137)
- **Citralekhā's magical portraiture** — The yoginī Citralekhā paints likenesses of gods and Yādavas, and Uṣā identifies her dream-lover as Aniruddha, Kṛṣṇa's grandson _(actors: Citralekhā, Uṣā, Aniruddha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/banasura-usha-aniruddha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.62; Harivaṃśa Viṣṇu Parva)
- **Citrāṅgada's pride and the gandharva's challenge** — The proud warrior-king Citrāṅgada is challenged by a gandharva king of the same name who takes the shared title as an affront _(actors: Citrāṅgada (Kuru prince), Citrāṅgada (gandharva))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vichitravirya-and-chitrangada.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~101)
- **Co-architect of the lacquer house** — Śakuni helps Duryodhana and Purocana plan the Lākṣāgṛha at Vāraṇāvata to burn the Pāṇḍavas alive; the plot is foiled _(actors: Śakuni, Duryodhana, Purocana, the Pāṇḍavas, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shakuni.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~141–151)
- **Composition of the Mahabharata** — Vyasa authors the 100,000-verse Mahabharata, dictated to Ganesha as scribe on the condition that Vyasa never pause and Ganesha never write without understanding _(actors: Vyasa, Ganesha)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 1)
- **Curse of the sage Kindama** — Pāṇḍu shoots the sage Kindama mating in deer form and is cursed to die the instant he approaches a wife in desire, barring natural fatherhood _(actors: Pāṇḍu, Kindama, Mādrī, Kuntī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/madri.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Damayantī's svayaṃvara and the five identical Nalas** — At the svayaṃvara the four Lokapālas mimic Nala's form; Damayantī picks the true Nala by divine signs and the gods grant him boons _(actors: Damayantī, Nala, Indra, Agni, Varuṇa, Yama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 53–56)
- **Death of Prasena and the lion** — Satrājit's brother Prasena wears the gem hunting and is killed with his horse by a lion, which carries off the jewel _(actors: Prasena, lion)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13)
- **Death of Vicitravīrya, widowhood** — After seven years Vicitravīrya dies childless of consumption, leaving both queens young widows and the Kuru line near extinction _(actors: Vicitravīrya, Ambikā, Ambālikā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ambika-ambalika.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~103)
- **Departure from Vraja and gopīs' grief** — The brothers leave Vṛndāvana on Akrūra's chariot; the gopīs' separation (viraha) is left unresolved _(actors: Krishna, Balarāma, Akrūra, the gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.39)
- **Descent of Vishnu as Krishna** — Vishnu descends as Krishna at the close of the Dvapara Yuga of this 28th Mahayuga, the defining reason this cycle matters above all others _(actors: Vishnu; Krishna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/README.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 1; Mahabharata)
- **Dhanvantari reborn as king of Kashi** — A later Dhanvantari is reborn into the royal house of Kashi (identified with Divodasa), where he systematizes and publicly teaches the Ayurvedic system _(actors: Dhanvantari, Divodasa)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/dhanvantari.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 9.17; Brahmanda Purana; Sushruta Samhita)
- **Durvāsā's mantra-boon to Kuntī and the birth of Karṇa** — Pleased by a year of flawless service from the young princess Kuntī, Durvāsā grants her a mantra to summon any deity and beget a child of his nature; testing it on Sūrya, she bears Karṇa before marriage, seeding the Kuru tragedy _(actors: Durvāsā, Kuntī, Sūrya, Karṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ambarisha-durvasa.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva 1.104; Vana Parva ~305–306)
- **Duryodhana hides in Lake Dvaipayana** — With his army destroyed, Duryodhana flees and by yogic power solidifies the waters of Lake Dvaipayana, lying hidden beneath the surface to regather strength _(actors: Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/09-shalya-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Shalya Parva (Hrada-pravesha))
- **Dushyanta meets Shakuntala at Kanva's ashrama** — King Dushyanta, hunting from Hastinapura, enters Kanva's hermitage in the sage's absence, is received and smitten by Shakuntala, and the two fall in mutual love _(actors: Dushyanta, Shakuntala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shakuntala-bharata.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva, Sambhava)
- **Duṣyanta & Śakuntalā Wed by Gandharva Rite** — Duṣyanta and Śakuntalā marry by gandharva vivāha (mutual consent without ritual) in Kaṇva's forest hermitage — the locus classicus of the love-match marriage _(actors: Duṣyanta, Śakuntalā)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/gandharvas-and-kinnaras.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva; Abhijñānaśākuntalam)
- **Ekalavya seals the dog's mouth with arrows** — On a hunt Ekalavya fires seven arrows by sound alone (śabda-vedha) into a barking dog's open mouth, sealing it without drawing blood, astonishing the Kuru princes _(actors: Ekalavya, Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ekalavya.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Gandhari's panoramic lament over the slain** — By her divine eye Gandhari surveys all of Kurukshetra and describes the carnage corpse by corpse to Krishna, naming Duryodhana, Abhimanyu, Karna, Drona, Jayadratha and the slaughtered warrior aristocracy _(actors: Gandhari, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/11-stri-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Stri Parva 16-25 (Stri-vilapa))
- **Gods reveal themselves and boon Shibi a son** — Indra and Agni resume true form, heal Shibi, and promise him a glorious son Kapotaroma, proving dharma upheld without limit overrides death _(actors: Indra, Agni, Shibi, Kapotaroma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shibi.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva)
- **Hiḍimbī falls for Bhīma** — Sent to seize the sleepers, the shape-shifting Hiḍimbī is smitten with desire for Bhīma, takes human-woman form, warns him of her brother, and offers love and rescue _(actors: Hiḍimbī, Bhīma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/hidimba-hidimbi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva (~154))
- **Humiliation of Jayadratha** — Bhīma shaves Jayadratha's head leaving five tufts and forces him to declare himself the Pāṇḍavas' slave before release _(actors: Bhīma, Jayadratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jayadratha.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva 271)
- **Indra's seven quenchings of the forest** — Indra, protector of Khāṇḍava and friend of Takṣaka, douses Agni's blaze with rain seven times _(actors: Indra, Agni, Takṣaka)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/khandava-vana-and-the-burning.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi-parva 215-216)
- **Indra's Sāṃvartaka deluge** — The slighted Indra commands the dissolution-clouds to destroy Vraja with a pralaya-like flood of rain, hail and gale _(actors: Indra, the Sāṃvartaka clouds, the people and cattle of Vraja)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/govardhana-dharana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.25; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa Book 5)
- **Institution of the Indra-dhvaja festival** — Vasu founds the annual Indra-maha / Indra-dhvaja banner-pole festival and bequeaths it to all kings as a royal rite of prosperity _(actors: Vasu Uparicara, Indra, later kings)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/uparichara-vasu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva (~63); Varāhamihira Bṛhat-saṃhitā (historical rite))
- **Irāvān's cavalry exploits at Kurukṣetra** — Mounted, Irāvān routs the cavalry of Śakuni's Gāndhāra brothers, slaying several in the early days of the war _(actors: Irāvān, Śakuni's brothers)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/aravan-iravan.md) — (Mahābhārata Bhīṣma Parva)
- **Journey to Dvārakā** — Sudāmā walks the long road to the opulent island-city of Dvārakā, desiring only the darśana of Kṛṣṇa _(actors: Sudāmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.80.14–20)
- **Kama reborn as Pradyumna and slays Sambara** — Kama is reborn as Pradyumna, son of Krishna and Rukmini; stolen by Sambara and raised by Mayavati (Rati), the grown Pradyumna slays Sambara and weds her _(actors: Pradyumna/Kama, Krishna, Rukmini, Sambara, Mayavati)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/kamadeva-and-minor-deities.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.55; Vishnu Purana 5.27)
- **Kṛṣṇa names Upamanyu as his source for the hymn** — Kṛṣṇa explains he learned the thousand names and Śiva-worship from the sage Upamanyu during his own austerities _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Upamanyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/shiva-sahasranama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Anuśāsana Parva XIII)
- **Karṇa overrules Vikarṇa** — Karṇa angrily rebukes the young Vikarṇa, rules Draupadī lawfully won and a slave, and orders her stripped, overruling the lone dharmic voice _(actors: Karṇa, Vikarṇa, Draupadī, Duḥśāsana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vikarna.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva ~61)
- **Kṛṣṇa revives the stillborn Parikṣit** — Parikṣit, struck in Uttarā's womb by Aśvatthāman's Brahmāstra and born seemingly dead, is restored to life by Kṛṣṇa's act of truth, securing the continuity of the Kuru line _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Parikṣit, Uttarā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.65-69)
- **Kṛṣṇa rides to Kuṇḍina; the hostile kings gather** — Kṛṣṇa drives to Vidarbha with Dāruka; Balarāma follows with the Yādava army; Śiśupāla's coalition (Jarāsandha, Śālva, Dantavakra and others) awaits _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Dāruka, Balarāma, Śiśupāla, Jarāsandha, Śālva, Dantavakra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rukmini-harana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.53)
- **Karkoṭaka Bites and Disguises Nala** — The black nāga Karkoṭaka bites and disfigures King Nala in a benign bite that hides him from Kali's persecution and later lets him resume his form _(actors: Karkoṭaka, Nala)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/nagas-and-serpents.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva, Nala-Damayantī episode)
- **Kaṃsa Deposes and Imprisons Ugrasena** — Kaṃsa deposes and imprisons his father Ugrasena, king of Mathurā, ruling as a despot allied to Jarāsandha of Magadha _(actors: Kaṃsa, Ugrasena, Jarāsandha)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10)
- **Krishna absorbs the Vaishnavastra; Arjuna slays Bhagadatta** — Bhagadatta of Pragjyotisha hurls the Vaishnavastra, which Krishna absorbs onto his chest as the Vaijayanti garland; Arjuna then slays Bhagadatta on his elephant Supratika _(actors: Bhagadatta, Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva 25-29)
- **Krishna delivers the Uddhava Gita** — At the eve of Kali Yuga Krishna gives Uddhava his final systematic teaching (the Uddhava/Hamsa Gita) synthesizing bhakti, jnana, yoga and Samkhya before departing _(actors: Krishna, Uddhava)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uddhava-gita.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11.6.40-11.29)
- **Krishna's Departure and Onset of Kali** — Krishna passes away and the Yadava line ends, after which Kali personified enters the world, marking the turn into the Kali Yuga _(actors: Krishna, Kali)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 1.15)
- **Krishna's exodus and the building of Dwaraka** — Krishna abandons Mathura and has Vishvakarma build the island-city Dwaraka on land reclaimed from the ocean, transferring the citizens there by yogic power _(actors: Krishna, Vishvakarma, Samudra (ocean), Yadavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/muchukunda-and-kalayavana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.50.49-57; Vishnu Purana V.23; Harivamsha)
- **Krishna's lap fulfils the sign** — When Shrutashrava places the child on Krishna's lap the third eye and extra arms drop away, revealing Krishna as the appointed killer _(actors: Krishna, Shrutashrava, Shishupala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shishupala.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha-parva 40)
- **Krishna Slays Shankhasura at Bet Dvaraka** — At Shankhoddhara (Bet Dvaraka) Krishna slays the conch-demon Shankhasura and recovers the Vedas, the myth tied to his Panchajanya conch _(actors: Krishna, Shankhasura)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dwarka-bet-dwarka-saurashtra.md) — (sthala-purana; Wikipedia)
- **Kṛtavarma enrolled as a Kaurava general** — The Yādava clan splits for the war; Kṛtavarma brings a Vṛṣṇi-Bhoja contingent to fight for Duryodhana while Sātyaki fights for the Pāṇḍavas _(actors: Kṛtavarma, Duryodhana, Sātyaki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kritavarma.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga/Bhīṣma Parvas)
- **Kuntī invokes Sūrya and bears Karṇa** — Testing the mantra, Kuntī summons Sūrya, who is compelled to beget Karṇa upon her, born with natural golden armor and earrings, while restoring her virginity _(actors: Kuntī, Sūrya, Karṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi/Vana Parva)
- **Kuntī invokes Vāyu and conceives Bhīma** — Using Durvāsā's mantra, Queen Kuntī invokes Vāyu and conceives Bhīma, strongest of the Pāṇḍavas, Vāyu's might made mortal _(actors: Kuntī, Vāyu, Bhīma)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/vayu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Lomaśa leads the Pāṇḍavas on the walked tīrtha-circuit** — The sage Lomaśa conducts the exiled Pāṇḍavas around the ford-network, the catalogue dramatised as a journey with inset legends at each station _(actors: Lomaśa, Yudhiṣṭhira and the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mahabharata-tirtha-yatra-fords.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 89–156)
- **Maha-Rasa — Krishna's circle-dance with the gopis** — On the Sharad full-moon night Krishna's flute summons the gopis to the rasa-lila, multiplying himself to partner each; tradition stretches the night to a night of Brahma _(actors: Krishna; the gopis (Braj/Vrindavana))_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/sharad-purnima-kojagari-lakshmi-puja.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.29-33; Harivamsha)
- **Marriage to Śāntanu and the bride-price** — Śāntanu seeks her in marriage; the fisher-chief demands that her son inherit the throne, prompting Devavrata's renunciation and terrible vow of celibacy that makes him Bhīṣma, after which she is wed as queen _(actors: Śāntanu, the fisher-chief, Devavrata/Bhīṣma, Satyavatī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/satyavati.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~100)
- **Mastery of all knowledge in sixty-four days** — The brothers master the Vedas, weapons, and sixty-four arts on single hearings, completing their education in sixty-four days _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Sandipani)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sandipani-pancajana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.45; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **Mudgala endures without anger; Durvāsā blesses him** — Through repeated ruin Mudgala remains serene and free of resentment; Durvāsā, won over, declares his anger-free virtue and pronounces he will ascend bodily to heaven _(actors: Mudgala, Durvāsā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva 246)
- **Māyāvatī (Rati) finds and rears Pradyumna** — The netted fish is given to Śambara's cook Māyāvatī, who finds the living boy; Nārada reveals he is Kāma and she, as Rati, secretly raises him _(actors: Māyāvatī (Rati), Nārada, Pradyumna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/pradyumna.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.55; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Nārada reveals Krishna's birth-secret to Kaṃsa** — After Ariṣṭa's fall Nārada visits Kaṃsa, discloses that Devakī's child survives as the Vraja boy, and sets the Keśin mission in motion _(actors: Nārada, Kaṃsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/keshi-arishta-keshava.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.36)
- **Śāntanu's accession seeds the Kuru succession crisis** — With Devāpi disqualified and Bāhlīka settled abroad, Śāntanu is consecrated at Hastināpura, setting up the later Kuru succession crisis _(actors: Śāntanu, Devāpi, Bāhlīka)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/devapi.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.20)
- **Peace through abandoning hope ('āśā hi paramaṃ duḥkham')** — The instant Piṅgalā gives up hope she finds deep calm and sleeps in peace, declaring hope the supreme misery and hopelessness the supreme bliss; she resolves on renunciation _(actors: Piṅgalā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pingala-gita.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śānti Parva, Mokṣa-dharma ~XII.168)
- **Ploughing of Kurukṣetra with the golden share** — Kuru ploughs the Samanta-pañcaka plain on the Sarasvatī with a golden ploughshare as prolonged tapas, in the Vāmana Purāṇa yoking Yama's buffalo and Śiva's bull and sowing the dharmic virtues _(actors: Kuru, Yama, Śiva)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/kuru-the-eponym.md) — (Mahābhārata Śalya Parva (Kurukṣetra-māhātmya stratum); Vāmana Purāṇa Kurukṣetra-māhātmya)
- **Pratīpa's boon and the naming of Śāntanu** — Gaṅgā sits on Pratīpa's right thigh and is promised to his son; Pratīpa later begets in tranquillity a son named Śāntanu _(actors: Pratīpa, Sunandā, Gaṅgā, Śāntanu)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~97; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.20)
- **Prince Uttara's boast and panic** — Left alone in the city, Prince Uttara boasts he would scatter the Kuru host, then panics at the sight of the army and flees, only to be dragged back by Brihannala _(actors: Uttara, Brihannala (Arjuna), Uttaraa, Draupadi/Sairandhri)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/virata-and-his-children.md) — (Mahabharata, Virata Parva 35-37)
- **Revival of the poisoned cowherds and cattle** — Krishna revives cowherd boys and cows killed by the tainted water with his nectarean glance _(actors: Krishna, gopa boys, cattle)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kaliya-mardana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.15-16)
- **Śārṅga passes to Kṛṣṇa (Śārṅgapāṇi)** — Viṣṇu's bow Śārṅga is borne by Kṛṣṇa, giving rise to his epithets Śārṅgin and Śārṅgapāṇi ('Śārṅga-in-hand'), the latter enshrined as the dedicatory name of the Sārangapāni temple at Kumbhakoṇam _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Viṣṇu)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Wikipedia (Sharnga); Ayudhapurusha tradition)
- **Samba cured by worshipping Surya** — The leprous Samba, son of Krishna, is cured by worshipping Surya, founding solar worship at Mitravana/Multan _(actors: Samba, Surya, Krishna)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/navagraha.md) — (Samba Purana; Bhavishya Purana)
- **Saṃvaraṇa's penance on the mountain** — The lovesick king performs austerities to win Tapatī, who insists her father Sūrya must give her in proper marriage _(actors: Saṃvaraṇa, Tapatī)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/samvarana-tapati.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~171)
- **Self-revealed Sūrya image and the cure** — Pleased Sūrya cures Sāmba, who recovers a divine Sun-image fashioned by Viśvakarmā from the river and installs it, founding Arka Kṣetra _(actors: Sāmba, Sūrya, Viśvakarmā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/konark-sun-temple-arka-kshetra.md) — (Sāmba Purāṇa; Brahma Purāṇa)
- **Shalya tricked to the Kaurava side** — The Madra king Shalya, intercepted by Duryodhana's hospitality, is bound by gratitude to fight for the Kauravas but secretly promises Yudhishthira to dishearten Karna as charioteer _(actors: Shalya, Duryodhana, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva, Sainyodyoga)
- **Sharadvat teaches Kripa the science of arms** — Sharadvat returns to Shantanu's court and imparts the full dhanurveda to his son, making Kripa a complete acharya _(actors: Sharadvat, Kripa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kripa.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~130)
- **Slaying of Jarasandha by Bhima** — Disguised as brahmanas, the three reach Magadha; Krishna's leaf-splitting hint lets Bhima tear Jarasandha in two and fling the halves apart, freeing the captive kings _(actors: Krishna, Bhima, Arjuna, Jarasandha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva 20-24)
- **Sūrya's gift of the Akṣaya-pātra** — Yudhiṣṭhira propitiates Sūrya, who gives the inexhaustible cooking-vessel that yields unlimited food daily until Draupadī has eaten _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Sūrya, Draupadī)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva 3)
- **Sudama's journey to Dwaraka** — Longing only for darshana, Sudama walks the long road and enters the opulent island-city of Dwaraka to reach Krishna's palace _(actors: Sudama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.80.14-20)
- **The agra-pūjā to Kṛṣṇa and beheading of Śiśupāla** — Yudhiṣṭhira awards the first-worship of the Rājasūya to Kṛṣṇa; when Śiśupāla protests and exhausts his hundred allotted offenses, Kṛṣṇa beheads him with his discus _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa, Śiśupāla)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yudhishthira.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva)
- **The double rejection of Ambā** — Śālva spurns Ambā as won by another; Vicitravīrya and the vow-bound Bhīṣma also refuse her, leaving her abandoned and fixing blame on Bhīṣma _(actors: Ambā, Śālva, Vicitravīrya, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/amba-shikhandi.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva ~174–178)
- **The faithful housewife humbles Kauśika** — A devoted wife tending her husband tells the impatient Kauśika she is no crane to be burnt, that household duty done in love is itself the path, and sends him to the butcher of Mithilā _(actors: Kauśika, the pativratā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyadha-gita.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva ~198)
- **The flute sounds** — Kṛṣṇa sets the veṇu to his lips and fills the forest with music; the gopīs hear it and begin praising it to one another _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/venu-gita.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.21.5–7)
- **The Garuḍa-flight to Prāgjyotiṣa** — Kṛṣṇa and Satyabhāmā mount Garuḍa and cross the mountain-girt, weapon-warded defences in an aerial assault _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Satyabhāmā, Garuḍa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/narakasura-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.59; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **The Golden Swan Carries Nala's Suit** — A captured golden swan, released by Nala, flies to Vidarbha and extols him to Damayantī, kindling her longing _(actors: Nala, Damayantī, the haṃsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 51-52)
- **The infant dashed on the rock rises as eight-armed Devī** — Kaṃsa seizes the infant girl to smash her on a stone; she slips free and rises into the sky as an eight-armed armed goddess _(actors: Kaṃsa, the infant goddess (Nidrā/Yogamāyā/Ekānaṃśā))_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/vindhyavasini.md) — (Harivaṃśa Āryā-stava; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.1; Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.4)
- **The iron club ground to powder and the eraka grass born** — Samba produces the cursed iron club; on Ugrasena's order it is ground to powder and cast into the sea, but one unground lump is swallowed by a fish (whose splinter the hunter Jara takes as an arrowhead) and the filings wash ashore to grow into the sharp eraka reed _(actors: Samba, Ugrasena, Jara, a fish)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/16-mausala-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 16.1; Vishnu Purana 5.37)
- **The Pandavas take disguises at Virata's court** — The five brothers and Draupadi assume hidden identities (Kanka, Vallabha, Brihannala, Granthika, Tantipala, Sairandhri) and enter service at Virata's court, Arjuna's eunuch disguise fulfilling Urvashi's curse _(actors: Yudhishthira, Bhima, Arjuna, Nakula, Sahadeva, Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/04-virata-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Virata Parva 1-12)
- **The provocation cycle — Durvāsā devours all the food repeatedly** — Durvāsā eats Mudgala's entire gleaned store and abuses him across successive visits (six in the tale); Mudgala re-gleans and feeds him afresh each time without anger _(actors: Durvāsā, Mudgala, his family)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Mārkaṇḍeya-samāsya (Mudgala-Upākhyāna))
- **The secret pact with Yudhiṣṭhira** — Śalya promises Yudhiṣṭhira to demoralize Karṇa from the charioteer's seat when the decisive duel comes _(actors: Śalya, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shalya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva)
- **The testing dialogue** — Kṛṣṇa feigns propriety and tells the gopīs to return to their husbands; they argue the supremacy of prema over social dharma _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, the gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rasa-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.29)
- **The water-boon** — At parting Ulūpī grants Arjuna invincibility in water and the obedience of all aquatic creatures _(actors: Ulūpī, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ulupi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~206)
- **The Yakṣa-praśna (riddles of dharma)** — Yudhiṣṭhira chooses to answer rather than drink, and the disguised guardian poses a rapid catechism of dharma — the mother heavier than earth, the greatest wonder being men's denial of death, conduct over birth _(actors: the Yakṣa, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/yaksha-prashna.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 312-313)
- **Trained by Droṇa** — Knowing the prophecy, Droṇa accepts his destined killer as a pupil and teaches him the full dhanurveda _(actors: Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Droṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dhrishtadyumna.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Tṛṇāvarta the whirlwind-demon** — A demon in whirlwind form carries the child into the sky; Krishna grows immovably heavy and throttles him, crashing him dead onto a rock _(actors: Krishna (infant), Tṛṇāvarta, Kaṃsa, Yaśodā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-balya-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.7; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Two-year pregnancy and the flesh-ball miscarriage** — Jealous of Kuntī, Gāndhārī strikes her womb and miscarries a lifeless ball of flesh after carrying it two years _(actors: Gāndhārī, Kuntī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/gandhari.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~107)
- **Uddhava delivers Kṛṣṇa's message to the gopīs** — At dawn the gopīs gather around Uddhava, who conveys Kṛṣṇa's impersonal jñāna-yoga consolation that the Lord is the Self of all and never separated from them _(actors: Uddhava, the gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhramara-gita-uddhava-sandesha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.47.1–37)
- **Urvashī curses Arjuna to be a eunuch** — When Arjuna refuses her advances, Urvashī curses him to spend a year as the eunuch Bṛhannala _(actors: Urvashī, Arjuna)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/apsaras.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva)
- **Uttanka and King Paushya trade and retract curses** — Over a perceived insult Uttanka curses King Paushya to blindness and Paushya curses Uttanka to childlessness, then both retract _(actors: Uttanka, Paushya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahabharata Paushya Parva)
- **Śveta released by a sage at the tīrtha** — A sage/ascetic intervenes at the holy ford and frees Śveta from the cycle of self-devouring, establishing the site's sanctity and the charter on charitable giving _(actors: King Śveta, a sage/ascetic)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/dana-upakhyanas-shveta-and-the-mongoose.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva)
- **Vidura's coded warning of the lac-house** — As the Pāṇḍavas depart for Vāraṇāvata, Vidura warns Yudhiṣṭhira in coded Mleccha speech of the planned arson and arranges a miner's tunnel and a boat for their escape _(actors: Vidura, Yudhiṣṭhira, Purocana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vidura.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva, Jatugṛha-parva)
- **Vow to fight for the losing side** — Barbarīka vows to his mother to always fight for whichever side is losing, then rides toward Kurukṣetra to join the war _(actors: Barbarīka, his mother)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/barbarika-khatu-shyam.md) — (Folk Mahābhārata traditions)
- **Vyāsa, Devala and Nārada console Yudhiṣṭhira** — The sages console the grieving king, with Vyāsa arguing the slain went to heaven by kṣatriya-dharma and that expiation, not flight, is the king's path _(actors: Vyāsa, Devala, Nārada, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/12-shanti-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 12.23-36)
- **Vyāsa grants Sañjaya divine sight (divya-dṛṣṭi)** — After Dhṛtarāṣṭra declines restored eyesight, Vyāsa confers divya-dṛṣṭi on Sañjaya, making him invulnerable, unwearied, and able to perceive every act and thought on the battlefield so he can narrate the war _(actors: Vyāsa, Sañjaya, Dhṛtarāṣṭra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sanjaya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Bhīṣma Parva (~2))
- **Yājñavalkya Teaches Janaka the Tattvas** — Yājñavalkya expounds the Sāṃkhya enumeration of 24 tattvas, the 25th puruṣa, and the supreme 26th Īśvara to King Janaka _(actors: Yājñavalkya, Janaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mokshadharma-dialogues.md) — (Mahabharata Shanti Parva 310-311)
- **Yudhishthira's pre-war call for defectors** — On the first day at Kurukshetra, Yudhishthira crosses to honor the elders and publicly invites anyone on the Kaurava side to join him _(actors: Yudhishthira, Bhishma, Drona, Kripa, Shalya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yuyutsu.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhishma Parva ~43)
- **Abandonment of the infant Karṇa on the river** — To hide her disgrace, Kuntī sets the armored infant Karṇa adrift in a basket; he is found and raised by the charioteer Adhiratha and his wife Rādhā _(actors: Kuntī, Karṇa, Adhiratha, Rādhā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva)
- **Abhimanyu breaks into the cakra-vyuha** — Day 13: Drona forms the spinning cakra-vyuha; Abhimanyu, knowing only how to enter, breaks in while Jayadratha (by Shiva's boon) seals the gap behind him, trapping the boy alone _(actors: Abhimanyu, Drona, Jayadratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva 33-41)
- **A celestial messenger offers bodily ascent to svarga** — A devadūta arrives in a flying chariot to escort Mudgala in his own body to heaven, his merit having ripened _(actors: Devadūta (celestial messenger), Mudgala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva 247)
- **Aghāsura the python killed from within** — The python-demon swallows the boys and calves; Krishna enters and swells until it chokes, and Aghāsura's liberated spirit merges into Krishna _(actors: Krishna, Aghāsura, cowherd boys, calves)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vraja-boyhood-demons.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.12)
- **Agni petitions Kṛṣṇa and Arjuna at the Yamunā** — Agni, disguised as a tawny brāhmaṇa, asks Kṛṣṇa and Arjuna by the Yamunā to satisfy his hunger _(actors: Agni, Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/khandava-vana-and-the-burning.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi-parva 216-217)
- **Śalya made Karṇa's charioteer** — Karṇa demands a driver equal to Kṛṣṇa and names Śalya; insulted at driving for a sūta, Śalya consents only on the condition he may say what he wishes _(actors: Karṇa, Śalya, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shalya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Karṇa Parva 26–32)
- **Among the six who killed Abhimanyu** — Kṛtavarma is one of the six warriors who jointly broke and slew the lone Abhimanyu inside the Cakravyūha _(actors: Kṛtavarma, Abhimanyu, Droṇa, Karṇa, Kṛpa, Aśvatthāman, Bṛhadbala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kritavarma.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva 7.48)
- **Arjuna's Brihannala reveal at the cattle-raid** — Brihannala retrieves the hidden Gandiva from the shami tree, takes the reins with Uttara as charioteer, and single-handedly routs the Kuru army with the Sammohana astra _(actors: Arjuna (Brihannala), Uttara, Bhishma, Drona, Karna, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/virata-and-his-children.md) — (Mahabharata, Virata Parva 38-62)
- **Arjuna wins the Pashupatastra from Shiva** — Performing fierce tapas, Arjuna duels Shiva disguised as a Kirata over a slain boar and is granted the supreme Pashupata weapon _(actors: Arjuna, Shiva, Muka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 38-41)
- **Balarama returns from his Saraswati pilgrimage** — Having stayed neutral on a tirtha-yatra along the Saraswati for the war's duration, Balarama returns to Kurukshetra exactly in time to preside as referee over his two pupils' mace-duel _(actors: Balarama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/09-shalya-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Shalya Parva (Saraswata/Gada-yuddha))
- **Balarāma's fury and Kṛṣṇa's mediation** — Balarāma rages at the seizure; Kṛṣṇa pacifies him and the Yādavas, and the marriage is celebrated openly _(actors: Balarāma, Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna, Subhadrā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subhadra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva (Harāṇa-hārika Parva))
- **Bhima Carries the Pandavas from the Lacquer-House** — When the Kauravas set the flammable house at Varanavata ablaze, Bhima carries Kunti and his four brothers out through the escape tunnel _(actors: Bhima, Kunti, the Pandavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhima.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~145-147)
- **Bhima's twin vows and Draupadi's vow** — Bhima vows to tear open Duhshasana's chest and drink his blood and to break Duryodhana's thigh; Draupadi vows to leave her hair unbound until washed in Duhshasana's blood _(actors: Bhīma, Draupadī, Duḥśāsana, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dushasana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva 61–63)
- **Bhishma's vow** — King Shantanu weds Ganga who bears Devavrata (Bhishma); to enable Shantanu's marriage to Satyavati, Bhishma takes the vow of lifelong celibacy and renounces the throne, earning the boon of death-at-will _(actors: Shantanu, Ganga, Bhishma, Satyavati)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Bhīṣma recites the Piṅgalā parable to Yudhiṣṭhira** — From the bed of arrows, Bhīṣma quotes Piṅgalā's song as a Mokṣa-dharma proof-text on the cessation of craving _(actors: Bhīṣma; Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pingala-gita.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śānti Parva, Mokṣa-dharma ~XII.168)
- **Bhīṣma refuses to continue the line** — Satyavatī begs Bhīṣma to beget heirs or take the throne; he refuses both, bound by his vow of celibacy _(actors: Satyavatī, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ambika-ambalika.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~103)
- **Birth and rearing of Irāvān** — Ulūpī bears Arjuna's son Irāvān (Tamil Arāvāṇ) and rears him in the Nāga world after Arjuna departs _(actors: Ulūpī, Irāvān, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ulupi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~206; Bhīṣma Parva)
- **Birth of Kṛṣṇa at Mathurā** — Krishna is born in the prison of his uncle Kamsa at Mathura on the Yamuna in the Dvapara Yuga, later slaying Kamsa _(actors: Krishna, Kamsa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sapta-puri-and-sacred-cities.md) — (Garuda Purana; Bhagavata Purana 10; Vishnu Purana)
- **Boon from Śiva** — After austerities Jayadratha wins from Śiva the power to check the four Pāṇḍavas (not Arjuna) for one day in battle _(actors: Jayadratha, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jayadratha.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva 271–272)
- **Chitrasena Captures Duryodhana** — The gandharva Chitrasena, who taught Arjuna music in Amarāvatī, defeats and captures Duryodhana at the Ghoṣa-yātrā, only for the Pāṇḍavas to rescue him _(actors: Chitrasena, Duryodhana, Arjuna, Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/gandharvas-and-kinnaras.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva, Ghoṣa-yātrā Parva)
- **Conscience without defection** — Despite his dice-hall righteousness, Vikarṇa never crosses to the Pāṇḍavas like Yuyutsu; he stays loyal and fights for Duryodhana in the war _(actors: Vikarṇa, Duryodhana, Yuyutsu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vikarna.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana–Udyoga and war parvas)
- **Death of Irāvān by Alambuṣa** — The rākṣasa Alambuṣa beheads Irāvān through illusion-war; his fall (placed on the first or eighth day) grieves Arjuna _(actors: Irāvān, Alambuṣa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/aravan-iravan.md) — (Mahābhārata Bhīṣma Parva)
- **Devāpi and Bāhlīka set aside** — Elder brother Devāpi (blemished/ascetic) and Bāhlīka are barred from Hastināpura's throne, which passes to Śāntanu _(actors: Devāpi, Bāhlīka, Śāntanu, Pratīpa)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.20; Bhāgavata Purāṇa IX.22)
- **Dhanvantari teaches Sushruta surgery** — Sages led by Sushruta approach the Kashi king-physician to learn medicine, and he teaches them Shalya-tantra (surgery), transmitted as the Sushruta Samhita _(actors: Dhanvantari, Sushruta)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/dhanvantari.md) — (Sushruta Samhita Sutrasthana 1)
- **Division of the one Veda into four and the four disciples** — Vyāsa splits the single Veda into Ṛg, Yajur, Sāman and Atharva and entrusts each to Paila, Vaiśampāyana, Jaimini and Sumantu, with the itihāsa-purāṇa to Romaharṣaṇa _(actors: Vyāsa, Paila, Vaiśampāyana, Jaimini, Sumantu, Romaharṣaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa.md) — (Viṣṇu Purāṇa 3.4; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 1.4; Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Durvāsā declares Mudgala conquered and blesses bodily ascent** — Defeated by the gleaner's unbroken forbearance and charity, Durvāsā praises his titikṣā and dāna and pronounces that he will ascend to heaven in his own body _(actors: Durvāsā, Mudgala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Mārkaṇḍeya-samāsya (Mudgala-Upākhyāna))
- **Ekalavya surrenders his thumb as guru-dakṣiṇā** — When Arjuna protests being outclassed, Droṇa demands Ekalavya's right thumb as his teaching-fee, and Ekalavya without hesitation severs his thumb, permanently crippling his archery and preserving Arjuna's primacy _(actors: Ekalavya, Droṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ekalavya.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **First dance and disappearance** — Kṛṣṇa dances with the gopīs but vanishes to curb the pride (garva) that arose in them _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, the gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rasa-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.29–30)
- **Gandhari's curse on Krishna** — Charging Krishna with allowing the slaughter, Gandhari curses that in the thirty-sixth year his Yadava kin will destroy one another as the Kurus did and that he will die alone in the wilderness; Krishna accepts it as his own will _(actors: Gandhari, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/11-stri-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Stri Parva 25)
- **Ganesha Gita spoken to King Varenya** — As Gajanana, Ganesha reveals the Ganesha Gita to King Varenya, teaching karma-, jnana-, and bhakti-yoga in a Ganesha-fied Bhagavad Gita _(actors: Gajanana (Ganesha), King Varenya)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/ganesha-and-mudgala-upapuranas.md) — (Ganesha Purana (Krida-khanda ch. 138-148))
- **Garga's naming and the vision of the universe in his mouth** — Sage Garga secretly names the child Kṛṣṇa, and Yaśodā, checking for eaten dirt, beholds the entire cosmos within his mouth before Yogamāyā restores her maternal love — the locus of vātsalya-rasa _(actors: Krishna (infant), Yaśodā, Garga (sage))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-balya-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.8)
- **Herald at Draupadī's svayaṃvara** — Presides over his sister's bride-choice and secretly tracks the disguised Pāṇḍavas, identifying them to Drupada _(actors: Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Draupadī, Arjuna, Drupada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dhrishtadyumna.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Her sons and the dynastic collapse** — Satyavatī bears Śāntanu two sons, Citrāṅgada (killed young) and Vicitravīrya, who dies childless of consumption, leaving her royal line on the brink of extinction _(actors: Satyavatī, Śāntanu, Citrāṅgada, Vicitravīrya, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/satyavati.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~101–103)
- **Hiḍimba-vadha (slaying of Hiḍimba)** — Enraged by his sister's betrayal, Hiḍimba attacks; Bhīma fights him hand to hand and kills him, the first demon Bhīma slays _(actors: Bhīma, Hiḍimba, Hiḍimbī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/hidimba-hidimbi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva, Hiḍimba-vadha Parva (~155))
- **Indra's boon consecrating Kurukṣetra** — Moved by Kuru's austerity, Indra (or Viṣṇu) grants that whoever dies at Kurukṣetra attains heaven, fixing it as the supreme dharma-kṣetra _(actors: Kuru, Indra, Viṣṇu)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/kuru-the-eponym.md) — (Mahābhārata Śalya Parva; Vāmana Purāṇa)
- **Jāmbavān seizes the Syamantaka** — The bear-king Jāmbavān slays the lion, takes the gem, and gives it to his infant to play with in his cave _(actors: Jāmbavān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56)
- **Kṛṣṇa delivers the Anugītā to Arjuna** — Arjuna confesses he has forgotten the Gītā's wisdom; unable to repeat it verbatim, Kṛṣṇa relays an old conversation of the same import (the Anugītā) on transmigration, karma, the guṇas, and mokṣa _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.16-51)
- **Kali vows to destroy Nala and possesses him** — The age-demon Kali, enraged Nala won the bride, waits twelve years for a ritual lapse then enters Nala; Dvāpara possesses the dice _(actors: Kali, Dvāpara, Nala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 56)
- **Karna cast on the river** — Fearing disgrace, Kunti seals the infant in a basket and floats him down the rivers to the Ganga _(actors: Kunti, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva ~308)
- **Karna defeats but spares the lesser Pandavas** — Karna overpowers Nakula, Sahadeva and Yudhishthira but spares each, honoring his promise to Kunti to kill none of her sons but Arjuna _(actors: Karna, Nakula, Sahadeva, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/08-karna-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva 21-49)
- **Kṛṣṇa's leaf-test** — Disguised as a brāhmaṇa, Kṛṣṇa tests Barbarīka; the marking arrow pierces every leaf of a Pīpala tree and hovers over Kṛṣṇa's foot where a leaf is hidden _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Barbarīka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/barbarika-khatu-shyam.md) — (Folk Mahābhārata; Khāṭū Śyām sthala-tradition)
- **Kṛṣṇa's penance and boon of sons from Śiva** — Kṛṣṇa narrates having propitiated Śiva by tapas to obtain sons (notably Sāmba) and boons, framing the Śaiva material _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/shiva-sahasranama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Anuśāsana Parva XIII)
- **Kṛṣṇa's visit and Draupadī's grievance** — Kṛṣṇa comes to Kāmyaka; Draupadī recounts the dice-hall outrage and the resolve for eventual war is renewed _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Draupadī, Yudhiṣṭhira, Dhṛṣṭadyumna)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva 12–13)
- **Kṛṣṇa's welcome and the washing of feet** — Kṛṣṇa rises from Rukmiṇī's couch, embraces his ragged friend, washes his feet and honours him as an equal while Rukmiṇī fans him _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Sudāmā, Rukmiṇī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.80.19–27)
- **Kicaka's harassment of Sairandhri** — Kicaka, the queen's brother and army-commander, becomes inflamed with lust for Sairandhri (Draupadi), assaults her, and humiliates her by kicking and dragging her in the assembly hall _(actors: Kicaka, Draupadi, Sudeshna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/04-virata-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Virata Parva 14-16)
- **Kripi married to Drona; birth of Ashvatthaman** — Kripi is wedded to Drona, making Kripa the brother-in-law of Drona and uncle of their son Ashvatthaman _(actors: Kripi, Drona, Ashvatthaman, Kripa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kripa.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva)
- **Krishna, Balarama and Sudama Study at Sandipani's Gurukula** — Krishna, Balarama and the poor brahmin Sudama study the Vedas, 64 arts and weaponry at Rshi Sandipani's gurukula in Ujjain, where the Krishna-Sudama friendship begins _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Sudama, Sandipani)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/ujjain-avantika.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X)
- **Krishna multiplies himself into every boy and calf** — Krishna expands himself to become every missing cowherd boy and every calf, identical in form, voice, dress, habit and relationships _(actors: Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/brahma-vimohana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.13.19-22)
- **Krishna's boon to forgive a hundred offenses** — At Shrutashrava's plea Krishna vows to pardon a full hundred offenses of Shishupala before exacting any penalty _(actors: Krishna, Shrutashrava)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shishupala.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha-parva 40)
- **Krishna's Vṛndāvana demon-slayings** — Growing among the cowherds, Krishna and Balarāma slay the pastoral demons Pūtanā, Śakaṭa, the twin Arjuna trees, Kāliya, Dhenuka, Pralamba, Ariṣṭa, and Keśin in early form _(actors: Krishna, Balarāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/harivamsha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva)
- **Krishna welcomes and honours the beggar** — Krishna rises from Rukmini's couch, embraces Sudama, seats him on his own bed, washes his feet, and Rukmini fans him _(actors: Krishna, Sudama, Rukmini, the queens)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.80.19-27)
- **Kuntī lends Mādrī the Durvāsa mantra** — At Pāṇḍu's request Kuntī shares one use of her deity-summoning invocation-mantra with Mādrī so the junior queen may also bear sons _(actors: Kuntī, Mādrī, Pāṇḍu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/madri.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Mahabharata War and Krishna's Departure** — The Mahabharata war is fought near the Dvapara-Kali junction; Krishna's departure (3102 BCE) marks Kali Yuga fully entering, the traditional onset of the present age _(actors: Krishna; Pandavas; Kauravas)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/README.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 1.15; Mahabharata; Surya Siddhanta epoch)
- **Nahuṣa freed by Yudhiṣṭhira's riddles** — Ages later the python Nahuṣa seizes Bhīma; Yudhiṣṭhira answers his dharma-riddles, freeing both Bhīma and the cursed king, who ascends to heaven _(actors: Nahuṣa, Bhīma, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nahusha.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 176-181)
- **Nocturnal abduction of Aniruddha** — Citralekhā flies by yogic power to Dwārakā and bears the sleeping Aniruddha through the air to Uṣā's apartment in Śoṇitapura _(actors: Citralekhā, Aniruddha, Uṣā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/banasura-usha-aniruddha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.62; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Omens at Dwarka and the divine weapons depart** — Portents multiply at Dwarka as the 36th year comes; Krishna's Sudarshana Chakra, conch Panchajanya, chariot and horses, and Balarama's plough rise and vanish, the instruments of the avatar leaving first, and Krishna orders the fatal pilgrimage to Prabhasa _(actors: Krishna, Balarama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/16-mausala-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 16.2)
- **Pañcaśikha Instructs Janaka** — Pañcaśikha teaches Janaka an austere atheistic Sāṃkhya of guṇas and discriminative knowledge, witnessing the Kapila-Āsuri-Pañcaśikha lineage _(actors: Pañcaśikha, Janaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mokshadharma-dialogues.md) — (Mahabharata Shanti Parva 211-212, 218-219)
- **Paraśurāma's duel with Bhīṣma** — Ambā appeals to Bhīṣma's guru Paraśurāma, who fights Bhīṣma in a multi-day stalemate at Kurukṣetra ended by the gods' and Nārada's intervention _(actors: Ambā, Paraśurāma, Bhīṣma, Nārada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/amba-shikhandi.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva ~178–186)
- **Prabhasa praised in the Tirtha-yatra Parva** — Pulastya and Lomasa/Dhaumya name Prabhasa as the foremost western tirtha, where gods and Soma bathe, during the Pandavas' forest pilgrimage _(actors: Pulastya, Lomasa, Dhaumya, Bhishma, the Pandavas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/prabhasa-somnath-tirtha.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva (Tirtha-yatra Parva))
- **Proposing the dice-game** — After Duryodhana's humiliation at the Rājasūya, Śakuni proposes a rigged dice-game and offers to play as proxy, knowing the secret of the dice _(actors: Śakuni, Duryodhana, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shakuni.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva ~51–58)
- **Rati reveals herself and teaches māyā** — Grown Pradyumna recoils from Māyāvatī's courtship until she explains he is Kāma and she his wife Rati, then teaches him illusion-magic to defeat Śambara _(actors: Pradyumna, Māyāvatī (Rati))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/pradyumna.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.55; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Recitation of the thousand names** — Bhīṣma recites the one thousand names of Viṣṇu (the sahasranāma) to Yudhiṣṭhira as the surest path freeing one from sorrow _(actors: Bhīṣma, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/vishnu-sahasranama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Anuśāsana Parva XIII.135 (Viṣṇu Sahasranāma main body))
- **Sañjaya narrates the war and recites the Bhagavad Gītā** — Answering Dhṛtarāṣṭra's question 'dharma-kṣetre kuru-kṣetre', Sañjaya frames the entire battle-narrative, reciting the Bhagavad Gītā verbatim ('Sañjaya uvāca'), narrating the Viśvarūpa, and pronouncing its closing benediction _(actors: Sañjaya, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sanjaya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Bhīṣma Parva / Bhagavad Gītā 1.1, 18.74–78)
- **Sanjaya's peace-embassy fails** — Dhritarashtra sends Sanjaya with a message offering no land; Yudhishthira and Krishna expose its emptiness, refusing to surrender their birthright _(actors: Sanjaya, Dhritarashtra, Yudhishthira, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva, Sanjaya-yana)
- **Slaying of Keśin the horse-demon** — Krishna thrusts his arm down the gaping throat of the giant horse-demon, swelling it until the demon bursts and splits in two _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Keśin)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/keshi-arishta-keshava.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.37; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5; Harivaṃśa)
- **Slaying of the washerman** — Krishna kills Kaṃsa's arrogant washerman who refuses festive garments, then dresses in the cloth _(actors: Krishna, Balarāma, the rajaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.41; Harivaṃśa)
- **Sudama's Reunion with Krishna at Bet Dvaraka** — The poor brahmin Sudama's pinch of beaten rice is repaid with untold wealth at Bet Dvaraka, a key bhakti parable of Krishna's love for the lowly _(actors: Sudama, Krishna)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dwarka-bet-dwarka-saurashtra.md) — (Bhagavata 10.80-81)
- **Takshaka steals the earrings** — Takshaka, disguised as a naked mendicant, snatches the earrings Uttanka has set down and dives into a hole leading to Nagaloka _(actors: Takshaka, Uttanka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahabharata Paushya Parva)
- **The avadhuta's teaching of the 24 gurus** — Within the Uddhava Gita Krishna retells how a wandering avadhuta-brahmana, questioned by King Yadu, named 24 natural gurus as the source of his serene bliss _(actors: Krishna, Uddhava, avadhuta, Yadu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uddhava-gita.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11.7-9)
- **The Aśvamedha prescribed as expiation** — To purge the sin of the war, Yudhiṣṭhira is told he must rule and later perform the horse-sacrifice as prāyaścitta _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, sages)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/12-shanti-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 12.36-37)
- **The Bhagavad Gita spoken** — Krishna delivers his 700-verse teaching resolving Arjuna's paralysis through the imperishable atman, nishkama-karma, and the yogas of knowledge, action and devotion, climaxing in the Vishvarupa-darshana _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva 25-42 (Bhagavad Gita))
- **The Bhramara-gītā — the gopī's Song to the Bee** — A bumblebee alights and the chief gopī (read as Rādhā by later tradition), mad with love-in-separation, addresses it as Kṛṣṇa's fickle messenger, alternately rebuking and praising the absent Kṛṣṇa _(actors: a chief gopī (traditionally Rādhā), the bee, Uddhava (witness))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhramara-gita-uddhava-sandesha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.47.12–21)
- **The chariot-seizure at the goddess temple** — As Rukmiṇī emerges from worshipping the goddess outside the city, Kṛṣṇa lifts her onto his chariot before the assembled kings and drives off toward Dwārakā _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Rukmiṇī, the assembled suitor-kings)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rukmini-harana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.53-54)
- **The deer and forest creatures enchanted** — Does, birds, cows and calves are stilled and transfixed by the flute-song, drawing near Kṛṣṇa in love _(actors: deer, birds, peacocks, cows, calves)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/venu-gita.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.21.11–14)
- **The Dharma-vyādha teaches Kauśika** — The righteous butcher of Mithilā delivers the Vyādha-gītā — that no duty is impure, svadharma done without malice does not defile, ahiṃsā is intention, and service to parents is the highest dharma _(actors: Dharma-vyādha, Kauśika)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyadha-gita.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva ~198-206)
- **The Digvijaya: conquest of the four quarters** — The four younger Pandavas subdue the four directions, bringing back vast tribute that ignites Duryodhana's envy _(actors: Arjuna, Bhima, Sahadeva, Nakula)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva 25-31)
- **The fallen seed and the hawk** — Called to the śrāddha hunt on his queen Girikā's fertile day, Vasu's seed falls; he sends it to her by a hawk, but it is dropped into the Yamunā during an aerial fight _(actors: Vasu Uparicara, Girikā, the hawks)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/uparichara-vasu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva (~63))
- **The Four Gods Make Nala Their Messenger** — En route to the svayaṃvara the four Lokapālas compel the truthful Nala to plead their suit to the very woman he loves _(actors: Nala, Indra, Agni, Varuṇa, Yama, Damayantī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 53-54)
- **The gandharva marriage with the heir-condition** — Dushyanta argues gandharva marriage lawful and weds Shakuntala on her binding condition that her son shall be heir-apparent; he departs giving no token, and Kanva blesses the union on his return _(actors: Dushyanta, Shakuntala, Kanva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shakuntala-bharata.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva, Sambhava)
- **The guru-dakshina request** — Asked his fee, Sandipani requests the return of his young son drowned in the sea at Prabhasa _(actors: Sandipani, Krishna, Balarama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sandipani-pancajana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.45; Harivamsha; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **The horse reaches Maṇipura at the Aśvamedha** — Years later the consecrated horse of Yudhiṣṭhira's sacrifice enters Maṇipura, where the grown Babhruvāhana greets Arjuna peaceably and is rebuked as unwarriorlike _(actors: Arjuna, Babhruvāhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/chitrangada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~79)
- **The Kashi Abduction of Amba, Ambika, Ambalika** — Bhishma storms the Kashi svayamvara and carries off the three princesses by force as brides for Vicitravirya, defeating the assembled kings and Shalva _(actors: Bhishma, Amba, Ambika, Ambalika, Shalva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhishma.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~102)
- **The leap from the kadamba tree** — Krishna climbs a kadamba on the bank and leaps into the venomous pool to purge the river _(actors: Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kaliya-mardana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.16; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5; Harivaṃśa)
- **The lifting of Mount Govardhana for seven days** — Kṛṣṇa uproots Govardhana and holds it aloft on his left little finger as an umbrella for seven days, sheltering the cowherds, gopīs and cattle until Indra's storm is spent _(actors: Kṛṣṇa (Govardhana-dhārī), the cowherds and cattle of Vraja)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/govardhana-dharana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.25; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa Book 5)
- **The meeting of Hanumān and Bhīma** — Seeking the saugandhika flower, Bhīma cannot lift an old monkey's tail blocking his path; the monkey reveals himself as Hanumān, his elder half-brother, and blesses him _(actors: Bhīma, Hanumān)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/vayu.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva)
- **The night massacre of the sleeping camp** — Ashvatthaman strangles Dhrishtadyumna, slays the five sons of Draupadi, cuts Shikhandin in two, and butchers the Panchala chiefs and host as they sleep while Kripa and Kritavarman fire the camp gates _(actors: Ashvatthaman, Dhrishtadyumna, Shikhandin, Upapandavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/10-sauptika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sauptika Parva 8)
- **The Pāṇḍavas reach Prabhāsa and the western Sarasvatī-mouth fords** — Lomaśa's circuit reaches the western arc — Dvārakā, Piṇḍāraka and the radiant sea-ford of Prabhāsa where the Sarasvatī meets the ocean _(actors: Lomaśa, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mahabharata-tirtha-yatra-fords.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 88, 118–120)
- **The prophecy of Kaṃsa's doom** — From the air the goddess mocks Kaṃsa that his slayer is already born elsewhere, sealing his fate _(actors: the risen goddess, Kaṃsa)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/vindhyavasini.md) — (Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.1; Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.4)
- **The putrakāmeṣṭi and the fire-birth of Dhṛṣṭadyumna and Draupadī** — Seeking revenge on Droṇa, Drupada has Yāja and Upayāja perform a son-desiring sacrifice from which fire-born Dhṛṣṭadyumna and Draupadī arise _(actors: Drupada, Yāja, Upayāja, Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drupada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~155–169)
- **The Ranachhoda flight** — Krishna walks out of Mathura unarmed and flees Kalayavana on foot, earning the epithet Ranachhoda ('deserter of battle') as bait for a trap _(actors: Krishna, Kalayavana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/muchukunda-and-kalayavana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.51.1-6; Vishnu Purana V.23)
- **The slaying of Mura** — Kṛṣṇa beheads the five-headed daitya Mura with the Sudarśana cakra, earning the epithet Murāri; Mura's sons and Pīṭha are also slain _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Mura, Mura's seven sons, Pīṭha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/narakasura-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.59; Harivaṃśa)
- **The three-year duel and death of Citrāṅgada** — The two Citrāṅgadas duel for three years at Kurukṣetra; the gandharva slays the Kuru prince, who dies young and childless _(actors: Citrāṅgada (Kuru prince), Citrāṅgada (gandharva), Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vichitravirya-and-chitrangada.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~101; Bhāgavata Purāṇa IX.22.20; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.20)
- **The Vasus Steal Nandinī; Vasiṣṭha's Curse** — The eight Vasus steal Vasiṣṭha's wish-cow Nandinī, and the enraged sage curses them to be born as mortals, with the instigator Dyaus condemned to a long mortal life _(actors: Vasus, Nandinī, Vasiṣṭha, Dyaus)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/kamadhenu-and-divine-cattle.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva, Sambhava Parva)
- **The viśvarūpa (cosmic-form) theophany** — Gaṇeśa grants Vareṇya divine sight and reveals his all-pervading universal form containing all worlds and beings — the Gāṇapatya answer to Bhagavad Gītā 11 _(actors: Gajānana (Gaṇeśa), King Vareṇya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ganesha-gita.md) — (Gaṇeśa Purāṇa, Krīḍā-khaṇḍa ch. 144)
- **Transfer of Balarāma to Rohiṇī's Womb** — The seventh embryo Śeṣa/Balarāma is mystically transferred by Yoga-māyā from Devakī's womb to Rohiṇī at Gokula, giving Balarāma the epithet Saṃkarṣaṇa _(actors: Balarāma, Devakī, Rohiṇī, Yoga-māyā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.2)
- **Ulupi goads Babhruvahana to fight** — The Naga princess Ulupi rises from the earth and incites Babhruvahana to take up arms against his father, overriding his reluctance _(actors: Ulūpī, Babhruvāhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/babhruvahana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~80)
- **Uttaṅka beholds Kṛṣṇa's Viśvarūpa and the Uttaṅka-megha boon** — In the Āśvamedhika doublet Uttaṅka would curse Kṛṣṇa for the war, is shown the Universal Form, fails the Caṇḍāla-amṛta test, and is granted desert rain-clouds _(actors: Uttaṅka, Kṛṣṇa, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahābhārata Āśvamedhika Parva 14.52–58)
- **Vasiṣṭha ascends to Sūrya and wins Tapatī's hand** — The purohita Vasiṣṭha rises to the solar orb, asks Vivasvān for Tapatī on Saṃvaraṇa's behalf, and Sūrya consents _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Sūrya (Vivasvān), Saṃvaraṇa, Tapatī)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/samvarana-tapati.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~172)
- **Vasudeva carries Krishna across the Yamuna** — Vasudeva bears the newborn across the flooding Yamuna to Gokula, swapping him for Yashoda's just-born daughter Yogamaya _(actors: Vasudeva, Krishna, Nanda, Yashoda)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.3)
- **Vidura delivers the Vidura-nīti** — Over eight chapters Vidura, incarnation of Dharma, pours out aphoristic counsel on the wise and the fool, kingship, truth and forgiveness, urging restraint of Duryodhana _(actors: Vidura, Dhṛtarāṣṭra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vidura-niti.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva 33-40)
- **Vidura protests Draupadī's outrage at the dice-game** — At the rigged dice-game Vidura alone rises to denounce the assembly and the king as Draupadī is dragged in and disrobed, warning that adharma will consume the house _(actors: Vidura, Draupadī, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Duḥśāsana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vidura.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva)
- **Vyāsa incubates the Kauravas in jars** — Vyāsa divides the flesh-mass into 101 pieces sealed in ghee-pots, yielding the hundred Kauravas and Duḥśalā _(actors: Vyāsa, Gāndhārī, Duryodhana, Duḥśalā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/gandhari.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~107)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira chooses Nakula** — Offered one brother's life, Yudhiṣṭhira passes over Bhīma and Arjuna and asks for Nakula so that both his father's wives have a living son, prizing impartiality _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, the Yakṣa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/yaksha-prashna.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 314)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira's fall at the dice-game** — Lured into a rigged dice-game played by the cheating Śakuni, Yudhiṣṭhira loses wealth, kingdom, his brothers, himself, and finally Draupadī, triggering the disrobing _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Śakuni, Duryodhana, Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yudhishthira.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira's imperial Aśvamedha completed** — After Arjuna's year-long horse-campaign the consecrated horse returns and Yudhiṣṭhira performs the full Aśvamedha with lavish gifts, proclaiming him cakravartin _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Vyāsa, the Pāṇḍava assembly)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/dana-upakhyanas-shveta-and-the-mongoose.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~85-89)
- **Yuyutsu defects to the Pandavas** — Yuyutsu alone of the entire Kaurava host answers Yudhishthira's invitation, renounces Duryodhana's cause, and crosses to fight on the Pandava side _(actors: Yuyutsu, Yudhishthira, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yuyutsu.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhishma Parva ~43)
- **Abhimanyu slain by six warriors** — Inside the formation Abhimanyu kills thousands and slays Duryodhana's son Lakshmana, but is ganged upon by six maharathis and finally clubbed to death by Duhshasana's son, an open breach of the war-code _(actors: Abhimanyu, Drona, Karna, Duhshasana's son)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva 47-48)
- **Adrikā the fish swallows the seed and bears twins** — The apsarā Adrikā, cursed to be a fish, swallows the seed; caught by fishermen, she yields a boy and a girl and is freed from her curse _(actors: Adrikā (apsarā-fish), fishermen)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/uparichara-vasu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva (~63))
- **Śalya demoralizes Karṇa and the crow-and-swan parable** — From the driver's seat Śalya praises Arjuna, predicts Karṇa's death, and mocks him with the haṃsa-kāka fable, sapping his spirit before the fatal duel _(actors: Śalya, Karṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shalya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Karṇa Parva 27–43)
- **Ambā's tapas, Śiva's boon, and self-immolation** — Out of human remedies, Ambā performs fierce austerities; Śiva grants she will cause Bhīṣma's death and remember her past life, and she immolates herself to hasten rebirth _(actors: Ambā, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/amba-shikhandi.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva ~187–188)
- **Appointed Pāṇḍava commander-in-chief** — Chosen as senāpati of the seven-akṣauhiṇī Pāṇḍava host on the strength of his destiny to break Droṇa _(actors: Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Yudhiṣṭhira, Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dhrishtadyumna.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva)
- **Arjuna nearly kills Yudhishthira; Krishna intervenes** — When the wounded Yudhishthira reviles Arjuna and insults the Gandiva, Arjuna draws his sword to honor a vow to kill any who insults it; Krishna resolves the dilemma through casuistry without bloodshed _(actors: Arjuna, Yudhishthira, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/08-karna-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva 68-69)
- **Arjuna's departure for the divine weapons** — From Kāmyaka Arjuna journeys north to win the Pāśupata from Śiva and celestial astras from Indra and the world-guardians _(actors: Arjuna, Śiva (kirāta), Indra)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Kairāta / Indralokābhigamana Parva ~38–41)
- **Ashwatthama's night slaughter of the Pandava camp** — Born as a Rudra-portion to Drona, the immortal cursed warrior Ashwatthama slaughters the Pandava camp by night _(actors: Ashwatthama (Rudra-amsha), the Pandavas)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/shiva-avatars-and-forms.md) — (Shiva Purana; Mahabharata)
- **Balarama's Sarasvati tirtha-yatra** — Declining to fight at Kurukshetra, Balarama makes a pilgrimage up the Sarasvati from its mouth at Prabhasa to its source _(actors: Balarama)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/prabhasa-somnath-tirtha.md) — (Mahabharata Shalya Parva 34-54)
- **Balarāma routs the pursuing coalition** — The Yādava force under Balarāma turns and shatters the pursuing army of Jarāsandha's allies, covering the escape of Kṛṣṇa's chariot _(actors: Balarāma, Yādava army, Jarāsandha's coalition)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rukmini-harana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.54)
- **Bhima Slays Hidimba and Weds Hidimba** — Bhima kills the cannibal rakshasa Hidimba in a wrestling match and weds his sister Hidimba, fathering Ghatotkaca _(actors: Bhima, Hidimba, Hidimba, Ghatotkaca)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhima.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~154-155)
- **Bhima slays Kicaka** — Lured to a night tryst in the dance-hall where Bhima lies in Sairandhri's place, Kicaka is seized and crushed into a shapeless ball of flesh, avenging Draupadi's humiliation _(actors: Bhima, Kicaka, Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/04-virata-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Virata Parva 21-22)
- **Birth and boyhood of Sarvadamana (Bharata)** — Shakuntala bears a son of extraordinary power, Sarvadamana, raised in the hermitage, so strong he ties up lion and tiger cubs in play _(actors: Shakuntala, Sarvadamana/Bharata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shakuntala-bharata.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva, Sambhava)
- **Birth of Dhritarashtra, Pandu, and Vidura** — After Satyavati's sons die heirless, Vyasa fathers by niyoga the blind Dhritarashtra, the pale Pandu, and the wise Vidura (an incarnation of Dharma) on the widowed queens and a maid _(actors: Vyasa, Ambika, Ambalika, Dhritarashtra, Pandu, Vidura)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Birth of Dhritarashtra, Pandu, Vidura** — By niyoga, Vyasa fathers the blind Dhritarashtra, pale Pandu, and wise Vidura on the Kuru queens _(actors: Vyasa, Ambika, Ambalika, Dhritarashtra, Pandu, Vidura)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
