- **Birth of Kṛṣṇa in Kaṃsa's Prison** — Kṛṣṇa is born at midnight on Janmāṣṭamī in the prison cell as Devakī and Vasudeva's eighth child; the chains fall and the guards sleep _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Devakī, Vasudeva)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.3-4)
- **Birth of the twins Nakula and Sahadeva by the Aśvins** — Mādrī cleverly invokes the twin Aśvins, who beget twin sons Nakula and Sahadeva in a single act, matching two of Kuntī's god-sons with one invocation _(actors: Mādrī, the Aśvins, Nakula, Sahadeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/madri.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Blessing of Sudāmā the garland-maker** — The flower-seller Sudāmā joyfully garlands the brothers and is blessed with wealth and devotion _(actors: Krishna, Balarāma, Sudāmā the mālākāra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.41)
- **Brahma-vimohana motivated by Aghāsura** — Astonished by the Aghāsura feat, Brahmā steals the calves and boys, and Krishna multiplies himself to replace them for a year _(actors: Krishna, Brahmā, cowherd boys, calves)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vraja-boyhood-demons.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.12–14)
- **Chitrasena teaches Arjuna music in Amarāvatī** — During Arjuna's sojourn in Indra's heaven, Indra appoints the gandharva Chitrasena to teach him music, song and dance — training Arjuna later uses while disguised at Virāṭa's court _(actors: Chitrasena; Arjuna; Indra)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/gandharvas-and-kinnaras.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Indralokābhigamana Parva)
- **Citrāṅgadā's lament and the gem-revival** — After Ulūpī goads Babhruvāhana into slaying Arjuna, Citrāṅgadā grieves and reproaches Ulūpī; Arjuna is revived by the Nāgamaṇi and the Vasus' curse is disclosed, and Citrāṅgadā is later invited to Hastināpura _(actors: Citrāṅgadā, Babhruvāhana, Ulūpī, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/chitrangada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~80–82)
- **Composition of the Mahābhārata, dictated to Gaṇeśa** — Vyāsa composes the Mahābhārata; Gaṇeśa scribes on condition of unbroken dictation, Vyāsa seeds knotted kūṭa-ślokas, and Gaṇeśa breaks his tusk to write _(actors: Vyāsa, Brahmā, Gaṇeśa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva (longer recensions; disputed/interpolated))
- **Damayantī Chooses Nala at the Svayaṃvara** — Confronted with five identical Nalas, Damayantī recognizes the true mortal by his shadow, sweat and dusty feet and garlands him _(actors: Damayantī, Nala, the four gods)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 54)
- **Day 1 — Bhishma slays Shveta; Shalya slays Uttara** — On the first day Bhishma kills Shveta son of Virata with a celestial weapon and Shalya kills prince Uttara, two of Virata's sons falling in a single day _(actors: Bhishma, Shveta, Shalya, Uttara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva 45)
- **Death of Vikarṇa and Bhīma's lament** — Bhīma kills Vikarṇa among the hundred but uniquely mourns him, recalling his lone stand for Draupadī and praising him as a knower of dharma _(actors: Vikarṇa, Bhīma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vikarna.md) — (Mahābhārata, Karṇa Parva)
- **Descent to the ocean and the sea-god's report** — At Prabhasa the sea-god Varuna reports the boy was seized by a demon dwelling in the ocean _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Samudra/Varuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sandipani-pancajana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.45; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **Dhṛtarāṣṭra rejects the counsel** — The blind king concedes Vidura's wisdom is just yet cannot break his love for Duryodhana and resigns to fate, the epic's tragedy in miniature _(actors: Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vidura-niti.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva 40)
- **Discovery and imprisonment of Aniruddha** — Bāṇa discovers the lovers, Aniruddha beats back the guards with an iron club, and Bāṇa binds him with the serpent-noose (nāgapāśa) _(actors: Bāṇāsura, Aniruddha, Uṣā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/banasura-usha-aniruddha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.62; Harivaṃśa Viṣṇu Parva; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Dāmodara — the butter-theft and binding to the mortar** — Krishna breaks the butter-pot and steals butter; Yaśodā tries to bind him to a grinding-mortar but the rope always falls two finger-widths short until he consents to be bound, earning the name Dāmodara _(actors: Krishna (toddler), Yaśodā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-balya-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.9)
- **Draupadi's vow of vengeance for the gem** — Learning all her sons and kin are dead, Draupadi vows to fast to death unless Ashvatthaman is killed and the invulnerability-gem on his forehead is brought to her; Bhima races ahead in pursuit _(actors: Draupadi, Bhima, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/10-sauptika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sauptika Parva 11)
- **Draupadī's svayaṃvara and the Pāṇḍava alliance** — Drupada sets a bow-and-target test; Arjuna wins Draupadī, who becomes the common wife of the five Pāṇḍavas, sealing the Pāñcāla–Pāṇḍava alliance _(actors: Drupada, Draupadī, Arjuna, the Pāṇḍavas, Kuntī, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drupada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~176–191)
- **Durvasa's Curse Separating Rukmini** — Sage Durvasa, yoked to Rukmini's chariot by Krishna and Rukmini, curses that she will thirst and dwell apart, explaining her temple's location outside Dvaraka town _(actors: Durvasa, Rukmini, Krishna)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dwarka-bet-dwarka-saurashtra.md) — (local sthala-purana)
- **Durvāsā's kheer-test and Kṛṣṇa's mortal heel** — Durvāsā orders Kṛṣṇa to smear boiling kheer over his body; Kṛṣṇa leaves only his soles unanointed, and the sage's boon makes his body invulnerable except at the unanointed sole — the fatal point later struck by the hunter Jara's arrow _(actors: Durvāsā, Kṛṣṇa, Jara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ambarisha-durvasa.md) — (Mausala Parva tradition with later Purāṇic/folk glosses (not in core Critical Edition, where Gāndhārī's and the Pindāraka sages' curses cause the doom))
- **Duryodhana's ill-omened birth spared** — Despite evil omens and sages' warning to abandon him, Gāndhārī and Dhṛtarāṣṭra rear Duryodhana _(actors: Gāndhārī, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Duryodhana, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/gandhari.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~108)
- **Ekalavya allies with Jarāsandha against the Yādavas** — As king of the Niṣādas, Ekalavya fights alongside Jarāsandha of Magadha in his invasions of Mathurā, hurling himself against the Yādavas _(actors: Ekalavya, Jarāsandha, Hiraṇyadhanus)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ekalavya.md) — (Harivaṃśa; Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva)
- **False accusation against Kṛṣṇa** — Dwārakā suspects Kṛṣṇa of murdering Prasena for the gem after his request was refused; the slander spreads _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Satrājit, citizens of Dwārakā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13)
- **Gaṅgā Bears the Vasus; Birth of Bhīṣma** — To discharge Vasiṣṭha's curse, Gaṅgā bears the eight Vasus by King Śāntanu, drowning seven at birth to free them; the eighth, Dyaus, survives as Devavrata-Bhīṣma _(actors: Gaṅgā, Śāntanu, Vasus, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/kamadhenu-and-divine-cattle.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Ganesha scribes the Mahabharata and breaks his tusk** — Ganesha agrees to write Vyasa's Mahabharata; when his pen breaks mid-dictation he snaps off his own tusk to keep writing, becoming Ekadanta _(actors: Ganesha, Vyasa)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganesha-and-kartikeya.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva)
- **Indra's submission and the Govinda-abhiṣeka** — Humbled Indra descends, praises Kṛṣṇa with the Indra-stuti, and with the cow Surabhi anoints him, conferring the title Govinda _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Indra, Surabhi (Kāmadhenu), Airāvata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/govardhana-dharana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.26–27; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa Book 5)
- **Jayadratha Seals the Breach** — Jayadratha, using a boon from Shiva, holds the entry-point and turns back the other Pandavas, stranding Abhimanyu alone inside the formation _(actors: Jayadratha, Shiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/abhimanyu.md) — (Mahabharata, Drona Parva)
- **Kṛṣṇa lodges with Vidura during the embassy** — Arriving as peace-envoy, Kṛṣṇa declines Duryodhana's lavish feast and stays at the humble home of the righteous Vidura _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vidura.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva)
- **Kṛṣṇa restores Bhīṣma's faculties for the teaching** — Kṛṣṇa grants the dying Bhīṣma freedom from pain and clarity of mind for the duration of his instruction, and Bhīṣma sings a hymn to Kṛṣṇa-Nārāyaṇa _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/12-shanti-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 12.47, 52-54)
- **Kṛṣṇa reveals the Viśvarūpa to Arjuna** — Kṛṣṇa reveals his cosmic all-form (Viśvarūpa) to Arjuna on the battlefield, an infinite theophany of all worlds and time devouring the armies _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/vishnu-forms-and-vyuhas.md) — (Bhagavad Gītā 11; Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva; Bhāgavata 10.7)
- **Karna found and raised by the suta couple** — The childless charioteer Adhiratha and his wife Radha recover the basket at Campa and raise Karna as their own, branding him Sutaputra _(actors: Adhiratha, Radha, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva ~308; Adi Parva)
- **King Pāṇḍu's Penance and the Birth of the Pandavas at Pandukeshwar** — King Pandu does penance at Yogadhyan Badri to be cleansed of killing two mating deer; the Pandavas are born there and Pandu attains salvation _(actors: Pandu, Pandavas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/pancha-kedar-pancha-badri.md) — (Mahabharata tradition)
- **Kāliya's coiling and Krishna's release** — Kāliya binds Krishna in his coils; the cowherds and parents swoon, Balarāma reassures them, and Krishna expands his body to break free _(actors: Krishna, Kāliya, Balarāma, Nanda, Yaśodā, gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kaliya-mardana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.16)
- **Kripa appointed first preceptor of the Kuru princes** — Bhishma appoints Kripa as the first formal weapons-master of the Kaurava and Pandava boys, before Drona's arrival _(actors: Bhishma, Kripa, the Kuru and Pandava princes)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kripa.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~130-131)
- **Krishna earns the name Keśava** — The slaying of Keśin becomes the narrative etiology of the epithet Keśava, 'slayer of Keśin', alongside the older lexical 'fine-haired' gloss _(actors: Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/keshi-arishta-keshava.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.37; Viṣṇu-Sahasranāma commentary (Śaṅkara))
- **Krishna lifts Mount Govardhana** — Krishna persuades the cowherds to worship Mount Govardhana instead of Indra, and when Indra sends rains he lifts the mountain as an umbrella, after which Indra consecrates him as Govinda _(actors: Krishna, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/harivamsha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva)
- **Krishna Restores Sandipani's Dead Son as Guru-Dakshina** — As guru-dakshina, Krishna and Balarama slay the demon Pancajana (taking the Pancajanya conch) and descend to Yama's realm to restore Sandipani's son lost in the sea at Prabhasa _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Sandipani, Pancajana)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/ujjain-avantika.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X)
- **Kuntī and Yudhiṣṭhira sanction the marriage** — Hiḍimbī presses her suit; Kuntī and Yudhiṣṭhira assent on conditions, treating duty to a benefactor-suppliant as dharma _(actors: Kuntī, Yudhiṣṭhira, Hiḍimbī, Bhīma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/hidimba-hidimbi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva (~155))
- **Kunti reveals Karna's birth to the Pandavas** — Kunti discloses that Karna, whom they killed, was their eldest brother born to her by Surya before her marriage, overwhelming the Pandavas with fresh grief and remorse _(actors: Kunti, Yudhishthira, Pandavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/11-stri-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Stri Parva)
- **Kuntī's svayaṃvara and marriage to Pāṇḍu** — At her self-choice ceremony Kuntī garlands the Kuru king Pāṇḍu, becoming his senior queen (Mādrī being the second) _(actors: Kuntī, Pāṇḍu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Kurukṣetra as the field of the Bhārata war** — The consecrated ground becomes the battlefield of the eighteen-day war, named dharma-kṣetre kurukṣetre in the Bhagavad Gītā _(actors: Kauravas, Pāṇḍavas, the fallen warriors)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/kuru-the-eponym.md) — (Mahābhārata Bhīṣma Parva 23 / Bhagavad Gītā 1.1)
- **Lakshmi incarnates as Rukmini with Krishna** — When Vishnu descends as Krishna, Lakshmi incarnates as his chief queen Rukmini (and as Radha in devotional tradition) _(actors: Lakshmi (Rukmini), Krishna)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/lakshmi.md) — (Vishnu Purana 1.9)
- **Luring Kalayavana into the cave** — Krishna leads the pursuing Kalayavana into a mountain cave where the ancient king Muchukunda sleeps, then slips aside _(actors: Krishna, Kalayavana, Muchukunda)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/muchukunda-and-kalayavana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.51.10-13)
- **Marriage and arrival at Indraprastha** — With dowry from the Vṛṣṇis, Arjuna brings Subhadrā to Indraprastha, where she wins over an initially displeased Draupadī _(actors: Arjuna, Subhadrā, Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subhadra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Marriage of Revati and Balarama** — On Brahma's counsel Revati is married to Balarama, who taps her with the tip of his plough to reduce her from her earlier-yuga height to the stature of his own age _(actors: Revati, Balarama, Kakudmi)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-05-raivata.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 9.3; Vishnu Purana 4.1)
- **Marriage of Saṃvaraṇa and Tapatī** — Vasiṣṭha conveys Tapatī down; the two are wed on the mountain, and Saṃvaraṇa dwells there twelve years _(actors: Saṃvaraṇa, Tapatī, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/samvarana-tapati.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~172–173)
- **Maya spared at the Khāṇḍava fire** — Fleeing Agni's conflagration of the Khāṇḍava forest, Maya takes refuge with Arjuna, who with Kṛṣṇa spares his life _(actors: Maya, Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa, Agni)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/maya-danava.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva, Khāṇḍava-daha)
- **Mudgala questions the messenger about heaven's nature** — Rather than accept blindly, Mudgala interrogates the envoy about svarga's joys and defects, learning that merit is exhausted and the soul falls back into rebirth _(actors: Mudgala, Devadūta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva 247)
- **Nala loses his kingdom at dice and is exiled** — Possessed Nala gambles away his entire kingdom to his brother Puṣkara and is stripped and exiled into the wilderness _(actors: Nala, Puṣkara, Damayantī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 57–58)
- **Nara and Nārāyaṇa reborn as Arjuna and Kṛṣṇa** — The twin sages take their final earthly descent as Arjuna (Nara) and Kṛṣṇa (Nārāyaṇa), their cross-yuga friendship explaining the inseparable Kurukṣetra bond _(actors: Nara/Arjuna, Nārāyaṇa/Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nara-narayana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śānti Parva / Nārāyaṇīya; Bhagavad Gītā)
- **Śāntanu's accession at Hastināpura** — Śāntanu is consecrated king of Hastināpura, ruling justly as a famed hunter-sovereign _(actors: Śāntanu)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~97–98)
- **One of the four Kaurava survivors of Day 18** — After the eighteenth day only Duryodhana (dying), Aśvatthāman, Kṛpa and Kṛtavarma survive of the Kaurava host _(actors: Kṛtavarma, Duryodhana, Aśvatthāman, Kṛpa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kritavarma.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śalya Parva (Hrada-praveśa))
- **Pandu's curse and birth of the Pandavas** — Cursed by sage Kindama, Pandu cannot father children; Kunti and Madri invoke gods to bear Yudhishthira, Bhima, Arjuna, Nakula and Sahadeva _(actors: Pandu, Kunti, Madri, Dharma/Vayu/Indra/Ashvins)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Pāṇḍavas Worship Śiva at Sthāneśvar before the War** — The Pāṇḍavas worship Śiva at Sthāneśvar Mahādev in Thanesar to secure victory before the Kurukṣetra war _(actors: Pāṇḍavas, Śiva)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kurukshetra-dharmakshetra.md) — (local sthala-purana)
- **Plot to seize Krishna** — Duhshasana joins the cabal of Duryodhana, Shakuni, and Karna to arrest Krishna during the peace embassy; Krishna foils it with his cosmic form _(actors: Duḥśāsana, Duryodhana, Śakuni, Karṇa, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dushasana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva ~129–130)
- **Prime mover of the niyoga** — After Bhīṣma twice refuses to take the throne or beget heirs, Satyavatī reveals her secret son Vyāsa, summons him by thought, and in haste arranges the niyoga that produces blind Dhṛtarāṣṭra, pale Pāṇḍu, and the maid's son Vidura _(actors: Satyavatī, Bhīṣma, Vyāsa, Ambikā, Ambālikā, the maid, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Pāṇḍu, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/satyavati.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~103–106)
- **Pulastya Names Pushkar First of Tīrthas to Bhīṣma** — In the Tirtha-yatra Parva sage Pulastya instructs Bhishma on holy fords, opening with Pushkar where Brahma and the gods are ever present, the textual root of its Adi Tirtha rank _(actors: Pulastya, Bhishma)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/pushkar-brahma-tirtha.md) — (Mahabharata (Vana Parva, Tirtha-yatra Parva))
- **Release of the sacrificial horse and Arjuna's campaign begins** — The consecrated stallion is set loose to roam for a year; Arjuna is appointed its protector, instructed to conquer without needless slaughter, and follows it with an army _(actors: Arjuna, Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.71-73)
- **Reminiscence of the gurukula storm** — The two friends recall their student days, especially the night they were caught in a forest storm gathering firewood for the guru _(actors: Krishna, Sudama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.80.28-34)
- **Rivers, clouds, trees and hill respond** — Rivers halt to embrace Kṛṣṇa's feet, a cloud canopies him, the trees thrill with horripilation, and Govardhana is blessed by his touch _(actors: rivers, cloud, trees, Govardhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/venu-gita.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.21.15–19)
- **Sañjaya reports the war-books and carries each great death's news** — Through the Bhīṣma, Droṇa, Karṇa and Śalya Parvas the battle is delivered as Sañjaya's running report to the king, including the news of the falls of Bhīṣma, Abhimanyu, Droṇa, Karṇa and finally Duryodhana _(actors: Sañjaya, Dhṛtarāṣṭra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sanjaya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Bhīṣma–Śalya Parvas (Books 6–9))
- **Satyavatī summons Vyāsa for niyoga** — Satyavatī calls her pre-marital son Vyāsa to father heirs on the widows by niyoga, warning the queens to bear his fearsome appearance _(actors: Satyavatī, Vyāsa, Ambikā, Ambālikā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ambika-ambalika.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~104–105)
- **Sealing the cakravyūha mouth** — Using Śiva's boon, Jayadratha holds the formation's entrance shut, stranding Abhimanyu alone inside to be killed _(actors: Jayadratha, Abhimanyu, Bhīma, Yudhiṣṭhira, Nakula, Sahadeva, Droṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jayadratha.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva 41)
- **Sparing Maya the Architect** — Arjuna and Krishna spare the asura-architect Maya, who in gratitude builds the Pandavas' illusion-hall at Indraprastha _(actors: Arjuna, Krishna, Maya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi/Sabha Parva)
- **The butcher's past-life curse revealed** — The butcher reveals he was a learned Brāhmaṇa in a former life, brought to the butcher's caste by a curse, now re-ascending through faultless duty and filial service _(actors: Dharma-vyādha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyadha-gita.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 205-206)
- **The celestial chariot descends for Mudgala** — A devadūta arrives with a divine swan-drawn chariot to carry Mudgala bodily to svarga as reward for his merit _(actors: Devadūta (celestial messenger), Mudgala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Mārkaṇḍeya-samāsya (Mudgala-Upākhyāna))
- **The death of Naraka** — Kṛṣṇa severs Naraka's head with the cakra; Bhūmi returns the stolen treasures and Bhagadatta is installed; a variant has Satyabhāmā as the slayer _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Narakāsura, Satyabhāmā, Bhūmi/Pṛthivī, Bhagadatta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/narakasura-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.59; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **The dāna of the head** — Recognising the danger of the vow, Kṛṣṇa begs Barbarīka's head as alms; bound by dāna-dharma Barbarīka offers it, asking to witness the war before he dies _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Barbarīka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/barbarika-khatu-shyam.md) — (Folk Mahābhārata; Khāṭū Śyām sthala-tradition)
- **The exile-stake and second dice-game** — After Dhṛtarāṣṭra's boons free the Pāṇḍavas, a single decisive throw lost commits them to twelve years' forest exile and a thirteenth incognito year _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Śakuni, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yudhishthira.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva)
- **The goddess departs to dwell on the Vindhyas** — Having prophesied, the goddess declares she will take her abode on the Vindhya mountains and departs—the origin of Vindhyavāsinī _(actors: the goddess (Vindhyavāsinī))_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/vindhyavasini.md) — (Harivaṃśa Āryā-stava; Devī-Bhāgavata)
- **The gopīs reproach the absent Kṛṣṇa** — Returning to Uddhava, the gopīs reproach Kṛṣṇa for abandoning them and ask only whether he remembers Vraja; Uddhava again assures them of his presence and return _(actors: the gopīs, Uddhava)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhramara-gita-uddhava-sandesha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.47.22–48)
- **The half-golden mongoose denounces the sacrifice** — A mongoose with one golden side and a human voice declares the entire Aśvamedha worth less than a poor brāhmaṇa's single prastha of barley-flour _(actors: The golden-headed mongoose (nakula), Yudhiṣṭhira's assembly)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/dana-upakhyanas-shveta-and-the-mongoose.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva 90)
- **The kaḷappali — Aravān's self-sacrifice to Kālī** — In the Tamil tradition Aravān, one of three flawless warriors bearing the thirty-two perfections, freely offers his own head to Kālī before the war to secure Pāṇḍava victory _(actors: Aravān, Kālī, Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/aravan-iravan.md) — (Tamil Mahābhārata (Villiputtūrār); Kūttāṇṭavar temple tradition)
- **The Lokapalas arm Arjuna** — The world-guardians appear and bestow the danda, pasha, and antardhana weapons, and Indra invites Arjuna to heaven _(actors: Arjuna, Yama, Varuna, Kubera, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 41-42)
- **The mace-duel and Duryodhana's broken thighs** — In the gada-yuddha Bhima cannot beat the more skilled Duryodhana fairly; on Krishna's thigh-slapping cue he strikes a forbidden blow below the navel, shattering Duryodhana's thighs to fulfill his dice-hall vow _(actors: Bhima, Duryodhana, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/09-shalya-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Shalya Parva (Gada-yuddha))
- **The mothers' and cows' affection deepens** — Re-entering Vraja as all the boys and calves, Krishna is fondled by the mothers and cows whose love grows even more intense _(actors: Krishna, mothers of Vraja, cows)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/brahma-vimohana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.13.23-35)
- **The Pandavas Pursue Shiva as a Bull (Pancha Kedar)** — Seeking absolution after Kurukshetra, the Pandavas pursue Shiva, who flees as a bull and dives into the earth, re-emerging in five parts to form the Pancha Kedar shrines _(actors: Pandavas, Shiva)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/temple-networks-and-circuits.md) — (Panch Kedar sthala-purana)
- **The Pāṇḍavas ascend to Badarī and the Nara-Nārāyaṇa-Gandhamādana heights** — The northern leg of the circuit brings the Pāṇḍavas to the Himalayan hermitage of Nara-Nārāyaṇa at Badarī and the Kailāsa/Gandhamādana fords _(actors: Lomaśa, Bhīma, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mahabharata-tirtha-yatra-fords.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 145–153)
- **The phala-śruti** — The closing 'fruit of hearing' promises freedom from sin, sorrow, and fear and the attainment of eternal Brahman for the devotee of Vāsudeva _(actors: Bhīṣma, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/vishnu-sahasranama.md) — (Viṣṇu Sahasranāma phala-śruti / uttara-bhāga)
- **The Rajasuya sacrifice performed** — Yudhishthira is consecrated samrat in the grand Rajasuya, the high-water mark of Pandava fortune _(actors: Yudhishthira, Vyasa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva 32-34)
- **The rigged dice-game and Yudhiṣṭhira's total loss** — Playing as Duryodhana's proxy with loaded dice, Śakuni wins Yudhiṣṭhira's wealth, kingdom, brothers, self, and finally Draupadī _(actors: Śakuni, Yudhiṣṭhira, Duryodhana, Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shakuni.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva ~58–65)
- **The search and Kṛṣṇa-anukaraṇa** — Maddened by separation the gopīs wander Vṛndāvana questioning nature and imitating Kṛṣṇa's deeds in total identification with him _(actors: The gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rasa-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.30)
- **The Yadava clan self-destructs in the brawl at Prabhasa** — At a drunken festival Satyaki beheads Kritavarma after mutual taunts; the mob kills Satyaki and Pradyumna, and every Yadava who grabs the eraka reeds finds it transformed into an iron club, so brother slays brother until almost the entire Vrishni-Andhaka race lies dead, fulfilling the curse _(actors: Satyaki, Kritavarma, Pradyumna, Krishna, the Yadavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/16-mausala-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 16.3)
- **The Yakṣa revealed as Dharma; brothers revived** — Pleased by the son's equitable virtue, the Yakṣa reveals himself as Dharma, Yudhiṣṭhira's divine father, restores all four brothers and returns the firesticks _(actors: Dharma (Yama), Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/yaksha-prashna.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 314)
- **Ulūpī goads Babhruvāhana against Arjuna** — During Yudhiṣṭhira's Aśvamedha the horse reaches Maṇipura; Ulūpī rises from the earth and incites the reluctant Babhruvāhana to fight his father Arjuna _(actors: Ulūpī, Babhruvāhana, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ulupi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~80)
- **Ulūpī Weds Arjuna and Bears Irāvān** — The nāga princess Ulūpī of the Airāvata line weds Arjuna in Nāgaloka and bears Irāvān, later reviving Arjuna after his death by Babhruvāhana _(actors: Ulūpī, Arjuna, Irāvān, Babhruvāhana)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/nagas-and-serpents.md) — (Mahābhārata)
- **Uttanka descends into Nagaloka and burns it with the Agni-horse** — Indra's vajra tunnels Uttanka into the serpent-world where, after the cosmic loom-vision, he blows into the fire-horse Agni, flooding Nagaloka with flame until the serpents return the earrings _(actors: Uttanka, Indra, Agni, Takshaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahabharata Paushya Parva)
- **Varuṇa grants the Gāṇḍīva, quivers, chariot, and Kṛṣṇa's weapons** — Via Agni and Varuṇa, Arjuna receives the Gāṇḍīva bow, inexhaustible quivers and the kapi-dhvaja chariot; Kṛṣṇa receives the Sudarśana-cakra and Kaumodakī mace _(actors: Varuṇa, Agni, Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/khandava-vana-and-the-burning.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi-parva 217)
- **Vicitravīrya consecrated as boy-king** — With Citrāṅgada dead and heirless, the minor Vicitravīrya is consecrated king with Bhīṣma governing as regent _(actors: Vicitravīrya, Bhīṣma, Satyavatī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vichitravirya-and-chitrangada.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~102; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.20)
- **Vidura-niti delivered** — During Dhritarashtra's sleepless night Vidura delivers a long aphoristic treatise on statecraft and ethics, urging the king to restrain Duryodhana and return the Pandavas' share _(actors: Vidura, Dhritarashtra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva 33-40 (Prajagara))
- **Virata's dice-blow and the reveal** — Mistakenly crediting his son, Virata strikes Yudhishthira's face with a die; the disguises then fall and Virata becomes the Pandavas' first great royal ally _(actors: Virata, Yudhishthira, Uttara, Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/virata-and-his-children.md) — (Mahabharata, Virata Parva 63-64)
- **Vyasa Fathers the Kuru Heirs by Niyoga** — When Vicitravirya dies childless and Bhishma refuses the widows, Satyavati summons Vyasa, who by niyoga fathers Dhritarashtra, Pandu, and Vidura _(actors: Vyasa, Satyavati, Dhritarashtra, Pandu, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhishma.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~104-106)
- **Vyāsa divides the Veda into four** — At the Dvāpara–Kali junction Kṛṣṇa-Dvaipāyana Vyāsa splits the single Veda into four for the diminished memory of Kali-Yuga humans _(actors: Vyāsa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/shruti-vedas-and-upanishads.md) — (Viṣṇu Purāṇa 3.3–4; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 1.4; Mahābhārata)
- **Vyāsa Teaches Śuka, Who Merges into Brahman** — Vyāsa teaches his son Śuka the path to Brahman; perfected, Śuka ascends and merges into the all-pervading Brahman _(actors: Vyāsa, Śuka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mokshadharma-dialogues.md) — (Mahabharata Shanti Parva 224-250)
- **Yogamaya escapes Kamsa as the goddess** — Kamsa dashes the substituted girl on a rock, but she rises as the eight-armed goddess, declares his slayer is born, and vanishes _(actors: Kamsa, Yogamaya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.4)
- **Yuyutsu fights the eighteen-day war on the Pandava side** — Yuyutsu serves through the Kurukshetra war under Yudhishthira as the hundred sons of Gandhari are slain one by one _(actors: Yuyutsu, Yudhishthira, the Pandavas, the Kauravas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yuyutsu.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhishma-Shalya-Sauptika Parvas)
- **Abduction of the Kashi princesses** — Bhishma seizes the three Kashi princesses for Vicitravirya; Amba, in love with Shalva, is released and vows vengeance, reincarnating as Shikhandin _(actors: Bhishma, Amba, Ambika, Ambalika, Shalva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 102-103)
- **Abhimanyu Slays Lakshmana** — Trapped within the array, Abhimanyu wreaks havoc and slays Lakshmana, Duryodhana's son, enraging the king _(actors: Abhimanyu, Lakshmana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/abhimanyu.md) — (Mahabharata, Drona Parva)
- **Śalya appointed fourth and final Kaurava commander** — After Karṇa's death the Kauravas make Śalya — who broke Karṇa's morale — the fourth generalissimo on Day 18 _(actors: Śalya, Duryodhana, the Kaurava host)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shalya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Karṇa Parva close / Śalya Parva (Śalya-vadha))
- **Ambikā shuts her eyes; birth of Dhṛtarāṣṭra** — Terrified, Ambikā keeps her eyes shut during the niyoga, and Vyāsa decrees her son will be born blind — Dhṛtarāṣṭra _(actors: Ambikā, Vyāsa, Dhṛtarāṣṭra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ambika-ambalika.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~105–106)
- **Aravān's three last wishes and the Mohinī marriage** — Granted last wishes, Aravān asks not to die unmarried; Kṛṣṇa takes the female form Mohinī to wed him for one night _(actors: Aravān, Kṛṣṇa (as Mohinī))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/aravan-iravan.md) — (Tamil Mahābhārata; Kūttāṇṭavar temple tradition)
- **Arjuna ascends to Indra's heaven** — Matali carries Arjuna to Amaravati, where he learns the vajra and celestial weapons and is taught song and dance by Citrasena _(actors: Arjuna, Mátali, Indra, Citrasena)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 42-46)
- **Arjuna's sunset vow (Pratijñā)** — Arjuna swears to slay Jayadratha before sunset the next day or enter the fire, for barring the rescue of Abhimanyu _(actors: Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa, Jayadratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jayadratha.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva 72–84)
- **Arjuna subdues the Trigartas** — The surviving grandsons of the Trigarta kings attack to avenge their war-dead elders; Arjuna subdues them after a hard fight _(actors: Arjuna, Trigarta warriors)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.73)
- **Arjuna's vow to slay Jayadratha** — Learning of his son's death, Arjuna swears to slay Jayadratha by next sunset or enter the fire himself; Drona walls Jayadratha off with three nested formations _(actors: Arjuna, Krishna, Jayadratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva 72-84 (Pratijna))
- **Babhruvāhana kills Arjuna** — In the duel Babhruvāhana's arrows strike Arjuna dead; Citrāṅgadā laments and reproaches Ulūpī _(actors: Babhruvāhana, Arjuna, Citrāṅgadā, Ulūpī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/babhruvahana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~80–81)
- **Babhruvāhana slays Arjuna** — In the duel before Maṇipura, Babhruvāhana's arrows strike Arjuna dead, while Citrāṅgadā reproaches Ulūpī for engineering the parricide _(actors: Babhruvāhana, Arjuna, Citrāṅgadā, Ulūpī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ulupi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~81)
- **Balarama perceives the secret** — Puzzled by the increased affection, Balarama perceives that all the boys and calves are Krishna's self-manifestations and Krishna reveals the truth to him _(actors: Balarama, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/brahma-vimohana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.13.37-41)
- **Balarama's fury and Krishna's justification** — Enraged at the illegal thigh-blow, Balarama raises his plough-weapon against Bhima and must be restrained by Krishna, who justifies the blow as retribution for Duryodhana's own adharma _(actors: Balarama, Krishna, Bhima)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/09-shalya-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Shalya Parva (Gada-yuddha))
- **Balarama's yogic departure as Shesha-Ananta** — Krishna finds Balarama seated in yoga giving up his body; a great white serpent issues from his mouth and glides into the sea received by Ocean and the Nagas, Balarama returning to his nature as the cosmic serpent Shesha/Ananta _(actors: Balarama, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/16-mausala-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 16.4)
- **Balarāma's Sārasvata tīrtha-yātrā ending at Samanta-pañcaka** — To avoid taking sides in the Kurukṣetra war, Balarāma undertakes an upstream pilgrimage along the Sarasvatī, touching Prabhāsa, Vinaśana, Saptasārasvata, Kapālamocana, Pṛthūdaka and the other fords in sequence, arriving back at Samanta-pañcaka just as the war closes; his route fixes the literary spine of Kurukṣetra's inner tīrtha-ring _(actors: Balarāma (Baladeva))_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kurukshetra-dharmakshetra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śalya-parva 34–54 (Sārasvata-tīrtha-yātrā))
- **Bhṛgu Teaches Bharadvāja on Cosmogony and Varṇa** — Bhṛgu teaches the Mind-born primeval Being and a color-coded varṇa, but Bharadvāja's objection resolves that caste is by conduct, not birth _(actors: Bhṛgu, Bharadvāja)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mokshadharma-dialogues.md) — (Mahabharata Shanti Parva 182-192)
- **Bhima kills Duhshasana and drinks his blood** — Bhima seizes Duhshasana, tears open his chest and drinks his blood, fulfilling his dice-hall vows; Draupadi at last binds up the hair she had left loose since her disrobing _(actors: Bhima, Duhshasana, Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/08-karna-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva 61/83)
- **Bhima Slays Bakasura of Ekachakra** — Bhima eats the food tribute meant for the man-eating rakshasa Baka and breaks his back across his knee, freeing the town _(actors: Bhima, Bakasura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhima.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~159-160)
- **Bhima slays the 105 Upakicakas** — When Kicaka's kinsmen seize Sairandhri to burn her on his pyre, Bhima uproots a tree, charges in, and kills all 105 Upakicakas, freeing her _(actors: Bhima, Upakicakas, Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/04-virata-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Virata Parva 23)
- **Bhīṣma accepts the role of teacher on the arrow-bed** — Empowered by Kṛṣṇa, the dying Bhīṣma agrees to instruct Yudhiṣṭhira in rāja-, āpad-, and mokṣa-dharma until the sun turns north _(actors: Bhīṣma, Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/12-shanti-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 12.54-55)
- **Birth of Abhimanyu** — Subhadrā bears Abhimanyu at Indraprastha, the prodigy who will die breaking the cakravyūha _(actors: Subhadrā, Arjuna, Abhimanyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subhadra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva (Sambhava/Subhadrā-haraṇa Parvas))
- **Birth of Matsya, founder of the Matsya line** — The male twin is kept by Vasu and becomes the righteous king Matsya, ancestor of the Cedi/Matsya royal stock _(actors: Vasu Uparicara, Matsya)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/uparichara-vasu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva (~63))
- **Birth of the Pandavas** — Cursed Pandu's heirs are born via Kunti's mantra summoning gods — Yudhishthira (Dharma), Bhima (Vayu), Arjuna (Indra), and via Madri the twins Nakula and Sahadeva (Ashvins); Kunti's earlier secret son by Surya is Karna _(actors: Pandu, Kunti, Madri, Yudhishthira, Bhima, Arjuna, Nakula, Sahadeva, Karna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Breaking the twin arjuna trees — liberation of Nalakūbara and Maṇigrīva** — Bound to the mortar, Krishna crawls between two arjuna trees and uproots them, freeing the Yakṣa sons of Kubera, Nalakūbara and Maṇigrīva, whom Nārada had cursed to be trees _(actors: Krishna (toddler), Nalakūbara, Maṇigrīva, Nārada, Kubera)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-balya-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.10–11; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Bāṇāsura Defeated by Kṛṣṇa** — The thousand-armed Śiva-devotee Bāṇāsura, whose daughter Uṣā loved Aniruddha, is defeated by Kṛṣṇa, his arms shorn to four, and spared at Śiva's plea _(actors: Bāṇāsura, Kṛṣṇa, Uṣā, Aniruddha, Śiva)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/asura-wars-and-roster.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.62-63)
- **Building and Rule of Dvārakā by Kṛṣṇa** — Krishna builds and rules the golden many-gated sea-fortress city of Dwarka after leading the Yadavas out of Mathura _(actors: Krishna)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sapta-puri-and-sacred-cities.md) — (Garuda Purana; Bhagavata Purana; Vishnu Purana)
- **Challenge to Karna at the graduation tournament** — Kripa challenges Karna to declare his royal lineage before combat with Arjuna, exposing his unknown birth _(actors: Kripa, Karna, Arjuna, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kripa.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~136)
- **Chitrasena captures Duryodhana at the Ghoṣa-yātrā** — At the Ghoṣa-yātrā the gandharva Chitrasena defeats and captures Duryodhana and his retinue; in a dharma-inversion the exiled Pāṇḍavas rescue their persecutor, Arjuna invoking his friendship with Chitrasena _(actors: Chitrasena; Duryodhana; Yudhiṣṭhira; Arjuna)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/gandharvas-and-kinnaras.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Ghoṣa-yātrā Parva)
- **Compilation of the eighteen Purāṇas and the Bhāgavata** — Vyāsa compiles the eighteen Mahā-Purāṇas; reproached by Nārada for not glorifying the Lord, he composes the Bhāgavata and teaches it to Śuka _(actors: Vyāsa, Nārada, Śuka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 1.4–7; Viṣṇu Purāṇa)
- **Counsel before the war** — Gāndhārī protests Draupadī's outrage and refuses Duryodhana victory, saying where there is dharma there is victory _(actors: Gāndhārī, Duryodhana, Draupadī, Dhṛtarāṣṭra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/gandhari.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā/Udyoga Parvas)
- **Day 3 — Krishna's first wheel-charge against Bhishma** — Seeing Arjuna fight half-heartedly while Bhishma devastates the army, Krishna breaks his vow, seizes a chariot-wheel and rushes at Bhishma; Arjuna restrains him and swears to fight in earnest _(actors: Krishna, Bhishma, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva 55-59)
- **Dharma's boon of non-recognition** — Dharma grants that during the coming thirteenth year of incognito living the Pāṇḍavas pass unrecognized, plus inner conquest over the six foes, easing the year in Virāṭa's kingdom _(actors: Dharma, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/yaksha-prashna.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 315)
- **Dhenukāsura the donkey-demon of the Tālavana slain** — Balarāma shakes down palm fruit, is kicked by the donkey-demon, and kills it and its herd by flinging them into the palms, opening the Tālavana _(actors: Balarāma, Krishna, Dhenukāsura, cowherd boys)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vraja-boyhood-demons.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.15; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Draupadī's disrobing triggered by the dice-loss** — Śakuni's victory leads directly to Draupadī being dragged and stripped in the assembly, seeding the war-vows including Sahadeva's vow to kill Śakuni _(actors: Śakuni, Draupadī, Duryodhana, Duḥśāsana, Sahadeva, Bhīma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shakuni.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva ~60–65)
- **Dushyanta's denial and the celestial voice naming Bharata** — At court Dushyanta publicly disowns Shakuntala, who rebukes him with a fierce dharma speech; a sky-voice declares the child his and decrees the name Bharata, after which the king reveals his denial was a deliberate strategy _(actors: Dushyanta, Shakuntala, Bharata, the akashavani)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shakuntala-bharata.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva, Sambhava)
- **Forest retirement and death** — Having secured the next generation, Satyavatī withdraws to the forest in vānaprastha with Ambikā and Ambālikā—warned by Vyāsa not to witness the coming Kuru doom—and there ends her life _(actors: Satyavatī, Ambikā, Ambālikā, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/satyavati.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~128)
- **Freeing and marriage of the 16,100** — Kṛṣṇa frees the 16,100 captive women and weds them so they live with honour, totalling 16,108 with the eight principal queens _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, the 16,100 women, the aṣṭa-bhāryā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/narakasura-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.59)
- **Gaṅgā marriage and the secret-vow condition** — Śāntanu weds Gaṅgā on the condition that he never question or restrain her _(actors: Śāntanu, Gaṅgā)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~98)
- **Grief of Chitrangada and Babhruvahana** — Babhruvahana resolves to die beside his slain father while Chitrangada laments and reproaches Ulupi for engineering the parricide _(actors: Babhruvāhana, Citrāṅgadā, Ulūpī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/babhruvahana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~81)
- **Śikhaṇḍin, the instrument of Bhīṣma's fall** — Drupada raises Śikhaṇḍin (rebirth of Ambā) as a son; the yakṣa sex-exchange makes the loophole by which Bhīṣma is felled _(actors: Drupada, Śikhaṇḍin, Ambā, Sthūṇākarṇa, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drupada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva ~188–193)
- **Incineration of Kalayavana by Muchukunda's glance** — Kalayavana kicks the sleeper, mistaking him for Krishna; the wakened Muchukunda's first glance burns Kalayavana to ashes by an old boon _(actors: Kalayavana, Muchukunda)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/muchukunda-and-kalayavana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.51.13-14; Vishnu Purana V.23; Harivamsha)
- **Kṛṣṇa eats the pinch of rice; Rukmiṇī stays his hand** — Kṛṣṇa snatches the hidden bundle and eats a fistful of flattened rice with relish; Rukmiṇī (Lakṣmī) catches his hand as he reaches for a second, the love-gift sufficing to grant Sudāmā all fortune _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Sudāmā, Rukmiṇī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.80.30–34)
- **Karna becomes Parashurama's disguised pupil** — Rejected by Drona, Karna goes to Parashurama claiming to be a brahmana to learn brahmastra-grade weapons _(actors: Karna, Parashurama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Santi/Vana back-narratives)
- **Kṛṣṇa slays Ekalavya** — Kṛṣṇa kills Ekalavya in battle, commonly by crushing him with a hurled boulder; in the Harivaṃśa's kinship framing this is the slaying of a lost Yādava cousin _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Ekalavya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ekalavya.md) — (Harivaṃśa; Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva)
- **Kṛṣṇa tracks the gem to the cave** — To clear his name Kṛṣṇa traces Prasena's route, finds the bodies and the dead lion, and follows bear-tracks to a cave _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, citizens)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56)
- **Kauśika sent home to serve his parents** — The butcher tells Kauśika he neglected his aged parents for tapas; the twice-humbled ascetic returns home, tends them, and is reconciled to true dharma _(actors: Kauśika, Dharma-vyādha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyadha-gita.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva ~206)
- **Krishna abducts Rukmini** — Krishna elopes with Rukmini on the eve of her wedding to Shishupala, publicly humiliating him and seeding his enmity _(actors: Krishna, Rukmini, Shishupala, Rukmi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shishupala.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.52-54)
- **Krishna eats the pinch of flattened rice** — Krishna snatches the hidden bundle, declares that even a leaf or fruit offered with love satisfies Him, and eats a fistful of rice; Rukmini stays His hand at the second _(actors: Krishna, Sudama, Rukmini/Lakshmi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.80.30-34)
- **Krishna slays Kaṃsa at Mathurā** — Summoned to Mathurā's bow-festival, Krishna and Balarāma kill the elephant Kuvalayāpīḍa and the wrestlers Cāṇūra and Muṣṭika, then Krishna drags Kaṃsa from his throne and kills him, reinstating Ugrasena _(actors: Krishna, Balarāma, Kaṃsa, Ugrasena)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/harivamsha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva)
- **Kuntī refuses a further mantra-use** — When Pāṇḍu asks for one more invocation so Mādrī might have more sons, Kuntī refuses, unwilling to be outdone, fixing the Pāṇḍava count at five _(actors: Kuntī, Pāṇḍu, Mādrī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/madri.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Loss of divine sight; capture and release by Sātyaki** — At Duryodhana's fall Vyāsa's boon lapses and Sañjaya loses his divine sight; seized by Sātyaki and about to be killed, he is freed at Vyāsa's intervention and returns to the king _(actors: Sañjaya, Duryodhana, Sātyaki, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sanjaya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śalya Parva (~28))
- **Marriage of Abhimanyu and Uttaraa** — Arjuna declines Uttaraa for himself (his dance-pupil) and weds her to his son Abhimanyu at Upaplavya, sealing the Matsya-Pandava-Yadava alliance _(actors: Virata, Arjuna, Uttaraa, Abhimanyu, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/virata-and-his-children.md) — (Mahabharata, Virata Parva 67)
- **Marriage of Bhīma and Hiḍimbī** — Bhīma weds Hiḍimbī on terms: she has him by day but he returns nightly to the Pāṇḍavas, and the union lasts only until a son is born — the first non-human marriage of the exile _(actors: Bhīma, Hiḍimbī, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/hidimba-hidimbi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva (~155))
- **Mass cremation and water-offering for the dead** — At Yudhishthira's command Vidura cremates the slain warriors with full Vedic rites, and both armies' survivors descend to the Ganga to offer water-libations, Kunti openly making the offering for Karna _(actors: Yudhishthira, Vidura, Kunti)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/11-stri-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Stri Parva 26-27 (Shraddha/Jalapradanika))
- **Maya fetches treasure from the Himalayan Lake Bindusaras** — Spared from the Khandava fire, the asura architect Maya brings gem-quality stone, a mace, and the conch Devadatta from Lake Bindusaras (once Vrishaparvan's) to build the Mayasabha at Indraprastha _(actors: Maya Danava, Krishna, Arjuna, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/bindu-sarovar-siddhpur.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva 1-3)
- **Mudgala refuses heaven and seeks mokṣa** — Learning that even paradise ends in a fall, Mudgala declines the chariot, abandons the gleaning-rite, cultivates supreme equanimity, and pursues the imperishable supreme station beyond rebirth _(actors: Mudgala, Devadūta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva 247)
- **Nala deserts Damayantī in the forest** — Tormented by Kali, Nala cuts away half his sleeping wife's garment and abandons her in the night _(actors: Nala, Damayantī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 59)
- **Nemīnātha's renunciation, omniscience and liberation on Girnar** — The 22nd Tīrthaṅkara Nemīnātha (Ariṣṭanemi), Kṛṣṇa's cousin, attains dīkṣā, kevala-jñāna and nirvāṇa on the Ujjayanta peak _(actors: Nemīnātha (Ariṣṭanemi), Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/girnar-raivataka-datta-tirtha.md) — (Jain Uttarapurāṇa; Hari-vaṃśa-purāṇa; Kalpa-sūtra)
- **Nārada brings news; Yādava march on Śoṇitapura** — Nārada reveals Aniruddha is Bāṇa's captive; Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma and Pradyumna lead the Vṛṣṇi host (on Garuḍa) to besiege Śoṇitapura _(actors: Nārada, Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, Pradyumna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/banasura-usha-aniruddha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.63; Harivaṃśa Viṣṇu Parva)
- **Nārada's Keśava-stuti prophecy** — Nārada hails the victorious Krishna with a hymn foretelling Kaṃsa-vadha, the Mathurā and Dvārakā deeds, and his unburdening role at Kurukṣetra _(actors: Nārada, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/keshi-arishta-keshava.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.37)
- **Pāṇḍu's curse by the sage Kindama** — Pāṇḍu kills the sage Kindama mating in deer form and is cursed to die the instant he approaches a wife in desire, making natural fatherhood impossible _(actors: Pāṇḍu, Kindama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Rebirth as Drupada's child Śikhaṇḍin(ī)** — Ambā is reborn to King Drupada of Pāñcāla as a daughter raised secretly as a son, Śikhaṇḍin, precipitating a marriage crisis with Daśārṇa _(actors: Ambā reborn as Śikhaṇḍin, Drupada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/amba-shikhandi.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva ~188–192)
- **Return to Dwārakā and recognition** — Māyāvatī flies Pradyumna back to Kṛṣṇa's palace; his likeness to Kṛṣṇa and Nārada's narration reveal him as Rukmiṇī's lost son, to the city's joy _(actors: Pradyumna, Māyāvatī, Rukmiṇī, Kṛṣṇa, Nārada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/pradyumna.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.55)
- **Rukmī defeated, spared at Rukmiṇī's plea, and disfigured** — Kṛṣṇa overpowers the pursuing Rukmī and is about to behead him; Rukmiṇī intercedes, so Kṛṣṇa instead shaves his head in patches as disgrace; Balarāma reconciles the family _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Rukmī, Rukmiṇī, Balarāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rukmini-harana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.54)
- **Sanatsujatiya teaching** — The boy-sage Sanatsujata teaches the still-sleepless king that death is not annihilation but ignorance, expounding Brahman, true knowledge, and liberation _(actors: Sanatsujata, Dhritarashtra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva 41-46 (Sanatsujata))
- **Slaying of Duhshasana and blood-drinking** — On the 17th day Bhima smashes Duhshasana from his chariot, tears open his chest, and drinks his blood, fulfilling the twin dice-hall vow _(actors: Bhīma, Duḥśāsana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dushasana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Karṇa Parva 61 / 83)
- **Slaying of Putana** — The demoness sent with poisoned breast-milk is drained of life by the suckling infant Krishna and granted liberation _(actors: Krishna, Putana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.6; Harivamsa)
- **Slaying of Sisupala** — After Sisupala exceeds his hundred forgiven offenses objecting to Krishna's first-honour, Krishna beheads him with the Sudarsana Cakra and his radiance merges into Krishna _(actors: Krishna, Sisupala, Bhishma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva 39-42; Bhagavata Purana 7.1)
- **Slaying of the conch-demon Panchajana** — Krishna kills the asura Panchajana, who lived in the sea in the form of a conch and had swallowed the boy _(actors: Krishna, Panchajana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sandipani-pancajana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.45; Harivamsha; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **Sole surviving son of Dhritarashtra** — After Kurukshetra every Kaurava is dead by Bhima's vow; Yuyutsu alone of Dhritarashtra's sons survives, having crossed sides and slept outside the doomed camp _(actors: Yuyutsu, Bhima, Duryodhana, the Kauravas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yuyutsu.md) — (Mahabharata, Shalya/Stri Parvas)
- **Straightening of Trivakrā (Kubjā)** — Krishna straightens the deformed hunchback maidservant Trivakrā, who had anointed him with sandal-paste _(actors: Krishna, Trivakrā/Kubjā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.42 (visit X.48); Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **The 18-Day Mahābhārata War at Kurukṣetra** — Eighteen akṣauhiṇīs assemble between the Sarasvatī and Dṛṣadvatī and fight the 18-day Kaurava–Pāṇḍava war that seals the Dvāpara–Kali yuga-turn _(actors: Pāṇḍavas, Kauravas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kurukshetra-dharmakshetra.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhishma-parva onward)
- **The Brahmashiras duel and Vyasa's intervention** — Cornered at Vyasa's hermitage, Ashvatthaman invokes the Brahmashiras on a blade of grass; Arjuna counters with his own Brahmastra, and Vyasa and Narada step between to halt the world-ending collision _(actors: Ashvatthaman, Arjuna, Vyasa, Narada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/10-sauptika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sauptika Parva 13-14 (Aishika))
- **The brahmin's curse and the iron pestle** — Sages cursed by Yadava youths' prank decree that Samba will bear an iron pestle destroying the clan; ground to powder and cast into the sea, it returns as eraka reeds and an iron splinter _(actors: Vishvamitra, Kanva, Narada, Samba, Ugrasena)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/prabhasa-somnath-tirtha.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva 1-3; Bhagavata Purana 11.30)
- **The burning of Khāṇḍava and the slaughter of fleeing creatures** — Agni blazes on every side while Arjuna and Kṛṣṇa drive escaping beasts and beings back into the flames _(actors: Agni, Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/khandava-vana-and-the-burning.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi-parva 218-219)
- **The dance upon the hoods (Kāliya-nartana)** — Krishna mounts the serpent's heads and dances upon them, crushing each rearing hood as gods drum and shower flowers _(actors: Krishna, Kāliya, the gods)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kaliya-mardana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.16; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5; Harivaṃśa)
- **The footprints and the favoured gopī** — The gopīs find paired footprints showing Kṛṣṇa led one favoured gopī away, then find her abandoned for her own pride; commentators read Rādhā here _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, the favoured gopī (read as Rādhā), the gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rasa-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.30.25–43)
- **The Gods Grant Nala Boons and the Marriage** — Pleased, the four gods grant Nala gifts of fire, water, dharma and cooking; the couple marries and bears Indrasena and Indrasenā _(actors: Indra, Agni, Varuṇa, Yama, Nala, Damayantī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 56)
- **The gopīs envy the flute (veṇu-asūyā)** — The gopīs voice jealousy of the bamboo flute that drinks the nectar of Kṛṣṇa's lips, wondering what austerities it performed to earn the touch _(actors: gopīs, the veṇu, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/venu-gita.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.21.9)
- **The Iron-Club Curse on the Yadavas** — Sages mocked when young Yadavas disguised Samba as a pregnant woman curse that he will bear an iron club destroying the clan; Samba births an iron bolt ground to powder and cast into the sea _(actors: Vishvamitra, Kanva, Narada, Samba)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dwarka-bet-dwarka-saurashtra.md) — (Mahabharata 16.1-2; Bhagavata 11.1)
- **The Kāśī svayaṃvara abduction** — Bhīṣma raids the joint svayaṃvara of the three Kāśī princesses and carries off Ambā, Ambikā, and Ambālikā by force as brides for Vicitravīrya _(actors: Bhīṣma, Ambā, Ambikā, Ambālikā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vichitravirya-and-chitrangada.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~102)
- **The messenger's honest account of svarga's defects** — Asked to describe heaven truthfully, the messenger reveals svarga is earned by merit, exhausted by enjoyment, accrues no new karma, breeds discontent, and ends in a sorrowful fall to rebirth _(actors: Devadūta, Mudgala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Mārkaṇḍeya-samāsya (Mudgala-Upākhyāna))
- **The Mārkaṇḍeya discourses** — The deathless sage Mārkaṇḍeya recounts to Yudhiṣṭhira the pralaya-vision, Naḷa–Damayantī, Sāvitrī, the Matsya-flood, the Dharma-vyādha and the Kali-yuga prophecy _(actors: Mārkaṇḍeya, Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Mārkaṇḍeya-samāsyā Parva ~182–232)
- **The Parashurama-Bhishma Duel** — Spurned Amba appeals to Parashurama, who fights Bhishma a multi-day duel at Kurukshetra that ends inconclusively when the gods intervene _(actors: Amba, Parashurama, Bhishma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhishma.md) — (Mahabharata, Udyoga Parva ~179-186)
- **The Pāṇḍavas Visit Pushkar** — The Pandavas visit Pushkar on their pilgrimage from the Kamyaka forest, attesting its established sanctity by the Pandava era _(actors: Pandavas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/pushkar-brahma-tirtha.md) — (Mahabharata (Vana Parva))
- **The Python Seizes Bhīma** — Ages later during the Pāṇḍava exile, the boon-strengthened serpent Nahuṣa seizes Bhīma in a mountain cave and drains his strength _(actors: Nahuṣa, Bhīma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nahusha.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 176-177)
- **The third Sauptika night-raider** — Kṛtavarma, after Kṛpa's failed dissent, guards the camp gates and sets the fires while Aśvatthāman slaughters the sleeping Pāñcāla–Pāṇḍava camp _(actors: Kṛtavarma, Aśvatthāman, Kṛpa, Dhṛṣṭadyumna, the Upapāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kritavarma.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sauptika Parva (adhy. 1-9))
- **The Uñchavṛtti gleaner-family's fourfold gift to Dharma** — A starving Kurukṣetra gleaner-brāhmaṇa, his wife, son and daughter-in-law each give their own portion of barley-flour to a guest who is Dharma in disguise, dying of hunger but ascending to heaven _(actors: The Uñchavṛtti brāhmaṇa family, Dharma (disguised as a guest))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/dana-upakhyanas-shveta-and-the-mongoose.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva 90-93 (cf. Anuśāsana 13.93))
- **The witnessing head over Kurukṣetra** — Kṛṣṇa immortalises Barbarīka's head and sets it on a high hill to watch the entire eighteen-day war _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Barbarīka's head)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/barbarika-khatu-shyam.md) — (Folk Mahābhārata traditions)
- **The yoga of liberation and surrender** — The Gītā closes with total surrender (prapatti/śaraṇāgati) to Gaṇeśa as the supreme secret and the promise of release, adapting Bhagavad Gītā 18 _(actors: Gajānana (Gaṇeśa), King Vareṇya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ganesha-gita.md) — (Gaṇeśa Purāṇa, Krīḍā-khaṇḍa ch. 148)
- **Twelve-year drought and ruin of the kingdom** — During the king's absence Indra withholds rain for twelve years; famine and disorder weaken Hastināpura _(actors: Indra, Saṃvaraṇa's realm)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/samvarana-tapati.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva, Tāpatya upākhyāna)
- **Uddhava departs for Badarikashrama** — After the final bhakti teaching Uddhava, overwhelmed, takes leave of Krishna and proceeds to Badarikashrama to practice renunciation _(actors: Uddhava, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uddhava-gita.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11.29)
- **Uddhava is humbled and exalts the gopīs' love** — After months in Vraja Uddhava declares the gopīs' unlettered love supreme over all jñāna and yoga, praying to be reborn as a shrub of Vṛndāvana to receive the dust of their feet _(actors: Uddhava, the gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhramara-gita-uddhava-sandesha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.47.58–63)
- **Unbroken generalship through the 18-day war** — Holds supreme Pāṇḍava command from day 1 to the end, arranging the army's daily counter-formations _(actors: Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Pāṇḍava army)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dhrishtadyumna.md) — (Mahābhārata, Bhīṣma–Śalya Parvas)
- **Uttanka discharges the fee before sunset** — The fire-horse carries Uttanka back to deliver the earrings in time, and Veda explains all the omens he met _(actors: Uttanka, Veda)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahabharata Paushya Parva)
- **Vasudeva Carries Kṛṣṇa Across the Yamunā to Gokula** — Vasudeva carries the infant Kṛṣṇa across the flooding Yamunā to Gokula, exchanging him for Nanda and Yaśodā's newborn daughter Yoga-māyā, who announces Kaṃsa's doom _(actors: Vasudeva, Kṛṣṇa, Nanda, Yaśodā, Yoga-māyā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.3-4)
- **Vidura speaks the Vidura-nīti and resigns his post** — On the eve of war Vidura pours out his eight-chapter aphoristic discourse on nīti to the sleepless king, and renounces his ministerial post and weapons to take no part in the war _(actors: Vidura, Dhṛtarāṣṭra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vidura.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva 33-40)
- **Vyāsa relays the Haṃsa-gītā to Yudhiṣṭhira** — In the post-war Mokṣadharma frame, Vyāsa recalls and recounts the ancient swan-Brahmā discourse to Yudhiṣṭhira as a purātana itihāsa on the highest good _(actors: Vyāsa; Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hamsa-gita.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śānti Parva, Mokṣadharma ~12.288–299)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira receives the akṣaya-pātra from Sūrya** — In exile Yudhiṣṭhira propitiates Sūrya, who grants the inexhaustible vessel that feeds the Pāṇḍavas' guests until Draupadī finishes her daily meal _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Sūrya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yudhishthira.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva)
- **Śalya's Day 18 rampage** — Śalya fights furiously, scattering the Pāṇḍavas and wounding Yudhiṣṭhira, Bhīma, Sātyaki and the twins, fighting like Yama _(actors: Śalya, Yudhiṣṭhira, Bhīma, Sātyaki, Nakula, Sahadeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shalya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śalya Parva (Śalya-vadha))
- **Amba's Rebirth as Shikhandin** — After tapas and Shiva's boon to be the cause of Bhishma's death, Amba immolates herself and is reborn as Shikhandin, child of Drupada, who obtains manhood by exchanging sex with a yaksha _(actors: Amba, Shiva, Shikhandin, Drupada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhishma.md) — (Mahabharata, Udyoga Parva ~187-193)
- **Ambālikā turns pale; birth of Pāṇḍu** — Ambālikā blanches with dread at Vyāsa, and her son is born pale — Pāṇḍu, the pallid king _(actors: Ambālikā, Vyāsa, Pāṇḍu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ambika-ambalika.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~106)
- **Ambā released and the marriages** — Ambā is freed after revealing her prior pledge to Śālva; Ambikā and Ambālikā are wed to Vicitravīrya at Hastināpura _(actors: Bhīṣma, Ambā, Ambikā, Ambālikā, Vicitravīrya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vichitravirya-and-chitrangada.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~102)
- **Arjuna defeats Vajradatta of Prāgjyotiṣa** — Vajradatta, son of the slain Bhagadatta, rides his war-elephant against Arjuna; a fierce three-day duel ends in Vajradatta's submission _(actors: Arjuna, Vajradatta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.74-75)
- **Arjuna slays Karna's son Vrishasena** — Keeping his fresh vow to avenge Abhimanyu, Arjuna kills Karna's son Vrishasena before his eyes with razor-arrows, breaking the grief-stricken Karna's spirit _(actors: Arjuna, Vrishasena, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/08-karna-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva 85)
- **Ashvatthaman redirects the weapon into Uttara's womb** — Unable to withdraw his weapon, Ashvatthaman redirects it into the wombs of the Pandava women, especially Uttara, to extinguish the line; Krishna vows to revive the slain embryo and names him Parikshit _(actors: Ashvatthaman, Uttara, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/10-sauptika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sauptika Parva 15-16 (Aishika))
- **Authorship of the Brahma Sūtras (Bādarāyaṇa)** — Under the name Bādarāyaṇa, Vyāsa is the traditional author of the Brahma Sūtras founding the Vedānta darśana, with a scholarly debate over the identity _(actors: Vyāsa / Bādarāyaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa.md) — (Tradition; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 1.4)
- **Bharata crowned Cakravartin and namesake of the land** — Dushyanta installs Bharata as crown prince; Bharata becomes a universal sovereign whose conquests give his name to the Bharatas and the subcontinent Bharatavarsha, continuing the Paurava-Kuru line _(actors: Dushyanta, Bharata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shakuntala-bharata.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva, Sambhava)
- **Bhima kills Jarasandha** — Jarasandha of Magadha is slain by Bhima, clearing the way for Yudhishthira's Rajasuya _(actors: Bhima, Jarasandha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shishupala.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha-parva 20-22)
- **Bhima Slays Kirmira and Jatasura** — During the exile Bhima kills the rakshasa Kirmira in the Kamyaka forest and later slays Jatasura who abducted his kin _(actors: Bhima, Kirmira, Jatasura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhima.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva ~11, ~157)
- **Birth of Ghaṭotkaca** — By rākṣasa speed Hiḍimbī conceives and bears Ghaṭotkaca the same day; he matures at once and departs with the boon to come at a thought _(actors: Hiḍimbī, Bhīma, Ghaṭotkaca)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/hidimba-hidimbi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva (~155))
- **Birth of Satyavatī / Matsyagandhā** — The female twin, fish-scented, is given to the fisherman-chief and grows up as Satyavatī (Matsyagandhā), secretly carrying royal Lunar blood _(actors: Vasu Uparicara, Satyavatī, the fisherman-chief)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/uparichara-vasu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva (~63))
- **Birth of the Kauravas** — Gandhari bears from a mass of flesh divided into a hundred jars the hundred Kauravas led by Duryodhana, plus a daughter Duhshala _(actors: Gandhari, Duryodhana, the Kauravas)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Birth of Yudhiṣṭhira by Dharma** — At Pāṇḍu's request Kuntī invokes Dharma and bears Yudhiṣṭhira, the eldest Pāṇḍava and embodiment of righteousness _(actors: Kuntī, Dharma/Yama, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Breaking of Kaṃsa's bow** — Krishna strings and snaps the great ceremonial bow of the Dhanur-yajña, then beats off the guards _(actors: Krishna, Balarāma, Kaṃsa's guards)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.42; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Condensed Mahābhārata and Kṛṣṇa carita** — The Agni Purāṇa gives a précis of the Yādava birth of Kṛṣṇa, his Vṛndāvana/Mathurā exploits, and the Pāṇḍava–Kaurava conflict and war _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Pāṇḍavas, Kauravas)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/agni-purana.md) — (Agni Purāṇa (Hari-vaṃśa/Mahābhārata digest))
- **Damayantī curses the hunter and reaches Chedi** — Abandoned Damayantī curses a lustful hunter dead by her chastity, then is taken in as an attendant in Chedi and recognized by her lotus-mark _(actors: Damayantī, the hunter, Sudeva, Subāhu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 60–66)
- **Day 8 — Iravan beheaded by Alambusha** — Iravan, Arjuna's son by the Naga princess Ulupi, fights brilliantly but is beheaded by the rakshasa Alambusha, while Ghatotkaca dominates the field with illusions _(actors: Iravan, Alambusha, Ghatotkaca)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva 83-91)
- **Death of Ketumāna at Kurukṣetra** — Ekalavya's son Ketumāna, a Niṣāda warrior, fights and is slain on the Kaurava side in the great Kurukṣetra war _(actors: Ketumāna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ekalavya.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva)
- **Death of Pāṇḍu by Mādrī's embrace** — Overmastered by desire in spring, Pāṇḍu embraces Mādrī despite her warning, and the Kindama curse strikes him dead at the touch _(actors: Pāṇḍu, Mādrī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/madri.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Deaths of Uttara and Virata in the war** — Prince Uttara is slain by Shalya on the first day (his brother Shveta by Bhishma); Virata is killed by Drona on the 17th day, ending the Matsya royal house _(actors: Uttara, Shveta, Virata, Shalya, Bhishma, Drona)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/virata-and-his-children.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhishma Parva; Drona Parva)
- **Draupadi Becomes Wife to the Five Pandavas** — On Kunti's unseeing instruction to 'share it among you,' Draupadi becomes the common wife of all five Pandavas _(actors: Kunti, Draupadi, the Pandavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~182-191)
- **Draupadi binds her hair in Duhshasana's blood** — Bhima carries the blood to Draupadi, who at last binds the hair loosed since the dice-hall, completing the vengeance loop _(actors: Bhīma, Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dushasana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Karṇa Parva vadha section)
- **Drowning of the seven Vasus** — Gaṅgā drowns her first seven newborn sons (the released Vasus) while the vow-bound Śāntanu watches in silence _(actors: Gaṅgā, Śāntanu, the seven Vasus)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~98–99)
- **Duryodhana humiliated in the Mayasabhā** — Duryodhana mistakes the illusion-floor for water and falls into a pool, his shame seeding the dice-game and the Kurukṣetra war _(actors: Duryodhana, Draupadī, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/maya-danava.md) — (Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva)
- **Duryodhana left dying appoints Ashvatthaman** — Mortally wounded but still alive at the lake, Duryodhana reviles Krishna for the unfair killings and survives into the night to appoint Ashvatthaman as the last commander _(actors: Duryodhana, Ashvatthaman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/09-shalya-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Shalya Parva (Gada-yuddha))
- **Ekalavya's thumb as guru-dakshina** — The self-taught Nishada prince Ekalavya surpasses Arjuna, and Drona demands his right thumb to preserve Arjuna's supremacy _(actors: Ekalavya, Drona, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ekalavya.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva; Harivamsha)
- **False-sunset slaying of Jayadratha** — Kṛṣṇa veils the sun to feign sunset; Jayadratha exposes himself and Arjuna beheads him as the sun reappears _(actors: Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa, Jayadratha, Droṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jayadratha.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva 85–152, esp. 146)
- **Founding of Dvārakā** — To escape Jarāsandha's relentless sieges and Kālayavana, Krishna leads the Yādavas west and builds with Viśvakarman the island-fortress city of Dvārakā in the sea _(actors: Krishna, Viśvakarman, Jarāsandha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/harivamsha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva)
- **Held off during Abhimanyu's death** — On day 13 he and the Pāṇḍava leaders are blocked by Jayadratha and cannot reach Abhimanyu trapped in the cakravyūha _(actors: Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Abhimanyu, Droṇa, Jayadratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dhrishtadyumna.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva)
- **Indra's failed defense of the forest** — Indra leads the gods and pours rain to save Khāṇḍava, but Arjuna's arrow-canopy holds them off until Indra desists _(actors: Indra, Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa, Yama, Varuṇa, Kubera, the Maruts)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/khandava-vana-and-the-burning.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi-parva 218-219)
- **Kṛṣṇa Delivers the Bhagavad Gītā at Jyotisar** — As the armies face off at Jyotisar, Kṛṣṇa speaks the Bhagavad Gītā to Arjuna and grants the viśvarūpa-darśana under the immortal banyan _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kurukshetra-dharmakshetra.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 1.1; Mahabharata, Bhishma-parva)
- **Kali Vows to Destroy Nala** — Arriving too late and enraged that a mortal won the bride the gods coveted, the age-demon Kali vows to destroy Nala and lurks twelve years for an opening _(actors: Kali, Dvāpara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 56)
- **Kṛṣṇa's Infant Līlās at Gokula** — At Gokula the infant Kṛṣṇa slays Pūtanā, Śakaṭāsura and Tṛṇāvarta, steals butter, and is bound to the mortar in the damodara-līlā _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Pūtanā, Śakaṭāsura, Tṛṇāvarta)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.3-11)
- **Kṛṣṇa subdues Śiva (Hari–Hara combat)** — Śiva sallies on Nandin to defend his devotee Bāṇa; Kṛṣṇa neutralizes him with the Jṛmbhaṇāstra (yawn-weapon) without insult _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/banasura-usha-aniruddha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.63; Harivaṃśa Viṣṇu Parva)
- **King Nala Released from Śani's Affliction** — Under Saturn's malefic grip King Nala loses his kingdom and is parted from Damayanti; bathing in the Nala Tirtham and worshipping at Tirunallar, he is freed from Sani's affliction and regains throne and queen _(actors: Nala, Damayanti, Shani)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/navagraha-temples.md) — (Mahabharata (Nalopakhyana); Sthala-purana)
- **Kripa present at the dice-hall** — Kripa is among the elders present at the dice game and the disrobing of Draupadi but does not prevent it _(actors: Kripa, Draupadi, Kaurava elders)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kripa.md) — (Mahabharata, Sabha Parva)
- **Krishna's false sunset and the slaying of Jayadratha** — Day 14: Krishna covers the sun feigning sunset; Jayadratha exposes himself and Arjuna beheads him, shooting the head into his meditating father Vriddhakshatra's lap so the boon recoils and bursts the father's head _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna, Jayadratha, Vriddhakshatra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva 85-146 (Jayadratha-vadha))
- **Krishna's peace embassy to Hastinapura** — Krishna goes personally to ask for only five villages; he lodges with Vidura, visits Kunti, and pleads in the Kaurava court, but Duryodhana refuses even a needle-point of land _(actors: Krishna, Duryodhana, Kunti, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva, Bhagavad-yana 74-150)
- **Krishna sustains the substitution for a full year** — Krishna maintains himself as all the boys and calves for one complete earthly year, a mere moment on Brahma's time-scale _(actors: Krishna, Brahma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/brahma-vimohana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.13.40)
- **Launch of the Tīrtha-yātrā** — From the exile-forest the Pāṇḍavas begin the great pilgrimage of Bhārata's fords, catalogued by Pulastya/Nārada and guided by Lomaśa _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Lomaśa, Pulastya, Nārada)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Tīrtha-yātrā Parva ~80–156)
- **Manu Instructs Bṛhaspati on the Self** — Bṛhaspati questions Manu, who teaches the field and knower of the field, the supremacy of jñāna, and the ascent to the supreme Self _(actors: Manu, Bṛhaspati)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mokshadharma-dialogues.md) — (Mahabharata Shanti Parva 194-199)
- **Marriage at Dwārakā and Rukmiṇī as Lakṣmī-incarnate chief queen** — Kṛṣṇa weds Rukmiṇī with full rites; she becomes paṭṭa-mahiṣī, identified as Lakṣmī/Śrī incarnate _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Rukmiṇī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rukmini-harana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.54, 10.60; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.26)
- **Marriage to Rukmavatī and birth of Aniruddha** — Pradyumna weds Rukmī's daughter Rukmavatī and begets Aniruddha; at the wedding-festivities Balarāma kills Rukmī in a dice-quarrel _(actors: Pradyumna, Rukmavatī, Aniruddha, Rukmī, Balarāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/pradyumna.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.61; Harivaṃśa)
- **Migration from Gokula to Vṛndāvana** — Alarmed by the prodigies and demon-deaths, the cowherds under Upananda move the community from Gokula to Vṛndāvana, closing the infancy arc _(actors: Nanda, Upananda, Krishna, the gopas of Gokula)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-balya-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.11)
- **Mohinī's widowhood and mourning** — After Aravān is sacrificed at dawn, Kṛṣṇa-as-Mohinī mourns him as a widow before resuming male form _(actors: Kṛṣṇa (as Mohinī), Aravān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/aravan-iravan.md) — (Tamil Mahābhārata; Kūttāṇṭavar temple tradition)
- **Mārkaṇḍeya narrates the upākhyāna to Yudhiṣṭhira** — Within the forest exile, the deathless sage Mārkaṇḍeya tells the Mudgala parable to Yudhiṣṭhira as the epic's definitive teaching that even svarga is to be renounced for Brahman _(actors: Mārkaṇḍeya, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva 246–247 (Mārkaṇḍeya-samāsya))
- **Muchukunda Incinerates Kalayavana** — Awakened from his boon of long sleep in Dvapara, Muchukunda's glance incinerates Kalayavana, linking the Solar line to Krishna's story _(actors: Muchukunda, Kalayavana, Krishna)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Bhagavata X; Vishnu Purana)
- **Mudgala refuses heaven and turns the chariot away** — Learning that svarga is impermanent (anitya), Mudgala reasons a reward that must be lost is no true gain and dismisses the chariot, declining bodily ascent _(actors: Mudgala, Devadūta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Mārkaṇḍeya-samāsya (Mudgala-Upākhyāna))
