- **Niyoga and the births of Dhritarashtra, Pandu, Vidura** — Vyasa fathers Dhritarashtra (born blind), Pandu (born pale), and Vidura (born wise to a maidservant) on the widows by niyoga _(actors: Vyasa, Satyavati, Ambika, Ambalika)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 105-107)
- **Nārada's tour of the 16,108 palaces** — Nārada finds Kṛṣṇa simultaneously present in all 16,100 palaces, each wife believing him hers alone, demonstrating his yoga-māyā _(actors: Nārada, Kṛṣṇa, the 16,108 queens)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/narakasura-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.69)
- **Pandavas recover weapons from the shami tree (Ayudha Puja gloss)** — The Pandavas retrieve their hidden weapons from the shami tree at the close of their incognito year, linked to Ayudha Puja's Shami-puja strand _(actors: the Pandavas)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Mahābhārata (Virāṭa Parva))
- **Parashurama's curse on Karna** — After Karna endures a worm boring his thigh without flinching, Parashurama deduces his kshatriya birth and curses that his memory of the supreme astra will fail at his greatest need _(actors: Parashurama, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata)
- **Pāñcāla seizure and forest exile** — Sudāsa of Pāñcāla drives Saṃvaraṇa from Hastināpura; the king lives in forest exile on the Sindhu for twelve years _(actors: Saṃvaraṇa, Tapatī, Sudāsa (Pāñcāla))_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/samvarana-tapati.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva, Sambhava/Tāpatya strata; Pauravā genealogy)
- **Pralambāsura slain by Balarāma's fist** — A demon disguised as a cowherd carries Balarāma off in a play-game, then is smashed dead by a single fist-blow to the head _(actors: Balarāma, Pralambāsura, Krishna, cowherd boys)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vraja-boyhood-demons.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.18; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Pre-war counsel and embassy** — Drupada urges diplomacy, sending his purohita as envoy to Hastināpura, and is named a commander-candidate of the Pāṇḍava host _(actors: Drupada, Drupada's purohita, the Pāṇḍavas, Dhṛṣṭadyumna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drupada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva ~4–5, ~151–155)
- **Procession to Kurukṣetra and scorched nails** — On the field her grief-glance through the blindfold scorches Yudhiṣṭhira's nails before she relents _(actors: Gāndhārī, Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/gandhari.md) — (Mahābhārata, Strī Parva XI)
- **Recitation of the thousand Rudra-Śiva names and phala-śruti** — Kṛṣṇa utters the thousand names (Sthāṇu, Hara, Mahādeva, etc.) closing with the fruit-promise of release and union with Śiva _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/shiva-sahasranama.md) — (Mahābhārata, Anuśāsana Parva XIII)
- **Revival by the Nāgamaṇi** — Ulūpī summons the serpent-realm resurrection jewel (Mṛtasaṃjīvanī / Nāgamaṇi) and revives the slain Arjuna _(actors: Ulūpī, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ulupi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~81)
- **Revival of Arjuna by the Nagamani** — Ulupi supplies the Naga revival-gem (Sanjivana/Nagamani), placed on Arjuna's chest, and Arjuna is restored to life _(actors: Ulūpī, Arjuna, Babhruvāhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/babhruvahana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~81)
- **Sañjaya follows the king to the forest and departs to the Himālaya** — Sañjaya consoles the broken Dhṛtarāṣṭra, accompanies him, Gāndhārī and Kuntī into forest vānaprastha, escapes the forest fire that kills them, and finally goes north to the Himālaya to end his days in asceticism _(actors: Sañjaya, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Gāndhārī, Kuntī, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sanjaya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Strī / Āśramavāsika Parvas (~37))
- **Sex-change via the yakṣa Sthūṇa** — The yakṣa Sthūṇakarṇa exchanges sex with Śikhaṇḍin; Kubera's curse fixes the maleness on Śikhaṇḍin for life, defusing the Daśārṇa war _(actors: Śikhaṇḍin, Sthūṇa, Kubera)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/amba-shikhandi.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva ~192–193)
- **Shiva Flees as a Bull at Kedarnath** — The Pandavas seek Shiva at Kedarnath to expiate the Kurukshetra slaughter; Shiva flees as a bull and dives into the ground, leaving the worshipped hump and the Panch Kedar _(actors: Pandavas, Shiva)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/char-dham-and-chota-char-dham.md) — (Shiva Purana; Mahabharata-derived sthala-purana)
- **Slaying of Shakatasura** — Baby Krishna overturns and destroys the cart-demon with a kick _(actors: Krishna, Shakatasura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.7)
- **Sātyaki beheads Kṛtavarma at Prabhāsa** — In the drunken Yādava brawl at Prabhāsa, Sātyaki taunts Kṛtavarma over the night-massacre and beheads him, igniting the Mausala self-slaughter of the clan _(actors: Kṛtavarma, Sātyaki, Pradyumna, the Bhojas and Andhakas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kritavarma.md) — (Mahābhārata, Mausala Parva 3; Bhāgavata Purāṇa XI.30)
- **Submergence of Dvārakā** — After Krishna's departure the city of Dwarka sinks into the sea, marking the hinge into the present Kali Yuga _(actors: Krishna, Yadavas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sapta-puri-and-sacred-cities.md) — (Mahabharata (Mausala Parva); Bhagavata & Vishnu Puranas)
- **Sudama departs without begging** — Content merely to have seen Krishna, Sudama never voices his poverty and leaves having received (he thinks) only the Lord's affection _(actors: Sudama, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.80.35-38)
- **Sudāmā departs without begging** — Too abashed to voice his poverty, Sudāmā never asks for anything and leaves content merely to have seen Kṛṣṇa _(actors: Sudāmā, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.80.35–38)
- **Susharma's Trigarta cattle-raid** — The Trigarta king Susharma raids Matsya's cattle and captures King Virata; the four Pandavas rescue Virata, rout the Trigartas, and capture Susharma, whom Yudhishthira mercifully frees _(actors: Susharma, Virata, Bhima)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/04-virata-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Virata Parva 30-33)
- **The 28-day cave-fight with Jāmbavān** — Kṛṣṇa duels the bear-king Jāmbavān alone for twenty-eight days until Jāmbavān's strength fails _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Jāmbavān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13)
- **The first game of dice** — Playing for Duryodhana, the cheating Shakuni wins everything from Yudhishthira in escalating stakes down to his brothers, himself, and finally Draupadi _(actors: Yudhishthira, Shakuni, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva 45-58)
- **The Gopī-gītā** — Reassembled on the Yamunā bank the gopīs sing the 19-verse Song of the Gopīs of longing, the locus classicus of love-in-separation _(actors: The gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rasa-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.31)
- **The half-heard cakravyūha womb-lesson** — In the folk motif, Subhadrā falls asleep during Arjuna's narration, so the unborn Abhimanyu learns only how to enter the cakravyūha, not exit _(actors: Subhadrā, Arjuna, unborn Abhimanyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subhadra.md) — (Mahābhārata-tradition (folk overlay; Critical Edition variant))
- **The hunter Jara's arrow and Krishna's departure** — As Krishna lies in meditation, the hunter Jara mistakes his raised foot for a deer and shoots him with the very iron splinter from the cursed club; Krishna consoles and blesses Jara, then withdraws into his supreme abode, ending the Krishna avatar and marking the onset of Kali Yuga _(actors: Jara, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/16-mausala-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 16.4; Bhagavata Purana 11.30-31)
- **The Killing of Abhimanyu** — A ring of Kaurava maharathis disarms the weaponless sixteen-year-old Abhimanyu and Duhshasana's son strikes him dead with a mace, the moral nadir of the war _(actors: Abhimanyu, Karna, Drona, Kripa, Duhshasana's son)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/abhimanyu.md) — (Mahabharata, Drona Parva)
- **The mongoose gilded by the gleaners' crumbs** — Rolling in the scattered leftover flour turned half the mongoose to gold; it wanders sacrifices ever after seeking to gild its other half, but no rite matches the gleaners' gift _(actors: The golden-headed mongoose)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/dana-upakhyanas-shveta-and-the-mongoose.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva 93)
- **The pleas of the Nāga-wives (Nāga-patnī-stuti)** — Kāliya's wives bow and hymn Krishna as the Supreme, begging mercy, and Kāliya himself submits _(actors: Nāga-patnīs, Kāliya, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kaliya-mardana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.16)
- **The second dice-game and the exile-stake** — Śakuni wins a second match staking thirteen years' exile, sending the Pāṇḍavas into the forest _(actors: Śakuni, Yudhiṣṭhira, Duryodhana, Dhṛtarāṣṭra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shakuni.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva ~66–72)
- **The verdict — Sudarśana alone won the war** — After the war the witnessing head declares it saw no human warrior win, only Kṛṣṇa's Sudarśana cakra and the Goddess's tongue drinking blood; the heroes were mere instruments _(actors: Barbarīka's head, Kṛṣṇa, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/barbarika-khatu-shyam.md) — (Folk Mahābhārata; Khāṭū Śyām sthala-tradition)
- **The Yadava Massacre at Prabhasa** — Drunk on maireya at the Prabhasa festival, the Yadavas quarrel and uproot the lethal eraka reeds as clubs, slaughtering one another until nearly all the Vrishnis die _(actors: Satyaki, Kritavarman, Pradyumna, Samba, Aniruddha)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dwarka-bet-dwarka-saurashtra.md) — (Mahabharata 16.3-4; Bhagavata 11.30)
- **The Yadava mausala at Prabhasa** — The Vrishni-Andhaka chiefs migrate to Prabhasa, fall to drink and quarrel, and slaughter one another with iron clubs sprung from the eraka reeds, annihilating the Yadava race _(actors: Satyaki, Kritavarman, Pradyumna, Aniruddha, Samba, the Vrishni-Andhakas, Krishna)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/prabhasa-somnath-tirtha.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva 3-4; Bhagavata Purana 11.30)
- **The Yakṣa Praśna** — After his four brothers drink and die at a guarded lake, Yudhiṣṭhira answers a Yakṣa's riddles, chooses to revive Nakula for impartial dharma, and the Yakṣa — his father Dharma — revives all four and grants undetected passage of the thirteenth year _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Dharma/Yama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yudhishthira.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva)
- **Third demonic birth: Sisupala and Dantavakra slain** — The pair are reborn as the human kings Sisupala (beheaded by the Sudarsana Cakra) and Dantavakra (killed by Krishna), their radiance merging into Krishna to discharge the curse _(actors: Sisupala, Dantavakra, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jaya-vijaya.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 7.1, 10.74, 10.78)
- **Tulādhāra Instructs Jājali** — A heavenly voice tells the penance-proud ascetic Jājali he is unequal to the humble grocer Tulādhāra, who expounds ahiṃsā and honest livelihood as true dharma _(actors: Jājali, Tulādhāra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/minor-upakhyanas-catalog.md) — (Mahabharata Shanti Parva 261-264)
- **Uddhava's farewell and report to Kṛṣṇa** — Bowing to the gopīs and declaring their devotion the rarest thing in the three worlds, Uddhava returns to Mathurā and reports their love to Kṛṣṇa _(actors: Uddhava, the gopīs, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhramara-gita-uddhava-sandesha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.47.64–69)
- **Urvashi's curse on Arjuna** — Spurned by Arjuna, the apsara Urvashi curses him to be a eunuch, which Indra softens to last only one year of his choosing _(actors: Urvashi, Arjuna, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 45-46)
- **Uttanka receives Krishna's Vishvarupa darshana** — In the Ashvamedhika version, an enraged Uttanka makes to curse Krishna for the war's carnage but is shown the Universal Form and taught the war's necessity for dharma _(actors: Uttanka, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahabharata Ashvamedhika Parva 14.53-54)
- **Veṇu-gītā as overture to the rāsa cycle** — The flute-song kindles the gopīs' longing that the rāsa-līlā will consummate, opening the mādhurya song-cycle before the vastra-haraṇa intervenes _(actors: gopīs, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/venu-gita.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.21 (with 10.22–23, 10.29–33))
- **Vidura's death and merger into Yudhiṣṭhira** — In forest penance Vidura casts his life-breaths and tejas into Yudhiṣṭhira by yogic power, both being portions of Dharma; a heavenly voice forbids cremating the yogin's body, closing Yama's curse-arc _(actors: Vidura, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vidura.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśramavāsika Parva)
- **Viśvakarmā raises Dvārakā in a single night** — At Kṛṣṇa's request Viśvakarmā raises the sea-girt capital Dvārakā in a single night on land reclaimed from the ocean _(actors: Viśvakarmā, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/vishvakarma-and-tvashtar.md) — (Mahābhārata; Bhāgavata Purāṇa; Harivaṃśa)
- **Winning of the conch Panchajanya** — From the slain demon's shell Krishna takes the conch Panchajanya, his lifelong emblem and war-conch _(actors: Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sandipani-pancajana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.45; Harivamsha; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira's Dharma-Catechism Frees Nahuṣa** — Yudhiṣṭhira answers the serpent's riddles on dharma and varṇa, satisfying Nahuṣa, who releases Bhīma, sheds the serpent body, and ascends to heaven _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Nahuṣa, Bhīma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nahusha.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 179-181)
- **Yuyutsu made steward of Hastinapura** — In the war's aftermath Yuyutsu is given charge of the surviving women, the treasury, and governance of a depopulated Hastinapura under Yudhishthira's reign _(actors: Yuyutsu, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yuyutsu.md) — (Mahabharata, Stri/Shanti Parvas)
- **Abhimanyu's death and Uttaraa widowed pregnant** — Abhimanyu is killed in the cakravyuha on the 13th day, leaving Uttaraa widowed while carrying his only child _(actors: Abhimanyu, Uttaraa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/virata-and-his-children.md) — (Mahabharata, Drona Parva; Stri Parva)
- **Arjuna destroys the Nivatakavachas and Hiranyapura** — As guru-dakshina to Indra, Arjuna destroys the Nivatakavacha danavas of the sea and the flying demon-city Hiranyapura _(actors: Arjuna, the Nivatakavachas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 168-173)
- **Arjuna's Oath to Kill Jayadratha** — Finding his son dead, Arjuna swears to kill Jayadratha before sunset the next day or enter fire, a feat Krishna later engineers _(actors: Arjuna, Jayadratha, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/abhimanyu.md) — (Mahabharata, Drona Parva)
- **Arjuna spares the Saindhava forces at Duḥśalā's plea** — In the Sindhu country of the dead Jayadratha, the bereaved widows and boy-heir give battle; Arjuna spares them when Duḥśalā pleads and her grandson faints _(actors: Arjuna, Duḥśalā, Jayadratha's son)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.78)
- **Arjuna's Union with Ulupi** — During his pilgrimage-exile Arjuna is drawn under the Ganga by the Naga princess Ulupi, fathering Iravan _(actors: Arjuna, Ulupi, Iravan)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~206)
- **Balarama's Departure at Prabhasa** — Balarama withdraws into yoga on the Prabhasa shore and the white serpent Shesha, whose portion he was, issues from his mouth into the sea, his return to source _(actors: Balarama, Shesha)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dwarka-bet-dwarka-saurashtra.md) — (Mahabharata 16.4; Bhagavata 11.30)
- **Balarama's yogic departure** — Balarama gives up his body in yoga on the Prabhasa shore; the serpent Shesha issues from his mouth into the sea _(actors: Balarama, Shesha)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/prabhasa-somnath-tirtha.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva 4; Bhagavata Purana 11.30)
- **Banishment of Kāliya to Ramaṇaka Dvīpa** — Krishna spares Kāliya and orders him with his kin back to Ramaṇaka, the footprint-mark protecting him from Garuḍa; the Yamunā is cleansed _(actors: Krishna, Kāliya, Garuḍa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kaliya-mardana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.16-17; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5; Harivaṃśa)
- **Beheading of Droṇa** — After the Aśvatthāman half-truth makes Droṇa lay down arms in yoga, Dhṛṣṭadyumna seizes him by the hair and severs his head, fulfilling the birth-prophecy _(actors: Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Droṇa, Yudhiṣṭhira, Bhīma, Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dhrishtadyumna.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva ~190-193)
- **Bhima Splits Jarasandha** — Bhima wrestles Jarasandha for many days and, on Krishna's split-leaf hint, rends him in two and hurls the halves apart so they cannot rejoin, freeing the captive kings _(actors: Bhima, Jarasandha, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhima.md) — (Mahabharata, Sabha Parva ~20-24)
- **Bhishma Appointed Kaurava Commander** — Duryodhana names Bhishma supreme commander; Bhishma accepts on condition he not kill the Pandavas and will not fight while Karna is in the field _(actors: Bhishma, Duryodhana, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhishma.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhishma Parva ~16)
- **Birth of Bhīma by Vāyu** — Kuntī invokes the wind-god Vāyu and bears the immensely strong Bhīma, making him a brother of Hanumān _(actors: Kuntī, Vāyu, Bhīma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Birth of Pradyumna** — Rukmiṇī's first son Pradyumna is born, recognized as Kāmadeva reborn, continuing the Vṛṣṇi line through his son Aniruddha _(actors: Rukmiṇī, Kṛṣṇa, Pradyumna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rukmini-harana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.55; Harivaṃśa)
- **Brahma's return and the theophany of four-armed Vishnu-forms** — Brahma returns to find both companies coexisting and sees every boy and calf revealed as a four-armed Narayana worshipped by all beings, falling into total vimohana _(actors: Brahma, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/brahma-vimohana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.13.42-57)
- **Condensed Kṛṣṇa biography (Brahma Purāṇa)** — A compact Kṛṣṇa-carita: birth at Mathurā, Gokula childhood, slaying of Kaṃsa, the Dvārakā years, and his role in the Mahābhārata era _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Kaṃsa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/brahma-purana.md) — (Brahma Purāṇa (from Harivaṃśa/Viṣṇu Purāṇa))
- **Day 9 — Krishna's second wheel-charge** — In despair at Arjuna's reluctance to kill his grandfather, Krishna again leaps down and charges Bhishma with a wheel; Arjuna restrains him a second time and vows to slay Bhishma next day _(actors: Krishna, Bhishma, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva 92-106)
- **Death in the Mausala fratricide** — Thirty-six years after Kurukṣetra, Pradyumna falls at Prabhāsa in the cursed club-slaughter of the Vṛṣṇi-Andhakas with almost the whole clan _(actors: Pradyumna, the Vṛṣṇi-Andhaka Yādavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/pradyumna.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 11.30; Mahābhārata, Mausala Parva 3)
- **Death of Śalya by Yudhiṣṭhira's dart** — At midday Yudhiṣṭhira hurls a consecrated golden śakti that pierces Śalya's chest, killing the last Kaurava commander; his brother and son fall soon after _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Śalya, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shalya.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śalya Parva (Śalya-vadha))
- **Death of Drupada at Droṇa's hands** — On the fifteenth day Drupada is slain by his lifelong enemy Droṇa, just before Droṇa is beheaded by Drupada's son Dhṛṣṭadyumna _(actors: Drupada, Droṇa, Dhṛṣṭadyumna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drupada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva ~186)
- **Death of Kālayavana by Mucukunda's gaze** — Krishna lures the foreigner Kālayavana into the cave of the sleeping king Mucukunda, whose boon-given gaze incinerates him — the origin of Krishna's epithet Raṇchoḍ _(actors: Krishna, Kālayavana, Mucukunda)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/harivamsha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva)
- **Disclosure of the Vasus' curse and expiation of Bhishma's death** — Ulupi reveals the death was orchestrated so Arjuna would fall by his son's hand, expiating the Vasus' curse for slaying Bhishma unfairly behind Shikhandi _(actors: Ulūpī, Arjuna, Babhruvāhana, the Eight Vasus, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/babhruvahana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~81–82)
- **Disclosure of the Vasus' curse and reconciliation** — Ulūpī reveals she orchestrated the death to expiate the Vasus' and Gaṅgā's curse for Arjuna's unfair slaying of Bhīṣma; the family is reconciled and invited to the Hastināpura sacrifice _(actors: Ulūpī, Arjuna, Babhruvāhana, Citrāṅgadā, Bhīṣma (referenced), the Eight Vasus)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ulupi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~81–82)
- **Dissolution of the marriage and Hiḍimbī's return** — The compact fulfilled by the son's birth, Hiḍimbī takes Ghaṭotkaca to the rākṣasa realm to raise him while Bhīma rejoins the Pāṇḍavas _(actors: Hiḍimbī, Ghaṭotkaca, Bhīma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/hidimba-hidimbi.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva (~155))
- **Drona defeats Drupada via his pupils** — Drona demands as guru-dakshina that his pupils defeat Drupada; Arjuna captures him and Drona takes half his kingdom, sowing Drupada's hunger for a son to kill Drona _(actors: Drona, Drupada, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 128, 137-138)
- **Drona vs Drupada and the guru-dakshina war** — Humiliated by Drupada, Drona has his pupils capture him and takes half Panchala, sowing Drupada's hunger for a son to kill Drona _(actors: Drona, Drupada, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Durvasa's curse and the lost ring (Kalidasa's version)** — In Abhijnanashakuntalam, Durvasa curses the distracted Shakuntala that the king will forget her until he sees his signet ring, which she then loses in a river, leading to Dushyanta's blameless rejection _(actors: Shakuntala, Durvasa, Dushyanta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shakuntala-bharata.md) — (Kalidasa, Abhijnanashakuntalam, Act 4-5)
- **Ekalavya's thumb** — Drona trains the princes in archery and demands Ekalavya's thumb as guru-dakshina _(actors: Drona, Arjuna, Ekalavya, Kripa)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Escape of Aśvasena the serpent** — The Nāga Aśvasena, son of Takṣaka, escapes the fire by his mother's aid and Indra's wind, beginning his enmity with Arjuna _(actors: Aśvasena, Takṣaka, Arjuna, Indra)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/khandava-vana-and-the-burning.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi-parva 219; cf. Karṇa-parva)
- **Fall of Bhīṣma on the Bed of Arrows** — On day 10 Bhīṣma is felled by Arjuna (with Śikhaṇḍin before him) and lies on a bed of arrows at Bhīṣma-kund, withholding death until uttarāyaṇa _(actors: Bhīṣma, Arjuna, Śikhaṇḍin)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kurukshetra-dharmakshetra.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhishma-parva)
- **Fathering Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Pāṇḍu and Vidura by niyoga** — Summoned by Satyavatī, Vyāsa begets Dhṛtarāṣṭra (blind), Pāṇḍu (pale) and Vidura (by the maidservant) on Vicitravīrya's widows by levirate _(actors: Vyāsa, Satyavatī, Ambikā, Ambālikā, the maidservant)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Fulfillment of the companion thigh-vow on Duryodhana** — The twin vow's other half is discharged when Bhima breaks Duryodhana's bared thigh in the Shalya Parva, closing the oaths sworn over Draupadi's outrage _(actors: Bhīma, Duryodhana, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dushasana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śalya Parva ~58)
- **Goading against peace and the plot to seize Kṛṣṇa** — Śakuni stands with the war-party, hardens Duryodhana against reconciliation, and joins the scheme to arrest Kṛṣṇa during the peace embassy _(actors: Śakuni, Duryodhana, Karṇa, Duḥśāsana, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shakuni.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva ~129–130)
- **Śikhaṇḍin as instrument of Bhīṣma's fall on day 10** — Refusing to fight one born a woman, Bhīṣma lowers his weapons before Śikhaṇḍin; Arjuna, shielded behind him, fells Bhīṣma onto the bed of arrows, fulfilling Ambā's vow of vengeance _(actors: Bhīṣma, Śikhaṇḍin, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/amba-shikhandi.md) — (Mahābhārata Bhīṣma Parva ~108, 119–120)
- **Journey to Yama's realm** — The boy not found in the demon, Krishna and Balarama go to Yama's city Samyamani and Krishna blows the Panchajanya to summon him _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Yama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sandipani-pancajana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.45; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **Kali Possesses Nala and Rigs the Dice** — Catching a ritual impurity, Kali enters Nala's body while Dvāpara possesses the dice, and goads Puṣkara to challenge Nala to gambling _(actors: Kali, Dvāpara, Nala, Puṣkara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 56)
- **Killing of the elephant Kuvalayāpīḍa** — At the arena gate Krishna fells Kaṃsa's rogue war-elephant and its mahout, tearing out a tusk _(actors: Krishna, Balarāma, Kuvalayāpīḍa, the mahout)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.43; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Kāliya-Damana** — Kṛṣṇa subdues the many-hooded serpent Kāliya in the poisoned Yamunā pool and dances on his hoods _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Kāliya)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.16)
- **Kripa among the killers of Abhimanyu** — On day 13 Kripa is one of the warriors who surround and kill the disarmed Abhimanyu inside the Chakravyuha _(actors: Kripa, Drona, Karna, Ashvatthaman, Duryodhana, Kritavarman, Abhimanyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kripa.md) — (Mahabharata, Drona Parva ~48)
- **Krishna honored with the agra-puja at the Rajasuya** — At Yudhishthira's consecration Bhishma nominates Krishna for the foremost guest-honor and Sahadeva performs the arghya to him _(actors: Bhishma, Krishna, Sahadeva, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shishupala.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha-parva 35-36)
- **Krishna manifests the Vishvarupa in court** — When Duryodhana's faction moves to seize the unarmed envoy, Krishna laughs and reveals his cosmic universal form, terrifying the court; the embassy formally fails _(actors: Krishna, Duryodhana, Dhritarashtra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva, Bhagavad-yana)
- **Krishna's curse on Ashvatthaman** — The head-gem is cut from Ashvatthaman's forehead leaving an unhealing wound, and Krishna curses him to wander the earth alone for 3,000 years, friendless and diseased, an immortal misery _(actors: Krishna, Ashvatthaman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/10-sauptika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sauptika Parva 16 (Aishika))
- **Krishna's Worship-Image Survives Dwaraka's Flood** — Krishna foretells that his worship-image will float free when the sea engulfs Dwaraka at the Dvapara-Kali hinge; Guru (Brhaspati) and Vayu recover it and install it at Guruvayur with Parashurama's aid _(actors: Krishna, Uddhava, Brhaspati, Vayu, Parashurama)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sthala-puranas-major-temples.md) — (Guruvayur sthala-purana)
- **Maya builds the illusion-hall of Indraprastha** — The asura-architect Maya Dānava designs the wondrous illusion-hall (Māyā-sabhā) for Yudhiṣṭhira at the Pāṇḍavas' capital Indraprastha _(actors: Maya Dānava, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/vishvakarma-and-tvashtar.md) — (Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva)
- **Mādrī entrusts the twins to Kuntī** — Mādrī commits Nakula and Sahadeva to Kuntī's care, asking she raise all five boys impartially, making Kuntī mother of five though she bore three _(actors: Mādrī, Kuntī, Nakula, Sahadeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/madri.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Medhāvin Urges His Father to Renounce** — The precocious son Medhāvin urges his complacent father to renounce now since death does not wait, reversing the expected hierarchy _(actors: Medhāvin, his father)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mokshadharma-dialogues.md) — (Mahabharata Shanti Parva 169/175)
- **Muchukunda's awakening, hymn, and departure to penance** — Waking in the Dvapara, Muchukunda recognizes Krishna, offers the Muchukunda-stuti, and departs north to Gandhamadana/Badarikashrama for austerities _(actors: Muchukunda, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/muchukunda-and-kalayavana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.51.24-63; Harivamsha)
- **Mudgala seeks mokṣa beyond heaven and rebirth** — Renouncing his livelihood and all desire, Mudgala fixes on the changeless supreme state, attaining the highest perfection (mokṣa) from which there is no return _(actors: Mudgala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Mārkaṇḍeya-samāsya (Mudgala-Upākhyāna))
- **Narakāsura slain by Kṛṣṇa** — The tyrant Narakāsura, who imprisoned 16,000 women, is slain by Kṛṣṇa with Satyabhāmā, commemorated as Naraka Caturdaśī (Diwali) _(actors: Narakāsura, Kṛṣṇa, Satyabhāmā)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/asura-wars-and-roster.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.59)
- **Pandavas Pursue Shiva and Kedarnath Hump Forms** — Seeking absolution for kin-slaying after the Mahabharata war, the Pandavas pursue Shiva, who flees as a bull and dives into the ground leaving his hump at Kedarnath, founding the jyotirlinga _(actors: Pandavas, Shiva)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/12-jyotirlingas.md) — (Skanda Purana (Kedara Khanda); Mahabharata-linked tradition)
- **Pradyumna routs Skanda** — Pradyumna wounds Skanda (Kārttikeya), who flees the field bleeding, while Balarāma scatters Bāṇa's ministers _(actors: Pradyumna, Skanda, Balarāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/banasura-usha-aniruddha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.63)
- **Regent during the elders' forest-retreat** — When Dhritarashtra, Gandhari, Kunti, and Vidura withdraw to the forest, Yuyutsu is left to look after Hastinapura as trusted caretaker-regent _(actors: Yuyutsu, Dhritarashtra, Gandhari, Kunti, Vidura, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yuyutsu.md) — (Mahabharata, Ashramavasika Parva)
- **Satyaki beheads the meditating Bhurishravas** — Arjuna shoots off Bhurishravas's sword-arm to save Satyaki; Bhurishravas sits in praya, and Satyaki, against all protests, beheads the one-armed meditating man, a flagrant Pandava breach of the war-code _(actors: Bhurishravas, Satyaki, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva 142-143)
- **Seizure of the Pārijāta and defeat of Indra** — Kṛṣṇa returns Aditi's earrings to Svarga, uproots the celestial Pārijāta tree for Satyabhāmā, and defeats Indra and the Devas who resist _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Satyabhāmā, Aditi, Indra, Garuḍa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/narakasura-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.59; Harivaṃśa)
- **Slaying of Trinavarta** — The whirlwind-demon carries Krishna aloft, but the child grows heavy and strangles him _(actors: Krishna, Trinavarta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.7)
- **Sparing of the eighth son and Gaṅgā's departure** — Śāntanu breaks his vow to save the eighth child (Dyaus, the future Bhīṣma); Gaṅgā explains the curse, names him Devavrata, and departs with him _(actors: Śāntanu, Gaṅgā, Devavrata/Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~99)
- **Subhadrā's grief at Abhimanyu's death** — After Abhimanyu is killed on the 13th day, Subhadrā laments alongside the widowed Uttarā _(actors: Subhadrā, Uttarā, Abhimanyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subhadra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa/Strī Parvas)
- **Substituted maid; birth of Vidura** — Asked to approach Vyāsa again, Ambikā sends her handmaid in her place; the unafraid maid is blessed with the wisest son, Vidura, the incarnation of Dharma _(actors: Ambikā, the handmaid (later named Pariśramī), Vyāsa, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ambika-ambalika.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~106)
- **The Brahmana's cow-curse on Karna** — Karna accidentally kills a brahmana's cow and is cursed that his chariot-wheel will sink into the earth when he most needs to be steady in battle _(actors: Karna, a Brahmana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva back-reference)
- **The Chandala-amrita test and the Uttanka-megha clouds** — Uttanka refuses water (secretly amrita) offered by Indra disguised as a Chandala, failing the test of equanimity; Krishna then grants that desert rain-clouds, the Uttanka-megha, will rise at his wish _(actors: Uttanka, Indra, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahabharata Ashvamedhika Parva 14.54)
- **The disrobing of Draupadi** — Duhshasana drags Draupadi into the assembly and attempts to strip her, but endless garments appear so she cannot be disrobed; she poses her unanswered question on dharma _(actors: Draupadi, Duhshasana, Krishna, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva 60-61)
- **The divine-eye threnody** — With a boon of divine sight Gāndhārī surveys the corpse-strewn field and laments the slain corpse by corpse to Kṛṣṇa _(actors: Gāndhārī, Kṛṣṇa, the slain warriors)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/gandhari.md) — (Mahābhārata, Strī Parva XI.16-25)
- **The falling-head boon recoils on Vṛddhakṣatra** — Arjuna shoots Jayadratha's severed head into his meditating father's lap; it rolls to the ground and Vṛddhakṣatra's own head bursts _(actors: Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa, Vṛddhakṣatra, Jayadratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jayadratha.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva 146)
- **The Ghoṣa-yātrā — capture and rescue of Duryodhana** — Duryodhana's cattle-expedition to Dvaitavana ends with the gandharva Citrasena capturing him; Arjuna and Bhīma rescue their enemy on Yudhiṣṭhira's order _(actors: Duryodhana, Karṇa, Śakuni, Citrasena, Arjuna, Bhīma, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Ghoṣa-yātrā Parva ~239–256)
- **The homeward reflection** — On the road back Sudāmā marvels at the Lord's love and concludes Kṛṣṇa withheld wealth lest prosperity make him forget God _(actors: Sudāmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.81.1–10)
- **The hovel transformed into a palace** — On returning, Sudama finds his hut turned into a resplendent jewelled palace and his wife radiant, Krishna having silently granted untold wealth unasked _(actors: Sudama, his wife, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.81.1-20)
- **The Kaurava cattle-raid (Go-grahana)** — The Kauravas strike undefended Matsya from the north to rustle the cattle and flush out the Pandavas, forcing the disguised Arjuna to come to its defense as charioteer to prince Uttara _(actors: Duryodhana, Bhishma, Drona, Karna, Arjuna, Uttara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/04-virata-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Virata Parva 34-37)
- **The Nagastra deflected by Krishna** — Karna looses a serpent-arrow animated by the snake Ashvasena; Krishna presses the chariot down with his foot so it strikes Arjuna's diadem instead of his head, and Karna refuses to fire it twice _(actors: Karna, Krishna, Arjuna, Ashvasena)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/08-karna-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva 90)
- **The return and consolation** — Kṛṣṇa reappears, explains he withdrew to deepen their love, and confesses he can never repay it (na pāraye 'ham) _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, the gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rasa-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.32)
- **Vasiṣṭha restores Saṃvaraṇa to the throne** — Returning as purohita, Vasiṣṭha re-consecrates Saṃvaraṇa, who recovers Hastināpura and the kingship from the Pāñcālas _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Saṃvaraṇa)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/samvarana-tapati.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva, Tāpatya upākhyāna ~173)
- **Vasudeva's death and the funerals at Dwarka** — Krishna's father Vasudeva gives up his life in yoga; Arjuna performs his funeral and the last rites for Balarama, Krishna and the slain Yadavas, and four of Vasudeva's wives and some of Krishna's and Balarama's wives mount the pyre as sati _(actors: Vasudeva, Arjuna, Devaki, Rohini, Bhadra, Madira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/16-mausala-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 16.6-7)
- **Vasu's sons seed the Cedi and Magadha dynasties** — Vasu's sons found regional kingdoms; Bṛhadratha establishes the Magadha (Bārhadratha) line leading to Jarāsandha, while another holds Cedi _(actors: Vasu Uparicara, Bṛhadratha, his other sons)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/uparichara-vasu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva (~63); Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.19)
- **Vicitravīrya's death of consumption, childless** — After seven years of marriage Vicitravīrya dies young of consumption (rāja-yakṣma), leaving both queens widowed and heirless and the Kuru line at the brink of extinction _(actors: Vicitravīrya, Ambikā, Ambālikā, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vichitravirya-and-chitrangada.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~103; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.20; Bhāgavata Purāṇa IX.22.21)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira as Kaṅka at Virāṭa's court** — In the incognito year Yudhiṣṭhira serves King Virāṭa as the brāhmaṇa dice-master Kaṅka, surviving undetected through the term _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Virāṭa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yudhishthira.md) — (Mahābhārata, Virāṭa Parva)
- **Annihilation of Drupada's line in the night-raid** — Aśvatthāman massacres the sleeping Pāñcālas, including Dhṛṣṭadyumna and Śikhaṇḍin, ending Drupada's house and his vengeance _(actors: Aśvatthāman, Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Śikhaṇḍin)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drupada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sauptika Parva ~8)
- **Arjuna defeats Meghasandhi of Magadha** — Meghasandhi, grandson of Jarāsandha, challenges Arjuna at Rājagṛha and is defeated, then submits _(actors: Arjuna, Meghasandhi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.82-83)
- **Arjuna retrieves his weapons and reveals himself** — Arjuna drives to the shami tree, recovers the hidden arms, strings the Gandiva and blows the conch Devadatta, then discloses his true identity to the astonished prince Uttara _(actors: Arjuna, Uttara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/04-virata-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Virata Parva 38-42)
- **Arjuna's Arrow Springs Bāṇgaṅgā for Bhīṣma** — Arjuna shoots an arrow into the earth at Narkatari and a jet of Gaṅgā-water springs up to slake the fallen Bhīṣma's thirst, naming the Bāṇgaṅgā _(actors: Arjuna, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kurukshetra-dharmakshetra.md) — (local tradition; Mahabharata)
- **Arjuna spares Maya Dānava** — The asura architect Maya flees Kṛṣṇa's discus to Arjuna, who grants him safety, sparing him from fire and Kṛṣṇa _(actors: Maya Dānava, Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/khandava-vana-and-the-burning.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi-parva 219)
- **Arjuna Weds Citrangada** — Arjuna marries the warrior-princess Citrangada of Manipura on terms that their son Babhruvahana inherits her matrilineal throne _(actors: Arjuna, Citrangada, Babhruvahana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~207-208)
- **Ashvatthaman's Brahmastra and Krishna's Revival of Parikshit** — Ashvatthaman directs the Brahmastra at Uttara's womb to end the line; the child is stillborn and Krishna revives him as Parikshit _(actors: Ashvatthaman, Uttara, Krishna, Parikshit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/abhimanyu.md) — (Mahabharata, Sauptika Parva; Bhagavata Purana 1.8)
- **Ashvatthaman's Brahmastra on Uttaraa's womb** — After the war Ashvatthaman aims the Brahmastra/Aishika weapon at Uttaraa's womb to extinguish the Kuru line, striking the unborn heir dead _(actors: Ashvatthaman, Uttaraa, the unborn Parikshit, Krishna, Vyasa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/virata-and-his-children.md) — (Mahabharata, Sauptika Parva; Bhagavata Purana 1.7)
- **Bhima Meets Hanuman in the Kadalivana** — On the Saugandhika lotus-quest Bhima cannot lift the tail of an old monkey, recognizes his half-brother Hanuman, and is blessed with added strength _(actors: Bhima, Hanuman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhima.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva ~147-150)
- **Bhishma Reveals the Secret of His Death** — Stalemated, the Pandavas come by night to Bhishma, who reveals he will lay down arms before Shikhandin, consenting to his own fall _(actors: Bhishma, the Pandavas, Shikhandin)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhishma.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhishma Parva ~119)
- **Bhishma reveals the secret of his own death** — At night the Pandavas ask the invincible Bhishma how to kill him; he reveals he will not raise a weapon against Shikhandin (born a woman), telling them to set Shikhandin before him _(actors: Bhishma, Pandavas, Krishna, Shikhandin)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva 107)
- **Birth of Arjuna by Indra** — Kuntī invokes Indra, king of the devas, and bears the supreme archer Arjuna _(actors: Kuntī, Indra, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Birth of Karṇa by Sūrya's boon** — Kuntī, using a boon-mantra before her marriage, conceives Karṇa by Sūrya, born with divine armour and earrings (kavaca-kuṇḍala) _(actors: Kuntī, Sūrya, Karṇa)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/surya-and-his-family.md) — (Mahābhārata)
- **Birth of Kuru — the solar graft onto the lunar line** — Saṃvaraṇa and Tapatī beget Kuru, eponym of the Kuru clan and Kurukṣetra, grafting Sūrya's solar blood onto the Lunar Pauravā line _(actors: Saṃvaraṇa, Tapatī, Kuru)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/samvarana-tapati.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva, Caitraratha- & Sambhava-parvans; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.19)
- **Birth of Śuka** — Vyāsa fathers the supreme renunciate Śukadeva by his austerity (variant: of the apsarā Ghṛtācī), who later recites the Bhāgavata to Parīkṣit _(actors: Vyāsa, Śuka, Ghṛtācī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vyasa.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 1.4; Devī Bhāgavata)
- **Curse on Kṛṣṇa setting the 36-year clock** — Gāndhārī curses Kṛṣṇa that in 36 years his Yādava kin will destroy each other and he will die alone; Kṛṣṇa accepts _(actors: Gāndhārī, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/gandhari.md) — (Mahābhārata, Strī Parva XI.25)
- **Death in the Sauptika night-raid** — Found asleep, Dhṛṣṭadyumna is trampled and strangled to death by Aśvatthāman, denied an honourable weapon-death in revenge for Droṇa's beheading _(actors: Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Aśvatthāman, Kṛpa, Kṛtavarman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dhrishtadyumna.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sauptika Parva)
- **Duel of the two fevers (Jvara)** — Bāṇa's three-headed Māheśvara-jvara scorches the Yādavas; Kṛṣṇa's Vaiṣṇava-jvara defeats it, and the praising Jvara is released _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Jvara (fever-demon), Bāṇāsura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/banasura-usha-aniruddha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.63; Harivaṃśa Viṣṇu Parva)
- **Duryodhana born of a portion of Kali** — The Aṃśāvataraṇa lists Duryodhana as born of a portion of the asura Kali, spirit of the dark age, framing the war as a clash of incarnate cosmic forces _(actors: Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~61)
- **Dwarka swallowed by the sea** — As Arjuna leads out the last surviving Yadava women, children and elders, the sea rises and submerges Dwarka behind them, reclaiming every part except the spot where Krishna had dwelt _(actors: Arjuna, the sea, surviving Yadavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/16-mausala-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 16.7; Vishnu Purana 5.38)
- **Ekalavya's thumb demanded** — The self-taught Nishada prince surpasses Arjuna; Drona demands his right thumb as dakshina to preserve Arjuna's supremacy _(actors: Ekalavya, Drona, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 123)
- **Eternal Rāsa-līlā in Goloka and on earth** — The Rāsa-līlā is presented first as an eternal event in Goloka between Kṛṣṇa and Rādhā, then re-enacted on earth at Vṛndāvana in the Dvāpara Yuga _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Rādhā, gopīs)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/brahmavaivarta-purana.md) — (Brahmavaivarta Purāṇa (Kṛṣṇa-janma-khaṇḍa))
- **Jara's arrow at Bhalka Tirtha** — The hunter Jara, mistaking Krishna's raised foot for a deer, pierces it with the last pestle-fragment arrow at Bhalka Tirtha _(actors: Krishna, Jara (the hunter))_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/prabhasa-somnath-tirtha.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva 4; Bhagavata Purana 11.30; Vishnu Purana 5.37)
- **Jayadratha's abduction of Draupadī** — Jayadratha of Sindhu carries off Draupadī from the hermitage; the brothers defeat him and shave his head leaving five tufts, sparing his life _(actors: Jayadratha, Draupadī, Bhīma, Arjuna)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Draupadī-haraṇa / Jayadratha Parva ~248–256)
- **Karna spends the Vasavi Shakti on Ghatotkaca** — In the night battle Ghatotkaca devastates the Kauravas until Karna is forced to hurl the one-use Vasavi Shakti (hoarded for Arjuna), killing him instantly; Krishna rejoices that the only weapon certain to kill Arjuna is now spent _(actors: Karna, Ghatotkaca, Krishna, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva 153-182 (Ghatotkaca-vadha))
- **Kripa one of the last three Kaurava survivors** — After Duryodhana falls, only Ashvatthaman, Kripa, and Kritavarman remain alive of the Kaurava host _(actors: Kripa, Ashvatthaman, Kritavarman, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kripa.md) — (Mahabharata, Sauptika Parva (Book 10))
- **Krishna persuades Vraja to worship Govardhana** — The boy Krishna argues that Mount Govardhana and the cows sustain the cowherds, not Indra, and persuades them to worship the hill instead, interrupting the Indra-puja _(actors: Krishna, the cowherds, Indra)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/indra.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.24-25)
- **Krishna reveals Karna's parentage** — Leaving Hastinapura, Krishna takes Karna aside and reveals he is Kunti's firstborn by Surya and the rightful heir, offering him kingship and Draupadi if he joins his brothers; Karna refuses out of loyalty to Duryodhana _(actors: Krishna, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva 138ff (Bhagavad-yana))
- **Krishna Shot by the Hunter Jara and His Ascension** — The hunter Jara mistakes Krishna's raised foot for a deer and shoots him in the sole with the iron-bolt fish-nub; Krishna consoles Jara, casts off his body and ascends, ending the Yuga _(actors: Krishna, Jara)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dwarka-bet-dwarka-saurashtra.md) — (Mahabharata 16.4; Bhagavata 11.30; Vishnu Purana 5.37)
- **Liberation of Nalakubara and Manigriva (Yamalarjuna)** — Cursed by Narada to become two arjuna trees, Kubera's sons Nalakubara and Manigriva are liberated when the infant Krishna, tied to a mortar, drags it between them and uproots them _(actors: Krishna, Nalakubara, Manigriva, Narada)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/kubera.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.9-10.10)
- **Marriage to Jāmbavatī** — Jāmbavān gives his daughter Jāmbavatī to Kṛṣṇa; she becomes a chief queen and mother of Sāmba _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Jāmbavatī, Jāmbavān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13)
- **Mādrī's sati on Pāṇḍu's pyre** — Winning the contest of devotion against Kuntī, Mādrī immolates herself on Pāṇḍu's funeral pyre; ascetics then escort Kuntī and the five boys to Hāstinapura _(actors: Mādrī, Pāṇḍu, Kuntī, the five Pāṇḍavas, forest ascetics)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/madri.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Mothers of the warring generation** — Through Dhṛtarāṣṭra come the hundred Kauravas and through Pāṇḍu come the five Pāṇḍavas, making Ambikā and Ambālikā the grandmothers of both warring sides _(actors: Ambikā, Ambālikā, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Pāṇḍu, the Kauravas, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ambika-ambalika.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva ~106–110)
- **Mārkaṇḍeya narrates the tale to the exiled Yudhiṣṭhira** — Within the Pāṇḍavas' forest exile, the sage Mārkaṇḍeya recites Mudgala's story to console Yudhiṣṭhira, teaching contentment, forbearance, and the renunciation of even heaven _(actors: Mārkaṇḍeya, Yudhiṣṭhira, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/mudgala.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Mārkaṇḍeya-samāsya (frame))
- **Mudgala Declines Heaven** — The gleaner-sage Mudgala feeds the testing Durvāsā without anger and, offered bodily ascent to heaven, declines it on learning svarga ends, choosing mokṣa _(actors: Mudgala, Durvāsā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/minor-upakhyanas-catalog.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 246-247)
- **Nala Gambles Away His Kingdom** — Maddened, Nala loses gold, chariots and his entire kingdom to Puṣkara; he and Damayantī are stripped and exiled with one garment each _(actors: Nala, Puṣkara, Damayantī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 57-58)
- **Nārada's embassy in the Sabhā Parva** — Nārada visits Yudhiṣṭhira's new assembly-hall, describes the celestial sabhās, and prompts the Rājasūya sacrifice by relaying Pāṇḍu's wish, setting the imperial-rivalry chain in motion _(actors: Nārada, Yudhiṣṭhira, (the late) Pāṇḍu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/narada.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā Parva)
- **Prophecy against Kamsa** — A voice warns Kamsa that Devaki's 8th child will kill him; he imprisons the couple and slays their first six infants _(actors: Kamsa, Devaki, Vasudeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.1-10.4)
- **Reconciliation and Babhruvahana's matrilineal succession** — Arjuna is reconciled with his son; Babhruvahana remains king of Manipura as matrilineal heir while the Kuru line passes through Parikshit _(actors: Arjuna, Babhruvāhana, Citrāṅgadā, Ulūpī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/babhruvahana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśvamedhika Parva ~82)
- **Relocation of the Cowherds to Vṛndāvana** — Troubled by demons and ill omens at Gokula, Nanda relocates the whole cowherd community by bullock-cart to Vṛndāvana _(actors: Nanda, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.11)
- **Restoration of Sandipani's son** — Yama yields the boy's soul; Krishna recovers the living son and presents him to the overjoyed Sandipani, who blesses them _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Yama, Sandipani's son, Sandipani)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sandipani-pancajana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.45; Harivamsha; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **Return of the grown prince Devavrata** — Gaṅgā restores the grown, fully-trained Devavrata to Śāntanu, who installs him as crown prince _(actors: Gaṅgā, Śāntanu, Devavrata/Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~99–100)
- **Satyavatī becomes the genealogical hinge of the Kuru line** — Vasu's daughter Satyavatī weds Śāntanu and, via Vyāsa, becomes ancestress of the Kauravas and Pāṇḍavas, making Vasu the upstream root of the Kuru tragedy _(actors: Satyavatī, Śāntanu, Parāśara, Vyāsa, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/uparichara-vasu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva (~63, 100–106))
- **Saudasa-Kalmashapada spares Uttanka (Ashvamedhika variant)** — To reach Queen Madayanti, Uttanka confronts her man-eating husband Saudasa cursed into cannibalism by Vasishtha, pledges to return to be eaten, and is let go _(actors: Uttanka, Saudasa, Madayanti)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahabharata Ashvamedhika Parva 14.53-54)
- **Shishupala's outburst and insults** — Shishupala rejects honoring Krishna as a mere cowherd, abuses Bhishma, and rallies kings to walk out, exhausting Krishna's hundred pardons _(actors: Shishupala, Krishna, Bhishma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shishupala.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha-parva 37-40)
- **Slaying of Kirmira at the forest threshold** — Bhima kills the rakshasa Kirmira haunting the edge of the Dvaita/Kamyaka forest as the exiled Pandavas enter _(actors: Bhima, Kirmira)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva (Kirmira-vadha ~11))
- **Swallowing the forest fire (davāgni)** — Krishna saves the cowherds and cattle trapped by a wildfire near the purified river by swallowing the blaze _(actors: Krishna, cowherds, cattle)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kaliya-mardana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.17)
- **The Ashta-Vasu steal Vasishtha's cow** — The eight Vasus steal sage Vasishtha's wish-cow Nandini, and he curses them to be born as mortals _(actors: the Ashta-Vasu, Vasishtha)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganga.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva 93-99)
- **The Brahma-stuti — Brahma's hymn of surrender** — Brahma offers the celebrated hymn of 10.14, confessing bhakti over jnana (jnane prayasam udapasya) and the inconceivability of Bhagavan, and begs forgiveness for his test _(actors: Brahma, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/brahma-vimohana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.14 (esp. 10.14.3, 4, 21, 30-34))
- **The complete life of Krishna** — Krishna is born at Mathura to Devaki and Vasudeva, performs the bala- and gopa-lila (Putana, Kaliya, Govardhana, rasa), slays Kamsa, founds Dvaraka, marries, and plays his Mahabharata-era role _(actors: Krishna, Devaki, Vasudeva, Kamsa, Rukmini)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/vishnu-purana.md) — (Vishnu Purana (Amsha 5); cf. Harivamsha, Bhagavata 10-11)
- **The death of Karna** — As Karna's chariot-wheel sinks (Earth-goddess's curse) and the Brahmastra incantation fails him (Parashurama's curse), Krishna rejects his appeal to dharma and Arjuna severs his head with the Anjalika-astra _(actors: Karna, Arjuna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/08-karna-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva 91)
- **The eight principal queens (Aṣṭa-bhāryā) of Dwārakā** — Consolidated account of Kṛṣṇa's eight chief wives — Rukmiṇī, Satyabhāmā, Jāmbavatī, Kālindī, Mitravindā, Satyā/Nāgnajitī, Bhadrā, Lakṣmaṇā — each won by a distinct episode _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Rukmiṇī, Satyabhāmā, Jāmbavatī, Kālindī, Mitravindā, Satyā/Nāgnajitī, Bhadrā, Lakṣmaṇā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rukmini-harana.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56-59, 10.83; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **The graduation tournament and Karna crowned king of Anga** — Karna matches Arjuna but is mocked as a charioteer's son; Duryodhana crowns him king of Anga, forging their lifelong alliance _(actors: Karna, Arjuna, Duryodhana, Kripa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **The graduation tournament and Karna's challenge** — At the Kuru princes' martial display Karna matches every feat of Arjuna and challenges him, but is humiliated as a Sutaputra when his lineage is demanded _(actors: Karna, Arjuna, Kripa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva ~135-136)
- **The Kuru line preserved through Parīkṣit** — Abhimanyu's posthumous son Parīkṣit, saved by Kṛṣṇa, survives as sole Kuru heir, making Subhadrā the genealogical bridge of the dynasty _(actors: Subhadrā, Parīkṣit, Uttarā, Kṛṣṇa, Aśvatthāman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subhadra.md) — (Mahābhārata; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 1.8–1.12)
- **The leap from Pravarshana hill and founding of the Yadava capital** — Pursued again by Jarasandha, Krishna and Balarama leap from the burning Pravarshana mountain and reach Dwaraka, which becomes the Yadava capital and a sacred tirtha _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Jarasandha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/muchukunda-and-kalayavana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.52.1-13; Vishnu Purana V.23)
- **The mahā-rāsa and the multiplication** — The great circle-dance: Kṛṣṇa multiplies himself between each pair of gopīs so each believes he dances at her side alone, the summit of mādhurya-bhakti _(actors: Kṛṣṇa (multiplied), the gopīs, the watching gods)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rasa-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.33)
- **The niyoga of Vyāsa producing the war generation** — Triggered by Vicitravīrya's childlessness and Bhīṣma's refusal, Satyavatī summons Vyāsa, whose niyoga on the widows produces Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Pāṇḍu, and Vidura, all reckoned as Vicitravīrya's line _(actors: Satyavatī, Bhīṣma, Vyāsa, Ambikā, Ambālikā, the handmaid, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Pāṇḍu, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vichitravirya-and-chitrangada.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~103–106; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.20; Bhāgavata Purāṇa IX.22.22)
- **The peace embassy fails over five villages** — Yudhiṣṭhira asks for a mere five villages to avert war, but Duryodhana refuses even a needlepoint of land, making the Kurukṣetra war unavoidable _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yudhishthira.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva)
- **The ring-in-the-fish recognition and heavenly reunion (Kalidasa)** — A fisherman finds the ring inside a fish, restoring Dushyanta's memory; aiding the gods, he later finds Bharata taming a lion cub at Marica's celestial hermitage and is reunited with Shakuntala _(actors: Dushyanta, the fisherman, Shakuntala, Bharata, Marica)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shakuntala-bharata.md) — (Kalidasa, Abhijnanashakuntalam, Act 6-7)
- **The Sage and the Worm (Kīṭa-saṃvāda)** — A sage tells a terrified worm crossing a road that even its humble life is precious and can climb the chain of rebirths toward liberation _(actors: a sage, a worm)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mokshadharma-dialogues.md) — (Mahabharata Shanti Parva (Mokshadharma))
- **The Syamantaka jewel and Krishna's marriages** — Accused of stealing the sun-gem Syamantaka, Krishna tracks it through Prasena's death and wrests it from the bear Jāmbavān, winning Jāmbavatī, clearing his name, and marrying Satyabhāmā _(actors: Krishna, Satrājit, Jāmbavān, Jāmbavatī, Satyabhāmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/harivamsha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva)
- **The terrible vows in the hall** — Bhima vows to break Duryodhana's thigh and drink Duhshasana's blood; Draupadi vows to leave her hair unbound until washed in Duhshasana's blood _(actors: Bhima, Draupadi, Duryodhana, Duhshasana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva 61-63)
- **The transformed palace — untold wealth granted unasked** — Sudāmā finds his hovel turned into a jewelled palace and his wife radiant, Kṛṣṇa having silently bestowed everything without a word _(actors: Sudāmā, Sudāmā's wife, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.81.11–20)
- **Ulūka's insulting embassy** — Śakuni's son Ulūka carries Duryodhana's provocative message to the Pāṇḍava camp on the eve of war _(actors: Ulūka, Śakuni, Duryodhana, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shakuni.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva)
- **Vyasa grants Sanjaya divine sight** — Vyasa bestows divya-cakshus (divine sight) on Sanjaya so he can relay the distant Kurukshetra battlefield to the blind Dhritarashtra, framing the whole Gita as a vision-within-a-recitation _(actors: Vyasa, Sanjaya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/01-arjuna-vishada-yoga.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhishma Parva)
- **Wealth without attachment and the supreme abode** — Sudama enjoys the bounty without pride, ascribes all to Krishna, and through unbroken devotion attains the supreme abode; Shuka gives the moral to Parikshit _(actors: Sudama, Krishna, Shukadeva, Parikshit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.81.21-41)
- **Wrestling deaths of Cāṇūra and Muṣṭika** — Krishna kills the champion wrestler Cāṇūra and Balarāma kills Muṣṭika, with other wrestlers dispatched _(actors: Krishna, Balarāma, Cāṇūra, Muṣṭika, Kūṭa, Śala, Tośala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.44; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Arjuna Abducts Subhadra** — On Krishna's counsel Arjuna abducts Subhadra from the Raivataka festival; she bears Abhimanyu, through whose son the Kuru line survives _(actors: Arjuna, Subhadra, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~218-223)
- **Arjuna and the Kirata; winning the Pashupatastra** — Arjuna's austerities win a duel with Shiva disguised as a mountain-hunter, earning the Pashupatastra _(actors: Arjuna, Shiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata-minor-episodes.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva, Kairata Parva)
- **Arjuna single-handedly defeats the Kuru host** — Alone, Arjuna defeats Karna, Kripa, Drona, Ashvatthaman, Bhishma and Duryodhana in succession, finally invoking the Sammohana-astra to swoon the entire Kuru army _(actors: Arjuna, Karna, Bhishma, Drona, Kripa, Ashvatthaman, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/04-virata-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Virata Parva 47-61)
- **Babhruvāhana slays Arjuna in battle** — At Maṇipura, Arjuna rebukes his peaceful son Babhruvāhana as unmanly; goaded by the Nāga princess Ulūpī, Babhruvāhana fights and kills Arjuna with his arrows _(actors: Babhruvāhana, Arjuna, Ulūpī, Citrāṅgadā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.79-81)
- **Bhima's Two Great Vows at the Dice-Hall** — After Draupadi's disrobing and Duryodhana's bared-thigh taunt, Bhima vows to drink Duhshasana's blood and to shatter Duryodhana's thigh _(actors: Bhima, Duhshasana, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhima.md) — (Mahabharata, Sabha Parva ~68)
- **Bhishma Falls on the Bed of Arrows** — On the tenth day Bhishma lowers his weapons before Shikhandin and Arjuna pierces him until he topples onto the sharashayya, choosing to await the uttarayana _(actors: Bhishma, Arjuna, Shikhandin)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhishma.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhishma Parva ~119-120)
- **Bhīṣma recites the Viṣṇu Sahasranāma** — Asked the supreme refuge and easiest path to liberation, Bhīṣma answers that meditating on Viṣṇu/Vāsudeva is best and recites the thousand names of Viṣṇu, the key Vaiṣṇava stotra _(actors: Bhīṣma, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/13-anushasana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 13.135/149)
- **Birth of Ghaṭotkaca to Bhīma and Hiḍimbā** — After Bhīma slays the rākṣasa Hiḍimba and weds his sister Hiḍimbā in the forest, she bears Ghaṭotkaca the same day by rākṣasa speed, born bald with a pot-shaped head _(actors: Bhīma, Hiḍimbā, Ghaṭotkaca)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ghatotkacha.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva, Hiḍimba-vadha Parva)
- **Citralekhā abducts Aniruddha** — The apsarā Citralekhā, skilled in magic and friend of Ūṣā, abducts Aniruddha by her arts in the Ūṣā-Aniruddha episode _(actors: Citralekhā, Ūṣā, Aniruddha)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/apsaras.md) — (Bhāgavata / Harivaṃśa)
- **Conception of Droṇa in a pot** — The sage Bharadvāja's seed escapes at the sight of the apsarā Ghṛtācī and is caught in a wooden trough, from which the ayonija boy Droṇa develops, named for the vessel of his birth _(actors: Bharadvāja, Ghṛtācī, Droṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~130)
- **Damodara and the liberation of the Yakshas** — Bound to a mortar by Yashoda, Krishna drags it between two arjuna trees, freeing the cursed Yakshas Nalakubara and Manigriva _(actors: Krishna, Yashoda, Nalakubara, Manigriva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.9-10)
- **Day 10 — the fall of Bhishma on the bed of arrows** — With Shikhandin screening him, Arjuna pierces Bhishma over his whole body; he falls but is held off the ground by the arrows themselves, the celebrated shara-shayya _(actors: Arjuna, Shikhandin, Bhishma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva 114-120)
- **Death of Śakuni at Sahadeva's hands** — On Day 18 Sahadeva beheads Śakuni Saubala after an arrow-duel, fulfilling his dice-hall vow; Śakuni's son Ulūka falls in the same engagement _(actors: Śakuni, Sahadeva, Ulūka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shakuni.md) — (Mahābhārata, Śalya Parva (Śalya-vadha P. ~27))
- **Dharmavyādha Teaches Kauśika (Vyādha-Gītā)** — The arrogant ascetic Kauśika, humbled by a butcher of Mithilā, receives the Vyādha-Gītā teaching that dharma lies in selfless svadharma over ritual purity _(actors: Kauśika, Dharmavyādha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/minor-upakhyanas-catalog.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 205-215)
- **Dhritarashtra's opening question to Sanjaya** — On the field of dharma at Kurukshetra, the blind king Dhritarashtra asks his bard Sanjaya what his sons and the Pandavas did, the single question that launches the entire Gita _(actors: Dhritarashtra, Sanjaya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/01-arjuna-vishada-yoga.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 1.1)
- **Draupadi's boons free the Pandavas** — Frightened by ill omens, Dhritarashtra grants Draupadi boons that free Yudhishthira and her other husbands and restore their kingdom _(actors: Dhritarashtra, Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva 63-64)
- **Drupada's vengeance-sacrifice for a son** — Humiliated and stripped of half his kingdom by Droṇa via Arjuna, King Drupada commissions the brahmins Yāja and Upayāja to perform a great fire-rite to obtain a son who will kill Droṇa _(actors: Drupada, Yāja, Upayāja)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Duryodhana crowns Karna King of Anga** — Duryodhana crowns Karna King of Anga on the spot so he may duel Arjuna, binding Karna to him in lifelong gratitude _(actors: Duryodhana, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva ~136)
- **Forest retirement of the elders** — The aged Kuru elders withdraw to the forest in vānaprastha; the matriarchs' deaths are folded into this general account and not individually dwelt upon _(actors: Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Gāndhārī, Kuntī, Vidura, Ambālikā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ambika-ambalika.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśramavāsika Parva ~1–37)
- **Gandhari's vow of blindfolding** — Gandhari blindfolds herself for life to share her blind husband's condition _(actors: Gandhari, Dhritarashtra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 109-110)
- **Ganga marries Shantanu on a condition** — Ganga agrees to bear the cursed Vasus and marries King Shantanu only on condition that he never question any act of hers _(actors: Ganga, Shantanu, the Vasus)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganga.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva 96-98)
- **Gāndhārī's flesh-ball and the hundred-and-one pots** — After a two-year pregnancy Gāndhārī strikes her womb and expels a lifeless ball of flesh; Vyāsa divides it into a hundred and one thumb-sized pieces sealed in ghee-pots to incubate _(actors: Gāndhārī, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~107)
- **Jaiminiya underworld quest variant** — In the later Jaiminiya Ashvamedha, Babhruvahana descends into Patala and wages war on the serpents to seize the amrita/Sanjivani jewel to revive Arjuna _(actors: Babhruvāhana, Nāgas, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/babhruvahana.md) — (Jaiminīya Aśvamedha Parva)
- **Jambavan battles Krishna over the Syamantaka jewel** — The bear-king Jambavan fights Krishna 21 days over the Syamantaka jewel, recognizes him as Rama returned, surrenders the gem and gives his daughter Jambavati in marriage _(actors: Jambavan, Krishna, Jambavati)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/the-chiranjivis.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.56)
- **Kṛṣṇa restores the gem and is vindicated** — Kṛṣṇa returns to Dwārakā and hands the Syamantaka back to Satrājit in open assembly, clearing the murder charge _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Satrājit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13)
- **Kṛṣṇa's birth and Vraja bāla-līlā** — Retelling of Kṛṣṇa's Mathurā birth, the move to Gokula/Vṛndāvana, childhood marvels, demon-slayings, and the Kaṃsa cycle, framed by Rādhā-bhakti _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Kaṃsa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/brahmavaivarta-purana.md) — (Brahmavaivarta Purāṇa (Kṛṣṇa-janma-khaṇḍa))
- **Kripa argues against the night-raid** — Kripa urges Ashvatthaman to abandon the massacre of the sleeping Panchalas, warning it is sinful and damning _(actors: Kripa, Ashvatthaman, Kritavarman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kripa.md) — (Mahabharata, Sauptika Parva adhy. 1-2)
- **Krishna and Draupadi's grievance at Kamyaka** — Krishna visits the exiles at Kamyaka and Draupadi pours out her grief over the dice-hall outrage, renewing the resolve for war _(actors: Krishna, Draupadi, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva 12-13)
- **Krishna beheads Shishupala with the Sudarshana** — When Shishupala insults past the hundredth offense, Krishna releases the Sudarshana Chakra and beheads him in the assembly _(actors: Krishna, Shishupala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shishupala.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha-parva 42)
- **Krishna teaches the parampara charter (Gita 4.1-3)** — Krishna recounts that the yoga was handed down by succession from Vivasvan to Manu to Ikshvaku, was lost, and is re-spoken by him to Arjuna _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna, Vivasvan, Manu, Ikshvaku)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/guru-shishya-parampara.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 4.1-3)
- **Kunti's appeal to Karna on the Ganges** — Kunti confesses she is Karna's mother and begs him to spare his brothers; Surya confirms her words from the sky, and Karna vows to kill only Arjuna so Kunti will keep five living sons _(actors: Kunti, Karna, Surya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva 144-146)
- **Mādrī bears the Aśvin twins Nakula and Sahadeva** — Kuntī teaches the mantra to Mādrī, who summons the twin Aśvins and bears Nakula and Sahadeva in a single act _(actors: Mādrī, Aśvins, Nakula, Sahadeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Nala Abandons Damayantī** — Tormented by Kali within, Nala cuts away half her single garment and steals away from the sleeping Damayantī into the night _(actors: Nala, Damayantī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 59)
- **Nala reunites with Damayantī and wins back his kingdom** — Through the second-svayaṃvara ruse Nala resumes his true form, reunites with Damayantī, and wins back his throne from Puṣkara at dice _(actors: Nala, Damayantī, Puṣkara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 73–78)
- **Pradyumna slays Śambara** — Krishna's son Pradyumna (incarnation of Kāmadeva), stolen as an infant by the demon Śambara and raised unknowingly, learns the truth from Māyāvatī (Rati) and slays Śambara before returning to Dvārakā _(actors: Pradyumna, Śambara, Māyāvatī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/harivamsha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva)
- **Prophecy and imprisonment by Kamsa** — A voice warns Kamsa, tyrant of Mathura, that the 8th child of his sister Devaki will kill him; he imprisons Devaki and Vasudeva and slays their first six infants _(actors: Kamsa, Devaki, Vasudeva)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.1-10.4)
- **Restoration of the original boys and calves** — Brahma departs and Krishna brings back the original boys and calves from the cave; to them no time has passed and the day's play resumes seamlessly _(actors: Krishna, cowherd boys, calves)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/brahma-vimohana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.14.58-60)
- **Shalya named fourth Kaurava commander** — With Karna fallen and the army broken, the demoralized Kauravas turn to Shalya as the fourth and last generalissimo _(actors: Duryodhana, Shalya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/08-karna-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva 93-96)
- **Shearing of Bāṇa's thousand arms** — Kṛṣṇa with the Sudarśana cakra lops off Bāṇa's thousand arms in succession, leaving only four (or two); his banner-staff breaks per Śiva's omen _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Bāṇāsura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/banasura-usha-aniruddha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.63; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Slaying of Droṇa** — On day 15 Droṇa is beheaded by Dhṛṣṭadyumna after the 'Aśvatthāmā is dead' stratagem _(actors: Droṇa, Dhṛṣṭadyumna)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kurukshetra-dharmakshetra.md) — (Mahabharata, Drona-parva)
- **Subhadrā survives the Yādava end** — Subhadrā outlives the Mausala destruction of the Vṛṣṇis, is escorted from Dvārakā by Arjuna, and ends her days with the Pāṇḍavas at Hastināpura _(actors: Subhadrā, Arjuna, Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subhadra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Mausala/Mahāprasthānika framing)
- **Sudama the fellow-pupil** — The poor brahmin Sudama (Kucela) was a classmate at Sandipani's school, seeding their later Dvaraka reunion _(actors: Krishna, Sudama (Kucela))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sandipani-pancajana.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.80-81)
- **Survival of the Śārṅgaka birds (Mandapāla-Jaritā upākhyāna)** — The four nestling sons of the sage Mandapāla and the bird Jaritā are spared by Agni's earlier pledge _(actors: Mandapāla, Jaritā, the four Śārṅgaka chicks, Agni)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/khandava-vana-and-the-burning.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi-parva 220-225)
- **Takshaka kills King Parikshit** — Doomed by the sage-boy Shringin's curse, King Parikshit is bitten and killed by the serpent-king Takshaka _(actors: Takshaka, Parikshit, Shringin)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva Astika sub-parva)
- **The death of Drona by the half-lie** — Day 15: Bhima kills an elephant named Ashvatthaman and Yudhishthira affirms 'Ashvatthaman is dead'; grief-stricken Drona lays down his arms and Dhrishtadyumna beheads the disarmed, meditating brahmin _(actors: Drona, Bhima, Yudhishthira, Dhrishtadyumna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva 185-193 (Drona-vadha))
- **The half-truth that fells Droṇa** — To break the invincible Droṇa, Yudhiṣṭhira declares 'Aśvatthāmā is dead — man or elephant,' the qualifier drowned out; the grieving Droṇa lays down arms and is beheaded, and Yudhiṣṭhira's chariot descends to earth _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Droṇa, Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yudhishthira.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva)
- **The house of lac (Lakshagriha)** — Duryodhana's agent Purocana builds a combustible mansion at Varanavata; warned by Vidura, the Pandavas escape via a tunnel and burn it _(actors: Duryodhana, Purocana, Vidura, Pandavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **The Rāsa-Līlā** — On the Śarad Pūrṇimā full-moon night Kṛṣṇa dances the rāsa with the gopīs on the Yamunā sands, multiplying himself to dance with each, the supreme emblem of divine love _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, gopīs)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.29-33)
- **The Satyavatī match and dowry demand** — Śāntanu loves the fisher-maiden Satyavatī, but the Dāśarāja demands her sons inherit the throne, leaving Śāntanu pining _(actors: Śāntanu, Satyavatī, the Dāśarāja)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~100; Bhāgavata Purāṇa IX.22)
- **The Sea Engulfs Dvaraka** — As Arjuna leads the surviving women and elders out, the ocean rushes in and engulfs Dvaraka leaving no trace, fixing the Dvapara-Kali hinge at 3102 BCE _(actors: Arjuna, Yadava refugees)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dwarka-bet-dwarka-saurashtra.md) — (Mahabharata 16.5-8; Vishnu Purana 5.38; Bhagavata 11.31)
- **The Yakṣa-praśna at the Dvaita lake** — Dharma in the guise of a Yakṣa quizzes Yudhiṣṭhira after the other four brothers drink and fall dead; Yudhiṣṭhira answers the riddles on dharma _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Dharma (Yakṣa), Nakula, Sahadeva, Arjuna, Bhīma)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Āraṇeya Parva ~311–313)
- **Śuka's apologia** — Narrator Śuka defends the transcendent rāsa to King Parīkṣit against the charge of impropriety, warning it is not to be imitated _(actors: Śuka, King Parīkṣit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rasa-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.33.27–40)
- **Vadha of Kaṃsa** — Krishna leaps the royal dais, drags Kaṃsa by the hair into the arena, and slays the tyrant _(actors: Krishna, Kaṃsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.44; Harivaṃśa Viṣṇu Parva; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Wealth without attachment and final liberation** — Sudāmā enjoys the bounty without pride, attributes all to Kṛṣṇa, and through unbroken devotion attains the supreme abode; Śuka gives the moral _(actors: Sudāmā, Śukadeva, Parīkṣit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sudama-kuchela.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.81.21–41)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira's Rājasūya at Indraprastha** — After the Pāṇḍavas conquer the four quarters and Jarāsandha is slain, Yudhiṣṭhira performs the royal-consecration Rājasūya sacrifice at Indraprastha to become samrāṭ _(actors: Yudhishthira, Pandavas, Jarasandha)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/yajnas-and-vedic-rituals.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva; Shatapatha Brahmana 5.2-5.4)
- **Arjuna frees the cursed apsarā-crocodiles** — Five apsarās led by Vargā, cursed to become crocodiles, are freed by Arjuna in the southern tīrthas during his pilgrimage _(actors: Vargā, Arjuna)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/apsaras.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva, Tīrtha-yātrā)
- **Arjuna's departure for divine weapons** — From Kamyaka Arjuna sets out north to win the Pashupata from Shiva and celestial astras from Indra _(actors: Arjuna, Shiva, Indra)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva, Kairata/Indralokabhigamana-parva ~38-41)
- **Arjuna's last rites and the drowning of Dvaraka** — Arjuna performs the Yadava funeral rites and evacuates survivors; the ocean reclaims Dvaraka and his Gandiva-power fails, closing the heroic age _(actors: Arjuna, Vasudeva, the Yadava survivors)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/prabhasa-somnath-tirtha.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva 5-8; Bhagavata Purana 11.31)
