- **Ashvatthaman unleashes the Narayanastra** — Enraged at his father's death, Ashvatthaman invokes the Narayanastra; Krishna orders the army to drop all weapons and submit, so the weapon, denied resistance, dissipates harmlessly _(actors: Ashvatthaman, Krishna, Arjuna, Bhima)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva 194-204 (Narayanastra-mokshana))
- **Bhima meets Hanuman over the saugandhika lotus** — Seeking the celestial lotus, Bhima cannot lift the tail of his half-brother Hanuman, who shows his cosmic Setu-bandha form and promises to roar on Arjuna's banner _(actors: Bhima, Hanuman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 146-153)
- **Bhima Slays Kichaka** — Disguised as the cook Ballava, Bhima ambushes Kichaka for molesting Draupadi and pounds him into a shapeless ball of flesh, then kills the 105 Upakichakas _(actors: Bhima, Kichaka, Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhima.md) — (Mahabharata, Virata Parva ~21-23)
- **Bhishma chooses death-by-will at uttarayana** — By his father Shantanu's boon Bhishma resolves not to die until the sun turns north, lingering on the arrow-bed through the rest of the war _(actors: Bhishma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva 120)
- **Bhishma's Dharma Teachings and the Vishnu Sahasranama** — From the bed of arrows the dying Bhishma delivers the epic's longest dharma-instruction to Yudhishthira and recites the Vishnu Sahasranama _(actors: Bhishma, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhishma.md) — (Mahabharata, Shanti & Anushasana Parvas)
- **Bhīṣma's double vow** — Devavrata renounces the throne and swears lifelong celibacy to win Satyavatī for his father, becoming Bhīṣma; Śāntanu grants him death-at-will _(actors: Devavrata/Bhīṣma, the Dāśarāja, Śāntanu, Satyavatī)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~100)
- **Birth of Duryodhana amid ill omens** — Duryodhana emerges first from the pots as jackals howl and fires blaze in evil directions; Vidura and the sages warn that he portends the dynasty's destruction, but Dhṛtarāṣṭra spares him _(actors: Duryodhana, Vidura, Dhṛtarāṣṭra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~107-108)
- **Birth of the hundred Kauravas** — Gandhari's flesh-lump is divided by Vyasa into 101 ghee-jar pieces, producing Duryodhana (born amid evil omens), 99 more sons, and the daughter Duhshala _(actors: Gandhari, Vyasa, Duryodhana, Duhshasana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 114-115)
- **Conception of Drona in a pot** — Seeing the apsara Ghritachi, Bharadvaja's seed escapes and is caught in a wooden trough (drona), from which his son Drona develops, ayonija _(actors: Bharadvaja, Ghritachi, Drona)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bharadvaja.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~130)
- **Crossing of the Yamuna** — Vasudeva carries baby Krishna across the parted, flooded Yamuna to Nanda and Yashoda, swapping for their newborn girl (Yogamaya) _(actors: Vasudeva, Krishna, Nanda, Yashoda, Yogamaya, Shesha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.3-10.4)
- **Damayantī's Trials in the Forest** — Abandoned, Damayantī is seized by a python, freed by a hunter whom she curses dead for lusting after her, then survives a caravan's destruction _(actors: Damayantī, a hunter)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 60-64)
- **Death of Pāṇḍu and Mādrī** — Pāṇḍu dies when he embraces Mādrī (fulfilling Kindama's curse), Mādrī follows him on the pyre, and Kuntī alone rears all five boys _(actors: Pāṇḍu, Mādrī, Kuntī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Draupadi rejects Karna at her svayamvara** — Karna rises to attempt Drupada's bow-test but Draupadi refuses him as a charioteer's son, hardening his hatred (treated as later interpolation in the Critical Edition) _(actors: Karna, Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva ~178-179)
- **Drupada's boyhood vow to share half his kingdom** — Reared together in Bharadvāja's āśrama, the boy Drupada vows in affection that as king he will share half his realm with Droṇa _(actors: Droṇa, Drupada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~129)
- **Duryodhana surveys the armies and speaks to Drona** — Seeing the Pandava army marshalled by Dhrishtadyumna, Duryodhana approaches his teacher Drona and recites the enemy's strength and his own champions to reassure himself _(actors: Duryodhana, Drona)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/01-arjuna-vishada-yoga.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 1.2-9)
- **Fire-birth of Dhṛṣṭadyumna and Draupadī** — From Drupada's fire-altar springs Dhṛṣṭadyumna armored and prophesied to slay Droṇa, and immediately after Draupadī, a full-grown dark maiden born of pure fire (ayonijā), destined to destroy the kṣatriyas _(actors: Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Draupadī, Yāja)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva 1.169)
- **Ganga drowns her seven sons** — Ganga bears and drowns seven newborn sons in the river in succession, each drowning releasing a Vasu from the curse, while the silent Shantanu watches _(actors: Ganga, Shantanu)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganga.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva 98)
- **Ghaṭotkaca's summon-at-a-thought boon** — Grown to full youth within a day, Ghaṭotkaca takes leave of his parents with the boon that he will come to the Pāṇḍavas the moment they think of him _(actors: Ghaṭotkaca, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ghatotkacha.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Indra consecrates Krishna as Govinda** — Defeated, Indra descends with the cow Surabhi and consecrates Krishna as Govinda, confessing his pride _(actors: Indra, Krishna, Surabhi)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/indra.md) — (Bhagavata Purana)
- **Śiva's intercession and Hari–Hara reconciliation** — Śiva pleads for his devotee's life; Kṛṣṇa spares Bāṇa and grants him fearlessness, the two gods reconciled in Hari–Hara non-difference _(actors: Śiva, Kṛṣṇa, Bāṇāsura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/banasura-usha-aniruddha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.63; Harivaṃśa Viṣṇu Parva)
- **Kṛṣṇa recites the Śiva Sahasranāma** — Asked for the names of Śiva, Bhīṣma defers and Kṛṣṇa recites the thousand names, having learned them from the sage Upamanyu _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Upamanyu, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/13-anushasana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 13)
- **Karna institutes Surya-worship (Chhath charter)** — An alternate Chhath charter holds that Karna, son of Surya, instituted the sun-worship that became Chhath Puja _(actors: Karna, Surya)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/regional-temple-festivals.md) — (folk Chhath tradition)
- **Killing of Kaṃsa's brothers** — Kaṃsa's eight brothers rush to avenge him and are killed by Balarāma _(actors: Balarāma, Kaṃsa's brothers)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.44; Harivaṃśa)
- **Kāma reborn as Pradyumna** — The ash-reduced Kāmadeva is reborn in body as Pradyumna, son of Kṛṣṇa and Rukmiṇī, in the Dvāpara age _(actors: Kāma, Kṛṣṇa, Rukmiṇī)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/shiva-myth-cycle.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa; Harivaṃśa)
- **Kripa guards the gate during the Sauptika massacre** — Yielding to his nephew, Kripa and Kritavarman guard the camp gates and fire the camp while Ashvatthaman slaughters the sleepers _(actors: Kripa, Kritavarman, Ashvatthaman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kripa.md) — (Mahabharata, Sauptika Parva adhy. 2, 8)
- **Marriage to Satyabhāmā** — A remorseful Satrājit gives Kṛṣṇa his daughter Satyabhāmā; Kṛṣṇa accepts the bride but returns the jewel _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Satyabhāmā, Satrājit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13)
- **Maya builds the Mayasabhā at Indraprastha** — In repayment Maya Dānava raises the wondrous assembly-hall for Yudhiṣṭhira on the cleared ground, with the Devadatta conch and Bhīma's mace _(actors: Maya Dānava, Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna, Bhīma)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/khandava-vana-and-the-burning.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sabhā-parva 1-3)
- **Panchajanya as the Kurukshetra war-conch** — Generations later Krishna blows the same conch Panchajanya to open the Bhagavad-gita at Kurukshetra _(actors: Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sandipani-pancajana.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhishma-parva)
- **Shishupala's radiance merges into Krishna** — A light rises from the slain body and enters Krishna before the court, marking the end of Jaya's curse and liberation by merger (dvesha-bhakti) _(actors: Krishna, Shishupala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shishupala.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha-parva 42; Bhagavata Purana 10.74)
- **Shiva as the Kirata and the Pashupata-astra** — Arjuna's tapas on Indrakila brings Shiva disguised as a hunter; after a duel Arjuna worships him and receives the supreme Pashupata weapon _(actors: Arjuna, Shiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva ~38-41)
- **Slaying of Śiśupāla** — During Yudhiṣṭhira's Rājasūya, the rite culminates in the killing of Śiśupāla _(actors: Krishna, Shishupala, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/yajnas-and-vedic-rituals.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva)
- **Slaying of Karṇa** — On day 17 Arjuna slays Karṇa while he lifts his mired chariot-wheel, the spot marked at Karṇa-vadh _(actors: Karṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kurukshetra-dharmakshetra.md) — (Mahabharata, Karna-parva)
- **Slaying of Keśī** — Kṛṣṇa slays the horse-demon Keśī at Keśī-ghāṭa in Vṛndāvana _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Keśī)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.37)
- **Slaying of Vatsasura and Bakasura** — Krishna slays the calf-demon and the crane-demon at Vrindavana _(actors: Krishna, Vatsasura, Bakasura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.11)
- **Sāmba founds sun-worship at Multan** — Sāmba, son of Krishna, founds sun-worship at Mitravana/Mūlasthāna (Multan) after Sūrya cures his leprosy, establishing the Maga Brahmin solar priesthood _(actors: Sāmba, Sūrya)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/surya-and-his-family.md) — (Sāmba Purāṇa; Bhaviṣya Purāṇa)
- **The armies muster at Kurukshetra and commanders named** — Eighteen akshauhinis march to Kurukshetra; Dhrishtadyumna is made Pandava commander and Bhishma the Kaurava commander on conditions, with rules of dharma-yuddha agreed _(actors: Dhrishtadyumna, Bhishma, Pandavas, Kauravas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva, Sainya-niryana 151-159)
- **The Bāṇāsura war (Aniruddha and Uṣā)** — After Uṣā's companion magically brings Aniruddha to Bāṇa's city and Bāṇa imprisons him, Krishna, Balarāma, and Pradyumna ride Garuḍa to war, Krishna fights even Śiva and Skanda, and shears off Bāṇa's arms before taking the couple to Dvārakā _(actors: Aniruddha, Uṣā, Bāṇāsura, Krishna, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/harivamsha.md) — (Harivaṃśa, Viṣṇu Parva)
- **The choice of Nakula and boon of concealment** — Granted one brother's revival, Yudhiṣṭhira chooses Nakula so both mothers have a living son; Dharma revives all four and grants undetected ajñātavāsa _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Dharma, Nakula, Kuntī, Mādrī)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva, Āraṇeya Parva ~313)
- **The Pativratā Humbles Kauśika** — A devoted housewife rebukes Kauśika's anger by reading his mind, showing wifely devotion equals a sage's power, and directs him to the Dharmavyādha _(actors: Kauśika, the devoted housewife)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/minor-upakhyanas-catalog.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 205-206)
- **The second dice game and the exile sentence** — A single decisive throw lost by Yudhishthira sends the Pandavas and Draupadi into 12 years' forest exile plus a 13th incognito year _(actors: Yudhishthira, Shakuni, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva 66-72)
- **Ulūpī revives Arjuna with the Nāga gem** — Ulūpī summons the serpent-realm jewel of resurrection (Mṛtasaṃjīvanī) and places it on Arjuna's chest, returning him to life, then reveals she engineered the death to expiate the Vasus' curse over Bhīṣma's unfair slaying _(actors: Ulūpī, Arjuna, Babhruvāhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.81-82)
- **Uttanka incites Janamejaya to the snake-sacrifice** — Bearing an undying grudge against Takshaka, Uttanka incites King Janamejaya to undertake the sarpa-satra to avenge his father Parikshit _(actors: Uttanka, Janamejaya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahabharata Paushya & Astika Parvas)
- **Virata strikes Yudhishthira with a dice** — Believing his son won the battle, Virata hurls a dice that bloodies Yudhishthira's face when Kanka credits Brihannala; Draupadi catches the blood lest it fall and cause drought _(actors: Virata, Yudhishthira, Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/04-virata-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Virata Parva 63)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira's Aśvamedha and the golden mongoose** — To expiate the war's bloodshed Yudhiṣṭhira performs the horse-sacrifice, at whose climax a half-golden mongoose declares a poor man's gift of barley-flour greater than the whole royal rite _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/yudhishthira.md) — (Mahābhārata, Aśvamedhika Parva)
- **Yugala-gītā** — A paired song of the gopīs in two half-choruses praising Kṛṣṇa's flute-play, sometimes counted with the gopī song-cluster _(actors: The gopīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rasa-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.35)
- **Bhima slays Bakasura** — At Ekachakra Bhima goes in a brahmin's place and kills the cannibal-demon Baka, freeing the town from its daily tribute _(actors: Bhima, Bakasura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Bhima slays Jatasura** — The rakshasa Jatasura, disguised as a brahmana, abducts Yudhishthira, the twins, and Draupadi, and Bhima returns to slay him _(actors: Bhima, Jatasura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 157)
- **Bhima Slays the Hundred Kauravas** — Across the eighteen-day war Bhima personally slays every one of the hundred sons of Dhritarashtra, leaving Duryodhana last _(actors: Bhima, the Kauravas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhima.md) — (Mahabharata, Drona/Shalya Parvas)
- **Bhishma blows his conch (the Kaurava war-signal)** — To cheer Duryodhana, the aged Bhishma roars like a lion and blows his conch, sounding the first battle-signal from the Kaurava side and triggering a tumult of war-instruments _(actors: Bhishma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/01-arjuna-vishada-yoga.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 1.12-13)
- **Bhishma-Karna reconciliation at the arrow-bed** — With Bhishma fallen, Karna comes to the arrow-bed; Bhishma reveals he knew Karna was Kunti's eldest son and urges him to join his brothers or fight nobly, but Karna returns to the field _(actors: Bhishma, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva 122-124)
- **Bhishma's Death at Uttarayana** — When the sun turns north Bhishma withdraws his life-breaths by yogic will and his spirit rises to the heavens, on the day observed as Bhishma Ashtami _(actors: Bhishma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhishma.md) — (Mahabharata, Anushasana Parva ~167-168)
- **Bhramara-gītā (Song to the Bee)** — After Kṛṣṇa leaves for Mathurā, Uddhava visits the grieving gopīs; the chief gopī addresses a bumblebee in delirium of separation, humbling Uddhava's jñāna with the gopīs' love _(actors: Uddhava, the gopīs, the chief gopī (read as Rādhā))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rasa-lila.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.47)
- **Birth of Bhishma and Ganga's departure** — At the eighth birth Shantanu breaks his vow; Ganga reveals her identity, takes the boy Devavrata (future Bhishma) to be reared, and later returns him grown _(actors: Ganga, Shantanu, Devavrata/Bhishma)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganga.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva 98-100)
- **Celestial voice at Draupadī's birth** — An aśarīravāṇī announces at Draupadī's birth that this dark woman will lead the kṣatriya race to ruin and accomplish the gods' purpose _(actors: Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Damayantī Recognized in Chedi** — Taken in as a sairandhrī at Subāhu's court, Damayantī is recognized by the Brahmin Sudeva via the lotus-mark on her brow and sent home _(actors: Damayantī, Sudeva, queen-mother of Chedi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 65-66)
- **Droṇa obtains Paraśurāma's celestial weapons** — Arriving after Paraśurāma's wealth is gone, Droṇa asks for his celestial astras with the secret of their recall and discharge, and is made unmatched in astra-vidyā _(actors: Droṇa, Paraśurāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~129)
- **Duryodhana poisons Bhīma in the Gaṅgā** — Duryodhana feeds Bhīma poison, binds him, and throws him into the Gaṅgā at Pramāṇakoṭi; in the Nāga realm the serpents' nectar grants Bhīma the strength of ten thousand elephants, backfiring the plot _(actors: Duryodhana, Bhīma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~119-120)
- **Escape from the house of lac and slaying of Hiḍimba** — The family escapes the burning lac-house through Vidura's tunnel; Bhīma slays the rākṣasa Hiḍimba and Kuntī sanctions his union with Hiḍimbā _(actors: Kuntī, Pāṇḍavas, Bhīma, Hiḍimba, Hiḍimbā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Fall of Duryodhana and the Sauptika Night-Massacre** — On day 18 Bhīma fells Duryodhana with a mace-blow to the thigh, followed by Aśvatthāmā's night-massacre of the sleeping Pāṇḍava camp _(actors: Duryodhana, Bhīma, Aśvatthāmā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kurukshetra-dharmakshetra.md) — (Mahabharata, Shalya-parva, Sauptika-parva)
- **Founding of Indraprastha on the cleared land** — The burnt Khāṇḍava tract (Khāṇḍava-prastha) becomes the Pāṇḍava capital Indraprastha _(actors: The Pāṇḍavas, Maya Dānava)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/khandava-vana-and-the-burning.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi-/Sabhā-parva)
- **Kṛṣṇa obtains sons and boons from Śiva through penance** — Kṛṣṇa narrates how, through austerity and Upamanyu's Śiva-bhakti, he obtained sons and boons from Śiva, framing the Śaiva material _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Śiva, Upamanyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/13-anushasana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 13)
- **Karna's digvijaya for Duryodhana** — As Angaraja, Karna conquers the four quarters subduing many kings for Duryodhana's Rajasuya/Vaishnava sacrifice _(actors: Karna, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Vana/Sabha references)
- **Krishna slays Dantavakra** — Shishupala's cousin Dantavakra attacks Krishna to avenge him and is killed, completing the third Jaya-Vijaya birth-pair _(actors: Krishna, Dantavakra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shishupala.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.78)
- **Markandeya's discourses in the forest** — The deathless sage Markandeya recounts the pralaya vision, Nala-Damayanti, Savitri, the Matsya flood, the righteous butcher, and the Kali-yuga prophecy to Yudhishthira _(actors: Markandeya, Yudhishthira, Krishna)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva, Markandeya-samasya ~182-232)
- **Marriage to Satyavatī** — Śāntanu weds Satyavatī and brings her to Hastināpura as queen _(actors: Śāntanu, Satyavatī)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~100; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.20)
- **Murder of Satrājit by Śatadhanvā** — Incited by Akrūra and Kṛtavarman, Śatadhanvā kills Satrājit in his sleep and steals the Syamantaka while Kṛṣṇa is away _(actors: Śatadhanvā, Satrājit, Akrūra, Kṛtavarman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.57; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13)
- **Pandavas/Draupadi perform Chhath to regain their kingdom** — Another Chhath charter holds the Pandavas and Draupadi performed the sun-vrata to regain their lost kingdom _(actors: Pandavas, Draupadi)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/regional-temple-festivals.md) — (folk Chhath tradition)
- **Pandu's curse by Kindama** — Pandu kills the sage Kindama coupling in deer-form and is cursed to die if he ever embraces a wife _(actors: Pandu, Kindama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 118)
- **Release of Devakī and Vasudeva** — Krishna and Balarāma free their imprisoned birth-parents after Kaṃsa's death _(actors: Krishna, Balarāma, Devakī, Vasudeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.44–45)
- **Slaying of Aghasura** — The python-demon swallows the cowherd boys, and Krishna kills him from within _(actors: Krishna, Aghasura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.12)
- **The crossing of the Yamuna** — Vasudeva carries baby Krishna across the flooded Yamuna (Shesha shielding with his hood) to Gokula, exchanging him for Nanda and Yashoda's newborn daughter, who slips from Kamsa's grasp and foretells his doom _(actors: Vasudeva, Krishna, Nanda, Yashoda, Yogamaya, Kamsa)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.3-10.4)
- **The Pandavas' identities revealed** — On the third day the brothers take royal seats and reveal that Kanka is Yudhishthira, Vallabha Bhima, Brihannala Arjuna, the grooms the twins, and Sairandhri is Draupadi; Virata begs forgiveness and offers alliance _(actors: Arjuna, Virata, Pandavas, Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/04-virata-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Virata Parva 65-66)
- **The Usha-parinaya (marriage and return to Dwārakā)** — Aniruddha is freed from the nāgapāśa, weds Uṣā, and is brought in triumph to Dwārakā; Bāṇa becomes a Gaṇa of Śiva _(actors: Aniruddha, Uṣā, Kṛṣṇa, Bāṇāsura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/banasura-usha-aniruddha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.63; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **Transmission of weapons-science and the Drona-Drupada boyhood** — Bharadvaja transmits the agneyastra and dhanurveda (received from Agnivesha) to Drona, and rears Drona alongside Drupada in his ashram _(actors: Bharadvaja, Drona, Drupada, Agnivesha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bharadvaja.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva ~129-130)
- **Vastra-haraṇa (Hiding the Gopīs' Clothes)** — Kṛṣṇa hides the gopīs' clothes at Cīra-ghāṭa while they bathe _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, gopīs)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (sthala tradition)
- **Vyāsa re-compiles the Vedas at the Dvāpara–Kali junction** — In the orthodox frame, all Upaniṣads are eternal Śruti seen by ṛṣis and re-compiled by Kṛṣṇa-Dvaipāyana Vyāsa at the Dvāpara–Kali junction (~3102 BCE) _(actors: Vyāsa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/principal-upanishads-detail.md) — (shruti tradition)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira performs the grand Aśvamedha at Hastināpura** — The returned horse is sacrificed in the full rite with Vyāsa officiating and lavish gifts, proclaiming Yudhiṣṭhira cakravartin and universal sovereign _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Vyāsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.85-89)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira's Aśvamedha (post-war atonement)** — Yudhiṣṭhira performs the horse sacrifice after the Kurukṣetra war as imperial atonement, the wandering stallion leading to the Babhruvāhana episode and the golden-mongoose appearance _(actors: Yudhishthira, Babhruvahana)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/yajnas-and-vedic-rituals.md) — (Mahabharata Ashvamedhika Parva; Shatapatha Brahmana 13)
- **Arjuna Slays the Nivatakavachas** — As Indra's champion Arjuna destroys the ocean-demon Nivatakavachas and the city Hiranyapura, earning the diadem and the name Kiritin _(actors: Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva ~168-172)
- **Balarāma Ends His Sarasvatī Pilgrimage; Post-War Śrāddha** — Baladeva (Balarāma) ends his Sarasvatī pilgrimage at Aujas-ghāṭ, where post-war śrāddha for the slain is performed _(actors: Balarāma)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kurukshetra-dharmakshetra.md) — (Mahabharata, Shalya-parva)
- **Balarāma Slays Dhenukāsura at Tālavana** — Balarāma slays the ass-demon Dhenukāsura at the palm-forest Tālavana, freeing the tāla fruits _(actors: Balarāma, Dhenukāsura)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.15)
- **Bhīṣma awaits the winter solstice by icchā-mṛtyu** — Bhīṣma uses his boon of death-at-will to delay his death until the sun turns north (uttarāyaṇa), the auspicious gate to the deathless worlds _(actors: Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/13-anushasana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 13.153-154)
- **Births of Citrāṅgada and Vicitravīrya** — Satyavatī bears Śāntanu two sons, Citrāṅgada and Vicitravīrya, both later dying young and childless _(actors: Śāntanu, Satyavatī, Citrāṅgada, Vicitravīrya)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~100–102; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.20)
- **Births of the three eldest Pandavas** — Using Durvasas' boon, Kunti bears Yudhishthira by Dharma, Bhima by Vayu, and Arjuna by Indra _(actors: Kunti, Dharma/Yama, Vayu, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 123)
- **Brahma's bewilderment** — Brahma steals the cowherd boys and calves to test Krishna, who multiplies himself into identical copies of all of them for a year _(actors: Krishna, Brahma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.13)
- **Draupadi's svayamvara and Arjuna's win** — Disguised as a brahmin, Arjuna strings the bow and pierces the revolving fish-target to win Draupadi; Karna is rebuffed as a suta _(actors: Arjuna, Draupadi, Drupada, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Droṇa weds Kṛpī** — Droṇa marries Kṛpī, sister of Kṛpa, tying him to the Kuru court before he enters it _(actors: Droṇa, Kṛpī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Duel with Bhīṣma over Ambā** — As guru-in-arms, Paraśurāma fights an indecisive multi-day duel with Bhīṣma over the cause of Ambā _(actors: Paraśurāma, Bhīṣma, Ambā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 5.176-186 (Udyoga Parva))
- **Duryodhana crowns Karṇa king of Aṅga** — At the graduation tournament Duryodhana crowns the unknown Karṇa king of Aṅga to legitimize a champion against Arjuna, sealing the lifelong Karṇa–Duryodhana friendship _(actors: Duryodhana, Karṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~136)
- **Ghosha-yatra: Pandavas rescue Duryodhana** — Duryodhana on a cattle-expedition to Dvaitavana is captured and humiliated by the gandharva Citrasena, and Arjuna and Bhima rescue their enemy on Yudhishthira's order _(actors: Duryodhana, Citrasena, Arjuna, Bhima, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva, Ghosha-yatra-parva ~239-256)
- **Hanuman humbles Bhima in the forest** — In the Gandhamadana forest the aged Hanuman lies with his tail across Bhima's path; unable to move it, the proud Bhima is humbled and recognizes his half-brother _(actors: Hanuman, Bhima)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva 146-150)
- **Indra begs Karna's armor and earrings** — Disguised as a brahmana, Indra exploits Karna's charity-vow to ask for his invulnerable kavacha-kundala; warned by Surya, Karna cuts them from his body and becomes mortal _(actors: Indra, Karna, Surya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva ~308-310)
- **Janamejaya's Sarpa-Sattra (snake sacrifice)** — Janamejaya performs the year-long snake sacrifice to annihilate all serpents in vengeance for his father Parikṣit's death by Takṣaka's bite; the sage Āstīka halts it, sparing Takṣaka, and the Mahābhārata is narrated during the rite _(actors: Janamejaya, Takshaka, Astika, Vaishampayana, Parikshit)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/yajnas-and-vedic-rituals.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Killing of Śatadhanvā** — Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma pursue Śatadhanvā, who hands the gem to Akrūra; Kṛṣṇa beheads him with the Sudarśana cakra but finds no jewel _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, Śatadhanvā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.57; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13)
- **Kirata grants the Pashupatastra to Arjuna** — In hunter form Shiva battles Arjuna over a slain boar and, after the duel, grants him the supreme weapon Pashupatastra _(actors: Kirata (Shiva), Arjuna)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/shiva-avatars-and-forms.md) — (Shiva Purana; Mahabharata Vana Parva)
- **Krishna and Arjuna blow their celestial conches** — From the white-horsed chariot Krishna blows Panchajanya and Arjuna blows Devadatta, joined by the Pandava champions, the answering blast tearing through earth and sky and shattering the Kauravas' hearts _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna, Pandava warriors)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/01-arjuna-vishada-yoga.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 1.14-19)
- **Kārkoṭaka Transforms Nala into Bāhuka** — Nala rescues the nāga-king Kārkoṭaka from a fire; the serpent's bite torments Kali, disguises Nala as a dwarf, and gives him form-restoring garments _(actors: Nala, Kārkoṭaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 66)
- **Kuntī's word makes Draupadī the common wife** — After Arjuna wins Draupadī, Kuntī unseeingly tells her sons to 'share it among yourselves,' and her binding command becomes the chief sanction for Draupadī's polyandry _(actors: Kuntī, Draupadī, Arjuna, Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Marriage of Abhimanyu and Uttara** — Arjuna takes Virata's daughter Uttara as daughter-in-law, marrying her to his son Abhimanyu in a great wedding at Upaplavya; their union will produce Parikshit _(actors: Abhimanyu, Uttara, Arjuna, Virata, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/04-virata-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Virata Parva 67)
- **Reinstatement of Ugrasena** — Krishna recoronates his deposed grandfather Ugrasena as Yādava king, declining the throne himself _(actors: Krishna, Balarāma, Ugrasena)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.44–45; Harivaṃśa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5)
- **The golden-headed mongoose deflates the Aśvamedha** — A half-golden mongoose with a human voice declares the grand sacrifice not equal to a poor brahmin's handful of barley flour, recounting how a starving gleaner family gave their last meal to a guest who was Dharma in disguise and ascended to heaven _(actors: the golden mongoose, the gleaner brahmin family, Dharma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/14-ashvamedhika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 14.90-93)
- **Uluka delivers Duryodhana's insulting message** — Duryodhana sends Uluka to the Pandava camp with a deliberately humiliating war-mongering message; each Pandava returns a defiant reply, ending any last hope of peace _(actors: Uluka, Duryodhana, Pandavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva 160-164 (Uluka-dutagamana))
- **Vidura Counsels Dhṛtarāṣṭra (Vidura-nīti)** — Vidura counsels the sleepless Dhṛtarāṣṭra with fables and maxims on statecraft and dharma, including the man-in-the-well allegory of the soul ensnared in saṃsāra _(actors: Vidura, Dhṛtarāṣṭra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/minor-upakhyanas-catalog.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva 33-40)
- **Vision of the universe in Krishna's mouth** — Yashoda, checking for eaten dirt, sees the entire cosmos within infant Krishna's mouth _(actors: Krishna, Yashoda)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.8)
- **Yudhishthira frees Bhima and Nahusa from the python-curse** — The serpent Nahusa seizes Bhima and releases him only after Yudhishthira answers his questions on dharma, freeing Nahusa from his curse _(actors: Yudhishthira, Bhima, Nahusa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 176-181)
- **Balarāma Slays Pralambāsura at Bhāṇḍīravana** — Balarāma slays the demon Pralamba at Bhāṇḍīravana, the forest of the great banyan _(actors: Balarāma, Pralambāsura)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.18)
- **Balarāma's rift and the gem left with Akrūra** — Balarāma, suspecting concealment, leaves for Mithilā; Kṛṣṇa traces the gem to Akrūra, proves his innocence, and lets Akrūra keep it _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, Akrūra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/syamantaka-jewel.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.57; Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13)
- **Bhima Shatters Duryodhana's Thigh** — In the final mace-duel Bhima, on Krishna's thigh-slap cue, delivers the forbidden below-the-navel blow that shatters Duryodhana's thighs, fulfilling his second vow _(actors: Bhima, Duryodhana, Krishna, Balarama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bhima.md) — (Mahabharata, Shalya Parva ~58)
- **Bhishma ranks the warriors and demotes Karna** — Bhishma classifies the host into rathi, atiratha and maharatha grades and pointedly rates Karna only an ardha-ratha, prompting Karna to vow not to fight until Bhishma is slain _(actors: Bhishma, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva 165-172 (Rathatiratha))
- **Bhīṣma dies by yoga and his soul ascends** — With the teaching complete and the solstice reached, Bhīṣma withdraws his life-breaths by yogic concentration; the arrows fall away, flowers rain from heaven, and his soul rises like a meteor _(actors: Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/13-anushasana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 13.154)
- **Bhīma slays the rākṣasa Baka at Ekacakrā** — Sheltering with a brāhmaṇa family at Ekacakrā, Kuntī volunteers Bhīma to take the household's place and kill the demon Baka _(actors: Kuntī, Bhīma, Baka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Bāhuka Serves King Ṛtuparṇa** — Disguised as Bāhuka, Nala becomes charioteer and cook at Ṛtuparṇa's court to obtain the secret of dice _(actors: Nala, Ṛtuparṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 67)
- **Birth and naming of Aśvatthāman** — Droṇa and Kṛpī's son is born neighing like the divine horse Uccaiḥśravas; a sky-voice names him Aśvatthāman, the beloved and lethal heir destined to be a Cirañjīvī _(actors: Droṇa, Kṛpī, Aśvatthāman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Births of the twins Nakula and Sahadeva** — Madri uses the mantra once to invoke the twin Ashvins, bearing Nakula and Sahadeva _(actors: Madri, the Ashvins)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 124)
- **Damodara and the twin trees** — Yashoda binds the butter-thief to a mortar; Krishna drags it through twin arjuna trees, freeing two cursed Yakshas _(actors: Krishna, Yashoda, Nalakubara, Manigriva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.9-10.10)
- **Damodara and the Yamala-Arjuna trees** — Yashoda binds the butter-stealing toddler Krishna to a mortar, and he uproots the twin Yamala-Arjuna trees, freeing two cursed Yakshas; he also shows the universe in his mouth to Yashoda _(actors: Krishna, Yashoda, the Yakshas)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.8-10.10)
- **Draupadi's polyandrous marriage** — On Kunti's word and Vyasa's sanction, Draupadi becomes wife to all five Pandava brothers _(actors: Draupadi, the five Pandavas, Kunti, Vyasa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Hanuman rides Arjuna's banner at Kurukshetra** — Hanuman resides on the flag of Arjuna's chariot throughout the Kurukshetra war, protecting it and giving Arjuna the epithet Kapidhvaja _(actors: Hanuman, Arjuna)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhishma Parva)
- **Indra grants the Vasava-shakti** — In exchange for the armor, Indra gives Karna the infallible one-use Vasava-shakti dart, which he later must spend on Ghatotkaca _(actors: Indra, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva ~310)
- **Jarāsandha's vendetta seeded** — Kaṃsa's father-in-law Jarāsandha of Magadha vows revenge, opening the cycle of sieges on Mathurā _(actors: Jarāsandha, Krishna, the Yādavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/kamsa-vadha.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa X.50; Harivaṃśa)
- **Jayadratha's abduction of Draupadi** — Jayadratha of Sindhu carries off Draupadi from the hermitage; the Pandavas pursue, defeat and shave his head as humiliation _(actors: Jayadratha, Draupadi, the Pandavas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva, Draupadi-harana-parva)
- **Kṛṣṇa identifies himself as Uccaiḥśravas** — In the Bhagavad-gītā (10.27) Kṛṣṇa declares 'Among horses know Me as Uccaiḥśravas, born of nectar,' the key teaching of the Vibhūti-yoga _(actors: Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/airavata-and-celestial-animals.md) — (Bhagavad-gītā 10.27)
- **Krishna slew the demon Panchajana (conch origin)** — Krishna's conch Panchajanya was made from the bones of the demon Panchajana whom he slew, recalled in the naming of the conches before the war _(actors: Krishna, Panchajana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/01-arjuna-vishada-yoga.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 1.15)
- **Mārkaṇḍeya Counsels Yudhiṣṭhira in Exile** — During the forest exile the deathless sage consoles the Pāṇḍavas with stories of dharma, devoted women, and cosmic time _(actors: Mārkaṇḍeya, Yudhiṣṭhira, Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/markandeya.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva)
- **Śāntanu's reign and death** — Śāntanu reigns long and justly and dies in old age while his sons are young, leaving Bhīṣma as guardian of the throne _(actors: Śāntanu, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/shantanu.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~100–101)
- **Piṅgalā the Courtesan Attains Detachment** — Wearied of waiting for a paying lover, the courtesan Piṅgalā suddenly attains detachment, realizing desire causes misery and the inner Lord is the only beloved _(actors: Piṅgalā, Dattātreya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/minor-upakhyanas-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11.8)
- **Slaying of Dhenukasura** — Balarama kills the donkey-demon of the Talavana palm-grove _(actors: Balarama, Dhenukasura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.15)
- **Teaching Droṇa** — Paraśurāma gives Droṇa his entire stock of celestial astras and the science of arms _(actors: Paraśurāma, Droṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 1.121-129 (Ādi Parva))
- **The lacquer house plot at Vāraṇāvata** — Duryodhana builds a mansion of lac and ghee to burn the Pāṇḍavas and Kuntī alive, but warned by Vidura they escape through a tunnel, leaving substitute corpses _(actors: Duryodhana, Śakuni, Purocana, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~141-151)
- **Urvashi Curses Arjuna** — Arjuna refuses the apsara Urvashi by calling her 'mother,' and she curses him to become a eunuch-dancer, softened by Indra to one year of his choosing _(actors: Arjuna, Urvashi, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva ~45-46)
- **Yama Fathers Yudhishthira** — As Dharma, Yama fathers Yudhishthira on Kunti via the boon-mantra, producing the truthful eldest Pandava _(actors: Yama/Dharma, Kunti, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/yama.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva)
- **Abduction of the Kashi princesses (Amba backstory)** — Recounted backstory: Bhishma carried off Amba, Ambika and Ambalika from their svayamvara for Vicitravirya; Amba, in love with Shalva and rejected by him, becomes vengeful against Bhishma _(actors: Bhishma, Amba, Ambika, Ambalika)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva 173-199 (Ambopakhyana))
- **Arjuna as Brihannala Routs the Kauravas** — In his concealment year as the eunuch Brihannala, Arjuna becomes Uttara's charioteer, retrieves his arms, and single-handedly routs the Kaurava raiders in the Gograhana _(actors: Arjuna, Uttara, the Kauravas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Virata Parva ~32-67)
- **Bhīṣma's cremation and Kṛṣṇa consoles Gaṅgā** — The Pāṇḍavas and Dhṛtarāṣṭra perform the funeral; Gaṅgā rises grieving from her river and Kṛṣṇa consoles her, revealing Bhīṣma's true nature as the Vasu Dyaus/Prabhāsa cursed to be born human _(actors: Pāṇḍavas, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Gaṅgā, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/13-anushasana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 13)
- **Birth and Renunciation of Śuka** — Vyāsa's son Śuka, born already liberated, tarries in the womb fearing māyā and at birth walks straight to the forest, later becoming reciter of the Bhāgavata _(actors: Śuka, Vyāsa, Janaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/minor-upakhyanas-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata 1-2 & 12; Mahabharata Shanti/Mokshadharma)
- **Damayantī's Second-Svayaṃvara Ruse** — Suspecting Bāhuka is Nala, Damayantī proclaims an impossible second svayaṃvara, knowing only Nala's charioteering could reach Kuṇḍina in time _(actors: Damayantī, Ṛtuparṇa, Nala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 68-71)
- **Death of Pandu and Madri's sati** — Pandu dies by the curse embracing Madri in spring; Madri immolates herself, entrusting her twins to Kunti _(actors: Pandu, Madri, Kunti)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 125)
- **Drupada rebuffs Droṇa and Droṇa's vow of revenge** — Impoverished Droṇa claims the boyhood promise but the now-proud King Drupada insults and turns him away; Droṇa leaves vowing to humble him, his private feud becoming the hidden engine of the epic _(actors: Droṇa, Drupada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~129)
- **Duryodhana humiliated at Yudhiṣṭhira's Rājasūya** — Dazzled by Maya's illusory palace, Duryodhana mistakes water for floor and is laughed at, the humiliation that triggers the dice-plot _(actors: Duryodhana, Maya, Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva ~46-47)
- **Krishna drives the chariot between the two armies** — At Arjuna's request Krishna halts the chariot before Bhishma, Drona, and all the kings, telling Arjuna to behold the assembled Kurus so that he sees not enemies but his own family _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/01-arjuna-vishada-yoga.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 1.20-25)
- **Krishna's secret offer to Karna** — On the war's eve Krishna reveals Karna's birth and offers him the throne and the Pandavas' allegiance, but Karna refuses to betray Duryodhana _(actors: Krishna, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva ~141-144)
- **Kuntī's war-message via Kṛṣṇa (Vidulā-ākhyāna)** — When Kṛṣṇa's peace-embassy fails, Kuntī sends through him the inset tale of Vidulā to goad Yudhiṣṭhira to fight for his right rather than live on charity _(actors: Kuntī, Kṛṣṇa, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva)
- **Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa Wedded by Brahmā at Bhāṇḍīravana** — In one tradition Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are wedded by Brahmā under the bhāṇḍīra-vaṭa banyan _(actors: Rādhā, Kṛṣṇa, Brahmā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Garga Samhita)
- **Subduing of Kaliya** — Krishna subdues the many-hooded serpent poisoning the Yamuna, dancing on his hoods and banishing him to Ramanaka _(actors: Krishna, Kaliya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.16-17)
- **Teaching and cursing Karṇa** — Paraśurāma teaches Karṇa (disguised as a brahmin) the Brahmāstra, then curses him to forget its mantra at his hour of greatest need _(actors: Paraśurāma, Karṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 8.29 (Karṇa Parva))
- **The Yaksha-Prashna** — Yama, disguised as a Yaksha at a lake, kills the four younger Pandavas, then revives them after testing Yudhishthira with riddles on dharma and revealing himself as his father _(actors: Yama, Yudhishthira, the Pandavas)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/yama.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva)
- **The Yaksha-prashna at the Dvaitavana lake** — Four Pandavas fall dead drinking from a Yaksha-guarded lake; Yudhishthira answers Dharma's riddles, revives them, chooses Nakula, and wins the boon of concealment for the ajnatavasa _(actors: Yudhishthira, the Yaksha (Dharma), Nakula, Sahadeva, Arjuna, Bhima)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva, Araneya-parva ~311-313)
- **Vyasa Divides the Eternal Veda into Four** — Krishna-Dvaipayana Vyasa re-divides the single eternal Veda into four at the Dvapara-Kali junction (c. 3102 BCE), establishing the Rig, Yajur, Sama, and Atharva recensions _(actors: Vyasa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/vedas/yajurveda.md) — (Vishnu Purana; Bhagavata Purana 12.6)
- **Abduction and marriage of Subhadra** — With Krishna's blessing Arjuna carries off Subhadra at Dvaraka; their son is Abhimanyu _(actors: Arjuna, Subhadra, Krishna, Balarama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Amba's tapas, Shiva's boon, and rebirth as Shikhandin** — After Parashurama's indecisive duel with Bhishma fails to win her redress, Amba wins Shiva's boon to cause Bhishma's death, immolates herself, is reborn as Drupada's child, and becomes male via the yaksha Sthunakarna _(actors: Amba, Shiva, Parashurama, Shikhandin, Sthunakarna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva, Ambopakhyana)
- **Arjuna's despair and collapse on the chariot** — Seeing kinsmen, teachers, and elders arrayed on both sides, Arjuna is overcome with pity, his limbs fail and his bow Gandiva slips, and he casts aside his bow and sinks onto the chariot seat refusing to fight _(actors: Arjuna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/01-arjuna-vishada-yoga.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 1.26-47)
- **Brihadbala Slain by Abhimanyu** — Brihadbala, the last great Suryavamshi of the epic age, fights for the Kauravas and is slain by Abhimanyu in the Mahabharata war, pinning the two dynasties' timelines together _(actors: Brihadbala, Abhimanyu)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva)
- **Death of Krishna at Prabhasa** — The death-place lore of Krishna at Prabhasa-kshetra is recorded in the Prabhasa-khanda _(actors: Krishna)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/skanda-purana-khanda-map.md) — (Skanda Purana (Prabhasa-khanda))
- **Droṇa appointed preceptor of the Kuru princes** — After Droṇa retrieves the princes' ball and ring from a well with a chain of grass-arrows, Bhīṣma recognizes his mastery and appoints him ācārya of the Kuru and Pāṇḍava princes _(actors: Droṇa, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~131)
- **Duryodhana poisons Bhima** — Duryodhana poisons Bhima and throws him into the Ganga, but he sinks to the naga realm where serpent-nectar gives him the strength of ten thousand elephants _(actors: Duryodhana, Bhima)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 119-120, 127)
- **Duryodhana's thigh-slap at the disrobing** — Duryodhana orders Draupadī dragged and stripped and bares his left thigh inviting her to sit on it — the obscene gesture Bhīma vows to avenge by breaking that thigh _(actors: Duryodhana, Duḥśāsana, Draupadī, Bhīma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva ~60-62)
- **Exchange of Dice and Horse Secrets; Kali Expelled** — Nala trades the horse-secret for the dice-secret with Ṛtuparṇa; the instant the dice-knowledge enters him, Kali is vomited out and takes refuge in the Vibhītaka tree _(actors: Nala, Ṛtuparṇa, Kali)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 72)
- **Govardhana-Dhāraṇa (Lifting the Hill)** — After Kṛṣṇa redirects worship from Indra to Govardhana, the affronted Indra looses catastrophic rains; Kṛṣṇa lifts the hill on his little finger for seven days to shelter all Braj _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Indra, cowherds)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.24-27)
- **Govardhana-lila** — Krishna stops Indra-worship, and when the enraged Indra sends torrential rain, Krishna lifts Mount Govardhana on his finger for seven days as an umbrella protecting Vraja, until Indra submits _(actors: Krishna, Indra, the cowherds)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.24-10.27)
- **Kunti's plea and Karna's vow to spare four brothers** — Kunti reveals she is his mother; Karna vows to fight only Arjuna and spare the other four Pandavas so Kunti will keep five living sons _(actors: Kunti, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva ~144-146)
- **Kuntī reveals herself to Karṇa** — Before the war Kuntī goes to Karṇa at his Sūrya-worship and reveals she is his mother, begging him to join the Pāṇḍavas; Sūrya corroborates but Karṇa refuses, vowing to fight only Arjuna and spare her other sons _(actors: Kuntī, Karṇa, Sūrya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Udyoga Parva)
- **Sāmba founds the Maga sun-cult** — Kṛṣṇa's son Sāmba founds the Maga/Sāmba sun-worship cult, recorded in the Sāmba Upapurāṇa _(actors: Sāmba, Sūrya)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-puranas.md) — (Sāmba Upapurāṇa)
- **Subduing Kaliya** — Krishna subdues and dances on the hoods of the poison-serpent Kaliya in the Yamuna, banishing him _(actors: Krishna, Kaliya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.16-10.17)
- **Swallowing the forest fire** — Krishna twice consumes a forest conflagration to save the herders _(actors: Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.17, X.19)
- **Tirtha-yatra launched from the exile-forest** — From the forest the Pandavas undertake the great pilgrimage tour narrated by Pulastya/Narada and Lomasha, threading Bharata's holy fords _(actors: Yudhishthira, Lomasha, Narada/Pulastya)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva, Tirtha-yatra-parva)
- **Vaishampayana Curses Yajnavalkya to Disgorge the Yajus** — Vaishampayana, quarreling with his pupil Yajnavalkya, orders him to vomit up all the Yajus he was taught; the rejected knowledge is pecked up by disciples who became partridges (tittiri), forming the Taittiriya/Krishna Yajurveda _(actors: Vaishampayana, Yajnavalkya, the disciples)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/vedas/yajurveda.md) — (Vishnu Purana 3.5)
- **Arjuna Slays Karna** — Across the war Arjuna fells Bhishma, Jayadratha, and finally Karna with the Anjalika arrow when Karna's chariot-wheel sinks on the 17th day _(actors: Arjuna, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Karna Parva)
- **Duryodhana captured by the gandharva Citrasena** — During the Ghoṣa-yātrā Duryodhana clashes with the gandharva Citrasena, is defeated and taken prisoner, and is humiliatingly rescued by the very exiled Pāṇḍavas _(actors: Duryodhana, Citrasena, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva ~239-251)
- **Gandiva given to Arjuna by Varuna at the Khandava burning** — Arjuna's divine bow Gandiva, which slips from his hand in despair, was given by Varuna via Agni at the burning of the Khandava forest _(actors: Arjuna, Varuna, Agni)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/01-arjuna-vishada-yoga.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva)
- **Karna consecrated Kaurava commander-in-chief** — After Drona's death Duryodhana makes Karna the third Kaurava senapati for days 16-17, with Shalya as his sabotaging charioteer _(actors: Karna, Duryodhana, Shalya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva)
- **Kṛṣṇa Summons the Gaṅgā to Form Mānasī-Gaṅgā** — Kṛṣṇa mentally summons the Gaṅgā to purify the cowherds, forming the lake Mānasī-Gaṅgā at Govardhana _(actors: Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Vraja-mahatmya)
- **Lifting Mount Govardhana** — Krishna stops Indra-worship; enraged Indra sends torrential rain and Krishna lifts Govardhana on his finger for 7 days to shelter Vraja _(actors: Krishna, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.24-10.27)
- **Nala and Damayantī Reunited** — Through the children's recognition and his resumed radiant form, Nala reunites with Damayantī, the Wind-god affirming her chastity from the sky _(actors: Nala, Damayantī, Vāyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 74-76)
- **Post-war disclosure of Karṇa's parentage** — After victory Kuntī reveals Karṇa was her firstborn and the Pāṇḍavas' eldest brother; the horrified brothers perform his obsequies and Yudhiṣṭhira reproaches her concealment _(actors: Kuntī, Kṛṣṇa, Pāṇḍavas, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Strī/Śānti Parva)
- **Rasa-lila** — Krishna performs the circular moonlit dance with the gopis, the supreme devotional episode, with Radha as the foremost beloved _(actors: Krishna, the gopis, Radha)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.29-10.33)
- **Slaying of Pralambasura** — Balarama kills the demon disguised as a cowherd with a fist _(actors: Balarama, Pralambasura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.18)
- **Surya Reveals the Shukla Yajurveda to Yajnavalkya** — Stripped of his learning, Yajnavalkya worships Surya, who in horse form (vajin) — in some tellings as Narayana — teaches him a fresh, untransmitted White (Shukla) Yajus, named Vajasaneyi after the equine revelation _(actors: Yajnavalkya, Surya/Narayana)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/vedas/yajurveda.md) — (Vishnu Purana 3.5; Bhagavata Purana 12.6)
- **The House of Lac plot and the fire** — Duryodhana's combustible mansion at Varanavata is foiled by Vidura's coded warning and escape tunnel; the Pandavas fire it over Purocana and flee, deceiving the Kauravas into thinking them dead _(actors: Duryodhana, Purocana, Vidura, the Pandavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 142-148)
- **The wooden-bird test confirms Arjuna supreme** — Droṇa tests the princes by asking what each sees aiming at a wooden bird's eye; only Arjuna answers 'the eye alone' and shoots true, confirming him the supreme pupil _(actors: Droṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Vyāsa compiles and disseminates the Purāṇas** — Vyāsa composes the Purāṇas at the start of Kali Yuga and disseminates them through his pupil Romaharṣaṇa and the bard Ugraśravas to the Naimiṣa-forest sages _(actors: Vyāsa, Romaharṣaṇa, Ugraśravas)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-puranas.md) — (Purāṇic frame-tradition)
- **Arjuna surrenders as Krishna's disciple** — Stricken with faint-heartedness and confused about dharma, Arjuna formally takes refuge in Krishna as his disciple, converting his battlefield refusal into the teaching occasion of the Gita _(actors: Arjuna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/02-sankhya-yoga.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 2.7-9)
- **Babhruvahana Slays Arjuna** — During Yudhishthira's Ashvamedha, Arjuna's son Babhruvahana, goaded by Ulupi, looses an arrow that kills Arjuna, fulfilling the Vasus' curse _(actors: Babhruvahana, Arjuna, Ulupi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Ashvamedhika Parva ~80)
- **Bhima slays Hidimba; birth of Ghatotkaca** — Bhima kills the rakshasa Hidimba, weds his sister Hidimba, and she bears the mighty son Ghatotkaca who vows to come whenever needed _(actors: Bhima, Hidimba, Hidimba, Ghatotkaca)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 154-155)
- **Bhīṣma completes the discourse and dismisses the assembly** — Having covered rāja-, āpad-, and mokṣa-dharma, Bhīṣma blesses Yudhiṣṭhira and dismisses the assembly until the sun's northward turn, still alive on the arrow-bed _(actors: Bhīṣma, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/12-shanti-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 12.365)
- **Birth of Rādhā at Barsānā** — Rādhā (Rādhārāṇī) is born/found at Vṛṣabhānupura (Barsānā), daughter of King Vṛṣabhānu and Queen Kīrtidā _(actors: Rādhā, Vṛṣabhānu, Kīrtidā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Garga Samhita; Braj tradition)
- **Droṇa rejects Ekalavya and claims his thumb** — Droṇa refuses the Niṣāda prince Ekalavya instruction; when the self-taught boy surpasses Arjuna, Droṇa claims his right thumb as guru-dakṣiṇā to preserve Arjuna's primacy _(actors: Droṇa, Ekalavya, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~132)
- **Durvāsā's test averted by the Akṣaya-pātra** — At Duryodhana's instigation the sage Durvāsā with ten thousand disciples descends on the forest-Pāṇḍavas, but Kṛṣṇa saves them via a single grain in the Akṣaya-pātra _(actors: Duryodhana, Durvāsā, Draupadī, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva ~263)
- **Jayadratha abducts Draupadi** — Jayadratha carries off Draupadi from the hermitage; the Pandavas overtake and humiliate him, and he wins a boon from Shiva enabling Abhimanyu's later death _(actors: Jayadratha, Draupadi, Bhima, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 263-272)
- **Karna spares the four lesser Pandavas** — True to his vow, Karna defeats but spares Yudhishthira, Bhima, Nakula, and Sahadeva in turn _(actors: Karna, Yudhishthira, Bhima, Nakula, Sahadeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva)
- **Killing of Kamsa** — Summoned to Mathura, Krishna and Balarama break Kamsa's sacrificial bow, kill the elephant Kuvalayapida and the wrestlers Chanura and Mushtika, then Krishna drags Kamsa from his throne and kills him, restoring Ugrasena _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Kamsa, Ugrasena)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.42-10.44)
- **Kuntī's retirement and death in the forest fire** — In old age Kuntī retires to the forest with the Kuru elders and perishes with them in a wildfire _(actors: Kuntī, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Gāndhārī, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Mahābhārata, Āśramavāsika Parva)
- **Maya builds the assembly hall** — The spared asura-architect Maya raises a wondrous illusory maya-sabha at Indraprastha for Yudhishthira _(actors: Maya Danava, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva)
- **Nala Wins Back His Kingdom** — Armed with the dice-secret, Nala wins all back from Puṣkara at a single throw, spares him, and is restored to his throne with Damayantī _(actors: Nala, Puṣkara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nala-damayanti.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 77-78)
- **The vastra-harana (stealing the gopis' clothes)** — Krishna takes the bathing gopis' garments to a kadamba tree, teaching surrender of ego _(actors: Krishna, the gopis)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.22)
- **Yajnavalkya Teaches Maitreyi, Gargi, and Janaka** — The sage Yajnavalkya delivers his dialogues with Maitreyi, Gargi, and King Janaka in the Brihadaranyaka, articulating 'neti neti' and the early Atman=Brahman doctrine _(actors: Yajnavalkya, Maitreyi, Gargi, Janaka)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/vedas/yajurveda.md) — (Brihadaranyaka Upanishad)
- **Arjuna captures Drupada as guru-dakṣiṇā** — Droṇa demands the princes capture Drupada; the Kauravas fail but Arjuna defeats and drags him bound to Droṇa, who seizes half of Pāñcāla in repayment _(actors: Droṇa, Arjuna, Drupada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~137)
- **Arjuna Revived by the Nagamani** — Ulupi fetches the Naga Sanjivana gem and lays it on Arjuna's breast, reviving him, reconciled with his son _(actors: Ulupi, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Ashvamedhika Parva ~81)
- **Arjuna routs the Kauravas at the Virāṭa cattle-raid** — In the incognito year Duryodhana joins the Kaurava raid on Matsya's cattle; Arjuna as Bṛhannalā routs the whole host single-handed and strips off Duryodhana's robe as a trophy _(actors: Arjuna, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Virāṭa Parva ~46-67)
- **Death of Karna** — In the final duel Karna's curses converge as his wheel sinks and his astra-memory fails; on Krishna's word Arjuna beheads the defenseless Karna with the Anjalika arrow _(actors: Karna, Arjuna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva)
- **Exodus to Dvaraka** — After Jarasandha attacks Mathura seventeen times, Krishna builds and relocates the Yadavas to the sea-fortress Dvaraka, earning the epithet Ranachhoda _(actors: Krishna, Jarasandha, the Yadavas)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.50-10.52; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **Govardhana-dharana** — Krishna persuades the cowherds to abandon Indra-worship; the enraged Indra sends rain, and Krishna lifts Mount Govardhana on his finger for seven days as an umbrella _(actors: Krishna, Indra, the cowherds)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.24-25; Harivamsa; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **Kṛṣṇa Slays Narakāsura** — Narakāsura, son of Bhūmi and Varāha, tyrant of Prāgjyotiṣa who imprisoned 16,000 women and stole Aditi's earrings, is slain by Kṛṣṇa with Satyabhāmā — commemorated as Naraka Caturdaśī _(actors: Narakāsura, Kṛṣṇa, Satyabhāmā)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/asura-wars-and-roster.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.59)
- **Krishna begins the Gita teaching (Samkhya of the deathless self)** — Smiling, Krishna begins his discourse, teaching the imperishability of the atman, reincarnation as a change of garments, and the duty of the kshatriya, planting the seeds of nishkama-karma _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/02-sankhya-yoga.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 2.11-72)
- **Kuntī's prayer (Kunti-stuti)** — As Kṛṣṇa departs Hastināpura, Kuntī sings a hymn paradoxically asking for more calamities so her mind stays fixed on him — a touchstone of bhakti _(actors: Kuntī, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kunti.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 1.8)
- **Mudgala rejects heaven for Brahman** — The gleaner-sage refuses bodily ascent to heaven on learning it too is impermanent, choosing the imperishable Brahman _(actors: Mudgala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata-minor-episodes.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva, Markandeya-Samasya Parva)
- **Slaying of Ariṣṭāsura and Digging of the Twin Kuṇḍas** — To atone for slaying the bull-demon Ariṣṭāsura, Kṛṣṇa and Rādhā each dig a pond, creating the twin Rādhā-kuṇḍa and Śyāma-kuṇḍa, holiest tīrtha of Braj for Gauḍīyas _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Rādhā, Ariṣṭāsura)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Garga Samhita; Gaudiya tradition)
- **Śaṅkhacūḍa Beheaded by Śiva** — The Dānava Śaṅkhacūḍa, protected by his wife Tulsī/Vṛndā's chastity, terrorizes the gods until Kṛṣṇa/Viṣṇu breaks the vow and Śiva beheads him, his conch-jewel passing to Kṛṣṇa _(actors: Śaṅkhacūḍa, Śiva, Viṣṇu, Tulsī)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/asura-wars-and-roster.md) — (Brahmavaivarta Purāṇa; Devī Bhāgavata)
- **Arjuna defeats the gandharva Citraratha** — Crossing the Ganga at night, Arjuna defeats and spares the gandharva king, who teaches him celestial lore and gifts horses _(actors: Arjuna, Angaraparna/Citraratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 169-172)
- **Draupadī's svayaṃvara — the rotating-fish test** — Drupada sets a near-impossible mark; Arjuna, disguised as a brahmin, strings the colossal bow and pierces the eye of the revolving fish seen only in reflection, winning Draupadī's hand _(actors: Drupada, Arjuna, Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Droṇa accepts his destined killer Dhṛṣṭadyumna as pupil** — Told the fire-born Dhṛṣṭadyumna is fated to kill him, Droṇa nonetheless takes him as a pupil and teaches him all his science, knowing his own death-instrument _(actors: Droṇa, Dhṛṣṭadyumna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva ~155-169)
- **Duryodhana chooses Kṛṣṇa's army over Kṛṣṇa** — Offered a choice between Kṛṣṇa unarmed and his Nārāyaṇī army, Duryodhana eagerly takes the army while Arjuna takes Kṛṣṇa as charioteer — a fatal preference of force over the divine _(actors: Duryodhana, Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva ~7)
- **Ekalavya learns from a clay image and gives his thumb** — Ekalavya, refused tuition by Drona, learns archery by worshipping a clay image of him, surpasses Drona's students, and surrenders his right thumb as guru-dakshina _(actors: Ekalavya, Drona)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/guru-shishya-parampara.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva)
- **Ghaṭotkaca slays Alāyudha** — Ghaṭotkaca duels and kills rākṣasa champions including Alambuṣa and beheads Alāyudha, who had entered the war to avenge Hiḍimba, hurling the head before Duryodhana _(actors: Ghaṭotkaca, Alāyudha, Alambuṣa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ghatotkacha.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva)
- **Indra's submission and Govinda consecration** — Indra and the cow Surabhi consecrate Krishna as Govinda _(actors: Indra, Surabhi, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.27)
- **Jarasandha's invasions and founding of Dwaraka** — Jarasandha attacks Mathura 17 times; Krishna relocates the Yadavas to the sea-fortress Dwaraka, earning the name Ranachhoda _(actors: Krishna, Jarasandha, Kalayavana, Mucukunda)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.50-10.52; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **Kaṃsa-Vadha and Restoration of Ugrasena** — The grown Kṛṣṇa, summoned to Kaṃsa's bow-sacrifice, kills the wrestlers Cāṇūra and Muṣṭika, then drags Kaṃsa from his throne and slays him, restoring Ugrasena to the throne of Mathurā _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, Kaṃsa, Cāṇūra, Muṣṭika, Ugrasena)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/mathura-vrindavan-braj.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.42-44)
- **Krishna lifts Govardhana (recalled)** — The Varaha Purana's Govardhana-tirtha glorification recalls Krishna's lifting of Govardhana hill against Indra's rain _(actors: Krishna, Indra)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/varaha-purana.md) — (Varaha Purana)
- **Krishna teaches Karma-yoga and action as yajna** — Krishna delivers the Karma Yoga teaching, declaring action inescapable, framing all work as sacrifice (yajna) in the cosmic wheel begun by Prajapati, and naming desire and anger as the great enemy _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/03-to-06-karma-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 3)
- **Marriage to Rukmini** — Krishna elopes with Princess Rukmini of Vidarbha from the temple, defeating her brother Rukmi; their son is Pradyumna (Kamadeva reborn) _(actors: Krishna, Rukmini, Rukmi, Shishupala, Pradyumna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.52-10.54)
- **Posthumous revelation of Karna's parentage** — After the war Kunti reveals Karna was the Pandavas' eldest brother; the anguished Yudhishthira curses all women never again to keep a secret _(actors: Kunti, Yudhishthira, the Pandavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Stri Parva; Santi Parva)
- **Svetaki's sacrifice sickens Agni** — King Svetaki's colossal twelve-year sacrifice gorges Agni on ghee until he falls ill and loses his lustre and appetite; Brahma prescribes consuming the Khandava forest as the cure _(actors: Svetaki, Agni, Brahma)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/agni.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva)
- **The Anugita Spoken** — Back at Indraprastha Arjuna confesses the Gita has slipped from memory and Krishna retells its essence as the Anugita through ancient narratives _(actors: Arjuna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/arjuna.md) — (Mahabharata, Ashvamedhika Parva ~16)
- **Vyāsa dictates the Mahābhārata to Gaṇeśa** — Vyāsa composes the Mahābhārata, dictating it to Gaṇeśa who writes it down on the condition Vyāsa never pause, prompting knotty kūṭa verses _(actors: Vyāsa, Gaṇeśa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva)
- **Yamuna Parts at Krishna's Birth** — On the storm-night of Krishna's birth, the flooding Yamuna rises to touch his feet and parts to make a path as Vasudeva carries the infant to safety in Gokula _(actors: Krishna, Vasudeva, Yamuna, Shesha)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/yamuna-and-river-goddesses.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.3)
- **Yudhishthira's Rajasuya consecration** — After the four brothers' digvijaya conquest, Yudhishthira is consecrated samrat in a grand sacrifice, the high-water mark of Pandava fortune _(actors: Yudhishthira, Vyasa, the Pandavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva)
- **Agni recruits Krishna and Arjuna for Khandava** — Disguised as a Brahmana, Agni asks Krishna and Arjuna to help him burn the Khandava forest by holding off Indra's protective rain, since Indra repeatedly extinguishes the fire to shield the Naga Takshaka _(actors: Agni, Krishna, Arjuna, Indra)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/agni.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva)
- **Arjuna wins Draupadi at the svayamvara** — Disguised as a brahmin, Arjuna strings Drupada's bow and pierces the revolving fish-target to win Draupadi, after Karna is rebuffed _(actors: Arjuna, Draupadi, Drupada, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 186-190)
- **Bhīma slays Hiḍimba** — The cannibal rākṣasa Hiḍimba sends his sister to fetch the sleeping Pāṇḍavas as food; Bhīma kills him in a wrestling-match when she falls in love instead _(actors: Bhīma, Hiḍimba, Hiḍimbā)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Births of the Kuru line and the Pāṇḍavas and Kauravas** — The Ādi Parva narrates the births of Bhīṣma, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Pāṇḍu and the Pāṇḍavas and Kauravas, founding the rival lines _(actors: Bhīṣma, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Pāṇḍu, Pāṇḍavas, Kauravas)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 1 (Ādi Parva))
- **Duryodhana refuses the five villages** — When Yudhiṣṭhira reduces his claim to five villages to avert war, Duryodhana refuses to yield even a needle-point of land without war _(actors: Duryodhana, Kṛṣṇa, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva ~70-127)
- **Kaliya-Damana (Taming of the Serpent)** — The boy Krishna leaps into the Yamuna pool poisoned by the Naga Kaliya, dances on his many hoods, and banishes him to Ramanaka, restoring the river _(actors: Krishna, Kaliya, the Nagapatnis)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/yamuna-and-river-goddesses.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.16)
- **Karṇa spends the Vāsavī Śakti on Ghaṭotkaca** — On the 14th night Kṛṣṇa sends Ghaṭotkaca against the rampant Karṇa; forced to save his army, Karṇa hurls his once-only Vāsavī Śakti, killing Ghaṭotkaca but spending the dart hoarded for Arjuna _(actors: Ghaṭotkaca, Karṇa, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ghatotkacha.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva, Ghaṭotkaca-vadha Parva)
- **Karna surrenders his armor to Indra** — Warned by Surya, Karna cuts his inborn armor and earrings from his body and gives them to the disguised Indra, receiving the one-use Vasavi Shakti in exchange _(actors: Karna, Indra, Surya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 300-309)
- **Krishna declares the avatara doctrine (yada yada hi dharmasya)** — Krishna reveals that though birthless he descends by his own maya age after age whenever dharma declines, to protect the good, destroy the wicked, and re-establish dharma, the charter of the Dashavatara _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/03-to-06-karma-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 4.5-8)
- **Kuntī's accidental command and Draupadī's polyandrous marriage** — Kuntī unseeingly tells her sons to share what they brought; Yudhiṣṭhira rules Draupadī wife of all five and Vyāsa sanctions the unprecedented polyandry by revealing her prior-birth boons _(actors: Kuntī, Yudhiṣṭhira, Vyāsa, Draupadī, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Maya Builds the Mayasabhā** — The asura architect Maya, spared by Arjuna and Kṛṣṇa from the Khāṇḍava fire, builds the illusion-palace Mayasabhā for Yudhiṣṭhira at Indraprastha, where Duryodhana's humiliation seeds the Mahābhārata war _(actors: Maya, Yudhiṣṭhira, Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/asura-wars-and-roster.md) — (Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva)
- **Slaying of Narakasura and freeing of 16,100 women** — Krishna with Satyabhama slays the demon Narakasura and frees the 16,100 captive women (festival Naraka Chaturdashi) _(actors: Krishna, Satyabhama, Narakasura)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.58-10.59)
- **Slaying of Shishupala** — When Shishupala's insults exceed Krishna's vowed hundred pardons, Krishna beheads him with the Sudarshana Chakra and his radiance merges into Krishna _(actors: Krishna, Shishupala, Bhishma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shishupala.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva; Bhagavata Purana)
- **The Rasa-lila** — On the autumn full-moon night Krishna summons the gopis with his flute and multiplies himself to dance the circle-dance with each, the theological heart of bhakti-rasa _(actors: Krishna, the gopis)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.29-33)
- **Agni gifts divine weapons to Krishna and Arjuna** — To enable them, Agni (via Varuna) gives Arjuna the bow Gandiva, two inexhaustible quivers, and a divine chariot, and gives Krishna the Sudarshana Chakra and the mace Kaumodaki _(actors: Agni, Varuna, Arjuna, Krishna)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/agni.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva)
- **Akrura's mission to bring Krishna to Mathura** — Kamsa sends Akrura to lure Krishna and Balarama to Mathura for a bow sacrifice; Akrura sees Krishna as Vishnu in the Yamuna _(actors: Kamsa, Akrura, Krishna, Balarama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.38-39)
- **Ambopakhyana: Amba reborn as Shikhandin** — Scorned by Bhishma, Amba immolates herself to be reborn as Shikhandin, the instrument of Bhishma's fall _(actors: Amba, Bhishma, Shikhandin)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata-minor-episodes.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva, Ambopakhyana Parva)
- **Draupadi married to the five Pandavas** — On Kunti's accidental word and Yudhishthira's ruling, Vyasa sanctions Draupadi's marriage to all five brothers, justified by her former-birth boon from Shiva _(actors: Draupadi, the Pandavas, Vyasa, Kunti)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 196-199)
- **Duryodhana's plot to seize Kṛṣṇa and the Viśvarūpa** — Duryodhana plots to arrest Kṛṣṇa in court; Kṛṣṇa reveals his cosmic Viśvarūpa form before the assembly and departs, ending hope of peace _(actors: Duryodhana, Śakuni, Karṇa, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva ~129-130)
- **Ghaṭotkaca's dying body crushes an akṣauhiṇī** — Mortally struck, Ghaṭotkaca magnifies his body to colossal size and falls upon the Kaurava ranks, crushing a full akṣauhiṇī of troops in death _(actors: Ghaṭotkaca)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ghatotkacha.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva)
- **Hiḍimbā weds Bhīma; birth of Ghaṭotkaca** — The rākṣasī Hiḍimbā weds Bhīma by rākṣasa union and bears Ghaṭotkaca, then releases Bhīma to rejoin his brothers _(actors: Hiḍimbā, Bhīma, Ghaṭotkaca)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Krishna teaches the renunciation-of-action and meditation yogas** — Krishna reconciles renunciation with selfless action and delivers a practical meditation manual (posture, moderation, steadying the restless mind) plus the reassurance that the fallen yogi is reborn favorably and perfected across many births _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/03-to-06-karma-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 5-6)
- **Lac-house plot and Draupadī's svayaṃvara** — The Kauravas' lac-house (Lākṣāgṛha) assassination plot fails, and Draupadī's svayaṃvara leads to her marriage to the five Pāṇḍavas _(actors: Pāṇḍavas, Draupadī)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 1 (Ādi Parva))
- **Nārada sets the rotation rule for Draupadī** — Warning of the Sunda–Upasunda strife, Nārada decrees Draupadī dwell one year with each husband in turn, any intruder going into exile — the rule that later sends Arjuna wandering _(actors: Nārada, Draupadī, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Saubhari's Curse on Garuda** — The sage Saubhari curses Garuda so he cannot enter Vrindavana, which is why Kaliya was able to hide in the Yamuna there _(actors: Saubhari, Garuda, Kaliya)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/yamuna-and-river-goddesses.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.17; Harivamsha)
- **Syamantaka jewel and Jambavati** — Krishna clears himself of stealing the gem, fights bear-king Jambavan for 28 days, and wins Jambavati and Satyabhama _(actors: Krishna, Jambavan, Jambavati, Satyabhama, Satrajit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.56-10.57; Vishnu Purana 4)
