- **The game of dice and Draupadi's disrobing** — Shakuni's foul dice strip Yudhishthira of kingdom, brothers and Draupadi; Duhshasana drags her in but the disrobing is foiled by endless cloth _(actors: Yudhishthira, Shakuni, Duryodhana, Duhshasana, Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva)
- **The Yaksha Prashna at the lake** — After four brothers drink and fall dead, Yudhishthira answers the Yaksha's riddles on dharma; the Yaksha (Dharma in disguise) revives them and grants a boon for their coming disguise _(actors: Yudhishthira, the Yaksha/Dharma, the Pandavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 311-315)
- **Bhīma kills Duryodhana's ninety-nine brothers** — Bhīma systematically kills all ninety-nine Kaurava brothers, most gruesomely tearing open Duḥśāsana's chest and drinking his blood in fulfillment of his dice-hall vow _(actors: Bhīma, Duḥśāsana, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Karṇa Parva)
- **Bhīma slays Bakāsura** — At Ekacakrā, Bhīma in disguise eats the food meant for the extorting cannibal Baka and breaks his back, freeing the town _(actors: Bhīma, Baka)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Birth of the Upapāṇḍavas (Draupadeyas)** — Draupadī bears one son by each husband — Prativindhya, Sutasoma, Śrutakarman, Śatānīka, Śrutasena — all later slain asleep in Aśvatthāman's night-raid _(actors: Draupadī, the five Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva; Sauptika Parva)
- **Brahma-vimohana-lila — the Bewilderment of Brahma** — The Brahma of our universe steals child-Krishna's cowherds and calves; Krishna duplicates them all and reveals countless universes each with its own Brahma, humbling his pride _(actors: Brahma, Krishna)_ → [details](../00-time-and-cosmology/brahma-lifetime.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.13-14)
- **Burning of the Khandava forest** — Agni surrounds Khandava with his seven flames while Krishna and Arjuna ring the forest and shoot down every fleeing creature and beat back Indra and the gods until Agni is fully sated _(actors: Agni, Krishna, Arjuna, Indra)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/agni.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi Parva)
- **Founding of Indraprastha** — Dhritarashtra grants the Pandavas the wild tract Khandavaprastha, which they transform into the splendid capital Indraprastha _(actors: the Pandavas, Dhritarashtra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 199-207)
- **Kṛṣṇa rejoices that Karṇa is disarmed** — Kṛṣṇa alone rejoices and embraces Arjuna, explaining that with the once-only dart spent on Ghaṭotkaca, Karṇa can no longer kill Arjuna — the strategic pivot of the war _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ghatotkacha.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva)
- **Krishna reveals his two natures and the four kinds of devotees** — Krishna distinguishes his lower (material) and higher (conscious) prakritis, declares all is strung on him like pearls, names the four classes of devotees, and teaches that maya is crossed only by taking refuge in him _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/07-to-12-bhakti-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 7)
- **Krishna straightens Kubja** — Krishna straightens the deformed maidservant who gives him sandalwood _(actors: Krishna, Trivakra/Kubja)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.42)
- **Krishna Weds Kalindi (Yamuna)** — The river-goddess Kalindi does austerities longing to wed Vishnu; Krishna finds her and takes her as the fourth of his eight chief queens, who bears him ten sons _(actors: Krishna, Kalindi, Arjuna)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/yamuna-and-river-goddesses.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.58)
- **Lakshagriha (House of Lac) escape** — Duryodhana plots to burn the Pandavas alive in a lac palace at Varanavata; warned by Vidura, they escape via a tunnel and fake their deaths _(actors: Duryodhana, the Pandavas, Vidura)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Slaying Narakasura and freeing the 16,100** — Krishna with Satyabhama slays Narakasura of Pragjyotisha and frees 16,100 captive women, later accepting them as wives _(actors: Krishna, Satyabhama, Narakasura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.59; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **The dice game and Draupadī's disrobing** — In the rigged dice match against Śakuni, Yudhiṣṭhira loses kingdom, brothers, self, and Draupadī, whose attempted disrobing is foiled by Kṛṣṇa's miracle, leading to 13 years' exile _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Śakuni, Duryodhana, Draupadī, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 2 (Sabhā Parva))
- **The terrible vows and the 13-year exile sentence** — Bhima vows to drink Duhshasana's blood and break Duryodhana's thigh; the second dice game sends the Pandavas to 12 years' exile plus one incognito _(actors: Bhima, Draupadi, Duryodhana, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/02-sabha-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva)
- **Añjanaparvan slain by Aśvatthāman** — Ghaṭotkaca's son Añjanaparvan is slain by Aśvatthāman during the war _(actors: Añjanaparvan, Aśvatthāman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ghatotkacha.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva)
- **Arjuna's self-exile and marriages to Ulupi and Citrangada** — Arjuna breaks Narada's rule and exiles himself on pilgrimage, wedding the naga princess Ulupi (mother of Iravan) and Citrangada (mother of Babhruvahana) _(actors: Arjuna, Ulupi, Citrangada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 213-219)
- **Bhīma slays Kirmīra** — Kirmīra, brother of Baka, ambushes the Pāṇḍavas entering the Kāmyaka forest to avenge his kin and is killed bare-handed by Bhīma _(actors: Bhīma, Kirmīra)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva)
- **Breaking the bow and killing Kuvalayapida** — Krishna snaps Kamsa's great bow and slays the rogue elephant Kuvalayapida at the arena gate _(actors: Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.42-43)
- **Draupadi's svayamvara** — Arjuna in Brahmin disguise wins Draupadi by stringing the bow and hitting the fish-eye target; by Kunti's word and a past boon Draupadi becomes the common wife of all five Pandavas _(actors: Arjuna, Draupadi, the Pandavas, Drupada)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Duryodhana hides in Dvaipāyana lake** — After his army's annihilation the weaponless Duryodhana solidifies the lake's waters by yogic power and hides submerged until the Pāṇḍavas taunt him out _(actors: Duryodhana, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Śalya Parva ~28-32)
- **Krishna teaches death, the kalpa-clock, and the two after-death paths** — Krishna teaches that the last thought at death determines one's destiny, reckons Brahma's day and night as a thousand yugas with the cosmic out-breath and in-breath of beings, and names the bright devayana and dark pitryana routes _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/07-to-12-bhakti-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 8)
- **The Pāṇḍavas' forest exile** — The Pāṇḍavas spend twelve years in the forest amid pilgrimages and sub-tales; Arjuna performs penance and wins divine weapons _(actors: Pāṇḍavas, Arjuna)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 3 (Vana Parva))
- **The six survivors of Khandava; Maya spared** — Khandava's destruction spares only six, including Takshaka's son Ashvasena, the architect-demon Maya (saved by Arjuna), and four sharngaka birds; the spared Maya later builds the Mayasabha at Indraprastha _(actors: Maya, Arjuna, Ashvasena, Mandapala's chicks)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/agni.md) — (Mahabharata, Adi & Sabha Parva)
- **War with Banasura over Aniruddha and Usha** — Krishna's grandson Aniruddha weds Usha; Krishna battles the asura Bana and Shiva's forces _(actors: Krishna, Aniruddha, Usha, Banasura, Shiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.62-10.63)
- **Abduction of Subhadra** — With Krishna's blessing Arjuna carries off Subhadra; their son is Abhimanyu _(actors: Arjuna, Subhadra, Krishna, Abhimanyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 220-223)
- **Arjuna in Indra's heaven and Urvashi's curse** — Arjuna ascends to Amaravati for celestial weapons; spurning the apsara Urvashi, he is cursed to be a eunuch for a year of his choosing _(actors: Arjuna, Indra, Urvashi, Citrasena)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva)
- **Bhīma slays Jaṭāsura** — Jaṭāsura, disguised as a brahmin, abducts Yudhiṣṭhira, Nakula, Sahadeva and Draupadī and is slain by Bhīma on his return _(actors: Bhīma, Jaṭāsura)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva)
- **Birth of Kṛṣṇa in Kamsa's prison** — Kṛṣṇa is born to Vasudeva and Devaki in Kamsa's prison and is smuggled to Nanda and Yasoda in Gokula _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Vasudeva, Devaki, Kamsa, Nanda, Yasoda)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10)
- **Kamsa's Tyranny and Krishna's Birth** — Following the prophecy that Devaki's eighth son will kill Kamsa, Krishna is born in prison and raised in Gokula/Vrindavana _(actors: Kamsa, Krishna, Devaki, Vasudeva)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Bhagavata X; Harivamsha; Vishnu Purana V)
- **Killing of Chanura and Mushtika** — Krishna and Balarama kill Kamsa's champion wrestlers in the arena _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Chanura, Mushtika)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.44)
- **Krishna proclaims the sovereign secret and accessible devotion** — Krishna declares the king of sciences, his immanent-yet-transcendent pervasion of all, that he accepts a leaf-flower-fruit-water offered in love, and that even the lowly-born and worst sinner who worship him reach the supreme goal _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/07-to-12-bhakti-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 9)
- **The mace-duel and the foul thigh-blow** — In a fair mace-duel Duryodhana is the superior fighter until Kṛṣṇa signals Bhīma, who strikes below the navel and shatters Duryodhana's thighs, the forbidden blow that fulfills his vow _(actors: Duryodhana, Bhīma, Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Śalya Parva ~57-58)
- **The year in disguise at Virāṭa's court** — In the 13th incognito year the Pāṇḍavas serve King Virāṭa; Bhīma kills Kīcaka and Arjuna as Bṛhannalā repels the Kaurava cattle-raid _(actors: Pāṇḍavas, Bhīma, Kīcaka, Arjuna)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 4 (Virāṭa Parva))
- **Death of Ghaṭotkaca by Karṇa's Śakti** — At Kurukṣetra Ghaṭotkaca is slain on the 14th night by Karṇa's one-use divine Śakti weapon, which Karṇa had hoarded for Arjuna, thereby saving Arjuna's life _(actors: Ghaṭotkaca, Karṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva)
- **Death of Shishupala** — At the Rajasuya, Shishupala insults Krishna past 100 forgiven offenses and is beheaded by the Sudarshana chakra _(actors: Krishna, Shishupala, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.74; Mahabharata Sabha Parva)
- **Duryodhana anoints Aśvatthāman commander** — The half-dead Duryodhana's final act is to anoint Aśvatthāman commander, who that night slaughters the sleeping Pāṇḍava camp _(actors: Duryodhana, Aśvatthāman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Śalya/Sauptika Parvas)
- **Karna surrenders his armour to Indra** — Indra, disguised as a brahmin, begs away Karna's invulnerable kavacha-kundala, giving in exchange the one-use Vasavi Shakti dart _(actors: Karna, Indra, Surya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva)
- **Kṛṣṇa's demon-slayings in childhood** — The infant and boy Kṛṣṇa slays Putana, Tṛnavarta, Sakatasura, Aghasura, Bakasura, and Dhenukasura _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Putana, Aghasura, Bakasura)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10)
- **Kṛṣṇa's failed peace embassy** — Kṛṣṇa's peace mission to Hastināpura fails when Duryodhana refuses even five villages, and the armies muster for war; Karṇa's parentage is revealed to him _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Duryodhana, Karṇa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 5 (Udyoga Parva))
- **Krishna, 8th Avatara of Vishnu** — Krishna slays Kamsa, founds Dvaraka, allies with the Pandavas and teaches the Bhagavad Gita; his departure marks the start of Kali Yuga in 3102 BCE _(actors: Krishna)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Bhagavata; Harivamsha; Mahabharata)
- **Krishna declares his divine glories (Vibhuti Yoga)** — Krishna enumerates his vibhutis as the foremost of every class (Vishnu among Adityas, the Ganges among rivers, the letter A, Time itself), declaring he supports the whole universe with a single fragment of himself _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/07-to-12-bhakti-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 10)
- **Rajasuya sacrifice and Duryodhana's humiliation** — Yudhishthira performs the imperial Rajasuya sacrifice; Duryodhana, humiliated in the illusory hall, nurses revenge _(actors: Yudhishthira, Duryodhana, Draupadi)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva)
- **Slaying of Kamsa** — Krishna drags Kamsa by the hair and kills him, freeing his parents and reinstating the deposed king Ugrasena _(actors: Krishna, Kamsa, Ugrasena)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.44; Harivamsa; Vishnu Purana 5)
- **The burning of the Khandava forest** — Agni, to cure his indigestion, devours the Khandava forest while Krishna and Arjuna (newly armed with Gandiva and Sudarsana) ring the blaze and repel Indra and the gods _(actors: Agni, Krishna, Arjuna, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 224-227)
- **Duryodhana's death and flower-rain** — Felled and broken, Duryodhana accuses Kṛṣṇa of every deceit and proclaims he dies a true kṣatriya bound for heroes' heaven; a flower-rain from the gods falls on him _(actors: Duryodhana, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Śalya Parva ~60)
- **Education under Sandipani and recovery of his son** — Krishna and Balarama study at Avanti and as guru-dakshina recover Sandipani's drowned son from the demon Panchajana and Yama's realm _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Sandipani)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.45)
- **Ghaṭotkaca slays Alambuṣa** — Alambuṣa, son of Jaṭāsura, fights for the Kauravas at Kurukṣetra to avenge his father and is finally killed by Ghaṭotkaca _(actors: Alambuṣa, Ghaṭotkaca)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva)
- **Kaliya-damana** — Kṛṣṇa subdues the venomous serpent Kaliya in the Yamuna, dancing on its hoods _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Kaliya)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10)
- **Krishna grants Arjuna the divine eye to behold the Vishvarupa** — At Arjuna's request Krishna confers a divya-cakshus and reveals his Universal Form containing all worlds, gods, and infinite radiance like a thousand suns _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/07-to-12-bhakti-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 11.1-14)
- **Maya Danava spared at Khandava** — The asura-architect Maya is spared by Arjuna at Krishna's word and pledges to build the assembly hall at Indraprastha _(actors: Maya Danava, Arjuna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/01-adi-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva 227-236)
- **Sudama's visit** — Krishna's poor Brahmin friend Sudama brings flattened rice; Krishna honors him and silently grants him wealth _(actors: Krishna, Sudama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.80-10.81)
- **The Bhagavad Gītā is spoken** — Before battle Kṛṣṇa delivers the Bhagavad Gītā to Arjuna, synthesizing karma-, bhakti- and jñāna-yoga — the pivotal teaching of the epic _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 6 (Bhīṣma Parva), Bhagavad Gītā)
- **The game of dice** — Shakuni plays dice for Duryodhana and Yudhishthira gambles away wealth, kingdom, his brothers, himself, and finally Draupadi _(actors: Shakuni, Duryodhana, Yudhishthira, Draupadi)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva)
- **The Yadava Self-Destruction (Mausala)** — Cursed by sages, the Vrishnis kill each other with iron maces at Prabhasa, Krishna and Balarama depart, and Dvaraka sinks into the sea, ending the Yadava line _(actors: the Vrishnis, Krishna, Balarama)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva; Bhagavata XI)
- **Bhīṣma's abduction of the Kāśī princesses (rākṣasa-vivāha exemplar)** — Bhīṣma carries off Ambā, Ambikā and Ambālikā by force, the epic exemplar of the rākṣasa marriage-by-capture sanctioned for kṣatriyas _(actors: Bhīṣma, Ambā, Ambikā, Ambālikā)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Mahābhārata; Manusmṛti 3.24)
- **Draupadi's disrobing** — Duhshasana drags Draupadi by the hair and attempts to strip her, but Krishna miraculously extends her sari; Bhima vows to break Duryodhana's thigh and drink Duhshasana's blood _(actors: Duhshasana, Draupadi, Krishna, Bhima, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Sabha Parva)
- **Duryodhana seen enthroned in heaven** — Ascending to heaven, Yudhiṣṭhira first sees Duryodhana radiant among the heroes; Nārada explains he earned svarga by dying fearlessly in battle, fulfilling kṣatriya-dharma _(actors: Duryodhana, Yudhiṣṭhira, Nārada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Svargārohaṇa Parva ~1-3)
- **Dushyanta and Shakuntala** — King Dushyanta meets and marries Shakuntala in Kanva's hermitage; after a ring-curse of forgetting, their son becomes the emperor Bharata _(actors: Dushyanta, Shakuntala, Bharata)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva; Abhijnanashakuntala)
- **Fall of Bhīṣma** — After ten days as commander, Bhīṣma falls onto the bed of arrows at Kurukṣetra _(actors: Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 6 (Bhīṣma Parva))
- **House of Lac escape** — Duryodhana plots to burn the Pandavas in a lac palace at Varanavata; they escape via a tunnel and fake their deaths _(actors: Duryodhana, Pandavas, Vidura)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Jarasandha's invasions of Mathura** — Kamsa's father-in-law Jarasandha attacks Mathura seventeen times and is repelled _(actors: Jarasandha, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.50; Harivamsa)
- **Kṛṣṇa lifts Mount Govardhana** — Kṛṣṇa lifts Mount Govardhana to shelter Vṛndavana, displacing Indra-worship with devotion _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Indra)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10)
- **The Anu-gita delivered** — After the war Krishna gives Arjuna a different, more austere restatement of the Gita's teaching, framed by inset tales _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata-minor-episodes.md) — (Mahabharata Ashvamedhika Parva, Anugita Parva)
- **The Vishvarurupa devours the warriors; Krishna declares 'I am Time'** — Arjuna beholds Bhishma, Drona, Karna and the kings rushing into the flaming mouths of the terrible form, and Krishna reveals himself as Time the destroyer of worlds, the warriors already slain, bidding Arjuna be the mere instrument _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/07-to-12-bhakti-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 11.15-34)
- **Bharata, the Eponymous Emperor** — The cakravartin Bharata, after whom Bharata-varsha (India) is named, gives rise to the Bharata clan _(actors: Bharata)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Mahabharata; Vishnu Purana)
- **Death of Abhimanyu and slaying of Droṇa** — Abhimanyu is killed in the cakra-vyūha and Jayadratha is slain; Droṇa is killed via the 'Aśvatthāmā is dead' ruse _(actors: Abhimanyu, Droṇa, Jayadratha)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 7 (Droṇa Parva))
- **Founding of Dwaraka and death of Kalayavana** — Facing Kalayavana, Krishna withdraws and builds the island-city Dwaraka, luring Kalayavana into the cave of the sleeping Mucukunda whose gaze incinerates him _(actors: Krishna, Kalayavana, Mucukunda)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.50-52)
- **Kṛṣṇa carries off Rukmiṇī (rākṣasa-vivāha exemplar)** — Kṛṣṇa's carrying-off of Rukmiṇī is cited as the epic exemplar of marriage by seizure permitted to the warrior class _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Rukmiṇī)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10)
- **Krishna offered Krishna-unarmed vs his army** — Arjuna chooses the unarmed non-combatant Krishna as charioteer while Duryodhana takes the Narayani army _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva)
- **Krishna withdraws the cosmic form and resumes his human shape** — At the awe-struck Arjuna's plea, Krishna withdraws the terrible cosmic form, shows the four-armed Vishnu form, then resumes his gentle two-armed human shape, declaring this vision attainable by undivided devotion alone _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/07-to-12-bhakti-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 11.45-55)
- **The half-golden mongoose at the Ashvamedha** — A half-golden mongoose declares Yudhishthira's imperial Ashvamedha worth less than a starving family's gift of barley-flour _(actors: the mongoose, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata-minor-episodes.md) — (Mahabharata Ashvamedhika Parva 90)
- **The thirteen-year exile** — The Pandavas spend twelve years in the forest plus one year incognito at King Virata's court in disguised roles _(actors: the Pandavas, Draupadi, Virata)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Vana and Virata Parvas)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira cremates Duryodhana** — After the war Yudhiṣṭhira himself performs the funeral rites for Duryodhana and the fallen Kauravas amid Gāndhārī's grief _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Gāndhārī, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/duryodhana.md) — (Mahābhārata Strī/Śānti Parvas)
- **Abduction of Rukmini** — Krishna abducts Rukmini from her own wedding to Sisupala at her secret request and defeats her brother Rukmi _(actors: Krishna, Rukmini, Rukmi, Sisupala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.52-54)
- **Arjuna kills Karṇa** — With his chariot wheel sunk, Karṇa is slain by Arjuna _(actors: Arjuna, Karṇa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 8 (Karṇa Parva))
- **Arjuna obtains the Pashupatastra** — During the forest exile Arjuna performs penance and obtains the Pashupatastra from Shiva (the Kiratarjuniya episode) and celestial weapons from the gods _(actors: Arjuna, Shiva)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva)
- **Bhishma's Vow of Celibacy** — Shantanu's son Devavrata vows lifelong celibacy and renounces the throne so his father can marry Satyavati, becoming Bhishma with the boon of death-at-will _(actors: Bhishma, Shantanu, Satyavati)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Bhishma teaches Yudhishthira on ahimsa** — Bhishma, on his deathbed, instructs Yudhishthira that non-violence is the highest dharma in the Anushasana Parva _(actors: Bhishma, Yudhishthira)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/ahimsa-and-hindu-ethics.md) — (Mahabharata, Anushasana Parva 13.116.1; 13.117.37-38)
- **Droṇa appointed Kaurava commander after Bhīṣma's fall** — On day 11, after Bhīṣma falls, Droṇa is made Kaurava senāpati and holds the post for five days, vowing to capture Yudhiṣṭhira alive _(actors: Droṇa, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva)
- **Kṛṣṇa slays Kamsa** — Kṛṣṇa goes to Mathura and slays the tyrant king Kamsa _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Kamsa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10)
- **Krishna revives Parikshit in the womb** — Krishna revives the still-born Parikshit struck by Ashvatthaman's Brahmastra, saving the dynastic line _(actors: Krishna, Parikshit, Ashvatthaman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata-minor-episodes.md) — (Mahabharata Ashvamedhika Parva)
- **Krishna's peace embassy and the Vishvarupa** — Krishna's plea for five villages is rejected by Duryodhana; when seized he manifests his cosmic universal form, and the embassy fails _(actors: Krishna, Duryodhana, Dhritarashtra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva)
- **Krishna teaches the supremacy of devotion and the beloved devotee** — Krishna affirms that worship of the personal Lord is easier and superior to meditation on the formless Absolute, gives a descending ladder of practice, and portrays the qualities of the devotee dear to him _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/07-to-12-bhakti-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 12)
- **Pandavas retrieve weapons from the shami tree** — On Vijayadashami the Pandavas recover their hidden weapons from the shami tree, ending their incognito year, basis of Shami-puja _(actors: Pandavas)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Mahabharata, Virata Parva)
- **Sun cures Samba of leprosy; founding of the Multan sun-temple** — Samba, Krishna's son, is cured of leprosy by the Sun and founds the great sun-temple at Mulasthana/Multan, introducing the Maga Brahmin sun-priests into Hindu society _(actors: Samba, Surya, Maga Brahmanas)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/upapuranas-overview.md) — (Samba Purana)
- **The rigged dice game and staking of Draupadī** — Śakuni plays for Duryodhana; Yudhiṣṭhira loses kingdom, wealth, brothers, himself, and finally stakes and loses Draupadī _(actors: Śakuni, Duryodhana, Yudhiṣṭhira, Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva)
- **Building of Dvaraka** — Amid conflicts with Jarasandha and Kalayavana, Kṛṣṇa builds the fortress-city of Dvaraka _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Jarasandha, Kalayavana)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10)
- **Draupadī dragged into the assembly by Duḥśāsana** — Duḥśāsana seizes the menstruating Draupadī, clad in a single cloth, by the hair and hauls her into the men's assembly _(actors: Duḥśāsana, Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva)
- **Fall of Duryodhana in the mace-duel** — Śalya is killed and Bhīma smashes Duryodhana's thigh in the mace-duel, mortally wounding him on the war's final day _(actors: Bhīma, Duryodhana, Śalya)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 9 (Śalya Parva))
- **Karna's parentage revealed; the vow to spare four** — Krishna and Kunti reveal Karna is Kunti's firstborn; loyal to Duryodhana he refuses the throne but vows to kill only Arjuna among the brothers _(actors: Karna, Krishna, Kunti, Surya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva)
- **Krishna's failed peace embassy** — Krishna goes as peace envoy but Duryodhana refuses to yield even five villages and tries to seize Krishna, who reveals his cosmic Vishvarupa, making war inevitable _(actors: Krishna, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva)
- **Krishna teaches the field and its knower (kshetra/kshetrajna)** — Krishna distinguishes the body-field from its conscious knower, names himself the universal knower of all fields, and describes the indwelling supreme Witness whose right perception yields liberation _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/13-to-18-jnana-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 13)
- **The Cakravyūha and death of Abhimanyu** — Droṇa marshals the wheel-formation; Abhimanyu, knowing only how to break in, is sealed inside by Jayadratha and killed many-against-one by a coordinated assault against the rules of combat _(actors: Droṇa, Abhimanyu, Jayadratha, Karṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva ~34-48)
- **The Syamantaka jewel and marriages to Jambavati and Satyabhama** — Krishna fights the bear-king Jambavan 28 days to recover the Syamantaka gem, winning Jambavati, and is given Satyabhama by the vindicated Satrajit _(actors: Krishna, Jambavan, Jambavati, Satyabhama, Satrajit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.56-57; Vishnu Purana 4)
- **Vyasa's Niyoga Fathers the Kuru Heirs** — After Vicitravirya dies heirless, sage Vyasa fathers Dhritarashtra, Pandu, and Vidura by niyoga, continuing the dynasty _(actors: Vyasa, Dhritarashtra, Pandu, Vidura)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Arjuna's vow fulfilled — Jayadratha beheaded** — On day 14 Arjuna vows to kill Jayadratha before sundown; despite Droṇa's shielding formations, Kṛṣṇa's feigned sunset draws Jayadratha out and Arjuna beheads him _(actors: Arjuna, Jayadratha, Kṛṣṇa, Droṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva ~121)
- **Aśvatthāmā's night massacre and curse** — Aśvatthāmā, Kṛpa and Kṛtavarmā slaughter the sleeping Pāṇḍava camp; the Brahmāstra duel ensues and Aśvatthāmā is cursed _(actors: Aśvatthāmā, Kṛpa, Kṛtavarmā)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 10 (Sauptika Parva))
- **Commanders named and rules of war agreed** — Bhishma is made Kaurava senapati and Dhrishtadyumna the Pandava commander; dharma-yuddha conventions are settled and Yuyutsu defects _(actors: Bhishma, Dhrishtadyumna, Yuyutsu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva)
- **Divine Paternity of the Pandavas** — Pandu cursed to die if he embraces a wife, Kunti's mantra summons gods to father Yudhishthira, Bhima, and Arjuna, while Madri bears Nakula and Sahadeva by the Ashvins _(actors: Kunti, Madri, Pandu, the Pandavas)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Mahabharata Adi Parva)
- **Draupadī's unanswerable dharma question** — Draupadī demands whether Yudhiṣṭhira, having already lost himself, could lawfully wager her; the silent sabhā cannot rule, and only Vikarṇa speaks in her defense _(actors: Draupadī, Vikarṇa, Bhīṣma, Droṇa, Vidura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva)
- **Kṛṣṇa's marriages and defeat of Narakasura** — Kṛṣṇa marries Rukmini, Satyabhama, and the 16,100, and defeats the demon Narakasura _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Rukmini, Satyabhama, Narakasura)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10)
- **Krishna delivers the Bhagavad Gita** — On the battlefield, as Arjuna collapses in despair at fighting kin, Krishna delivers the 700-verse Bhagavad Gita teaching karma, bhakti, and jnana yoga and revealing his universal form, and Arjuna resolves to fight _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva 23-40)
- **Krishna teaches the three gunas and the guna-transcender** — Krishna expounds how sattva, rajas, and tamas bind the embodied self and shape rebirth, and portrays the gunatita who, by unswerving devotion, crosses the gunas to attain immortality _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/13-to-18-jnana-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 14)
- **Slaying of Narakasura and freeing the 16,100** — Krishna with Satyabhama on Garuda slays Narakasura of Pragjyotisha and frees the 16,100 imprisoned royal women, later taking them as wives _(actors: Krishna, Satyabhama, Narakasura)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.59; Vishnu Purana 5; Harivamsa)
- **Amba/Shikhandin back-story** — Abducted and rejected, Amba immolates herself vowing Bhishma's death and is reborn as Drupada's child Shikhandin who becomes male _(actors: Amba, Bhishma, Shikhandin, Shiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/05-udyoga-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga/Adi Parva)
- **Destruction of the Yadava clan** — A brahmana-curse causes the Yadava clan to annihilate itself in a drunken brawl at Prabhasa _(actors: Yadavas)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11)
- **Fall of Bhishma** — On the tenth day, with Shikhandi shielding Arjuna, Bhishma falls on a bed of arrows, choosing to wait for the winter solstice to die _(actors: Bhishma, Arjuna, Shikhandi)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva)
- **Gāndhārī curses Kṛṣṇa** — Mourning the dead, Gāndhārī curses Kṛṣṇa, foreshadowing the ruin of the Yādavas _(actors: Gāndhārī, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 11 (Strī Parva))
- **Krishna teaches the inverted cosmic tree and Purushottama** — Krishna figures samsara as an inverted ashvattha tree to be cut by detachment, traces the jiva as an eternal fragment of himself, and names himself Purushottama, the Supreme Person beyond the perishable and imperishable _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/13-to-18-jnana-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 15)
- **Kurukshetra War and Revival of Parikshit** — The 18-day war nearly annihilates the Kuru host; Parikshit, Arjuna's grandson, is revived in the womb by Krishna and becomes the post-war king opening the historical Kali Yuga _(actors: the Pandavas, Kauravas, Parikshit, Krishna)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Mahabharata; Bhagavata I)
- **The 13-year exile** — The Pandavas spend 12 years in forest exile plus 1 year incognito at King Virata's court _(actors: Pandavas, Draupadi, Virata)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Vana, Virata Parva)
- **The endless cloth — Kṛṣṇa's grace at the disrobing** — As Duḥśāsana pulls at her single garment, it multiplies without end until he collapses exhausted and Draupadī stays covered — in devotional tellings Kṛṣṇa supplies the infinite cloth _(actors: Duḥśāsana, Draupadī, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva)
- **The Nārāyaṇāstra crisis neutralized** — In grief and rage Droṇa and Aśvatthāman unleash the Nārāyaṇāstra; Kṛṣṇa saves the army by having all warriors throw down their weapons, neutralizing it _(actors: Droṇa, Aśvatthāman, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva ~199)
- **The Parijata episode** — To please Satyabhama, Krishna takes the celestial Parijata tree from Indra's heaven, defeating Indra _(actors: Krishna, Satyabhama, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.59; Harivamsa)
- **Bhīma's and Draupadī's vows at the dice-hall** — Bhīma swears to break Duḥśāsana's chest and drink his blood and to smash Duryodhana's thigh; Draupadī vows to leave her hair unbound until she binds it with Duḥśāsana's blood _(actors: Bhīma, Draupadī, Duḥśāsana, Duryodhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva)
- **Bhīṣma teaches dharma and dies at the solstice** — On his arrow-bed Bhīṣma instructs Yudhiṣṭhira on rāja-, āpad- and mokṣa-dharma and delivers the Viṣṇu Sahasranāma, then departs at the winter solstice _(actors: Bhīṣma, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 12–13 (Śānti, Anuśāsana Parvas))
- **Birth and return of Pradyumna** — Krishna and Rukmini's son, Kamadeva reborn, is stolen by Shambara, raised by Mayavati, and returns to slay him _(actors: Pradyumna, Shambara, Mayavati)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.55)
- **Death of Abhimanyu** — On the thirteenth day Arjuna's son Abhimanyu penetrates the Chakravyuha but cannot exit and is cornered and killed by six maharathis _(actors: Abhimanyu, Drona, Karna, Duryodhana, Jayadratha)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva)
- **Dvapara Yuga — the Age of Half Dharma** — Virtue and vice balance evenly; disease and discord appear and Vyasa divides the Vedas as human intellect weakens, with humans living ~1,000 years _(actors: Vyasa, Krishna, humanity)_ → [details](../00-time-and-cosmology/yugas-and-mahayuga.md) — (Vishnu Purana 1.3, Bhagavata Purana 1.4, 12.3)
- **Krishna contrasts divine and demonic natures** — Krishna catalogs the divine and demonic endowments, portrays the demonic mentality and its fate of being hurled into ever-lower wombs, and names lust, anger, and greed as the threefold gate to hell _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/13-to-18-jnana-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 16)
- **Krishna's Birth and the Slaying of Kamsa's Demons** — Devaki's eighth child Krishna is carried across the river to Yashoda and as an infant slays Putana, Trinavarta, Shakatasura and the Yamala-Arjuna twins _(actors: Krishna, Devaki, Yashoda, Putana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.1-11)
- **The Bhagavad Gita delivered** — Arjuna's despondency between the armies triggers Krishna's 700-verse teaching on dharma, karma-yoga and bhakti, restoring his resolve _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna, Sanjaya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva; Bhagavad Gita)
- **The lie of Aśvatthāman's death** — Bhīma slays an elephant named Aśvatthāman and Yudhiṣṭhira confirms 'Aśvatthāman is dead — the elephant,' the qualifier drowned by Kṛṣṇa's conches, breaking Droṇa; Yudhiṣṭhira's floating chariot thereafter touches earth _(actors: Bhīma, Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa, Droṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva ~191)
- **Uddhava Gita delivered** — Kṛṣṇa delivers his final teaching, the Uddhava Gita (Hamsa Gita), to his friend Uddhava on jnana, bhakti, and renunciation _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Uddhava)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11)
- **Aniruddha weds Usha and the war with Banasura** — Krishna's grandson Aniruddha weds Usha, daughter of the asura Bana, and Krishna battles Bana and Shiva's forces _(actors: Aniruddha, Usha, Banasura, Krishna, Shiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.62-63)
- **Arjuna's despondency** — Surveying his kin arrayed for battle, Arjuna's limbs fail and his bow slips; he refuses to fight, posing the Gita's central crisis _(actors: Arjuna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 1; Mahabharata Bhishma Parva)
- **Death of Iravan** — On day 8 Arjuna's naga-born son Iravan fights brilliantly but is beheaded by the rakshasa Alambusha _(actors: Iravan, Alambusha, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva)
- **Death of Jayadratha** — Arjuna vows to kill Jayadratha by sunset; Krishna conceals the sun to draw him out then reveals it, and Arjuna beheads him _(actors: Arjuna, Jayadratha, Krishna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva)
- **Dhṛtarāṣṭra grants Draupadī two boons** — Frightened by evil omens, the blind Dhṛtarāṣṭra grants Draupadī two boons, freeing Yudhiṣṭhira and then all the Pāṇḍavas with their arms; she refuses a third _(actors: Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Draupadī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva)
- **Droṇa beheaded by Dhṛṣṭadyumna** — Crushed by the false report, Droṇa lays down his bow and sits in yoga; Dhṛṣṭadyumna seizes the defenseless brahmin by the hair and severs his head, fulfilling his birth-purpose _(actors: Droṇa, Dhṛṣṭadyumna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Droṇa Parva ~192-193)
- **Kaliya-damana and the Lifting of Govardhana** — Krishna subdues the serpent Kaliya, swallows the forest-fire, slays Aghasura, Bakasura and Dhenukasura, and lifts Mount Govardhana to snub Indra _(actors: Krishna, Kaliya, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.12-27)
- **Kṛṣṇa's disappearance, struck by Jara** — Kṛṣṇa is struck in the heel by the hunter Jara and departs the earth, marking the full onset of Kali Yuga _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Jara)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11)
- **Krishna teaches the three kinds of faith and Om Tat Sat** — Krishna analyzes faith, food, sacrifice, austerity, and charity by the three gunas and expounds the threefold designation of Brahman, Om Tat Sat, used since creation to consecrate sacred acts _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/13-to-18-jnana-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 17)
- **Yudhiṣṭhira's Aśvamedha and the Anu-Gītā** — Yudhiṣṭhira performs the horse sacrifice to expiate the war, and Kṛṣṇa recaps his teaching in the Anu-Gītā _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 14 (Aśvamedhika Parva))
- **Aśvatthāman's grief-driven night-massacre** — Learning of his father's deceitful beheading, Aśvatthāman slaughters the sleeping Pāñcālas including Dhṛṣṭadyumna and is cursed by Kṛṣṇa to wander deathless for 3,000 years _(actors: Aśvatthāman, Dhṛṣṭadyumna, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/drona.md) — (Mahābhārata Sauptika Parva)
- **Death of Ghatotkacha** — Bhima's rakshasa son Ghatotkacha rampages by night and Karna spends his infallible Shakti spear, reserved for Arjuna, to kill him — a decisive turn _(actors: Ghatotkacha, Karna, Bhima)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva)
- **Death of the elders in the forest fire** — Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Gāndhārī and Kuntī retire to the forest and perish in a wildfire _(actors: Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Gāndhārī, Kuntī)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 15 (Āśramavāsika Parva))
- **Fall of Bhishma on the bed of arrows** — On day 10 Arjuna, shielded by Shikhandin, fells Bhishma, who lies on a bed of arrows choosing to die only at uttarayana _(actors: Bhishma, Arjuna, Shikhandin)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/06-bhishma-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Bhishma Parva)
- **Krishna delivers the final teaching of surrender (Charama Shloka)** — In the grand synthesis Krishna distinguishes sannyasa from tyaga, grades all things by the gunas, and gives the supreme final word, sarva-dharman parityajya, calling Arjuna to abandon all dharmas and take refuge in him alone _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/13-to-18-jnana-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 18.1-66)
- **Slaying of Jarasandha** — Krishna with Bhima and Arjuna engineers Bhima's wrestling victory that kills Jarasandha, freeing the kings he imprisoned and clearing the way for the Rajasuya _(actors: Krishna, Bhima, Arjuna, Jarasandha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.72; Mahabharata Sabha Parva)
- **The second dice game and thirteen-year exile** — A fresh dice game sends the Pāṇḍavas and Draupadī into thirteen years' exile, the casus belli of Kurukṣetra _(actors: Yudhiṣṭhira, Draupadī, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Sabhā Parva)
- **Arjuna's delusion is dispelled and he resolves to fight** — Arjuna declares his delusion destroyed and his memory regained by Krishna's grace, standing firm and vowing to carry out Krishna's word, fulfilling the teaching's purpose of moving him from despair to resolved action _(actors: Arjuna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/13-to-18-jnana-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 18.73)
- **Death of Abhimanyu in the cakra-vyuha** — Day 13: the 16-year-old Abhimanyu breaks into the wheel formation but cannot exit and is killed by six warriors ganging up unfairly _(actors: Abhimanyu, Drona, Karna, Jayadratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva)
- **Death of Drona** — Through Krishna's stratagem of the ambiguous report of Ashvatthama's death, the grieving Drona lays down arms and is beheaded by Dhrishtadyumna _(actors: Drona, Krishna, Bhima, Yudhishthira, Dhrishtadyumna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva)
- **Death of Sisupala at the Rajasuya** — At the Rajasuya, honored first, Krishna beheads Sisupala with the Sudarsana cakra after he exceeds the hundred forgiven offenses _(actors: Krishna, Sisupala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.74; Mahabharata Sabha Parva)
- **Fall of the Yādavas and death of Kṛṣṇa** — Gāndhārī's curse ripens as the Yādavas slay each other at Prabhāsa, Kṛṣṇa is killed by the hunter Jarā (marking Kali's onset) and Dvārakā sinks into the sea _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Yādavas, Jarā)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Mahābhārata 16 (Mausala Parva))
- **Krishna Slays Kamsa at Mathura** — Akrura brings Krishna to Mathura, where he breaks the great bow and kills the tyrant Kamsa _(actors: Krishna, Akrura, Kamsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.38-44)
- **The Vishvarupa theophany** — Granted a divine eye, Arjuna beholds Krishna's Universal Form, Time the world-destroyer, with all beings rushing into its mouths _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 11)
- **Sanjaya's epilogue benediction** — Sanjaya, having relayed the whole dialogue to blind Dhritarashtra, marvels at the wondrous discourse and pronounces the closing benediction that wherever Krishna and Arjuna are, there are fortune, victory, and morality _(actors: Sanjaya, Dhritarashtra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavad-gita/13-to-18-jnana-shatka.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 18.74-78)
- **Slaying of Jayadratha at false sunset** — Day 14: Krishna feigns sunset with his discus so Arjuna can fulfil his vow and behead Jayadratha, the boon recoiling on his father _(actors: Arjuna, Krishna, Jayadratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva)
- **Sudama's visit and reward** — Krishna's impoverished boyhood friend brings flattened rice and is silently granted lavish wealth, the paradigm of devotion and divine reciprocity _(actors: Krishna, Sudama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.80-81)
- **Uddhava's Mission to Vraja** — Krishna sends Uddhava to console the grieving gopis, occasioning the Bhramara-gita addressed to a bee _(actors: Krishna, Uddhava, the gopis)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.46-47)
- **Death of Bhurishravas** — Bhurishravas, about to behead Satyaki, loses his arm to Arjuna and is then beheaded by Satyaki while sitting in praya-fast _(actors: Bhurishravas, Satyaki, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva)
- **Deaths of Shalya and Shakuni** — On the eighteenth day Shalya is killed by Yudhishthira and Shakuni by Sahadeva as the last Kaurava brothers fall _(actors: Shalya, Yudhishthira, Shakuni, Sahadeva)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Shalya Parva)
- **Krishna Beheads Shishupala at the Rajasuya** — Krishna's Sudarshana beheads the rival cousin Shishupala when his hundred offences are filled at Yudhishthira's Rajasuya _(actors: Krishna, Shishupala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.74)
- **Reunion at Kurukshetra during the eclipse** — On an eclipse pilgrimage the Vraja folk and the Pandavas reunite with Krishna at Samanta-panchaka _(actors: Krishna, the gopis, Nanda, Yashoda, the Pandavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.82)
- **Arjuna's despondency at Kurukshetra** — Seeing kin arrayed for battle, Arjuna lays down his bow in moral crisis over killing teachers and family, opening the Gita's vishada-yoga _(actors: Arjuna, Krishna)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/ahimsa-and-hindu-ethics.md) — (Bhagavad Gita ch. 1)
- **Arjuna wins the Pāśupatāstra from Śiva** — After single combat with Śiva in his Kirāta hunter form, Arjuna wins the Pāśupatāstra — the central boon of his Himālayan tapas _(actors: Arjuna, Śiva)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva; Kirātārjunīya)
- **Bhishma waits for Uttarayana to die** — Bhishma famously withholds his death until the auspicious northward course of the Sun (Uttarayana) begins, illustrating the devayana half-year _(actors: Bhishma)_ → [details](../00-time-and-cosmology/hindu-calendar-and-panchanga.md) — (Mahabharata)
- **Birth and childhood of Kṛṣṇa (Harivaṃśa)** — The Harivaṃśa appendix supplies Kṛṣṇa's birth and childhood, absent from the main epic, bridging itihāsa to the Kṛṣṇa-centric Purāṇas _(actors: Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Harivaṃśa)
- **Death of Ghatotkaca and spent Vasavi Shakti** — In the night battle Karna is forced to spend his one-use Vasavi Shakti on the rampaging Ghatotkaca, secretly saving Arjuna's life _(actors: Ghatotkaca, Karna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva)
- **Duryodhana's fall** — Drawn out of hiding in a lake, Duryodhana fights Bhima with maces and Bhima smashes his thigh with an unfair blow on Krishna's hint, fulfilling his vow _(actors: Duryodhana, Bhima, Krishna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Shalya Parva)
- **Jayadratha's attempted abduction of Draupadī** — The Sindhu king Jayadratha tries to carry Draupadī off from the forest hermitage; the brothers rescue her and shave his head in shame _(actors: Jayadratha, Draupadī, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva)
- **Maya Builds the Pandavas' Hall at Indraprastha** — The asura architect Maya constructs the Pandavas' illusory assembly hall at Indraprastha, cited as a foundational feat of Sthapatyaveda architecture _(actors: Maya, the Pandavas)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/upavedas-vedangas-darshanas.md) — (Mahabharata)
- **Recovering a Brahmin's dead sons from Maha-Vishnu** — Krishna and Arjuna travel beyond the world to retrieve a Brahmin's lost children, revealing Krishna's supreme identity to Arjuna _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana X.89)
- **The Mausala Destruction of the Yadus** — The Yadava sages are cursed, and the iron-club fratricide at Prabhasa destroys the Yadavas as Krishna withdraws _(actors: The Yadavas, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11.1, 11.30-31)
- **Vyasa divides the Vedas** — In Dvapara Yuga the Vedas are divided by Vyasa because human memory and intellect have weakened _(actors: Vyasa)_ → [details](../00-time-and-cosmology/yugas-and-mahayuga.md) — (Vishnu Purana 1.3; Bhagavata Purana 1.4, 12.3)
- **Bhīma slays Kīcaka for harassing Sairandhrī** — Serving as the maid Sairandhrī in Virāṭa's court, Draupadī lures the lustful Kīcaka to a night tryst where Bhīma pounds him into a shapeless ball; the Upakīcakas who try to burn her on the pyre are also killed _(actors: Draupadī, Kīcaka, Bhīma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Virāṭa Parva)
- **Curse of the sages and Gandhari on the Yadava clan** — Yadava boys mock the rishis who curse the clan to destruction by an iron club, and the bereaved Gandhari also curses Krishna's line to perish _(actors: Yadava boys, sages, Gandhari, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana XI.1; Mausala Parva 1)
- **Sauptika night massacre and revival of Parikshit** — Ashvatthama with Kripa and Kritavarma raid the sleeping camp slaughtering Dhrishtadyumna, Shikhandi, and Draupadi's five sons, then loose the Brahmashiras at Uttara's womb; Krishna revives the unborn Parikshit and curses Ashvatthama to wander 3,000 years _(actors: Ashvatthama, Kripa, Kritavarma, Parikshit, Krishna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Sauptika Parva)
- **Aravan's battlefield self-sacrifice** — Aravan, son of Arjuna and the Naga princess Ulupi, offers himself to Kali before the Mahabharata war for Pandava victory, asking to be wed for one night; Krishna as Mohini weds and mourns him _(actors: Aravan, Arjuna, Ulupi, Kali, Krishna/Mohini)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/regional-and-oral-epics.md) — (Tamil Aravan Kalappali; Parata Venpa (9th c.); Villiputturar Makaparatam)
- **Dattatreya's Twenty-Four Gurus Recounted** — Within the Uddhava-gita, Krishna recounts the avadhuta Dattatreya's dialogue with King Yadu listing the twenty-four gurus he took from nature _(actors: Krishna, Dattatreya, Yadu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11.7-9)
- **Narayanastra released and countered** — Ashvatthama unleashes Vishnu's Narayana weapon; Krishna has the army submit unresisting so it dissipates harmlessly _(actors: Ashvatthama, Krishna, Bhima, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/07-drona-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Drona Parva)
- **The Uddhava-Gita delivered** — Krishna gives his last great discourse to Uddhava on jnana, bhakti, and the avadhuta's 24 gurus, then sends him to Badarikashrama _(actors: Krishna, Uddhava)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana XI.7-29)
- **Death of Duhshasana** — Bhima tears open Duhshasana's chest and drinks his blood, fulfilling his dice-hall vow; Draupadi binds her hair in the blood _(actors: Bhima, Duhshasana, Draupadi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/08-karna-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Karna Parva)
- **Krishna's Departure by the Hunter Jara** — The hunter Jara mistakes Krishna's foot for a deer and shoots him; Krishna ascends and Dvaraka sinks beneath the sea _(actors: Krishna, Jara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11.30-31)
- **Yadava fratricide at Prabhasa** — Drunken Yadavas turn the cursed reeds-turned-clubs on each other and almost the whole clan dies at Prabhasa _(actors: the Yadavas, Balarama, Satyaki, Kritavarma, Pradyumna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana XI.30; Mausala Parva 3)
- **Yudhishthira's reign and the Ashvamedha** — Yudhishthira is crowned at Hastinapura, Bhishma delivers the Shanti and Anushasana teachings before dying at the solstice, and a horse-sacrifice is performed _(actors: Yudhishthira, Bhishma)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Shanti, Anushasana, Ashvamedhika Parvas)
- **Departure of Balarama** — Balarama merges into yoga by the sea as a great white serpent issues from his mouth into the ocean _(actors: Balarama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana XI.30; Mausala Parva)
- **Fall of Duryodhana** — Drawn from a lake, Duryodhana duels Bhima with maces; Bhima smashes his thigh on Krishna's hint, fulfilling his vow _(actors: Duryodhana, Bhima, Krishna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Shalya Parva)
- **The sages' curse on Samba** — Krishna's son Samba, dressed as a pregnant woman to mock visiting sages, is cursed to bear an iron club that will doom the Yadava clan; ground to powder it regrows as deadly reed-grass _(actors: Samba, Vishvamitra, Durvasa, Narada)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva)
- **Death of Krishna marks onset of Kali Yuga** — The death of Krishna, traditionally on 17/18 February 3102 BCE, marks the onset of the Kali Yuga and the epoch of the Kali-samvat era _(actors: Krishna)_ → [details](../00-time-and-cosmology/hindu-calendar-and-panchanga.md) — (Surya Siddhanta; Puranic tradition)
- **Karṇa born with the Kavaca-Kuṇḍala; Indra extracts it** — Karṇa is born with invulnerable golden armor and earrings fused to his body, the gift of his father Sūrya; Indra extracts them by guile to make Karṇa killable _(actors: Karṇa, Sūrya, Indra)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divine-objects-and-treasures.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana/Karṇa Parvas)
- **The Mahabharata war and Krishna's departure mark the Dvapara-Kali transition** — The great Mahabharata war and Krishna's departure mark the turn from Dvapara into Kali Yuga, traditionally dated to 3102 BCE _(actors: Krishna, Pandavas, Kauravas)_ → [details](../00-time-and-cosmology/yugas-and-mahayuga.md) — (Mahabharata; Bhagavata Purana 1.18, 11.31, 12.2)
- **The Sauptika night massacre** — Ashvatthama raids the sleeping camp, killing Draupadi's five sons; his Brahmashiras aimed at Parikshit's womb is countered by Krishna, who curses him _(actors: Ashvatthama, Draupadi's sons, Krishna, Parikshit)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Sauptika Parva)
- **Yadava civil war at Prabhasa** — On pilgrimage the drunken Yadavas quarrel, the reeds turn to iron clubs in their hands, and the entire clan slaughters itself _(actors: the Yadavas, Satyaki, Kritavarma, Pradyumna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva)
- **Death of Balarama** — Balarama withdraws into yoga at the seashore and Shesha-naga issues from his mouth into the ocean as he departs the world _(actors: Balarama, Shesha)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva; Bhagavata Purana 11.30)
- **Death of Shakuni** — Sahadeva beheads Shakuni, the dice-master architect of the war, fulfilling his own dice-hall vow _(actors: Sahadeva, Shakuni, Uluka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/09-shalya-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Shalya Parva)
- **Garga names Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma** — The Yādava family-priest Garga performs the naming rite for Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma _(actors: Garga, Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rishi-lineages-and-gotras.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.8; Garga Saṃhitā)
- **Planets align in conjunction at the start of Kali Yuga** — The Surya Siddhanta sets all planets in conjunction at the start of Kali Yuga (18 Feb 3102 BCE), synchronizing the planetary clock with the cosmological clock _(actors: the grahas (planets))_ → [details](../00-time-and-cosmology/jyotisha-and-the-zodiac.md) — (Surya Siddhanta)
- **Submersion of Dwaraka** — As Krishna departs the sea reclaims Dwaraka; Arjuna evacuates the surviving women and children, who are raided by bandits en route _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Mausala Parva 7-8)
- **Death of Krishna** — Krishna sits in meditation under a pipal tree and the hunter Jara, mistaking his reddish sole for a deer, shoots an arrow piercing it; Krishna ascends to his abode, ending the avatar and marking the onset of Kali Yuga _(actors: Krishna, Jara)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva; Bhagavata Purana 11.30-11.31)
- **Onset of Kali Yuga at Krishna's departure** — Krishna's departure (traditionally 3102 BCE) marks the start of Kali Yuga and the Pandavas' final journey _(actors: Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana XI; Mausala Parva)
- **The mace-duel and fall of Duryodhana** — In the gada-yuddha Bhima, cued by Krishna, breaks Duryodhana's thighs with a foul below-the-belt blow, effectively ending the war _(actors: Bhima, Duryodhana, Krishna, Balarama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/09-shalya-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Shalya Parva)
- **The Uddhava-Gita** — Krishna gives his last great discourse to his devotee Uddhava on jnana, bhakti, and the avadhuta's 24 gurus before the end _(actors: Krishna, Uddhava)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/krishna-lila.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 11.7-11.29)
- **Viṣṇu slays the demon Pañcajana and takes the conch** — In the Mahābhārata/Skanda variant, Viṣṇu/Kṛṣṇa slays the demon Pañcajana (in whale-form, who had swallowed Sāndīpani's son) and seizes the conch he dwelt in, naming it Pāñcajanya _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Pañcajana, Sāndīpani)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divine-objects-and-treasures.md) — (Mahābhārata; Skanda Purāṇa; Panchajanya (Wikipedia))
- **Ashvatthama's night massacre** — Empowered by Shiva, Ashvatthama with Kripa and Kritavarman slaughters the sleeping Pandava camp, killing Dhrishtadyumna, Shikhandin and the Upapandavas _(actors: Ashvatthama, Kripa, Kritavarman, Dhrishtadyumna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/10-sauptika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sauptika Parva)
- **Curse of the sages on Samba** — Krishna's son Samba is dressed as a pregnant woman to mock sages, who curse the clan to be doomed by an iron club _(actors: Samba, Vishvamitra, Durvasa, Narada)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva)
- **Krishna's Vishvarupa theophany — 'I am Time'** — At the climax of the universal-form vision Krishna declares 'kalo'smi' — I am Time, the world-destroyer — revealing the cosmic person as the cosmic clock and Arjuna as its instrument _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../00-time-and-cosmology/kalachakra-wheel-of-time.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 11.32-33)
- **Submergence of Dvaraka** — As Krishna leaves, the sea swallows Dvaraka; Arjuna evacuates the survivors but is robbed en route, his powers fading with the age _(actors: Arjuna, the survivors)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva)
- **Sudarśana Cakra beheads Śiśupāla** — Kṛṣṇa's Sudarśana Cakra beheads Śiśupāla after his hundred offenses are exhausted _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Śiśupāla)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Sudarshana Chakra (Wikipedia); Mahābhārata)
- **Brahmashiras duel and curse of Ashvatthama** — Ashvatthama aims the Brahmashiras at Uttara's womb; Krishna vows to revive the child Parikshit, takes Ashvatthama's gem and curses him to wander 3,000 years _(actors: Ashvatthama, Arjuna, Krishna, Uttara, Vyasa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/10-sauptika-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Sauptika Parva)
- **Brahma-vimohana-lila (Bewilderment of Brahma)** — Doubting child-Krishna, the Brahma of our universe steals Krishna's cowherd boys and calves; Krishna duplicates them all for a year and reveals countless universes each with its own Brahma, humbling his pride _(actors: Brahma, Krishna)_ → [details](../00-time-and-cosmology/brahma-lifetime.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10.13-14; Brahma Samhita 5.40-48)
- **Draupadī demands Aśvatthāman's head after the night-raid** — After all five Draupadeyas are slain in the night-raid, Draupadī demands Aśvatthāman's head but accepts the gem from his crest instead _(actors: Draupadī, Aśvatthāman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Sauptika/Strī Parvas)
- **Karṇa loses his Nāgāstra against Arjuna** — Karṇa looses the nāgāstra (the serpent Aśvasena) at Arjuna's head, but Kṛṣṇa presses the chariot into the earth so it strikes only Arjuna's crown _(actors: Karṇa, Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa, Aśvasena)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Mahābhārata, Karṇa Parva)
- **Krishna delivers the Bhagavad Gita to Arjuna** — Krishna answers Arjuna's crisis by teaching svadharma, niskama-karma, and the imperishable atman, reconciling the warrior's duty with ahimsa _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/ahimsa-and-hindu-ethics.md) — (Bhagavad Gita 2.19-25, 2.47-48, 3.35, 18.47)
- **Krishna slays Narakasura (Naraka Chaturdashi)** — Krishna with Satyabhama slays the asura Narakasura of Pragjyotishapura and frees 16,000 captives, the South Indian Deepavali charter _(actors: Krishna, Satyabhama, Narakasura)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Vishnu Purana; Harivamsha)
- **Posthumous revelation of Karna's birth** — After the war Kunti reveals Karna was the eldest Pandava; the anguished Yudhishthira curses all women never to keep a secret _(actors: Kunti, Yudhishthira, Karna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/karna.md) — (Mahabharata Stri/Shanti Parva)
- **Bhagadatta's Vaiṣṇavāstra absorbed by Kṛṣṇa** — Bhagadatta looses the infallible Vaiṣṇavāstra at Arjuna, but Kṛṣṇa absorbs it onto his own chest, where it becomes the Vaijayantī garland _(actors: Bhagadatta, Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva)
- **Draupadī's death on the Himālayan ascent** — On the great departure toward heaven, Draupadī is the first of the six to fall dead, attributed by Yudhiṣṭhira to her partiality for Arjuna _(actors: Draupadī, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Mahāprasthānika Parva)
- **Sage's curse on Samba's iron club** — Yadava princes mock rishis with Samba dressed as a pregnant woman; the cursed iron club is ground to powder that becomes the deadly eraka grass _(actors: Samba, Vishvamitra, Narada, Kanva, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/16-mausala-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva; Vishnu Purana)
- **Aśvatthāman looses the Nārāyaṇāstra; Pāṇḍavas survive by submission** — Aśvatthāman discharges Viṣṇu's Nārāyaṇāstra, and Kṛṣṇa has the Pāṇḍava host drop their weapons and dismount so the auto-multiplying barrage finds no resistance _(actors: Aśvatthāman, Kṛṣṇa, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva)
- **Draupadī restored as Śrī in heaven** — In heaven Yudhiṣṭhira finds Draupadī revealed as the goddess Śrī/Lakṣmī, closing her prior-birth incarnation _(actors: Draupadī, Yudhiṣṭhira)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/draupadi.md) — (Mahābhārata Svargārohaṇa Parva)
- **Submergence of Dwaraka** — As Krishna leaves, the sea swallows Dwaraka; Arjuna evacuates survivors but is robbed en route as his powers fade _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/03-dvapara-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva)
- **The Yadava clan self-destructs at Prabhasa** — Thirty-six years after the war the drunken Vrishni-Andhakas slay one another with eraka-turned-iron clubs, fulfilling Gandhari's and the sages' curses _(actors: Satyaki, Kritavarman, Pradyumna, Krishna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/16-mausala-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva)
- **Death of Balarama and Krishna; sinking of Dvaraka** — Balarama departs as Shesha; the hunter Jara's arrow pierces Krishna's foot and he ascends, after which the sea swallows Dvaraka, ending the Dvapara Yuga _(actors: Krishna, Balarama, Jara, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/16-mausala-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Mausala Parva; Bhagavata Purana)
- **Śeṣa incarnates as Balarāma** — When Viṣṇu descends as Kṛṣṇa, Śeṣa descends as his elder brother Balarāma, whose serpent-nature departs his mouth as a white nāga at his death _(actors: Śeṣa, Balarāma, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/shesha-ananta.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa; Viṣṇu Purāṇa)
- **Indra gives Karṇa the Vāsava-śakti for his kavaca-kuṇḍala** — Indra trades his one-use infallible dart, the Vāsava-śakti, to Karṇa in exchange for his birth-armor and earrings (kavaca-kuṇḍala) _(actors: Indra, Karṇa)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Mahābhārata, Karṇa/Droṇa Parva)
- **Karṇa spends the Vāsava-śakti on Ghaṭotkaca** — Forced by Ghaṭotkaca's rampage, Karṇa expends his one-use Vāsava-śakti dart (reserved for Arjuna) to kill Bhīma's son Ghaṭotkaca _(actors: Karṇa, Ghaṭotkaca, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Mahābhārata, Droṇa Parva)
- **Arjuna beheads Karṇa with the Añjalikāstra** — Arjuna uses Indra's crescent-bladed Añjalikāstra to behead Karṇa, who had lost his Bhārgavāstra knowledge to a curse at the decisive moment _(actors: Arjuna, Karṇa)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Mahābhārata, Karṇa Parva)
- **Śiva grants Barbarīka the three infallible arrows** — Śiva grants Ghaṭotkaca's son Barbarīka three infallible arrows able to end the entire war in a moment — one marks targets to destroy, one to save, the third destroys/binds the marked _(actors: Śiva, Barbarīka)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (folk Mahābhārata traditions (Khāṭū Śyām))
- **Sudarśana obscures the sun to trap Jayadratha** — Kṛṣṇa's Sudarśana Cakra obscures the sun at Kurukṣetra to trick Jayadratha into the open so Arjuna can fulfill his vow _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna, Jayadratha)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Sudarshana Chakra (Wikipedia); Mahābhārata)
- **Brahmaśiras duel of Arjuna and Aśvatthāman; killing and revival of Parīkṣit** — After Kurukṣetra, Arjuna and Aśvatthāman loose Brahmaśiras at each other; Vyāsa and Nārada intervene, Arjuna withdraws his but Aśvatthāman cannot, redirecting it at Uttarā's womb to kill the unborn Parīkṣit, whom Kṛṣṇa revives _(actors: Arjuna, Aśvatthāman, Vyāsa, Nārada, Kṛṣṇa, Parīkṣit, Uttarā)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sauptika Parva; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 1)
- **Aśvatthāman cursed for the Brahmaśiras misuse** — For aiming the unwithdrawable Brahmaśiras at the Pāṇḍava line, Aśvatthāman is cursed with the loss of his head-jewel and a festering wound enduring for millennia _(actors: Aśvatthāman, Kṛṣṇa)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Mahābhārata, Sauptika Parva; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 1)
- **Burning of the Khāṇḍava forest and gift of the Gāṇḍīva to Arjuna** — When Agni begs Arjuna and Kṛṣṇa to help him consume the Khāṇḍava forest, Varuṇa gives Arjuna the bow Gāṇḍīva (made by Brahmā) with two inexhaustible quivers _(actors: Agni, Arjuna, Kṛṣṇa, Varuṇa)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Mahābhārata, Ādi Parva; Gandiva (Wikipedia))
- **Pārijātaharaṇa — Kṛṣṇa steals the Pārijāta from Indra** — Kṛṣṇa uproots the Pārijāta tree from Indra's heaven and plants it in Satyabhāmā's courtyard, provoking a brief war with Indra _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Satyabhāmā, Rukmiṇī, Indra)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divine-objects-and-treasures.md) — (Hari-vaṃśa / Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10; Parijata (Wikipedia))
- **Sūrya gifts the Syamantaka gem to Satrājit** — The sun-gem Syamantaka, source of Sūrya's brilliance and yielding eight bhāras of gold daily, is gifted by Sūrya to the Yādava king Satrājit _(actors: Sūrya, Satrājit)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divine-objects-and-treasures.md) — (Syamantaka (Wikipedia); Vishnu Purana; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56)
- **The Syamantaka affair — Kṛṣṇa fights Jāmbavān and weds Jāmbavatī** — Falsely accused of stealing the Syamantaka after Prasena is killed by a lion and the gem taken by Jāmbavān, Kṛṣṇa fights the bear-king for 28 days (or 21), who yields the gem and his daughter Jāmbavatī in marriage; Kṛṣṇa returns it and weds Satyabhāmā _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Jāmbavān, Jāmbavatī, Prasena, Satrājit, Satyabhāmā)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divine-objects-and-treasures.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.56–57; Viṣṇu Purāṇa; Syamantaka (Wikipedia))
- **Kṛṣṇa sounds the Pāñcajanya to open the Kurukṣetra war** — Kṛṣṇa blows his conch Pāñcajanya, with Arjuna's Devadatta and the Pāṇḍava conches, to open the Bhagavad-gītā and the Kurukṣetra war _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Arjuna, the Pāṇḍavas)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divine-objects-and-treasures.md) — (Bhagavad-gītā 1.15–18; Panchajanya (Wikipedia))
- **Sūrya gives Yudhiṣṭhira the Akṣaya-pātra** — After Yudhiṣṭhira's penance in the Gaṅgā, Sūrya gives the Pāṇḍavas the inexhaustible food-vessel Akṣaya-pātra, yielding unlimited food each day until Draupadī takes her own meal _(actors: Sūrya, Yudhiṣṭhira, Draupadī, Dhaumya)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divine-objects-and-treasures.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva; Akshaya Patra (Wikipedia))
- **Kṛṣṇa sates Durvāsā with a single rice-grain from the Akṣaya-pātra** — When Durvāsā and his thousand disciples arrive after Draupadī has eaten, Kṛṣṇa eats a single clinging rice-grain from the empty pot and thereby sates the sage's whole party _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Durvāsā, Draupadī)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divine-objects-and-treasures.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva)
- **Kāliya-mardana — Kṛṣṇa dances on the serpent Kāliya** — The boy Kṛṣṇa leaps into the Yamunā at Vṛndāvana, is coiled by the venomous Nāga Kāliya, then dances on his hoods until he submits and departs, sparing him at his wives' plea _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Kāliya)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/sacred-flora-and-fauna.md) — (tell-a-tale, Kaliya Daman; Wikipedia)
- **Kṛṣṇa masters the 64 kalās and 14 vidyās in 64 days** — After slaying Kaṃsa and his upanayana, Kṛṣṇa (with Balarāma) studies under guru Sāndīpani at Avantī/Ujjayinī and masters the 14 vidyās and 64 kalās within 64 days _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, Sāndīpani)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/sixty-four-kalas.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.45)
- **Kṛṣṇa restores Sāndīpani's drowned son as guru-dakṣiṇā** — As guru-dakṣiṇā, Kṛṣṇa restores Sāndīpani's drowned son from the sea/Yama's realm _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Sāndīpani)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/sixty-four-kalas.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 10.45)
- **Krishna's life and the Bhagavad Gita** — Krishna slays his tyrant uncle Kamsa, lifts Govardhana hill, and as Arjuna's charioteer at Kurukshetra delivers the Bhagavad Gita; his departure in 3102 BCE marks the start of Kali Yuga _(actors: Krishna, Kamsa, Arjuna)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/dashavatara.md) — (Mahabharata; Bhagavata Purana 10-11; Harivamsa)
- **Balarama's deeds** — Balarama, incarnation of the serpent Shesha and Krishna's elder brother, slays the asuras Dhenuka and Pralamba and drags the Yamuna river with his plow _(actors: Balarama, Dhenuka, Pralamba)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/dashavatara.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 10; Vishnu Purana)
- **Kṛṣṇa destroys Śālva's flying fortress Saubha** — Kṛṣṇa destroys Saubha, the airborne fortress-city of the asura king Śālva, after it attacks Dvārakā _(actors: Kṛṣṇa, Śālva)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/vahanas-and-celestial-vehicles.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva)
- **Vyāsa redacts the Vedas at the Dvapara–Kali junction** — Vyāsa (Krishna Dvaipayana) arranges and redacts the four Vedas at the Dvapara–Kali junction (~3102 BCE in the scriptural frame), earning the title Veda-Vyāsa _(actors: Vyāsa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/README.md) — (Vishnu Purana; Vayu Purana)
- **Vyāsa composes the Mahābhārata** — Vyāsa composes the Mahābhārata, chronicling the Kuru succession war at Kurukṣetra and containing the Bhagavad Gītā, with Krishna's departure marking the start of Kali Yuga _(actors: Vyāsa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/README.md) — (Mahabharata (Vyasa); Itihasa (Wikipedia))
- **The eight Vasus steal Vasiṣṭha's cow Nandinī** — The eight Vasus, led by Prabhāsa at his wife's urging, steal the wish-cow Nandinī from the sage Vasiṣṭha and are cursed to be born among mortals _(actors: Vasus, Prabhāsa, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/the-thirty-three-devas.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva (Sambhava Parva 1.99))
- **Gaṅgā drowns seven Vasu-newborns; the eighth becomes Bhīṣma** — The river-goddess Gaṅgā bears the cursed Vasus by King Śāntanu and drowns the first seven at birth to release them; Śāntanu stays her hand at the eighth, Prabhāsa, who lives on as Bhīṣma _(actors: Gaṅgā, Śāntanu, Prabhāsa, Bhīṣma)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/the-thirty-three-devas.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Arjuna requests the Gita teaching again (Anugita frame)** — After the Kurukshetra war, as Krishna prepares to leave for Dvaraka, Arjuna confesses he has forgotten the supreme teaching of Kurukshetra and begs Krishna to repeat it _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/anugita.md) — (Mahabharata 14.16 (Ashvamedhika Parva))
- **Krishna smears color on Radha and the gopis (Holi)** — Krishna playfully smears color on Radha and the gopis in Vrindavan, the devotional origin of Holi's play of colors _(actors: Krishna, Radha, gopis)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Krishna Lila)
- **Krishna declines to repeat the Gita verbatim** — Krishna rebukes Arjuna's lapse and declares he cannot reproduce the original discourse word-for-word since it was uttered in a yogic trance, choosing instead to convey the same truths through an old narrative _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/anugita.md) — (Mahabharata 14.16)
- **Krishna speaks the Anugita to Arjuna** — In peacetime after the war, Krishna delivers the Anugita, a jnana-and-Samkhya seminar restating the Gita through embedded ancient stories, within the Ashvamedhika Parva _(actors: Krishna, Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/anugita.md) — (Mahabharata 14, Anugita (Telang, SBE vol. 8))
